Method: 构建基于单一俯视RGB相机的模块化框架，集成目标检测、手部运动跟踪、区域感知交互检测、有限状态机流程验证、面向任务的姿态分类以及可配置视觉引导；在气动活塞装配与拆卸任务中，8名参与者在完整引导、中等引导、无引导三种辅助条件下进行实验，采用混合效应模型分析运动质量复合指标、完成时间与失败阶段数。

Result: 混合效应分析发现辅助条件与运动质量复合指标存在关联，中等引导与无引导条件下的估计得分低于完整引导；但条件×试次交互不显著，学习轨迹差异仅为描述性；完成时间与失败阶段数未检出条件差异。

Conclusion: 研究证明视觉感知、流程验证与可配置操作反馈可在单相机工作流中集成；样本量有限导致学习效应证据不足；依据操作者专业水平自适应选择引导策略是后续设计目标。

Abstract: Manual assembly remains difficult to monitor because operator actions, components, and task progress must be interpreted together. This paper presents a modular framework based on a single overhead red-green-blue (RGB) camera, combining object detection, hand-motion tracking, region-aware interaction detection, finite-state verification, task-specific pose classification, and configurable visual guidance. The system was evaluated during pneumatic-piston assembly and disassembly with eight participants under three assistance conditions: full, mid, and no guidance. Mixed-effects analysis identified an association between assistance condition and a composite measure of motion quality, with lower estimated scores under mid and no guidance relative to full guidance. However, the condition-by-trial interaction was not statistically significant, and the estimated differences in learning trajectories remain descriptive. No condition differences were detected in completion time or failed-stage counts. The study demonstrates the integration of visual perception, procedural verification, and configurable operator feedback in a single-camera workflow. Selecting assistance according to operator expertise remains a design objective for future investigation.

</details>


### [70] [Can Reasoning and Image Description Enhance Yes/No Visual Mathematical Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27302)
*Guangcheng Song,Weichuan Wang,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建配对评测集，检验显式推理（CoT提示）与文本图像描述对开源VLM二分类视觉数学判断题的影响，发现CoT能提升性能（尤其配对一致性准确率），而文本描述替代或增补图像时收益不稳定甚至有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在通用视觉任务上表现强劲，但对数学图像的理解决策能力仍有限；作者希望厘清显式推理与文本化图像描述这两种常见干预手段能否真正提升VLM在yes/no视觉数学题上的判断能力。

Method: 构建配对（paired）评测集，在多种提示与输入条件下基准测试主流开源VLM；采用Chain-of-Thought提示引入显式推理，并以文本图像描述替换或增补原始视觉输入，从而隔离并评估数学理解能力，同时以配对一致性准确率衡量判断稳定性。

Result: 显式推理可提升整体性能，在配对一致性准确率上提升尤为明显；文本图像描述并未带来稳定增益，且可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 推理能力对稳定的视觉数学判断更为关键，而基于描述的中介表征需要更精细的设计；配对评测范式可作为诊断VLM视觉数学理解的有效工具。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on general visual tasks, yet their ability to understand mathematical images remains limited. This paper investigates whether explicit reasoning and textual image descriptions can enhance VLMs' performance on binary yes/no visual mathematical questions. To achieve this, we curated a paired evaluation set to benchmark prominent open-source VLMs under varying prompt and input conditions. In particular, we leverage Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting and use textual descriptions, either replacing or augmenting visual inputs, to isolate and evaluate mathematical comprehension. Our results show that explicit reasoning improves performance, especially in paired consistency accuracy, while textual image descriptions do not consistently help and may degrade performance. These findings suggest that reasoning is important for stable visual mathematical judgement, whereas description-based intermediates require more careful design.

</details>


### [71] [BioCL-POS: Biological Completer-aware Learning with Predictive Observation Selection - A Wild Ginseng Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27316)
*Yulu Wang,Tingting Yao,Taolue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BioCL-POS——一个外挂式输入选择器，在不重新训练补全器的情况下，从已观测点云中挑选子集，使冻结的点云补全模型对缺失区域的几何恢复更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人参根形态是种植者与贸易商判断野山参的重要依据，但根体断裂会遮蔽关键结构特征，因此需要点云补全提供几何参考。通常假设"观测点越多越好"，但本文质疑该假设：在补全器固定的情况下，输入更多观测点是否一定带来更好的缺失区域恢复效果。

Method: 提出 BioCL-POS 外部选择器，预测固定补全器对候选输入的空间响应：对每个候选输入，预测共享空间位置到其补全结果最近点的位移与距离，据此估计恢复质量，并用真实补全结果的测量值进行监督。推理阶段从观测几何中选出输入，补全器仅运行一次。实验在人参与树木数据的 5%、10%、20% 缺失设置下评估四个冻结补全器，并对人参额外测试 30% 缺失。

Result: 在 5–20% 缺失率下，BioCL-POS 在两个数据集、四个补全器上均优于"全输入补全"以及五种替代选择器，获得更低的平均重建误差、更高的平均 F-score 与覆盖率。30% 缺失的人参实验亦被纳入考察。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过选择观测子集可在不重训补全器的前提下提升几何恢复质量，为后续形态学分析提供更准确的几何参考，也说明"观测越多越好"并非普遍成立。

Abstract: Growers and traders rely on root morphology to assess wild ginseng, yet breakage can obscure important structural traits. We study point cloud completion to provide geometric references for morphological analysis. Does providing more observed points always improve 3D completion? We test whether selected subsets improve recovery of the original missing region while keeping the pretrained completer fixed. We introduce BioCL-POS, an external selector that predicts how the completer responds to candidate inputs. For each candidate, we predict the displacement and distance from shared spatial locations to the nearest points in its completion. We use these spatial responses to estimate recovery quality and supervise them with measurements from actual completions. At inference, we select an input from the observed geometry and run the completer once. We evaluate four frozen completers on controlled gaps in ginseng and tree data at 5%,10%, and 20% omission, and examine 30% omission on ginseng. At 5--20% omission, we outperform full-input completion and all five alternative selectors on both datasets with all four completers. We achieve lower mean reconstruction error and higher mean F-score and coverage. We thus show that selecting observations can improve geometric recovery without retraining the completer, providing more accurate geometric references for subsequent morphological analysis.

</details>


### [72] [SAVIOR: Sample-efficient Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for OCR Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27319)
*Akshata A Bhat,Sharath Naganna,Saiful Haq,Prashant Khatri,Krishna Chaitanya Reddy Tamataam,Neha Arun,Niyati Chhaya,Pushpak Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SAVIOR样本高效数据策管方法与PaIRS结构感知评测指标，通过微调Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct提升金融场景OCR鲁棒性，词召回率达0.93，下游文档问答F1达0.89，优于GPT-4o、Mistral-OCR、PaddleOCR-VL和DeepSeek-OCR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR流水线与视觉语言模型在标准基准上准确率很高，但在金融流程中关键的真实文档模式上系统性失效，如垂直/旋转文本、Logo内嵌供应商名称、细小字体的合规条款、退化扫描件和复杂多栏排版。这类样本在公开数据集中占比不足，却构成实际失败案例的相当大比例。

Method: 提出SAVIOR——一种样本高效的数据策管方法，针对上述高影响失败场景筛选/构造训练数据（SAVIOR-Train），用于适配视觉语言模型的金融OCR能力；同时提出PaIRS——结构感知评测指标，通过比较token之间的成对空间关系来度量版面保真度。

Result: 用SAVIOR-Train微调Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct后，词召回率达0.93；将所得版面感知OCR输出作为文档问答上下文时，F1达0.89，超过GPT-4o、Mistral-OCR、PaddleOCR-VL、DeepSeek-OCR等开源与闭源模型。

Conclusion: 针对高影响失败模式的样本高效数据策管可在少量数据下显著提升金融OCR鲁棒性，且结构感知指标PaIRS能更好衡量版面保真度；版面感知的OCR输出可作为高质量上下文直接增益下游文档问答性能。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) pipelines and vision-language models exhibit high accuracy on standard OCR and document-understanding benchmarks, yet they systematically underperform on document patterns that are operationally critical in financial workflows. These patterns include vertical or rotated text, logo-embedded vendor names, fine-print regulatory clauses, degraded scans, and complex multi-column layouts. While underrepresented in public datasets, they constitute a substantial portion of real-world failure cases. We introduce SAVIOR, a sample-efficient data curation methodology that targets such high-impact failure scenarios to adapt vision-language models for robust financial OCR. We further propose PaIRS, a structure-aware evaluation metric that measures layout fidelity by comparing pairwise spatial relationships between tokens. Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct with SAVIOR-Train achieves a word recall of 0.93. When the resulting layout-aware OCR output is used as context for document question answering, it attains an F1 score of 0.89, outperforming both open- and closed-source models such as GPT-4o, Mistral-OCR, PaddleOCR-VL, and DeepSeek-OCR.

</details>


### [73] [Zing-1.0: A Playable World System Built on Controllable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27336)
*Wenbo Zhang,Guoli Zhang,Xuewen Yu,Xueliang Yang,Jiangqiao Yan,Teng Xia,Xinmiao Wu,Zeyang Tian,Haoze Sun,Jinghui Niu,Hongli Ning,Zhiyuan Liu,Xinna Liu,Weimin Liu,Jialin Liu,Tianpeng Li,Kejun Li,Jun Li,Jiawen Li,Yirong He,Haoyuan Guo,Bosheng Gong,Junhao Gao,Xiaoxiao Fu,Xuezhi Chen,Shengdong Chen,Mingyang Chen,Diying Chen,Yinglun Chai,Yiwen Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Zing-1.0是一个基于可控渲染（Controllable Rendering）的“可玩世界系统”（Playable World System）：可执行引擎按世界规则解算玩家动作并生成代理视频，流式视频模型据此合成玩家视角画面，运行时将其回传以影响后续动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可玩世界/交互式视频生成缺乏与实现无关的统一规范，且需同时兼顾规则一致的可执行行为与高质量、低延迟的实时视觉合成。

Method: 1) 提出Controllable Rendering范式，定义世界构建、世界执行、观测合成与交互运行时的接口，给出首个与实现无关的可玩世界系统规范；2) WorldKit提供结构化创作工具与可复用角色/相机/控制组件，供编码智能体构建可执行代理世界，分离可执行行为与最终外观；3) 将MiniMax-H3改造为块因果自回归生成与增量解码，代理视频约束结构与运动，文本与场景/主体参考指定外观，多任务训练并蒸馏为少步去噪；4) 交互运行时分发动作并支持局内prompt与参考更新，采用跨块联合执行与压缩K/V的稀疏注意力降低推理开销。

Result: 渲染器在OpenVE（3.90）与LongV2VBench（3.44）上领先对比的流式编辑器；StreamEval上指令编辑57.86、参考编辑54.06，均超已公布基线。服务端在单H200跑DiT、两张H100分别编解码，832×480下稳态吞吐23.82 FPS。

Conclusion: 该工作以“可控渲染”为核心，首次给出可玩世界系统的实现无关规范，并通过AI原生引擎、流式渲染器与交互基础设施的组合，在视觉质量与实时性上同时取得较优表现。

Abstract: We present Zing-1.0, a Playable World System built on Controllable Rendering. An executable engine resolves player actions under the world's rules and produces proxy video from the updated state. A streaming video model synthesizes the player's view from this proxy, and the runtime delivers it to inform subsequent actions. (1) Principle and system. We formulate Controllable Rendering and define the interfaces among world construction, world execution, observation synthesis, and the interaction runtime. To our knowledge, this is the first implementation-independent specification of a Playable World System. (2) AI-native engine. WorldKit provides structured authoring tools and reusable character, camera, and control components for coding agents to construct executable proxy worlds. It supports extensible interactions and precise geometric motion control while separating executable behavior from final visual appearance. (3) Streaming renderer. We adapt MiniMax-H3 for block-causal autoregressive generation and incremental decoding. Proxy video constrains structure and motion, while text and separate scene and subject references specify appearance. We train the generator on diverse tasks and distill it to generate each block in a few denoising steps. The renderer leads the compared streaming editors on OpenVE (3.90) and LongV2VBench (3.44). On StreamEval, Zing-1.0 scores 57.86 on instruction editing and 54.06 on reference editing, exceeding the published baselines on both tracks. (4) Interaction infrastructure. We build an Interaction Runtime that routes player actions to WorldKit while supporting in-play prompt and reference updates. Cross-block joint execution and sparse attention with compressed K/V reduce inference overhead. With the DiT on a single H200 and encoding and decoding on one H100 each, the serving system achieves a steady-state throughput of 23.82 FPS at $832\times480$.

</details>


### [74] [Unimodal Shortcuts and Joint Scene-Intent Dependence in Vision-Language Safety Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27339)
*Guijia Zhang,Hongbo Qiu,Guohao Cai,Hao Zheng,Jiajun Zha,Hanqing Wang,Zhenhao Zhang,Huihao Jing,Shing-Chi Cheung,Haoxuan Che,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有情境安全基准可通过单模态捷径被解决，线性探针虽能线性解码"场景×意图"联合信息，但并非证据表明存在融合机制，多模态安全评测需引入排除单模态捷径的对照设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型的安全性判断常依赖图像（新鲜食材问烹饪方法无害，变质食材则有害），而现有情境安全基准的高准确率无法说明模型的安全表征是否同时编码场景与用户意图，因为基准往往仅凭单一模态即可被解出。

Method: 审计五个公开情境安全基准，检验其是否存在单模态捷径（仅图像预测标签、同图提示对的纯文本探针）；构建受控评测，将安全/危险场景与观察/行动请求交叉，使任何单模态规则组排序准确率不超过0.500；在 Qwen3-VL-8B 与 SmolVLM2-2.2B 中间状态上训练线性探针，并与拼接独立编码的图像、文本状态的方案对比，同时测试跨图像、跨基准的泛化性。

Result: 五个基准均存在单模态捷径，同图提示对下纯文本探针达 0.9645 AUROC；在受控评测上，线性探针分别达 0.958 与 0.930 组排序准确率，说明场景-意图联合是可线性解码的；但拼接独立编码的图像与文本状态即可恢复大部分信号，探针跨未见图像与其他基准泛化很差。

Conclusion: 可解码性不等于融合机制；线性探针适合廉价筛查但泛化有限；可靠的多模态安全评测必须先用对照设计排除单模态捷径，才能将准确率解读为联合理解证据。

Abstract: The safety of a request to a vision-language model often depends on the image: asking how to serve a dish is harmless for fresh food and harmful for spoiled food. High accuracy on situational safety benchmarks does not tell us whether a model's safety representation encodes both the scene and the user's intent, because a benchmark can often be solved from one modality alone. Studying frozen vision-language safety representations, we audit five public situational benchmarks and find that each admits a unimodal shortcut: images alone predict the labels, and on same-image prompt pairs a probe that never sees the photograph reaches 0.9645 AUROC. We therefore build a controlled evaluation that crosses safe and hazardous scenes with observation and action requests, so that no single-modality rule can exceed 0.500 group-ranking accuracy. On this control, linear probes on intermediate states of Qwen3-VL-8B and SmolVLM2-2.2B reach 0.958 and 0.930, showing that the scene-intent conjunction is linearly decodable; concatenating separately encoded image and text states already recovers most of this signal, so decodability is not evidence of a fusion mechanism. The same probes screen cheaply but generalize poorly to unseen images and other benchmarks. Reliable multimodal safety evaluation therefore needs controls that rule out unimodal shortcuts before accuracy is read as evidence of joint understanding.

</details>


### [75] [CoGITAtion: Collaborative Gaze-based Interpretable Task-adaptive Representation for Privacy-sensitive Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27340)
*Mayar Elfares,Yasmine Elfares,Andreas Bulling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoGITAtion 是一个面向垂直分布式注视数据的隐私保护推理框架，将变长注视记录转化为可解释的注视转移图与描述符，并用任务与可用性门控实现任务自适应建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于注视的分析在隐私敏感应用中能提供丰富的行为信息，但实际部署受制于数据异构、样本稀缺与分布式存储等问题。

Method: 本地将变长注视记录转换为紧凑可解释的注视转移图与描述符；采用任务自适应模型，通过任务门控与可用性门控兼容异构及缺失的特征块，避免不必要的私有计算；并引入公开可用性门控裁剪可被公开信息确定的分支。

Result: 共享组件捕获可跨任务泛化的模式，任务特定组件在不增大模型规模的前提下提供额外容量；公开可用性门控在不改变预测的情况下减少加密计算量。

Conclusion: CoGITAtion 支持注视模型复用、高效的隐私保护部署，并在数据所有者可用性不完整时保持稳健推理，同时保留可解释的注视表征。

Abstract: Gaze-based analysis provides rich behavioural information for privacy-sensitive applications, but deployment is challenged by heterogeneous, scarce, and distributed data. We present CoGITAtion (Collaborative Gaze-based Interpretable Task-Adaptive Representation), a framework for privacy-preserving inference over vertically distributed gaze data. CoGITAtion transforms variable-length gaze recordings into compact, interpretable gaze-transition graphs and descriptors computed locally by each data owner. Task-adaptive models accommodate heterogeneous and missing feature blocks through task and availability gating, avoiding unnecessary private computation. Results show that shared components capture patterns that generalise across tasks, while task-specific components provide capacity without increasing model size. Public availability gating further reduces encrypted computation by removing branches determined by publicly known task and owner availability without altering predictions. Overall, CoGITAtion enables gaze model reuse, efficient privacy-preserving deployment, and robust inference under incomplete owner availability while retaining interpretable gaze representations.

</details>


### [76] [ReSA: Preserving Reference-Specified Foreground in In-Context Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27341)
*Dezhao Su,Bingxu Hou,Jingdi Lei,Lin Qian,Likai Wang,Yunyun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReSA框架，用共享参考核统一查询适应与掩码选择，在十个基准上取得最高平均mIoU（1-shot 62.6%，5-shot 71.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文分割需将参考指定的前景准确迁移到查询图像，既要恢复目标区域又要抑制无关预测，现有方法在预测精化与掩码形成之间缺乏一致的参考信息利用。

Method: 以共享参考核为核心：用前景/背景参考原型约束适应过程中的预测变化，并通过参考加权重建引导前景选择；在适应后的多预测间进行选择，并使边界精化与所选前景阈值对齐；冻结视觉编码器加轻量测试时适应，无需离线任务训练；同时给出连接参考响应变化与软重建的理论界。

Result: 消融显示前景保持改善、假阳性区域被有效抑制；定性结果掩码更完整、孤立伪影更少；在十个物体、部件及跨域分割基准上平均mIoU最高，1-shot 62.6%、5-shot 71.8%。

Conclusion: 共享参考引导能在预测精化与掩码形成间保持一致的参考使用，配合测试时适应即可实现高效且泛化性强的上下文分割。

Abstract: In-context segmentation uses annotated references to specify the target and its extent in a query image. Accurately transferring this reference-specified foreground requires recovering target regions while suppressing irrelevant predictions. We propose ReSA, a reference-guided framework that unifies query adaptation and mask selection through a shared reference kernel. ReSA leverages foreground and background reference prototypes to constrain prediction changes during adaptation and guide foreground selection through reference-weighted reconstruction. This shared guidance promotes consistent use of reference information across prediction refinement and mask formation. ReSA further selects among adapted predictions and aligns boundary refinement with the selected foreground threshold. We establish a theoretical bound connecting reference-response changes during adaptation and soft reconstruction. With a frozen visual encoder and lightweight test-time adaptation, ReSA requires no offline task training. Ablation studies demonstrate improved foreground retention and effective suppression of false-positive regions, while qualitative results show more complete masks with fewer isolated artifacts. Across ten object, part, and cross-domain segmentation benchmarks, ReSA achieves the highest average mIoU among the evaluated methods, reaching 62.6% in 1-shot and 71.8% in 5-shot.

</details>


### [77] [PAVE: A Harness for Geometrically Consistent Road Network Modeling with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27352)
*Yajun Liu,Jinxi Wu,Juan Pablo Millán Raya,Xuesong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 交通模型依赖精确到车道与转向的路网，但手工提取费时、开放地图数据常不完整。作者提出PAVE框架：由代码做可测量的事、以交通工程规则作校验、VLM只做开放式判断且绑定网络ID，从而把信号交叉口车道布局的完全正确率从49–64%提升到86–91%，并在真实路网编辑任务中16/18通过全部检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通仿真与规划模型的可信度取决于底层路网的精度（车道、交叉口转向）。实践中，从业者要么从GIS文件手工补齐交叉口与转向数据（每个基准年、每个设计方案都要重复），要么使用开放地图数据；而OSM车道标签在测试的44个进口道中仅13个布局正确，开放数据普遍缺失或错误。卫星与街景影像覆盖全部44个进口道且能显示标线，但VLM直接读取时会丢失几何一致性（同一车道需在卫星裁剪图、不同位姿街景与ID标注网络间对应）。

Method: 提出PAVE（Perceive–Anchor–Verify–Edit）流水线式框架，核心是任务分工：代码负责一切可量测的量；交通工程知识以通用规则与检查形式注入；VLM仅承担开放式判断，且每个判断锚定到网络ID，使检查能够捕获与测量值、二次读数或交通规则相矛盾的答案。车道布局提取时按进口道逐个读取、在描迹车道上进行（主要增益来源）。路网编辑时，模型仅命名设施各部分对应的link与node，由代码依据设计模板生成编辑。

Result: 在信号交叉口与人标注对比评分：单次请求的完全正确率为49–64%，PAVE使两个VLM达到86–91%；增益主要来自"逐进口道、基于描迹车道"的读取方式。真实路网编辑至规划任务书：18项编辑中16项通过全部网络检查（模型自写代码时为0项）；仅2项含设计或编码错误（对照为7项）。另给出VLM几何一致性失效位置分析，并匹配每类失败对应的检查项。

Conclusion: 将可测量工作交给代码、领域知识写成规则检查、把开放式判断留给模型并以网络ID锚定，可显著提高车道级路网构建与编辑的准确性与可验证性；单独的VLM或让模型自行编写代码均不可靠，而该分工方式兼具精度与审计能力。

Abstract: Transportation models, whether they simulate traffic or support planning, are only as reliable as the road networks they run on, down to the lanes and turns at each intersection. Practitioners still take such a network from GIS files and supply its junction and movement data by hand, a task that recurs with each new base year and design alternative, or they generate it from open map data. OpenStreetMap's lane tags give the correct layout for only 13 of the 44 approaches we test; open map data are often incomplete or inaccurate. Satellite and street-level imagery, in contrast, cover all 44 and show their lanes as painted. Vision-language models (VLMs) can read such imagery, but asked directly they lose geometric consistency when the same lane must be matched across a satellite crop, street-level photos taken from different poses and an ID-labelled network. We therefore present PAVE (Perceive, Anchor, Verify, Edit), a harness in which code measures what can be measured, traffic engineering knowledge enters as general rules and checks, and the model makes only the open judgements, each anchored to network IDs so that checks catch answers that contradict a measurement, a second reading or a traffic rule. Scored against human labels at signalised intersections, PAVE raises the share of exactly correct lane layouts from 49-64% with a single request to 86-91% for two VLMs, with most of the gain coming from reading one approach at a time on traced lanes. The same division of work edits a real network to a planning brief: the model names the links and nodes of each part of a facility, and code builds the edit from a design template. Of 18 edits, 16 pass every network check, against none when the model writes its own code, and 2 contain a design or coding error, against 7. An analysis shows where VLMs lose geometric consistency and which check catches each failure.

</details>


### [78] [Borrow the Style, Follow the Prompt: Progressive Reference-Guided Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27360)
*Yu Lin,Zi-Jun Wang,Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProStyle，通过跨内容监督学习"内容抑制的风格先验"，并以弱条件注入辅助层，结合残差修正双通路，实现可控的参考引导文本到图像风格化；同时构建ReConStyle数据集与评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考引导的风格化中，参考图的主体与背景会干扰生成内容，而抑制这些信息又会丢失可迁移的纹理与材质线索，导致"内容控制"与"风格忠实保留"之间存在张力。

Method: 1) 在同风格、不同主体与背景的成对数据上做跨内容监督，学习内容抑制的风格先验；2) 将该先验以弱条件形式注入辅助层以增强风格表达；3) 冻结内容抑制风格通路，由另一互补通路从完整参考patch特征学习残差修正；4) 两通路在同一目标下联合精化；5) 构建ReConStyle：134K训练对、两种测试设置，含目标内容保真与参考物体重叠的物体级度量，以及风格相似度与文本对齐度量。

Result: 在ReConStyle-Full上优于所有对比的SD1.5系基线，四项自动指标与三项人工指标平均排名1.86；用户研究中文本对齐与综合适用性选择率分别为84.36%与50.90%；在外部Style-Rank基准上取得最高CLIP-Text与ImageReward分数。

Conclusion: 通过解耦"内容抑制的风格先验"与"全特征残差修正"并联合优化，可在有效抑制参考内容泄漏的同时保留风格迁移能力，且配套的跨内容评测基准能更全面地衡量该任务。

Abstract: Reference-guided text-to-image stylization transfers visual appearance to content specified by text, but the reference subjects and backgrounds can interfere with what the generated image depicts. Suppressing this unwanted information can also discard transferable texture and material cues, creating a tension between content control and faithful preservation of the reference style. Accordingly, we propose ProStyle, which learns a content-suppressed style prior through cross-content supervision on same-style pairs with different subjects and backgrounds and injects it with weak conditioning at auxiliary layers to strengthen style expression. Building on this prior, a complementary pathway learns residual corrections from full reference patch features with the content-suppressed style pathway frozen, before both pathways undergo joint refinement under the same objective. For cross-content learning and evaluation, we introduce ReConStyle, which provides 134K training pairs, two test settings, and object-level measures of target-content fidelity and reference-object overlap alongside style similarity and text alignment. ProStyle outperforms all compared SD1.5-based baselines and achieves the best average rank of 1.86 across four automatic and three human-evaluation criteria on ReConStyle-Full. In the user study, it substantially outperforms the baselines in text alignment and combined suitability, with selection rates of 84.36% and 50.90%, respectively. On the external Style-Rank benchmark, it also achieves the highest CLIP-Text and ImageReward scores.

</details>


### [79] [VMSA: Video Memory Skill Agent for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27381)
*Changin Choi,Jungwon Park,Jungmin Ko,Jimyeong Kim,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VMSA框架，联合设计结构化视频记忆与"记忆技能"，在7个长视频问答基准上取得最优或具竞争力表现，周级第一人称基准上最高超强基线9.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答需从长达数小时甚至数天的视频中检索并整合证据。现有智能体方法将视频信息组织为外部记忆并迭代检索，但智能体仍需判断"检索什么信息、访问哪种记忆源、如何跨越远距离时间区间连接证据"，检索与推理仍具挑战。

Method: VMSA构建三种互补记忆：Segment Caption Memory（片段字幕记忆）与Entity Event Memory（实体事件记忆）作为可检索证据源；Entity-Aware Episode Memory（实体感知情节记忆）提供既宏观又细致的视频上下文以引导检索与推理。同时从成功智能体轨迹中归纳"记忆技能"，以紧凑技能库的形式增量精炼/扩展，指导迭代的证据检索、推理与验证。

Result: 在7个长视频问答基准上，3个周级第一人称基准全部取得最高准确率，Ego-R1 Bench上最高领先最强基线9.4%；其余4个基准达到最优或具竞争力表现。消融实验验证三种记忆组件与记忆技能的贡献，且记忆技能还能提升时序证据定位能力。

Conclusion: 结构化多源视频记忆与可学习、可增量演化的记忆技能相结合，是提升长视频检索-推理解耦问题的有效范式，并能改善时序证据定位。

Abstract: Long-video question answering requires retrieving and integrating relevant evidence from videos spanning hours or even days. Recent agentic approaches organize video information into external memories and iteratively retrieve evidence to answer questions. However, effective retrieval and reasoning remain challenging, as agents must determine what information to search for, which memory sources to access, and how to connect evidence across distant temporal intervals. To address these challenges, we propose Video Memory Skill Agent (VMSA), a framework that jointly designs structured video memories and memory skills for long-video understanding. VMSA constructs three complementary memory components. Segment Caption Memory and Entity Event Memory serve as searchable evidence sources, while Entity-Aware Episode Memory provides broad yet detailed video context to guide evidence retrieval and reasoning. To further support effective memory utilization, VMSA induces memory skills from successful agent trajectories, incrementally refining or extending a compact skill library to guide iterative evidence retrieval, reasoning, and verification. Across seven long-video question-answering benchmarks, VMSA achieves the highest accuracy on all three week-long egocentric benchmarks, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 9.4% on Ego-R1 Bench, and the best or competitive performance on four additional benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate the contributions of all three memory components and memory skills, with memory skills also improving temporal evidence localization.

</details>


### [80] [PanoUAV-Track: Full-Spherical UAV Visual Tracking in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27383)
*Bingyu Li,Lichang Yang,Zhiyuan Zhao,Junyu Gao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向无人机平台全向相机的全景跟踪基准PanoUAV-Track（约40小时8K全景UAV视频，1,895个序列、507,961帧、2,872个标注目标），并提出几何感知跟踪框架PUTrack以应对ERP投影下的纬度畸变与接缝不连续问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV跟踪基准多基于视场受限的透视相机；全向相机可实现全空域观测，但运动UAV平台上的全景跟踪尚属空白。与地面全景跟踪不同，UAV载全向相机需同时在方位角与俯仰角上观测目标，在等距柱状投影（ERP）下引入新的几何挑战。

Method: 通过约40小时真实8K全景UAV视频采集，经时间片段抽取、目标标注与仿真序列融合构建PanoUAV-Track；基准包含昼夜真实场景与仿真数据，支持视觉与语言两类目标指定方式。提出PUTrack，将纬度自适应搜索（latitude-adaptive search）与接缝感知周期采样（seam-aware periodic sampling）相结合的几何感知跟踪框架。

Result: 大规模评测揭示明显的"透视→球面"泛化鸿沟，并定位到与纬度相关畸变及ERP接缝不连续相关的跟踪性能退化。PUTrack在零样本与域内跟踪上均有提升，对跨接缝目标尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了UAV全向视觉跟踪新设定，提供了大规模多模态基准，并证明显式建模ERP几何特性（纬度畸变与接缝）是提升全景跟踪性能的关键途径。

Abstract: Existing UAV tracking benchmarks predominantly rely on perspective cameras with limited fields of view. Omnidirectional cameras enable full-spherical observation, yet tracking from moving UAV platforms remains largely unexplored. Unlike ground-level panoramic tracking, UAV-mounted omnidirectional cameras observe targets across both azimuth and elevation, introducing geometric challenges under equirectangular projection (ERP). To investigate this setting, we capture approximately \textbf{40 hours of real-world 8K panoramic UAV video} and construct \textbf{PanoUAV-Track} through temporal clip extraction, target annotation, and the integration of simulated sequences. The resulting benchmark comprises 1,895 sequences, 507,961 frames, and 2,872 annotated objects, covering real-world daytime and nighttime scenes alongside simulation, and supporting both visual and language-based target specification. Through extensive evaluations, we reveal a clear \textbf{perspective-to-spherical} generalization gap and identify tracking degradation associated with latitude-dependent distortion and ERP seam discontinuity. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{PanoUAV-Tracker (PUTrack)}, a geometry-aware tracking framework combining latitude-adaptive search and seam-aware periodic sampling. PUTrack improves zero-shot and in-domain tracking, particularly for seam-crossing targets. Code and benchmark resources will be available\footnote{\textcolor{tabpink}{https://libingyu01.github.io/PanoUAV-Track/}}.

</details>


### [81] [What Stays in the Picture? From Redundant Instructions to Dependency-Aware Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27388)
*Zi-Jun Wang,Yu Lin,Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"复杂冗余指令图像编辑"任务，并提出CRIE-Agent——将修订历史整合为唯一的最终编辑目标以联合指导规划与验证，同时构建CRIE-S基准（1万条含精确最终意图标注的指令），使完整编辑成功率从直接编辑的0%提升至27.5–50.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑通常只处理单一、无冲突的指令。而真实场景中，一个请求可能同时指定多对象、多属性与空间关系，并在其中修订甚至取消先前的编辑要求（冗余/自相矛盾的指令）。直接编辑无法还原这种最终意图；逐步分解执行又会保留已作废的编辑和视觉错误，导致最终结果不可靠。

Method: 核心洞见是将修订历史"解算"为一个最终编辑目标，用其同时指导规划与验证，从而避免作废请求变成实际视觉编辑。据此构建CRIE-Agent：(1) 将修订历史归并（consolidate）为对象级的最终意图；(2) 以"引用优先"的分块方式执行保留下来的对象，即先建立被引用对象再处理其依赖对象；(3) 引入验证机制，验证失败时从源图完整重执行，并辅以条件化布局提示，防止失败尝试中的视觉错误被带入后续步骤。同时构建CRIE-S基准，包含10,000条复杂冗余编辑指令及精确的最终意图标注。

Result: 在CRIE-S上对五种图像编辑后端进行评测，CRIE-Agent将完整编辑成功率（complete editing success）从直接编辑的0%提升到27.5–50.0%。

Conclusion: 显式地将冗余修订历史解析为唯一最终意图，并将该意图作为规划与验证的共同依据，是处理复杂冗余编辑指令的关键；配合引用优先的依赖排序执行与基于源图的完整重执行验证，可显著提升完整编辑成功率。该工作同时提供了首个带精确最终意图标注的冗余指令编辑基准。

Abstract: We introduce complex redundant-instruction image editing, where a single request specifies multiple objects, their attributes, and spatial relations while revising or canceling earlier editing requirements. Direct editing can fail to recover this intent, while splitting the request into successive steps can preserve obsolete edits and visual errors. Our key insight is to resolve the revision history into a final editing target that jointly guides planning and verification, so that obsolete requests do not become visual edits. Based on this insight, CRIE-Agent learns to consolidate revisions into object-level final intent and executes the retained objects in reference-first blocks, establishing referenced objects before their dependents. Verification then guides complete re-execution from the source image, with conditional layout hints, preventing visual errors from failed attempts from being carried into subsequent ones. We also construct CRIE-S, a benchmark containing 10,000 complex and redundant editing instructions with exact final intent annotations. Across five image-editing backends on CRIE-S, CRIE-Agent raises complete editing success from 0% under direct editing to 27.5--50.0%.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond What They Say: Cue Acknowledgment and Robustness in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27389)
*Ali Rahimiakbr,Arvin Baghal Asl,Nima Niroumand,Mohammad Parsa Jafarnejady,Hossein Shahabadi,Amirmohammad Izadi,Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文质疑"解释中提及线索即代表鲁棒"的假设：在控制问题与正确答案不变、配对比较视觉/文本提示（有帮助、误导、冲突）时，模型可能更频繁地选择被诱导的错误答案，同时也更频繁地承认线索存在，说明"减少沉默"未必意味着抗干扰能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测常用解释来判断视觉语言模型是依赖视觉证据还是误导性提示；但"明确点名线索"的回应仍可能实际跟随该线索，因此仅凭披露（disclosure）判断鲁棒性是不充分的。作者由此提出核心问题：更强的线索承认是否伴随更强的抵抗能力？

Method: 采用配对比较设计：固定底层问题与正确答案，构造匹配的答案与解释，在有帮助、误导、冲突的视觉与文本线索条件下进行对照；在同一实例上同时测量"线索跟随率"与"线索承认率"，并测试"要求给出证据/承认线索"的提示是否提升抵抗能力。

Result: 配对比较显示，模型可以在更频繁承认线索的同时，更频繁选择被诱导的错误答案；解释还可能提及文本提示却遗漏同时存在的视觉线索。要求证据与承认线索在所测设置中并不能稳定提升抵抗能力。结果随模型与设置而变化，且该比较未能分离出"承认"的因果效应。

Conclusion: "减少沉默/更多披露"不等于鲁棒性提升。评测应在同一批实例上同时报告线索跟随与线索承认，并区分视觉线索与文本线索；不应以解释中是否提及线索作为鲁棒性的替代指标。

Abstract: Explanations are used to assess whether vision-language models rely on visual evidence or misleading suggestions. A response that explicitly names a cue can still follow it, making disclosure alone an incomplete basis for judging robustness. We ask whether greater cue acknowledgment accompanies greater resistance. We compare matched answers and explanations under helpful, misleading, and conflicting visual and textual cues while holding the underlying question and correct answer fixed. A paired comparison shows that a model can select the endorsed wrong answer more often while acknowledging the cues more often. Reduced silence therefore need not indicate improved resistance. Explanations may also mention a textual suggestion while omitting an accompanying visual cue. Requesting evidence and acknowledgment does not consistently improve resistance in the tested settings. Outcomes vary across models and settings, and these comparisons do not isolate a causal effect of acknowledgment. Evaluations should report cue following and acknowledgment on the same instances, distinguishing visual from textual cues.

</details>


### [83] [Taking Apart and Putting Together: Tracking Objects Through Physical Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27398)
*Davide Gardenal,Mingxuan Liu,Flavio Vella,Quentin Guimard,Elisa Ricci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TTU任务与免训练框架TIPO，通过提案生成—评估—判定流水线持续推断物体的拆分/合并变换，并构建VISTA数据集；在VISTA上取得最优跟踪与状态图精度，速度比先前方法快6–15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标跟踪（如SAM 2/3）虽能在外观变化下维持轨迹，但物体发生物理组装/拆分时无法更新语义身份；已有工作仅处理拆分，且依赖代价高昂的、仅在跟踪失败后才触发的搜索机制。

Method: TIPO为免训练框架：(1) 由互不相交的掩码分量生成拆分与合并提案；(2) 利用内部重叠与外部重叠，对照掩码解码器先验对提案打分；(3) 对得分最高的提案调用MLLM进行视觉验证与语义标注；同时维护关系状态图。

Result: 构建VISTA数据集，含50个视频序列、超过26000帧、124个组装事件与132个拆分事件及状态图标注；TIPO在VISTA上的跟踪与状态图精度均达SOTA，且速度较先前方法提升6–15倍。

Conclusion: 将持续跟踪扩展到物理组装/拆分场景是可行且高效的：免训练、持续主动推断变换的TIPO优于仅靠跟踪失败触发的昂贵搜索范式。

Abstract: We introduce Transformation Tracking and Understanding (TTU), extending video object tracking to environments where objects undergo physical assemblies and disassemblies. TTU requires continuously tracking objects through transformations, identifying new entities, maintaining a state graph of their relationships, and assigning semantic labels to transformations. While foundation models (e.g., SAM 2/3) maintain tracklets through appearance changes, they cannot update semantic identities as objects physically evolve. Prior work addresses only disassemblies, relying on expensive searches triggered only when tracking fails. We propose Tracking Instances via Proposals and Overlaps (TIPO), a training-free framework that continuously infers transformations via a generate-evaluate-judge pipeline. TIPO generates split and merge proposals from disjoint mask components, scores them against mask-decoder priors using inner and external overlap, and queries an MLLM on the top proposal for visual verification and semantic labeling. To benchmark TTU, we curate Video Instances, States, and Transformation Annotations (VISTA), a dataset including state graphs with 124 assembly and 132 disassembly events across 50 sequences and over 26,000 frames. TIPO achieves state-of-the-art tracking and state-graph accuracy on VISTA, while operating $6\times$ to $15\times$ faster than prior methods. Project page: https://davidegardenal.github.io/tipo-tracking

</details>


### [84] [BladeR: Scalable, Shape-Guided 3D Reconstruction of Wind Turbine Blades from UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27407)
*Jonathan Sterckx,Michiel Vlaminck,Hiep Luong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BladeR是一个面向风机叶片无人机巡检影像的可扩展三维重建流程：以GPS/IMU与叶片形状先验引导的分层稀疏重建，配合分块高斯泼溅在原生分辨率下处理45MP影像，实现叶片与背景分离及前缘细节自适应增强，在完整性与细节上显著优于商业与开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶片结构完整性对安全高效发电至关重要，精确三维重建可定位与量化磨损、实现早期损伤检测。无人机巡检虽已普及，但高分辨率影像存在弱纹理、光照变化剧烈、计算代价高等问题，常规重建流程难以兼顾精度、细节与可扩展性。

Method: 1) 稀疏重建采用分层策略，利用GPS/IMU位姿与叶片形状先验约束搜索范围，引导先进关键点匹配器在全分辨率影像上工作；2) 稠密重建采用分块（tiled）高斯泼溅框架，直接在45MP原生分辨率处理；3) 对叶片进行背景隔离（掩膜/分割），并沿前缘区域自适应增强细节恢复。

Result: 与商业及开源基线方法的对比验证表明，所提方法在模型完整性和细节保真度上均有显著提升，同时保持对大规模巡检数据集的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 将几何先验、先进匹配与分块高斯泼溅相结合，可有效缓解弱纹理、光照变化与高分辨率计算瓶颈，为叶片磨损量化与早期损伤检测提供高保真三维基础；后续可进一步面向在线/边缘部署与缺陷量化评估。

Abstract: The structural integrity of wind turbine blades is critical for safe, efficient energy production. Accurate 3D reconstruction allows precise localization and quantification of wear and early damage detection. While drone-based inspections are standard, their high-resolution imagery poses challenges such as weak texture, lighting variability, and high computational cost. We present BladeR, a scalable pipeline for high-quality 3D reconstruction from inspection imagery. A hierarchical sparse reconstruction strategy leverages GPS/IMU data and blade shape priors to guide advanced keypoint matchers on full-resolution images. For dense reconstruction, a tiled Gaussian Splatting framework processes 45 MP images at native resolution, isolates the blade from background, and adaptively enhances detail recovery along the leading edge. Validation against commercial and open-source baselines shows significant improvements in model completeness and detail.

</details>


### [85] [Projector Time Map Calibration for Event-based Structured Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27412)
*Buu-Hoang-Lam Le,Kota Mizutani,Shohei Nobuhara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有基于事件的结构光依赖"投影仪平行光栅扫描"和"传感器逐像素独立时间戳"两个假设，二者均不成立；本文改用直接标识投影仪像素的时间编码获取对应关系，无需先验标定，并从同一段记录中自标定投影仪时间映射，再通过数秒前导段的时空自标定恢复系统几何与时钟偏移，无需参考物或第二相机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出并纠正事件结构光领域两个被普遍接受但错误的前提假设，从而摆脱对先验标定、参考对象和额外相机的依赖。

Method: (1) 采用时间编码（temporal code）直接编码投影仪像素身份，建立稠密的投影仪-相机对应关系；(2) 从同一段录制数据中标定投影仪时间映射（time map）；(3) 在数秒的前导段（preamble）内执行时空自标定，联合求解系统几何与时钟偏移。

Result: 获得稠密投影仪-相机对应关系、投影仪时间映射、系统几何及时钟偏移估计，全过程仅需单次录制与数秒前导数据，无需参考物与第二相机。

Conclusion: 事件结构光可摆脱平行光栅扫描与逐像素独立时间戳的假设；基于时间编码与时空自标定的一体化流程实现了免标定、免参考物的系统标定与对应关系恢复。

Abstract: Existing event-based structured light obtains a dense projector-camera correspondence from the timestamp of an event. It assumes that the projector scans a parallel raster and that the sensor timestamps each pixel independently, and we show that neither assumption holds. We obtain the correspondence from a temporal code that identifies the projector pixel directly and therefore needs no prior calibration, we calibrate the projector time map from the same recording, and we recover the geometry of the system and the offsets between its clocks by a spatio-temporal self-calibration in a preamble of a few seconds that needs no reference object and no second camera.

</details>


### [86] [Ego2Ego: Generative Visual Perspective Taking Across Collaborators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27459)
*Chuhan Jiao,John Gideon,Anshul Gupta,Guy Rosman,Andreas Bulling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"生成式视觉视角采择"任务：给定自我视角视频、场景点云与协作者相机轨迹，生成协作者视角的连续自我中心视频；并提出Ego2Ego——与骨干无关的几何模块集，为预训练视频扩散模型注入双方共享的3D场景几何，在CoMind数据集上跨三个骨干均优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将视角采择简化为状态估计、几何重建或离散问答；跨视角视频生成方法或仅从输入视频推断目标视角，或需额外的外部相机观测目标人物。二者均未利用双方共享的3D场景几何，难以应对极端视角偏移、动态遮挡与稀疏视觉重叠。

Method: Ego2Ego为backbone-agnostic的几何模块：(1) 向每个视角渲染稠密、时序一致的情景坐标图（scene coordinate maps）作为共享空间参照；(2) 通过相对几何注意力（relative geometric attention）在3D射线空间中做跨视角token对齐；(3) 利用自我主体的视觉记忆，恢复其当前视野之外的区域。

Result: 在CoMind数据集的三个视频扩散骨干上，Ego2Ego一致提升视觉质量、Level-2场景布局精度与协作者运动建模，且在每一骨干上均超过SOTA跨视角视频生成基线。

Conclusion: 通过更准确地生成协作者的自我中心视频，Ego2Ego将视觉视角采择引入人机协作：可推断人类协作者所见与所做，并能将单人录制转化为同步的多视角视频以服务机器人学习。

Abstract: Visual perspective taking, the ability to infer what another person sees from their position, is fundamental to human collaboration. However, prior works reduced perspective taking to state estimation, geometric reconstruction, or discrete question answering, whereas existing cross-view video generation methods either inferred the target view from the input video alone or required an external camera that observes the target person. We introduce generative visual perspective taking: Given an ego person's egocentric video, a scene point cloud, and a collaborator's camera trajectory, the task is to generate the continuous egocentric video from the collaborator's perspective. This task is challenging given extreme viewpoint shifts, dynamic occlusions, and sparse visual overlap. We address these challenges with Ego2Ego -- a set of backbone-agnostic geometric modules that provide a pretrained video diffusion model with the 3D scene geometry shared by both interactants. Ego2Ego renders dense, temporally consistent scene coordinate maps into every view as a shared spatial reference, aligns cross-view tokens in 3D ray space through relative geometric attention, and recovers regions outside the ego person's current view from the ego person's visual memory. Experiments on the CoMind dataset across three video diffusion backbones show that Ego2Ego consistently improves visual quality, Level-2 scene layout accuracy, and collaborator motion, outperforming state-of-the-art cross-view video generation baselines on every backbone. By generating the collaborator's egocentric video more accurately, Ego2Ego brings visual perspective taking to human-robot collaboration, where it can help infer what a human collaborator sees and does, and can turn single-person recordings into synchronised multi-perspective videos for robot learning.

</details>


### [87] [Pose-based Co-speech Gesture Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27415)
*Frances Yung,Ninlawat Phuangschoke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在统一交叉验证与多层级评估协议下重新基准测试共语手势检测方法，发现既有性能无法稳定复现，任务远未解决，并提出CTR-GCN+BiLSTM模型在CABBS上优于MPSL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共语手势检测（识别说话者做节拍或语义手势的时间片段）是手势标注与人类交际行为研究的关键第一步。尽管已有自动工具与模型，该任务仍研究不足；现有方法在不同数据集、实验设置与指标下评估，性能难以横向比较，当前最优状态不清。

Method: 构建统一基准：在三个数据集（MULTISIMO、GESRes、CABBS）上使用相同的交叉验证折划分评估现有检测器，并在帧级、手势片段级（gesture-span）与窗口级三个层次进行评价；同时提出CTR-GCN + BiLSTM组合模型作为新方法。

Result: 先前报告的性能在统一评估设置下不能稳定迁移；不同数据集间性能差异显著，且随手势频率变化明显；所提CTR-GCN+BiLSTM在CABBS上超过原最优方法MPSL。

Conclusion: 该工作为比较现有方法提供了更一致的基准，表明共语手势检测仍远未解决，并指出了未来研究的关键挑战。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture detection identifies the spans in which a speaker gestures (e.g., beat or semantic gestures) in video. As a first step in gesture annotation, it is crucial for studying human communicative behaviour. Despite the availability of automatic tools and models, co-speech gesture detection remains underexplored, and its current state of the art is unclear: existing methods have been evaluated using different datasets, experimental setups, and metrics, making their performance difficult to compare. To address this gap, we benchmark existing detectors using the same cross-validation folds across three datasets (MULTISIMO, GESRes, and CABBS), with evaluation at the frame, gesture-span, and window levels. Our results show that previously reported performance does not consistently transfer to this common evaluation setting. Performance varies substantially across datasets, particularly with gesture frequency, indicating that the task remains far from solved. We further propose a CTR-GCN + BiLSTM model that outperforms the previous best method, MPSL, on CABBS. Our results provide a more consistent basis for comparing existing approaches and highlight key challenges for future research on co-speech gesture detection.

</details>


### [88] [AppCon-Bench: Perception and Misperception of Material Appearance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27453)
*Santiago Jimenez-Navarro,Ana Serrano,Belen Masia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AppCon-Bench 基准，含五项任务，在受控几何与光照变化下考察视觉语言模型（VLM）与人类观察者的材质外观恒常性，揭示二者相似性与系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材质外观恒常性（跨光照、几何变化识别同一材质）是鲁棒视觉系统的关键能力，人类表现优异；而 VLM 虽在语义视觉任务上表现强劲，其对细微中层次知觉属性的解读能力仍缺乏理解，尤其缺乏对其在观察条件与实验情境变化下判断一致性的系统评估。

Method: 构建 AppCon-Bench：设计五项任务，在几何与光照受控变化的条件下评估材质外观恒常性；将代表性 VLM 与人类观察者并列评测，分析其在跨观察条件和跨实验情境下保持材质判断一致性的能力，并进行深入分析。

Result: 分析发现人类与 VLM 在材质外观感知上既存在相似之处，也存在系统性差异；结果表明仅看总体准确率不足以刻画知觉能力的成败，需针对具体成功与失败模式进行评估。

Conclusion: AppCon-Bench 为评估 VLM 的中层次知觉恒常性提供了受控基准；超越总体准确率的分析范式可揭示 VLM 的改进方向，并将其与人类知觉特性进行对照。

Abstract: Identifying the same material across changes in illumination or geometry is a critical ability for robust vision systems. Humans exhibit remarkable material appearance constancy, being able to recognize the intrinsic physical properties of an object's surface material across variations in extrinsic, confounding factors such as the lighting or the shape of such object. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong performance across a wide range of semantic vision tasks, yet their ability to interpret subtle mid-level perceptual attributes remains less understood. We introduce AppCon-Bench, a benchmark comprising five tasks designed to assess material appearance constancy under controlled variations in geometry and illumination, and evaluate representative VLMs alongside human observers, investigating their ability to maintain consistent material judgments across viewing conditions and experimental contexts. After thorough analysis, we identify several key findings that reveal both similarities and systematic differences between human and VLM material appearance perception, highlighting the value of evaluating perceptual successes and failures beyond overall accuracy and informing areas of improvement for current VLMs.

</details>


### [89] [Self-Supervised Continuous-Time Depth Learning and Ego-Motion Estimation from Asynchronous Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27509)
*Sadiq O. Macaulay,Dipon Kumar Ghosh,Hadfield Simon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督方法，为每个事件在连续时间上赋予独立深度，并从同一观测中恢复相机运动；以事件间运动补偿对齐为几何监督信号，经可微渲染生成目标时刻的稠密深度图。在MVSEC上多数序列和深度范围内优于此前仅用事件的自监督方法，夜间优势最大，且旋转估计更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机以微秒精度逐像素记录亮度变化，但可用于监督的稠密深度获取代价高，且仅存在于孤立时刻。将事件流压缩为帧便于学习，却牺牲了事件感知赖以区别的精确时间信息。因此需要一种不依赖稠密深度真值、同时保留连续时间特性的监督方式。

Method: 自监督连续时间深度与自运动估计：为每个事件预测其在连续时间上的深度，同时从相同观测估计相机运动（含时间主干网络）。监督来自几何一致性——将事件按预测深度提升至3D，并按事件触发瞬间的相机运动位移，同一边缘产生的事件应相互重合，未对齐即暴露深度或运动误差（对齐目标）。再利用可微渲染将逐事件估计聚合为每个窗口内目标时刻的稠密深度图。消融实验分离局部时空上下文、时间主干与对齐目标三部分的作用。

Result: 在MVSEC数据集上，该方法在大多数序列与深度范围内优于先前的仅事件自监督方法，夜间场景提升幅度最大；相机旋转估计精度高于此前的无监督事件方法。

Conclusion: 运动补偿的事件对齐可作为连续时间深度与自运动估计的原理性监督来源，验证了以几何一致性替代稠密深度真值的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Event cameras register brightness changes pixel by pixel with microsecond precision, whereas the dense depth that could supervise them is costly to acquire and exists only at isolated instants. Condensing the stream into frames eases learning, but at the expense of the very timing that sets event sensing apart. We propose a self-supervised approach in which each event is assigned its own depth in continuous time, and the camera motion is recovered from the same observations. Supervision arises from geometry: lifted into 3D by its predicted depth and displaced by the camera motion at the instant it fired, an event should coincide with others produced by the same edge, so any misalignment exposes an error in depth or motion. Differentiable rendering then turns these per-event estimates into dense depth maps at target times within each window, faithful to the events behind them. On MVSEC, the approach outperforms prior event-only self-supervised methods in most sequences and depth ranges, with the widest margins at night, and recovers camera rotation more accurately than an earlier unsupervised event-based method. Ablations isolate the roles of local spatiotemporal context, the temporal backbone, and the alignment objective. These results position motion-compensated event alignment as a principled source of supervision for continuous-time depth and ego-motion estimation.

</details>


### [90] [StereoVLM: Binocular Vision-Language Models for Metric 3D Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27915)
*Chi Yan,Guoyang Zhao,Wentao Yuan,Yixiao Ge,Yanning Zhou,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双目几何感知VLM StereoVLM，通过"跨视角对应→视差估计→标定条件化度量深度→语义空间推理"的分层学习，从标定双目观测中学习可迁移的度量三维表示，并发布Stereo3D-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM虽具备强语义理解，但仅凭单/多张未标定图像推断三维结构，缺乏跨视角几何约束，度量三维推理本身具有歧义性；难以判断其空间预测究竟由视觉证据支撑还是由语义先验驱动。标定双目提供显式跨视角几何，可消除该歧义，是更可靠的度量三维感知基础。

Method: 首个几何感知双目VLM，采用分层学习范式：跨视角对应→视差估计→标定条件化度量深度感知→语义空间推理；构建三级基准Stereo3D-Bench（点级几何到物体级空间推理）；并通过干预实验（有效跨视角几何、额外视觉token、语义先验）与视差监督vs直接双目深度监督的对比进行分析。

Result: StereoVLM-8B在Stereo3D-Bench上取得95.17%深度精度δ1、物体级平均分66.98；视差监督在未见环境上的泛化显著优于直接双目深度监督；干预实验表明性能依赖有效跨视角几何，无法仅由额外视觉token或语义先验解释；分层几何监督在五个空间基准上平均提升3.54分，RoboCasa操作成功率提升4.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 标定双目几何可为VLM提供可靠、可迁移的度量三维表示基础；以视差为中间监督的分层几何学习优于直接深度监督，并能正迁移到下游空间推理与机器人操作任务。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong semantic understanding but remain limited in their ability to reason about metric 3D space in the physical world. Existing VLMs typically infer 3D scene structure from one or more images without known camera calibration or cross-view geometric constraints. This makes metric 3D inference inherently ambiguous and leaves it unclear whether their spatial predictions are grounded in visual evidence or driven primarily by semantic priors. In contrast, calibrated stereo provides explicit cross-view geometry that resolves this ambiguity and offers a more reliable foundation for metric 3D perception and geometric generalization. We investigate how VLMs can learn transferable 3D representations directly from calibrated binocular observations. To this end, we introduce StereoVLM, the first geometry-aware binocular VLM for metric 3D spatial reasoning. StereoVLM follows a hierarchical learning paradigm spanning from cross-view correspondence and disparity estimation to calibration-conditioned metric depth perception and semantic spatial reasoning. We further find that disparity supervision generalizes substantially better than direct binocular depth supervision on unseen environments. To evaluate these capabilities, we introduce Stereo3D-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark across three levels, from point-level geometry to object-level spatial reasoning. StereoVLM-8B achieves 95.17% depth accuracy ($δ_1$) and a 66.98 average object-level score on Stereo3D-Bench. Intervention results further indicate that the model's performance on Stereo3D-Bench benefits from valid cross-view geometry and cannot be explained solely by additional visual tokens or semantic priors. Hierarchical geometric supervision also yields average gains of 3.54 points across five spatial benchmarks and 4.3 percentage points in RoboCasa manipulation success.

</details>


### [91] [Cecilia: Towards Layout-Inclusive Optical Music Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27465)
*Congren Dai,Zhancheng Guo,Xiaobing Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出紧凑的乐谱序列表示 Cecilia（规范化 MusicXML 的保留音乐内容、歌词附着与排版指令的表示），以及基于共享视觉记忆、按小节分段预测该表示的 Cecilia-OMR 模型（含学习式分段计数头）。平均 MusicXML 与 Cecilia 序列长度比为 27.73；在 Camera String Quartets 与两个 Lieder 条件上优于 Legato 2，OpenScore 四条件宏平均归一化编辑距离 0.2232 vs 0.2998。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建可编辑乐谱需同时包含音乐事件、歌词文本及其页面组织的排版指令，但现有识别目标常缺失其中部分信息；直接预测 MusicXML 又会重复大量结构化标记，序列冗长、效率低。因此需要一种既信息完整又紧凑的中间表示及相应识别方法。

Method: 1) Cecilia 表示：规范化 MusicXML 的紧凑序列化，保留音乐内容、歌词附着关系与排版指令；2) Cecilia-OMR：以共享视觉记忆为特征来源，结合元数据与"小节有界"的音乐分段进行预测，用学习式计数头（count head）确定输出分段数量；3) 评测：在统一分词器下比较序列长度，与 Legato 2 对比端到端编辑距离，并分别评估歌词、符号树与渲染排版，另做训练阶段、奖励与候选选择分析。

Result: 相同完整乐谱配对、同一分词器下，MusicXML 与 Cecilia 平均序列长度比为 27.73（显著压缩）；Cecilia-OMR 在 Camera String Quartets 与两个 Lieder 条件上超过 Legato 2，OpenScore 四条件宏平均归一化编辑距离为 0.2232，对比 0.2998；分项评估显示歌词、符号树与渲染排版反映互补的重建维度，并量化了排版指令的贡献。

Conclusion: 紧凑且信息完整的 Cecilia 表示配合分段式视觉记忆识别，可在显著缩短序列的同时提升端到端乐谱重建质量；歌词、符号结构与排版需分项评估，排版指令对可编辑重建有实质贡献，模型适配与解码策略亦影响识别表现。

Abstract: Reconstructing an editable music score requires musical events, sung text and the instructions that organise them on the page. Existing recognition targets often omit part of this information, while direct MusicXML prediction repeats substantial structural markup. We introduce Cecilia, a compact representation of canonicalised MusicXML that retains musical content, lyric attachments and layout instructions. Cecilia-OMR predicts this representation through metadata and measure-bounded music segments from shared visual memory. A learned count head determines the number of output segments. Across paired complete scores under a common tokeniser, the ratio of mean MusicXML to Cecilia sequence lengths is 27.73. Cecilia-OMR outperforms Legato 2 on Camera String Quartets and both Lieder conditions, with a four-condition OpenScore macro normalised edit distance of 0.2232 against 0.2998. Separate evaluations of lyrics, notation trees and rendered layout reveal complementary aspects of reconstruction and quantify the contribution of layout instructions. Training-stage, reward and candidate-selection studies further examine how recognition changes across model adaptation and decoding.

</details>


### [92] [PathOPEN: A pathologist-curated histopathology dataset for visual question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27469)
*Awais Naeem,Zhongling Xu,George Huang,Amy Coffey,Nkechi Okonkwo,Mario Saab-Chalhoub,John Lee Sang,Dina Kokh,Muhammad Zulfiqar,Ankita Shukla,Hairong Wang,Chandra Krishnan,Ying Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathOPEN是一个面向视觉问答的组织病理学数据集，含157个病例、229张原始图像及多格式（开放式/五选一/是-否）问答，并提供元数据、增强图像、配对答案与专家及模型评分，支持子组评估与偏好样本构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理视觉问答缺乏带细粒度元数据标注、多样问答格式与可评测偏好的公开数据集，难以进行子组分析与偏好建模。

Method: 由两名临床病理学家筛选157例、229张原图并撰写开放式、五选一及是/否问题，为309道开放式题中的201道提供一对一正误答案；记录病例器官、病理类别、区域解剖及图像放大倍数；构建6,870张增强图与四份按元数据字段平衡的子集清单；由五名病理学家与两个视觉语言模型裁判按评分量表打分，并以六个模型在三种格式下做零样本评测。

Result: 数据集配套专家与模型双重评分，元数据可支撑亚组评估，配对正误答案可直接用于构建偏好样本；六个模型在三种题型上的零样本表现得到系统记录。

Conclusion: PathOPEN为病理视觉问答提供了可细分评估、可做偏好对齐研究的多格式基准资源。

Abstract: PathOPEN is a histopathology dataset for visual question answering. Two clinical pathologists selected 157 cases with 229 available original images and wrote open-ended, five-option multiple-choice and close-ended (yes/no) questions. They supplied both a correct and a wrong answer for 201 of the 309 open-ended questions. Each case records the organ, pathology category and regional anatomy, and each image records its magnification. The dataset includes 6,870 augmented images and four manifests that select subsets to improve balance by individual metadata field. Five additional pathologists and two vision-language model judges rated items with scoring rubrics, and six models answered questions zero-shot across the three formats. The metadata support subgroup evaluation, and the paired answers can be used to construct preference examples.

</details>


### [93] [Which Dwarf Is This? Doppeldwarf-306 and Near-Identical Instance Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27490)
*Turhan Can Kargin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Doppeldwarf-306数据集（306座波兰弗罗茨瓦夫青铜小矮人雕像、1,691张开放许可照片），在无训练条件下用冻结DINOv2特征的最近邻检索，留一查询首选正确率达93.1%，13个骨干中最优94.7%；并系统评估了候选集规模、地理位置先验与局部特征匹配的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例检索基准高度集中于地标类目标，缺乏对"视觉高度相似的同系列小型雕塑"这一互补场景的评测；该场景对细粒度实例区分能力构成更严峻考验。

Method: 构建Doppeldwarf-306数据集（含摄影师元数据，便于留出查询）。以冻结特征做无训练最近邻检索，比较7种预训练范式共13个骨干；分析候选数翻倍时的精度衰减；模拟手机导览场景，对比"按位置筛选候选"与"等量随机子采样"，并用置换检验检验布局效应；将位置作为加权而非硬过滤；在withheld摄影师设定下引入学习式局部特征匹配；用两候选比较降低未知雕像的误接受率。

Result: DINOv2留一首选93.1%，最优骨干94.7%；候选每翻倍精度约降0.8个百分点；按位置筛选优于无筛选但劣于等量随机子采样（说明随机子采样高估其价值），置换检验p=0.005；位置作权重时，距雕像25 m的模拟用户精度由93.4%升至98.7%；withheld摄影师下局部特征匹配将DINOv2由81.8%提至85.5%，DINOv3亦提升；等误接受约束（90%已知接受）下，比较两最佳候选把未知雕像误接受率从38.3%降至19.6%。

Conclusion: 视觉相似小雕塑构成对实例检索的有效细粒度挑战；冻结自监督特征已具强基线，位置先验应作为软权重使用，二候选比较可显著抑制误接受，随机子采样会高估位置过滤收益。

Abstract: Most established instance-retrieval benchmarks emphasise landmarks; small sculptures from visually similar series offer a complementary challenge. We introduce Doppeldwarf-306: 1,691 openly licensed photographs of 306 bronze dwarf statues in Wrocław, Poland, many made in themed series that share one style, with the photographer of each image recorded. With no training, nearest-neighbour search over frozen DINOv2 features names the right statue first for 93.1% of leave-one-out queries, and the best of thirteen backbones from seven pretraining paradigms reaches 94.7%. Accuracy falls slowly as candidates are added: for DINOv2, by about 0.8 points each time their number doubles. A natural design for a phone guide compares a photograph only with statues near the user. In simulation this helps, but less than an equal number of randomly chosen statues does, so random subsampling overstates its value; a permutation test ties the penalty to the real layout (p = 0.005). Used as a weight instead of a filter, location raises DINOv2 from 93.4% to 98.7% for a simulated user 25 m from the statue. With the query's photographer withheld, learned local-feature matching raises DINOv2 accuracy from 81.8% to 85.5% and also improves DINOv3. Comparing the two best candidates cuts DINOv2's false acceptance of unknown statues from 38.3% to 19.6% at 90% known acceptance.

</details>


### [94] [Solar-Conditioned Flow Matching for Multitemporal Aerial Visible-to-Infrared Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27501)
*Wenwen Fang,Bingwei Hui,Xin Chen,Zhongzhen Sun,Zhenfeng Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Solar-Flow，将航拍可见光到红外图像翻译从静态映射升级为多时相生成：用太阳高度角、热相位时钟(TPC)与飞行高度三个物理因子做条件，通过 adaLN 注入预训练流匹配生成器的每个 Transformer 块，并构建覆盖 14 个时段组的配对航拍 V2IR 数据集，实现单张可见光图像在多个时刻（含未见时刻）生成合理红外图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航拍可见光-红外(V2IR)翻译方法只在狭窄采集窗口内学习静态映射；随昼夜热状态演化，同一可见光图像可对应多种合理的红外外观，因此 V2IR 本质上是多时相任务，静态映射无法刻画该一对多关系。

Method: 1) 设计三个可计算的物理因子作为条件：太阳高度角、热相位时钟(TPC)、飞行高度；TPC 以日出为锚点在连续 24 小时轴上累计加热/冷却小时数，使无训练样本的时段热状态仍有定义。2) 通过自适应层归一化(adaLN)将条件注入预训练流匹配生成器的每个 Transformer 块，兼顾翻译保真度与多时相生成能力。3) 构建覆盖 14 个时段组的配对航拍 V2IR 数据集。

Result: 在新数据集上实现多时相生成：单张可见光图像可翻译为一天中多个时刻的合理红外图像，包括训练中从未出现的时刻。

Conclusion: 以物理太阳/热状态因子为条件的潜空间流匹配框架，可有效把航拍 V2IR 翻译由静态映射推广为连续时间轴上的多时相生成，具备向未见时段外推的能力。

Abstract: Aerial visible-to-infrared (V2IR) image translation provides scalable thermal data across the full diurnal cycle. However, existing methods predominantly learn a static mapping within narrow acquisition windows. As the thermal state evolves throughout the day, a single visible image may correspond to multiple plausible IR appearances, making V2IR translation an inherently multitemporal task. This letter proposes solar-conditioned latent flow matching (Solar-Flow), which upgrades aerial V2IR translation from static mapping to multitemporal generation. First, we design three computable physical factors, namely the solar elevation, the thermal phase clock (TPC), and the flight altitude. The TPC counts the elapsed heating and cooling hours on a continuous 24-hour axis anchored at sunrise, so the thermal state stays well defined even at hours without training samples. Second, we inject the condition into every Transformer block of a pretrained flow matching generator through adaptive layer normalization. This design preserves translation fidelity and enables multitemporal generation. Finally, we construct a paired aerial V2IR dataset covering 14 hour groups. Solar-Flow achieves multitemporal generation on this dataset, where a single visible image is translated into plausible IR images at multiple times of day, even at hours that were never seen in training.

</details>


### [95] [PGS-PCQA: Perceptually Gated Synthetic Supervision for No-Reference Point Cloud Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27512)
*Qinyuan Fan,Meng Zhang,Clemens Gühmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PGS-PCQA：利用合成失真排序监督提升无参考点云质量评估的跨数据集泛化能力，在WPC/BASICS上SRCC达0.709/0.751，参考不交叉验证下四个数据集SRCC为0.955/0.934/0.906/0.791。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NR-PCQA方法在单一数据集内表现良好但跨数据集泛化差。作者将主因归为训练数据失真覆盖不足：面对未见失真类型时，模型对严重程度的排序接近随机甚至反向。

Method: 由源参考点云构建合成失真库，以"同类型失真内的严重程度排序"作为额外监督，无需合成样本的主观分数；考虑到严重程度更高并不必然意味着感知质量更低，引入感知门控，依据渲染视图中的感知差异软化小差异样本对的排序目标。合成监督仅在训练阶段使用，且不需要目标域数据。

Result: 在SJTU-PCQA上单次训练即可在WPC取得SRCC 0.709、BASICS取得0.751（已发表DSP-PCQA对应为0.595与0.723）。参考不交叉验证下，SJTU-PCQA为0.955、WPC 0.934、BASICS 0.906、LS-PCQA Part I 0.791。消融中缩减合成库仅保留全库相对无合成库SRCC增益的58–65%。

Conclusion: 以合成失真排序作为辅助监督、并用感知门控对齐主观感知，可有效缓解失真覆盖不足导致的跨库泛化瓶颈，且不依赖目标域数据与额外主观标注，具有良好的可迁移性与实用价值。

Abstract: Existing no-reference point cloud quality assessment (NR-PCQA) methods perform well within individual datasets but generalize poorly across datasets. We identify insufficient distortion coverage in training data as a key cause: on unseen distortion types, models rank severity levels near chance or even in reverse. We propose perceptually gated synthetic supervision for no-reference point cloud quality assessment (PGS-PCQA), an NR-PCQA model trained with synthetic distortion ranking. It builds a synthetic distortion bank from source reference point clouds and uses within-type severity ranking as supervision in addition to the source mean opinion scores (MOS), requiring no subjective scores for the synthetic samples. Since higher severity does not always mean lower perceived quality, a perceptual gate softens the ranking targets of pairs with small perceptual differences in rendered views. The synthetic supervision is used only during training and needs no target-domain data. In a single training run on SJTU-PCQA, PGS-PCQA achieves Spearman rank correlation coefficients (SRCCs) of 0.709 on WPC and 0.751 on BASICS. The corresponding published DSP-PCQA scores are 0.595 and 0.723. Under reference-disjoint cross-validation, PGS-PCQA achieves SRCCs of 0.955 on SJTU-PCQA, 0.934 on WPC, 0.906 on BASICS, and 0.791 on LS-PCQA Part I. In the exclusion experiment, the reported scores for the reduced synthetic bank represent 58-65% of the full-bank SRCC gain relative to training without the bank. Code is available at https://github.com/Fan-QY/PGS-PCQA.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond a Single Human Demonstration: Learning from Scored Trajectory Alternatives for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27515)
*Xinyao Rao,Zecheng Tang,Liuxuan Sun,Zheyuan Lai,Minjun Yu,Xun Cao,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveSTA：用离线生成并打分的候选轨迹监督自回归提案生成与残差流精化，配合共享JEPA编码器降低推理开销，在NAVSIM v1 Navtest与HUGSIM上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类驾驶日志每个场景仅记录一条轨迹，缺乏其他可行驾驶选择的显式轨迹目标与质量标签，导致"生成+精化多候选轨迹"的端到端规划器监督不足；同时以大视觉语言模型作为精化条件会带来显著的在线推理成本。

Method: 1）离线为每个训练场景通过对记录轨迹做平滑形变生成候选替代轨迹，并以与日志交通流的仿真执行结果进行评估打分；2）高质量多样化替代轨迹用于监督不同提案，并作为质量引导去噪的残差目标；3）覆盖完整分数区间的候选训练驾驶质量打分器；4）共享JEPA（经掩码视频特征预测适配）仅编码一次观测帧，为精化与打分提供时序上下文；5）整体框架为自回归提案生成 + 以提案为条件的残差流精化。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1 Navtest上，DriveSTA在仅使用NAVSIM数据训练的对比规划器中排名第一；在扩展训练数据后，DriveSTA-Scale在Navtest与闭环HUGSIM上于对比方法中达到SOTA，且在匹配评估中将平均规划器前向延迟较ChainFlow-VLA降低73%。

Conclusion: 无需大视觉语言模型在线条件化，通过离线构造并打分的轨迹替代样本即可为多提案生成与残差精化提供有效监督，兼得性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Human driving logs record only one trajectory per scene, leaving other feasible driving choices without explicit trajectory targets or quality labels. This limits supervision for end-to-end planners that generate and refine multiple candidate trajectories. Meanwhile, conditioning refinement on a large vision-language model adds substantial online inference cost. We propose DriveSTA, which extends autoregressive proposal generation and proposal-conditioned residual flow refinement with scored trajectory alternatives. For each training scene, we generate alternatives offline through smooth deformations of the recorded trajectory and evaluate their simulated execution against logged traffic. Diverse, high-quality alternatives supervise distinct proposals and define residual targets for quality-guided denoising, while candidates spanning the full score range train a scorer to assess driving quality. A shared joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), adapted through masked video-feature prediction, encodes observed frames once and provides temporal context for both refinement and scoring. On NAVSIM v1 Navtest, DriveSTA ranks first among the compared planners trained on NAVSIM data alone. With expanded training data, DriveSTA-Scale achieves state-of-the-art results among the compared methods on Navtest and closed-loop HUGSIM, while reducing mean planner forward-pass latency by 73% relative to ChainFlow-VLA in matched evaluations.

</details>


### [97] [PhiJEPA: Denoising Latent Predictions for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27516)
*Makoto Yamada,Yao-Hung Hubert Tsai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhiJEPA将PhiNet的两阶段预测迁移到掩码Transformer上：先由Transformer从稀疏、光度退化的上下文预测独立退化教师视图的隐token，再由token级MLP映射到干净的教师token，从而学到对光度退化鲁棒、支持遮挡补全的视觉表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA类方法通常只用单阶段隐空间预测，且预训练结束后丢弃预测器。作者希望验证：若预测器被训练为"从退化上下文中推断被遮挡区域"，其两阶段形式能否同时提升密集特征质量，并在世界模型的部分观测规划中继续发挥作用。

Method: 提出PhiJEPA联合嵌入预测架构：对稀疏、经光度破坏的上下文，用Transformer块预测独立退化教师视图的隐token；再用逐token MLP将这些预测映射到干净的教师token，构成两阶段目标。在ImageNet-1K上预训练（5.6×10^19 FLOPs），通过受控消融对比单阶段替代方案；线性PASCAL VOC分割评测；并将冻结编码器用于DINO-WM的PushT任务，复用预训练预测器在无微调下补全被遮挡token。

Result: 线性PASCAL VOC分割达69.3 mIoU，优于所评测的I-JEPA与DSeq-JEPA检查点，且训练FLOPs比I-JEPA少25%；隐藏区域分割49.1 mIoU，优于Simple I-JEPA的44.3。在PushT开环规划（同时存在物体遮挡与σ=2像素高斯模糊）中，预测器补全将成功率从33%提升至65%，而Simple I-JEPA（含其预测器）为25%，iBOT像素修复为40%。

Conclusion: 两阶段隐空间预测是一种高效学习视觉表征的途径：所得表征在光度退化下仍保持有用，并能在部分观测下支持补全；预训练预测器不应被丢弃，可作为世界模型中的即插即用补全模块。

Abstract: Can a predictor trained to infer hidden image regions still help a world model plan when part of an object disappears from view and the visible context is blurred? We introduce PhiJEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architecture that adapts PhiNet's two-stage prediction to masked Transformers. From sparse, photometrically corrupted context, a Transformer block predicts hidden tokens of an independently corrupted teacher view. A token-wise MLP then maps these predictions to clean teacher tokens. Controlled ablations show that the combined objective improves dense features over the tested one-stage alternatives. Pre-trained on ImageNet-1K with $5.6\times10^{19}$ training FLOPs, PhiJEPA achieves 69.3 mIoU on linear PASCAL VOC segmentation, exceeding the evaluated I-JEPA and DSeq-JEPA checkpoints in segmentation accuracy, with 25\% fewer training FLOPs than I-JEPA. It also improves hidden-region segmentation over Simple I-JEPA (49.1 vs.\ 44.3 mIoU). As an application, we use the frozen encoder in DINO-WM on PushT, and reuse the pre-training predictor, which JEPAs usually discard, to complete occluded tokens without fine-tuning. In open-loop planning with simultaneous object occlusion and Gaussian blur ($σ=2$ pixels), predictor completion raises success from 33\% to 65\%, compared with 25\% for Simple I-JEPA with its predictor and 40\% for iBOT with pixel inpainting. Together, these results support two-stage latent prediction as an efficient approach to learning visual representations that remain useful under photometric degradation and support completion under partial observation.

</details>


### [98] [EchoAlign: Echocardiography foundation model with multilevel report alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27517)
*Ahmed Aly,Darya Taratynova,Lotfi Abdelkrim Mecharbat,Roman A. Sandler,Joseph Z. Sokol,Joshua Penn,Klaus H. Maier-Hein,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉-语言基础模型EchoAlign，通过结构-视频-报告的对齐与可见性矩阵约束监督，在百万级超声心动图数据上训练，实现分类、分级、测量与分割多任务统一建模，并在外部数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声心动图AI基础模型将整段视频与完整临床报告配对训练，但报告中包含该视图并未显示的结构与发现，导致模型把发现与偶然或伪相关信号关联，而非视觉证据本身，从而削弱准确性、解剖学接地性与临床可信度。

Method: 构建EchoAlign视觉-语言基础模型，基于159家临床中心的287万段超声心动图视频与图像训练；将每个心脏结构、视频与检查分别与其报告文本对齐，并引入可见性矩阵把监督信号限制在真正呈现该结构的视图上。

Result: 在来自25家以上临床中心的10个未见外部数据集的47万测试视频上，与5个公开基础模型比较，EchoAlign取得最高平均AUROC 0.890（95% CI 0.881–0.897）与最高平均二次加权kappa 0.530（95% CI 0.511–0.544）；在62项临床发现上，单一模型同时支持发现检测、严重程度分级、测量估计与结构分割。

Conclusion: 显式的视图-结构可见性约束可有效缓解报告监督中的噪声与伪相关，使单一基础模型统一覆盖检测、分级、测量与分割任务，并在大规模外部数据上验证了泛化性与临床可用性。

Abstract: Echocardiography assesses the structure and function of the heart. Cardiologists read each study across several standard views and report the findings visible in those views by cardiac structure. AI foundation models can learn to analyze these studies from clinical reports, reducing annotation burden and supporting clinical interpretation. However, existing models are trained by pairing each video with the full clinical report, including structures and findings its view does not show. This mismatch lets a model associate a finding with incidental or spurious signals rather than its visual evidence, which limits accuracy, anatomical grounding and clinical trust. Here we introduce EchoAlign, a vision-language foundation model developed on 2.87 million echocardiographic videos and images from 159 clinical centers. EchoAlign aligns each cardiac structure, video and study with its report text, and a visibility matrix restricts supervision to the views that actually depict each structure. We compare it with five public foundation models on 0.47 million test videos across ten unseen external datasets from more than 25 clinical centers. EchoAlign achieves the highest mean AUROC of 0.890 (95% CI, 0.881 to 0.897) for classification and the highest mean quadratic weighted kappa of 0.530 (95% CI, 0.511 to 0.544) for severity grading. Across 62 clinical findings, EchoAlign provides one model that detects findings, grades their severity, estimates measurements and segments cardiac structures.

</details>


### [99] [PaMuk-Bench: A Parallel Multilingual Multitask Benchmark for Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27521)
*Silviu-Florin Gheorghe,Radu-Stefan Popa,Adriana Valentina Costache,Adrian-Gabriel Chifu,Alessia Amelio,Paul Irofti,Anders Skaarup Johansen,Lallouani Bouchakour,Mohamed Lichouri,Khaled Lounnas,Neelu Madan,Yashvir Malik,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PaMuk-Bench：一个面向视觉-语言任务的平行多语言多任务基准，含10K+英文提示及六种语言（阿拉伯语、丹麦语、法语、印地语、意大利语、罗马尼亚语）的平行译文与配套生成/检索图像，覆盖五类任务，用于评测与缩小VLM在低资源语言上的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在英语等高资源语言上表现优异，但在低资源语言上性能显著下降，主要瓶颈是缺乏标注数据集。作者希望构建可跨语言比较的平行基准，以系统评估并缓解VLM的语言差距。

Method: 构建平行多语言多任务基准：精选10K+条英文提示，经半自动流程翻译为六种语言，并配套生成与检索式图像；任务覆盖文本到图像生成、图像描述、基于内容的图像检索、图像生成难度估计、图像检索难度估计五个方向，语言间平行对齐以支持跨语言对比评测。

Result: 实证显示，零样本英文预训练VLM与多语言预训练VLM的性能一致低于经微调的同类模型，验证了基准的有效性与判别力，并表明该基准能够显著缩小多种VLM架构与任务上的语言差距。

Conclusion: PaMuk-Bench是一个有价值的资源，可作为微调与评测的统一平台，用于减少VLM跨语言性能落差；资源已开源发布。

Abstract: While vision-language models (VLMs) currently obtain notable results for highly-studied languages, e.g. English, they exhibit significant performance drops in certain low-resource languages, especially due to the lack of annotated datasets. To this end, we propose PaMuk-Bench, a novel parallel multilingual multitask benchmark for vision-language tasks. Our benchmark includes a set of over 10K curated English prompts that are translated into six languages: Arabic, Danish, French, Hindi, Italian, Romanian. Parallel translations, which are obtained via a semi-automatic process, along with accompanying generated and retrieved images enable the comparative development and evaluation of VLMs across a wide range of tasks: text-to-image generation, image captioning, content-based image retrieval, image generation difficulty estimation, image retrieval difficulty estimation. We empirically show that zero-shot English and multilingual pre-trained VLMs compare consistently worse than their fine-tuned counterparts, confirming that PaMuk-Bench is a valuable resource that can drastically reduce the language gap across a variety of VLM architectures and tasks. PaMuk-Bench is available at https://github.com/ghesil/pamuk_bench.

</details>


### [100] [Fix Where It Fails: Root-Cause-Guided Agentic Harness Optimization for Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27529)
*Jee Eun Kim,Donghyeon Choi,Sangjune An,Hae Beom Lee,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RoHa-SGG，将"智能体化框架优化（agentic harness optimization）"迁移到场景图生成（SGG）任务：由根因分析智能体定位失败组件并诊断原因，再由提案智能体搜索能修复这些原因的推理代码（harness），在冻结模型的前提下免重训提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PredCls、SGCls、SGDet 等标准评测只显示去掉真值框/标签后性能下降多少，却不说明模型在哪里失败、为何失败。若知道失败位置与成因，就能在不重新训练模型的情况下直接修复。

Method: RoHa-SGG 以冻结的 SGG 模型为中心，优化其外围推理代码（harness）。包含两个 LLM 智能体：(1) 根因分析（RCA）智能体，识别模型失败的具体组件并诊断原因；(2) 提案智能体，在根因引导下搜索可修复这些原因的 harness。在 Visual Genome 与 PSG 上，用两阶段、单阶段、多模态 LLM 三类代表性 SGG 模型进行验证。

Result: 分析显示，真值三元组丢失的组件因模型与数据集而异。RoHa-SGG 在全部 5 个模型-数据集组合上所有指标均有提升：图约束 R@100 与 mR@100 在多模态 LLM 上最高提升 15.8 与 26.4 点，在两阶段/单阶段模型上最高提升 3.6 与 10.1 点。根因引导使 harness 搜索达到无引导搜索最终分数的最多 3 倍加速。

Conclusion: 免重训的根因引导 harness 优化是提升 SGG 性能的有效路径，且根因引导显著提升搜索效率；不同模型/数据集的失败组件不同，说明失败诊断具有模型与数据依赖性。

Abstract: Scene graph generation (SGG) aims to detect objects in an image and predict the relationships between them. The standard PredCls, SGCls, and SGDet settings show how much performance drops without ground-truth boxes and labels, but not where or why a model fails. Knowing this would allow fixing a model where it fails, without retraining. We propose RoHa-SGG (Root-cause-guided Harness optimization for SGG), which extends agentic harness optimization to SGG. It optimizes the harness, the inference code around a frozen SGG model. RoHa-SGG has two main LLM agents. A root-cause analysis (RCA) agent finds the components where the model fails and diagnoses why, and a proposer agent searches for a harness that fixes these causes. We evaluate RoHa-SGG on Visual Genome and PSG with representative two-stage, one-stage, and multimodal-LLM SGG models. The analysis shows that the components where ground-truth triplets are lost differ across models and datasets. RoHa-SGG improves every metric on all five model-dataset combinations. Graph-constrained R@100 and mR@100 rise by up to 15.8 and 26.4 points for the multimodal LLM and by up to 3.6 and 10.1 points for the two-stage and one-stage models. With root-cause guidance, the harness search reaches the final score of an unguided search up to three times faster.

</details>


### [101] [When Visually Rich Documents Meet Multi-Vector Retrieval for Region-level Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27542)
*Xu Yuan,Liangbo Ning,Yujuan Ding,Qing Li,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MV-VisRAG 是一个区域级 Vision-RAG 框架，用语义完整区域替代整页图像作为文档表示与视觉增强单位，并通过多向量晚交互检索实现低成本、高精度的检索与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉丰富文档（VRD）承载文本、视觉与版面三重信息，以文本为中心的 Text-RAG 无法充分利用；而现有 Vision-RAG 直接对文档图像检索与生成，虽保留多模态信息，却普遍采用粗粒度检索表示与整页视觉上下文，导致局部证据被淹没、冗余大、计算开销高。

Method: 提出 MV-VisRAG：先用内容自适应区域化模块（Content-Adaptive Regionalization）切分出语义完整区域；再用基于 VLM 的检索器将其编码为局部区域向量与页面级全局向量，检索器经多模态重构预训练与检索微调两阶段训练；基于紧凑多向量表示进行晚交互检索，并由匹配分数估计各区域贡献度，据此筛选相关区域，重排为紧凑视觉上下文供生成使用。

Result: 在五个基准上相较对应基线取得检索与生成性能提升，同时区域级增强显著减少视觉 token 数量与计算成本。

Conclusion: 以语义区域为粒度的多向量晚交互 Vision-RAG 能在保留多模态信息的同时压缩视觉上下文，是提升 VRD 检索增强生成效率与效果的有效方案；代码将开源。

Abstract: Visually rich documents (VRDs) convey knowledge through textual, visual, and layout information that text-centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Text-RAG) struggles to fully exploit. Recent Vision-RAG approaches enable retrieval and generation directly from document images, retaining this multimodal information. However, earlier Vision-RAG methods commonly adopt coarse retrieval representations and full-page visual contexts, which can obscure local evidence and introduce substantial redundancy and computational overhead. To address these challenges, we propose MV-VisRAG, a region-level Vision-RAG framework that uses semantically complete regions for both document representation and visual augmentation. Specifically, MV-VisRAG first constructs these regions through a ContentAdaptive Regionalization module. It then encodes them into local region vectors and a page-level global vector using a VLM-based retriever trained through multimodal reconstruction pretraining and retrieval fine-tuning. With this compact multi-vector representation, MV-VisRAG performs late-interaction retrieval and estimates region contributions from the resulting matching scores. These contributions guide the selection of relevant regions, which are then rearranged into a compact visual context for generation. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate retrieval and generation gains over corresponding baselines, while region-level augmentation substantially reduces visual tokens and computational cost. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/xandery-geek/MV-VisRAG.

</details>


### [102] [GameScrub: Learning HUD Removal from Real Gameplay with Recovered Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27547)
*Mohammad Areeb Qazi,Yiyan Hu,Yanan Wang,Xuezhi Liang,Xin Li,Fan Yang,Xin Gao,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GameScrub-Data训练免恢复管线，从真实游戏录像中通过视频扩散模型修复HUD区域生成伪干净目标，据此训练无需推理掩码的HUD去除模型GameScrub。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏录像可用于学习虚拟世界动态，但画面中的HUD遵循界面规则而非场景动态，干扰学习。现有方法靠合成配对数据（把HUD贴到干净录像上）需要单独整理的干净语料，且合成HUD与真实HUD有差距，训练出的模型对真实HUD元素仍有40–50%未被去除。

Method: 从真实录像直接恢复伪干净目标：用预训练视频扩散模型对HUD区域做修复。为防止HUD痕迹泄漏进恢复目标（潜在视频自编码器会跨掩码边界混合信息），管线采取三项措施：编码前用光流引导的背景估计预填充HUD区域、将HUD排除在场景条件之外、通过像素空间合成把修复限制在掩码区域内。由此得到的配对数据用于训练GameScrub——一个推理时无需掩码或干净参考帧的视频扩散去除模型。

Result: 在真实场景基准Game2World上，GameScrub的伪影校正去除得分为71.95，强于最强无参考GameCleaner配置的57.86；标注HUD元素去除率为90.06%，对比59.07%。

Conclusion: 真实录像配合恢复出的目标即可支撑有效的HUD去除，为世界模型与可控视频生成提供无HUD的游戏数据。

Abstract: Gameplay videos are a rich source of experience for learning virtual-world dynamics, yet their frames contain heads-up displays (HUDs) that follow interface rules rather than scene dynamics. Removing HUDs would make this footage directly usable for learning world dynamics. However, training a removal model requires paired clips with and without HUDs, whereas real recordings contain only HUD-overlaid footage. Existing work synthesizes pairs by compositing HUD graphics onto HUD-free footage, requiring a separately curated clean corpus and producing HUDs that only approximate real ones. Models trained on such pairs leave 40 to 50% of real HUD elements untouched. We instead recover pseudo-clean targets directly from real recordings by inpainting HUD regions with a pretrained video diffusion model. Preventing HUD traces from leaking into recovered targets is challenging because latent video autoencoders mix information across mask boundaries. Our training-free recovery pipeline, GameScrub-Data, pre-fills HUD regions with flow-guided background estimates before encoding, excludes HUDs from scene conditioning, and confines restoration to masked regions through pixel-space compositing. The resulting pairs train GameScrub, a video diffusion removal model that handles diverse HUDs without inference-time masks or clean reference images. On the in-the-wild Game2World benchmark, GameScrub achieves an artifact-adjusted removal score of 71.95, compared with 57.86 for the strongest reference-free GameCleaner configuration, and removes 90.06% of annotated HUD elements versus 59.07%. These results demonstrate that real recordings with recovered targets can support effective HUD removal, advancing HUD-free gameplay data for world models and controllable video generation.

</details>


### [103] [Leakage-Free Is Not Confounder-Free: What a Patient-Level Split Cannot Certify About a Medical Imaging Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27585)
*Ignazio Gallo,Mattia Gatti,Leonardo Sgroi,Darwin Castillo,Yuliana Jiménez-Gaona,Nicola Landro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"五问审计"框架检验候选混杂因素：DMR-IR乳腺热成像数据集中，采集日期单独预测恶性达AUROC 0.980，超过九条影像模型最佳值0.949；一旦控制日期，判别力即崩塌或仅存于基准无法归因的比较；第二队列以患者年龄为优势混杂通道复制同样结论——缺乏跨标签采集支持，该基准上的任何评估都无法证明剩余信号是病理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者级划分是医学影像评估防泄漏的标准手段，但无法移除每折共同继承的混杂：一个在整个队列中与诊断对齐的采集变量。多数乳腺热成像机器学习结果依赖DMR-IR公开数据集，其良性/恶性患者在采集日期上系统性错位，可能使全部既往结论被采集变量而非病理学解释。作者希望给出可复用的审计流程，回答"基准上的高AUROC究竟来自什么"。

Method: 提出五问审计：(1)混杂因素对诊断的预测力；(2)能否从像素中解码出该因素（同诊断患者内）；(3)诊断分数是否依赖该因素；(4)图像在混杂之外是否提供增量预测价值；(5)控制混杂后还剩多少判别力。在DMR-IR上按严格患者不相交协议对236例患者评估九个影像模型分支，进行配对折比较、跨标签同日期配对分析，并在第二个独立采集队列（优势通道为年龄）上复现审计。

Result: 采集日期单独预测恶性AUROC 0.980，高于九个影像分支中最好的0.949。采集年份可在同诊断患者间由像素解码，主要经由乳腺区域外的全局热偏移；但仅"整次就诊全帧合并"分支可证明将该信息带入诊断分数。任何分支在配对折上均未超过日期基线；控制日期后判别力崩塌，或仅保留在基准无法归因的比较上。数据集仅含8对跨标签同日期样本（来自10名患者），限制了日期组内的可靠评估。第二队列以患者年龄为主导混杂通道，给出相同的审计结论。

Conclusion: 患者级划分不足以保障评估有效性；采集变量可构成与诊断完全对齐的隐性混杂，且可被模型从图像中解码利用。缓解措施或可抑制此类混杂，但在缺乏跨标签采集支持的情况下，任何基于该基准的评估都无法证明剩余判别力源自病理学。后续工作应报告采集变量审计、扩充跨标签采集覆盖，并将日期/设备等元数据作为强制基线比较对象。

Abstract: Patient-level splitting is the standard safeguard against subject leakage in medical imaging evaluation, but it cannot remove a confounder that every fold inherits: an acquisition variable aligned with the diagnosis across the whole cohort. We present a five-question audit of a candidate confounder: how well it predicts the diagnosis, whether it can be decoded from the images, whether diagnostic scores depend on it, whether the image adds predictive value beyond it and how much discrimination survives once it is controlled. We apply it to DMR-IR, the public dataset on which most machine-learning results in breast thermography rest. On 236 patients under a strict patient-disjoint protocol, the acquisition date alone predicts malignancy at AUROC 0.980, above the best of nine imaging arms (0.949), because benign and malignant patients were recruited in different years.
