The acquisition year is decodable from the pixels among patients who share a diagnosis, largely through a global thermal offset outside the breast, yet only one arm, the one pooling every frame of a visit, demonstrably carries it into its diagnostic score. No arm improves on the date on paired folds, and once the date is controlled, discrimination either collapses or survives only on comparisons the benchmark cannot attribute. The dataset contains only eight cross-label same-date pairs, from ten patients, which limits reliable evaluation within recorded acquisition-date groups. A second, independently acquired cohort, whose dominant channel is patient age, returns the same audit outcome from a different confounder. Mitigation may suppress such a confounder, but without cross-label acquisition support no evaluation on the benchmark can certify that what remains is pathology.

</details>


### [104] [Sketch2TikZ: Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning for Converting Hand-Drawn Sketches to Executable TikZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27620)
*Shubham Paliwal,Arushi Jain,Monika Sharma,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出强化学习框架 Sketch2TikZ，用多奖励序列策略把噪声手绘草图转成可编译的 TikZ 代码，在 SkeTikZ 与 FlowChart-3B 上接近完美的编译成功率并提升视觉/文本对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手绘草图转 TikZ 可提供结构化图表表示，但输入噪声大、歧义多，token 级监督难以保证全局图表有效性，导致可靠解析困难。

Method: 强化学习框架，采用序贯多奖励策略：Code-Structure、OCR、CleanDIFT、Self-Similarity 四项奖励，分别约束文本、视觉与结构正确性；并引入课程式奖励调度（reward scheduling）以降低奖励干扰。

Result: 在 SkeTikZ 和 FlowChart-3B 基准上以 CSR、图像相似度、代码相似度、字符相似度评价：编译成功率接近完美，视觉与文本对齐优于强开源基线，与闭源 GPT 类 VLM 具竞争力。消融显示课程式奖励调度对减少奖励冲突、稳定训练、缓解代理指标过优化至关重要。

Conclusion: 参考感知（reference-aware）的多目标强化学习对从噪声草图生成结构化图表有效。

Abstract: Automatic digitization of hand-drawn sketches into editable formats is crucial for modern document workflows and LaTeX integration. Converting sketches into executable TikZ code provides a structured representation of diagrams, but remains challenging due to noisy and ambiguous inputs, which hinder reliable interpretation and limit the effectiveness of token-level supervision for ensuring global diagram validity. To address these challenges, we propose Sketch2TikZ, a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes sketch-to-code generation via a sequential multi-reward strategy, combining Code-Structure ($\mathcal{R}_{cs}$), OCR ($\mathcal{R}_{ocr}$), CleanDIFT ($\mathcal{R}_{cd}$), and Self-Similarity ($\mathcal{R}_{ss}$) rewards to enforce textual, visual and structural correctness. We evaluate Sketch2TikZ on the SkeTikZ and FlowChart-3B benchmarks using metrics including compilation success rate (CSR), image similarity, code similarity, and character similarity. Our method achieves near-perfect compilation success and improves visual and textual alignment over strong open-source baselines, while remaining competitive with proprietary GPT-based vision-language models. Extensive ablations demonstrate that curriculum-based reward scheduling is critical for reducing reward interference, stabilizing training and mitigating proxy over-optimization. Overall, our results highlight the effectiveness of reference-aware, multi-objective reinforcement learning for structured diagram generation from noisy sketches.

</details>


### [105] [Automating Multi-View Quality Assessment of 3D Annotation Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27621)
*Hongyi Yang,Jinao Wu,Yanwei Zhang,Shengwen Fu,Haibin Ling,Bingyao Huang,Jingwei Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态大模型（MLLM）的多视点标注布局质量评估方法，并构建ALQA数据集（3,216个布局、5视点、5质量维度、3人独立评分），在人类评分一致性和布局排序偏好上优于MLLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D可视化中文本标签与目标位置的连接构成标注布局，其质量评估可用于比较不同布局方案。但现有质量指标多只关注布局的特定属性，难以完整反映人类对整体质量的判断；同时布局在某一视点清晰，换一个视点可能被遮挡或显得杂乱，单视点评估不足。

Method: 以人类评分的结构化监督信号微调一个紧凑型MLLM，对每个视点、每个质量维度分别预测评分；再将各维度评分逐级聚合为视点级与样本级质量分数。为此构建ALQA数据集：1,072个3D物体（13类）共3,216个布局样本，每个布局从5个预定义视点渲染，每个视点由3名评分者在5个质量维度上独立打分。

Result: 在ALQA上，该方法与人类参考分数的一致性高于所评估的MLLM基线；用户研究显示其在布局排序上与人偏好的吻合度较高。

Conclusion: 多视点、多维度的MLLM评分聚合可有效逼近人类对标注布局整体质量的判断，ALQA为训练与评测提供了带人类评分基准的资源。

Abstract: Text labels provide descriptive information about elements in visual scenes. In 3D visualization, the arrangement of these labels and their connections to target locations forms an annotation layout. Assessing layout quality supports comparisons between alternative layouts. However, existing quality measures often focus on specific layout properties and may not fully reflect human judgments of overall quality. Moreover, a layout may appear clear from one viewpoint but suffer from occlusion or clutter from another. To address these issues, we propose a multi-view quality assessment method based on a multimodal large language model (MLLM). We fine-tune a compact MLLM using structured supervision from human ratings to predict a rating for each quality dimension in each view. These ratings are aggregated into view-level and sample-level quality scores. To support training and evaluation, we construct the Annotation Layouts with Quality Assessment (ALQA) dataset, containing 3,216 layout samples from 1,072 3D objects across 13 categories. Each layout is rendered from 5 predefined viewpoints, and each view is independently rated by 3 human raters across 5 quality dimensions. On ALQA, our method agrees more closely with human reference scores than the evaluated MLLM baselines, and a user study demonstrates strong agreement with human preferences in layout ranking.

</details>


### [106] [Points, Not Pixels: Keypoints, Masks and Oblique Geometry for Floating-Roof Tank Fill Estimation from Overhead Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27624)
*Mohd Shahid Kaleem,Mohd Hozaifa Khan,Maaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从光学俯视影像中测量浮顶油罐充装量的方法：以子像素关键点回归罐缘与阴影边界，并用建模斜视视角的闭式估计器反演浮顶深度；同时发布含24,987张物理渲染图的SynTank-25k数据集。该方法在SynTank-25k上将充装误差较分割基线降低22%，在窄于6像素的阴影上优于真实二值掩膜转换结果，斜视估计器在最陡视角下将天底公式误差降低约11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮顶罐充装量仅以罐缘在浮顶上投下的细窄月牙形阴影呈现，俯视分辨率下仅数像素宽，难以精确测量；且基于天底视角的高度换算在斜视影像中会引入显著误差；公开油罐数据集普遍缺乏浮顶位置标注，制约监督学习。

Method: 在局部仿射相机假设下建立罐缘—阴影位移与浮顶深度之间由太阳与相机几何决定的关系；将罐缘与阴影边界作为子像素关键点进行回归，并用可建模斜视视角的闭式估计器反演该关系；构建SynTank-25k数据集（24,987张基于物理的渲染图像，含精确浮顶、阴影与相机几何标注）。

Result: 在SynTank-25k上，采用斜视估计器的关键点流程使充装误差较分割基线降低22%；对窄于6像素的阴影，其误差低于用真实二值掩膜转换所得结果；斜视估计器在最陡视角下将天底公式误差降低约11倍；在6,558个由边界框定义的真实NAIP油罐裁剪图上，约半数充装估计通过内部一致性检验（NAIP无充装标签）。

Conclusion: 以子像素边界关键点结合斜视感知闭式几何反演，可显著提升细窄阴影条件下的浮顶深度测量精度，并优于传统分割掩膜与天底公式方案；SynTank-25k为该任务提供了带精确几何真值的基准，数据与代码将公开。

Abstract: The floating roof of an oil storage tank rests on the stored liquid, so its depth below the rim measures how full the tank is. In optical overhead imagery, this depth appears only as the crescent shadow the rim casts on the roof, often a few pixels wide. Thin shadows are difficult to measure at overhead-image resolution, and nadir-based height conversions introduce errors in oblique views. Under a local affine camera, roof depth determines the rim-to-shadow displacement through the sun and camera geometry. We regress the boundaries as sub-pixel keypoints and invert this relation with a closed-form estimator that models oblique views. Available public tank datasets do not label roof position, so we introduce SynTank-25k, 24,987 physically based renders with exact roof, shadow and camera geometry. Using the oblique estimator, the keypoint pipeline reduces fill error by $22\%$ relative to the segmentation baseline on SynTank-25k. On shadows narrower than $6\text{ px}$, its error is also lower than that obtained by converting true binary masks. Our oblique estimator reduces the error of the nadir formula elevenfold at the steepest views. On 6,558 real NAIP tank crops defined by bounding boxes, about half of the fill estimates pass internal consistency checks; NAIP has no fill labels. Data and code will be released.

</details>


### [107] [Segment Everything, Materialize Nothing: Exact Streaming Inference for Many-Class 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27630)
*Fabian Isensee,Jakob Wasserthal,Lalith Kumar Shiyam Sundar,Maximilian Rokuss,Moritz Langenberg,Constantin Ulrich Harsy,Stefan Michael Happold,Klaus H. Maier-Hein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无近似的流式推理方案，消除nnU-Net/MONAI中随类别数×体积增长的内存瓶颈，使单模型125类全身CT分割可部署，速度提升5.8–15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TotalSegmentator、MOOSE等全身分割工具因内存限制需将100+类拆分到5–6个网络。标准3D推理会为每个类别和体素在多个网格上保存logit，125类中位全身CT需76 GB内存。作者认为该内存墙源于推理的物化方式而非任务本身。

Method: 观察到网络之后所有步骤沿单一轴局部，因此可用一个patch厚的环形缓冲区流式处理输出；分类头与blending、重采样可交换，故环中传递网络特征而非类别logit。方法要求模型具pointwise affine head，无需重训练，可直接替换nnU-Net、TotalSegmentator、MOOSE。

Result: 内存不再随类别数×体积增长，扫描仪网格上标签图保持精确、无近似。单模型多类在分割质量上匹配或略超工具箱的5–6个专家模型，端到端快5.8–15倍，RAM少3.2–7.6倍，VRAM低于8 GB。

Conclusion: 以流式推理替代logit物化，可将此前不可行的多类模型变得可部署；代码已并入nnU-Net，并计划发布两套工具箱的统一模型。

Abstract: Whole-body segmentation tools such as TotalSegmentator and MOOSE split more than 100 classes across five or six networks. The reason is memory. Standard 3D inference, as implemented in nnU-Net and MONAI, holds a logit for every class and voxel, on the working grid and again on the scanner's grid. For a median whole-body CT at 125 classes, that is 76 GB. We show that this memory wall lies in what inference materializes, not in the task, and remove it without approximation. Every step after the network is local along one axis, so its outputs can be streamed through a ring one patch thick. The classification head commutes with blending and resampling, so the ring carries the network's features instead of the class logits. Memory thus no longer grows with classes times volume, and the label map on the scanner's grid stays exact. This holds for any model with a pointwise affine head, without retraining. Many-class models, previously impractical, become deployable. One model trained on all classes matches or slightly exceeds the toolkits' five or six specialists in segmentation quality and runs 5.8-15x faster end to end, with 3.2-7.6x less RAM and under 8 GB of VRAM. We provide the method as a drop-in for nnU-Net, TotalSegmentator and MOOSE. The code is part of nnU-Net (https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/nnUNet), and unified models for both toolkits will be released.

</details>


### [108] [MoHA: Model-Conditioned Harness Adaptation for Long-Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27646)
*Jianghan Chao,Wenhui Tan,Hang Su,Qiuyu Tian,Tanxiao Li,Bingzi Zhang,Zequn Liu,Ruihua Song,Yingce Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoHA：将长视频理解的执行harness视为模型条件化优化对象，针对不同planner-observer模型栈自动适配harness，在Video-MME Full上提升6.2-27.1个百分点，Video-Holmes提升3.1-20.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解日益依赖主动获取证据的agentic系统，其执行harness需协调语言层规划与视频层观察、证据获取，但现有harness对不同模型栈是固定不变的，无法匹配hosted模型的能力特性。

Method: 提出MoHA框架：从共同的初始harness出发，通过分析失败的校准轨迹诊断模型-harness不匹配，进而适配planner侧推理支持（概览、记忆、验证）与observer侧能力分配（OCR、ASR专用器）的结构；结构适配后，再单独为宿主模型栈校准observer执行配置。

Result: 在三个冻结的planner栈上，相较固定初始harness，Video-MME Full准确率提升6.2-27.1个百分点，Video-Holmes提升3.1-20.1个百分点；跨模型迁移实验表明适配后的harness仅能部分泛化到其他模型。

Conclusion: harness应作为长视频理解中模型条件化的优化层，需针对具体planner-observer栈定制，而非跨模型栈通用；跨模型迁移的部分性进一步印证harness与模型的强耦合。

Abstract: Long-video understanding increasingly relies on agentic systems that actively acquire evidence rather than process fixed video representations. Their execution harnesses must coordinate language-level planning with video-level observation and evidence acquisition, yet are typically fixed across model stacks. We therefore introduce MoHA, a framework for model-conditioned harness adaptation that adapts a common initial execution harness to the specific planner and observer models it hosts. MoHA treats the harness itself as a model-conditioned optimization object: under the same task distribution and initialization, different planner-observer stacks can benefit from different executable harnesses. Starting from a common initial harness, MoHA analyzes failed calibration traces to diagnose model-harness mismatches and adapts both planner-side reasoning support, such as overview, memory, and verification, and observer-side capability assignments, including OCR and ASR specialists. After structural adaptation, MoHA separately calibrates observer execution configurations for the hosted model stack. Across three frozen planner stacks, MoHA improves Video-MME Full accuracy by 6.2-27.1 percentage points and Video-Holmes accuracy by 3.1-20.1 points over the fixed initial harness. Cross-model transfer further shows that the adapted harnesses generalize only partially to other models, highlighting the harness as a model-conditioned optimization layer for long-video understanding. Code is available at https://github.com/roverx12345/MoHA.

</details>


### [109] [GeoScaffold: From Latent to Realized Geometry in Collaborative Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27667)
*Kang Yang,Yance Fang,Gaochen Cui,Xiaoqiang Jin,Deying Li,Yongcai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoScaffold是一种仅训练期使用的轻量辅助目标，通过在正锚点上预测前景掩码提供密集空间监督，缓解协同3D检测中分类分数无���反映定位质量的问题，在UrbanIng-V2X上对11种融合方法平均提升AP@0.5/AP@0.7达0.98/0.55点，且推理无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同3D检测融合BEV特征蕴含丰富几何信息，但沿用的单智能体标准读出流程（锚点评分、框解码、NMS）未能充分利用这些几何线索。作者在UrbanIng-V2X上量化该差距：协同使约2/3正锚点的定位改善，但NMS在两框重叠时保留定位更优框的比例不足一半。根因是监督失配——分类分数仅训练区分前景/背景，训练中无任何约束要求其按定位质量排序。

Method: 提出GeoScaffold：轻量、仅训练期的辅助目标。在每个正锚点上预测前景掩码，即覆盖该锚点的网格中落在GT框内的单元，为7维框编码所隐含的空间信息提供显式密集监督。其损失与检测损失相加，经由融合BEV反向更新编码器与融合模块，促使每个正锚点（含最高分锚点）回归更优定位框。推理时移除辅助头，保持原检测器结构、通信逻辑与推理开销不变。

Result: 在UrbanIng-V2X验证集上，对全部11种融合方法平均提升AP@0.5 0.98点、AP@0.7 0.55点；增益可迁移至未见交叉路口上的多数方法，并在V2V4Real上对全部10种产生检测结果的方法均有效。

Conclusion: 分类分数与定位质量之间的监督失配是协同3D检测读出阶段的性能瓶颈；通过训练期密集空间监督（前景掩码辅助任务）可有效校正，且不影响推理效率与通信/融合流程，具良好通用性与可移植性。

Abstract: Most collaborative 3D detectors fuse bird's-eye-view (BEV) features from connected vehicles and roadside infrastructure to aggregate complementary observations. Although modern communication, alignment, and fusion pipelines encode rich latent geometry in the fused BEV features, the standard readout pipeline inherited from single-agent detectors, i.e., anchor scoring, box decoding, and non-maximum suppression (NMS), does not fully exploit these geometric cues. We empirically identify and quantify this gap on UrbanIng-V2X. Collaboration improves localization at about two-thirds of positive anchors, yet when NMS resolves two overlapping collaborative boxes of one object, it keeps the better-localized box less than half of the time. A key cause is a supervision mismatch: classification scores are trained only for foreground-background discrimination, so nothing in training requires them to rank boxes by localization quality. To address this limitation, we introduce GeoScaffold, a lightweight training-only auxiliary objective. It predicts a foreground mask at each positive anchor, i.e., the cells of a grid over the anchor that lie inside the ground-truth box, to supply the dense spatial supervision that the seven-dimensional box code leaves implicit. Its loss, added to the detection loss, updates the encoder and fusion modules through the fused BEV and trains the detector toward a better-localized box at every positive anchor, including the top-scored one. The auxiliary head is removed at inference, preserving the original detector architecture, communication logic, and inference cost. GeoScaffold raises AP@0.5 and AP@0.7 by 0.98 and 0.55 points on average across all 11 fusion methods on the UrbanIng-V2X validation split, with gains extending to most methods on an unseen intersection and to all ten methods that produce detections on V2V4Real.

</details>


### [110] [CanopyFormer: Attention-Guided SegFormer for Explainable Forest Cover Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27674)
*Dhinanjaya Fernando,Dinura Ginige,Kalana Lakshan,Chanupa Gurusinghe,Lasana Pahanga,Dileesha Manamperi,Thimira Sahan,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CanopyFormer，一种以注意力一致性损失引导 SegFormer-B0 注意力对齐森林冠层掩膜的注意力可解释性方法，AAMO 提升约 22 倍而 Dice 仅小幅下降，但删除实验显示注意力对齐与忠实性并不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空森林分割中，注意力通常仅作为架构组件、训练后才做事后可视化，缺乏对注意力的监督，且可解释性多为定性评估、缺少定量指标与忠实性检验。

Method: 在 SegFormer-B0 上引入注意力一致性损失（Attention Consistency Loss），使注意力与真实森林掩膜对齐；针对空间缩减注意力的非方阵问题，将梯度加权注意力 Rollout 限制在注意力矩阵为方阵的最后一个编码阶段；提出平均注意力-掩膜重叠度 AAMO 定量衡量对齐程度，并加入删除测试（deletion test）评估忠实性；另考察边界精化模块与 MSE / KL 两类注意力损失的影响。

Result: 在 5108 张图像上，AAMO 由 0.0334 提升至 0.7476（约为原始 SegFormer-B0 的 22 倍），Dice 由 0.8743 轻微降至 0.8577。删除测试显示注意力对齐提升并未带来忠实性提升：删除被关注度最高的 25% 图块使森林得分下降 12.1%，而删除随机图块下降 33.0%。注意力监督不增加推理开销，但因双重反向传播使每步训练慢 2.6–2.8 倍。对齐增益可迁移至 LoveDA 数据集。边界精化模块未带来一致的 Dice 提升且降低 AAMO；MSE 显著优于 KL 散度作为注意力损失。

Conclusion: 注意力监督可有效提升分割模型注意力与目标区域的对齐度且几乎不损精度，但注意力对齐不等于注意力忠实性，二者需分别评估；方法具有零推理成本和一定跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: In transformer segmentation models, attention is usually visualized after training rather than supervised. Although attention supervision has been explored for natural images and driving scenes, aerial forest segmentation typically uses attention only as an architectural component and evaluates interpretability qualitatively. We present CanopyFormer, an attention-guided SegFormer-B0 that uses an Attention Consistency Loss to align attention with the ground-truth forest mask, encouraging attention to focus on canopy regions. Since SegFormer uses spatial-reduction attention, we adapt Gradient-weighted Attention Rollout by restricting it to the final encoder stage, where the attention matrix is square. We introduce Average Attention-Mask Overlap (AAMO) to measure attention alignment and incorporate a deletion test to evaluate faithfulness. On 5108 images, our method increases AAMO from 0.0334 to 0.7476, approximately 22 times the vanilla SegFormer-B0 result, with a modest decrease in Dice score from 0.8743 to 0.8577. The deletion test reveals that improved alignment does not necessarily translate into improved faithfulness: removing the most-attended 25% of patches reduces the forest score by 12.1%, compared with 33.0% when removing random patches. Attention supervision introduces no additional inference cost, although double backpropagation makes each training step 2.6 to 2.8 times slower. The attention alignment improvement also generalizes to a second dataset, LoveDA. Additionally, a Boundary Refinement Module provides no consistent Dice improvement and reduces AAMO, while mean squared error substantially outperforms Kullback-Leibler divergence as the attention loss.

</details>


### [111] [VeilGS: Solving Darkness as a Veil of 2D Basis Maps over Retinex 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27719)
*Mohab Kishawy,Chengzhou Tang,Shahram Shirani,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VeilGS：将暗光退化建模为覆盖在清晰场景上的"面纱"（空间变化的增益+全局色调曲线），用albedo与shading latent替代球谐函数的3DGS方法，实现无配对正常光监督的低光照新视角合成与复原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光捕获中光照在空间上不均匀（近光源暗物体与无光角落亮物体照明差异大），且相机用色调曲线压缩动态范围。现有低光3D重建方法两类缺陷：一是对整幅视图所有像素套用同一曲线，无法区分空间差异；二是把暗光直接存入3D表示中，使黑暗信息吸收场景细节。

Method: 1) 每个高斯携带albedo与shading latent，取代球谐函数，解耦表面颜色与光照；2) 每视角先经空间变化增益、再经全局色调曲线形成"面纱"致暗，去掉面纱渲染即得复原场景；3) 小型网络从观测预测增益的粗糙基，其系数按视角求解（solve）而非学习；4) 通过该求解过程反向传播训练，抑制高斯吸收面纱可解释的成分；无需配对正常光监督，不存储逐视角参数。

Result: Gaussian-DK上复原渲染在无参考NIQE与CLIP-IQA指标最优，55人盲测用户研究评分最高；LOM数据集上与SOTA结果相当（competitive）。

Conclusion: 显式建模空间变化光照增益与全局色调曲线、并将二者排除在3D表示之外，是低光新视角合成的有效范式，可在无监督设置下取得领先的复原质量。

Abstract: In a dark capture, the darkness is not uniform. How much light reaches a surface depends on where it lies relative to the few light sources, and the camera compresses what remains with a tone curve. Most low-light 3D reconstruction methods either apply one curve to every pixel of a view, so a dark object beside a lamp and a bright one in an unlit corner receive the same correction, or store the darkness in the 3D representation, where it can absorb scene detail. We present VeilGS, a 3D Gaussian Splatting method for low-light novel view synthesis that treats the degradation as a veil drawn over a clear scene. Each Gaussian carries an albedo and a shading latent instead of spherical harmonics, separating surface colour from illumination. Each view is darkened by a spatially varying gain followed by a global tone curve, and rendering without this veil gives the restored scene. A small network predicts a coarse basis for the gain from the observation, and its coefficients are solved for each view rather than learned. Training through this solve discourages the Gaussians from absorbing what the veil can explain. VeilGS needs no paired normal-light supervision and stores no per-view parameters. On Gaussian-DK, its restored renders score best on no-reference NIQE and CLIP-IQA and are rated highest in a blind user study with 55 participants. On LOM, it achieves results competitive with the state of the art.

</details>


### [112] [PAWS: A Parametric Model of Canine Pose and Shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27720)
*Theo Wieland,Zala Žgank,Silvia Zuffi,Ylva Mellbin,Elin Hernlund,Hedvig Kjellström*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAWS——基于真实犬类3D扫描学习的犬科形状与姿态参数化模型，可支撑表面重建与图像回归等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 犬类品种在体型尺寸、比例和身体形态上差异巨大，捕获其完整形态多样性极具挑战；现有模型多非源自真实犬只的多姿态数据，难以真实建模犬的姿态与形状。

Method: 采集31只不同品种、不同体型的犬只在多种自然动作下的3D扫描数据；在此基础上构建参数化模型，将解剖学上合理的骨架与从3D扫描中学到的线性形状空间相结合。

Result: 模型源自多姿态真实犬只，可实现逼真的犬类姿态与形状建模；作为基础框架在表面重建与图像回归等下游任务中表现稳健，且实验表明仅需少量解剖学有效的自由度即可准确估计犬的姿态与形状。

Conclusion: PAWS为犬类形状与姿态建模提供了真实数据驱动、解剖合理且自由度紧凑的参数化框架，具有良好的泛化性与下游任务适用性。

Abstract: We introduce PAWS, a parametric model of canine shape and pose, learned from 3D scans of real dogs. Capturing the full diversity of dogs is challenging, as different breeds vary in size, proportion, and body shape. To capture this range, we scanned 31 dogs across multiple breeds and sizes performing a range of natural movements. From this dataset, we build a parametric model that combines an anatomically faithful skeleton with a linear shape space learned from the 3D scans. Unlike previous models, our model is learned from real dogs in varying poses, allowing for realistic modelling of canine pose and shape.
  We demonstrate that building on PAWS provides a robust framework for downstream tasks, such as surface reconstruction and image regression. In particular, we show that a limited number of anatomically valid degrees of freedom is sufficient to accurately estimate dog pose and shape.

</details>


### [113] [Quantitative Measurement of Pavement Distress based on Vehicle Camera and Perspective Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27728)
*Sike Wang,Yizhou Lin,Shuo Li,Yi Jiang,Chengcheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需现场标定的车载相机图像路面病害定量测量方法：先基于车道检测确定四边形感兴趣区域并构建透视变换矩阵进行图像校正，再利用路面标线比例与给定尺度完成尺寸校正，最后用目标检测算法得到病害的定量尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于提高病害检测精度，而缺乏长度、面积等定量测量；现场相机标定虽可提供尺度信息，但面对来自不同车辆的海量车载图像不具可行性。

Method: 1）基于车道线检测识别四边形感兴趣区域，构建透视变换矩阵完成图像透视校正；2）利用图像中道路标线的比例和指定尺度进行尺寸校正，同时估算所选区域的实际宽度；3）对校正后的图像应用目标检测算法，获得病害的定量测量结果。同一车辆相机采集的图像只需极少人工即可完成透视校正。

Result: 测得的路面修补（patch）长度与路面摩擦数据呈现一致的变化趋势，表明测量结果具有合理性。

Conclusion: 仅利用图像信息即可完成车载相机标定，实现无需额外现场实验的定量路面病害检测，为处理海量车载相机数据提供了实用方案，尤其适用于网联车辆产生的大量未标定道路图像。

Abstract: Using image data is becoming an important method for highway agencies to automatically evaluate pavement conditions. However, most studies focus on improving detection accuracy rather than obtaining quantitative measurements, such as the length or area of pavement distress. Although field camera calibration can provide such measurements, it is impractical for large volumes of onboard images collected from different vehicles. This study develops a method to quantitatively measure pavement distress directly from in-vehicle camera images without on-site camera calibration. A quadrilateral region of interest is first identified based on lane detection and used to construct a perspective transformation matrix for image correction. Size correction is then performed using the proportions of road markings in the image and a specified scale, which also enables estimation of the actual width of the selected region. The corrected images are subsequently processed using object detection algorithms to obtain quantitative distress measurements. The proposed method can efficiently perform perspective correction for images collected by the same vehicle camera with minimal manual effort. The measured pavement patch lengths show a trend consistent with pavement friction data. By calibrating vehicle cameras using only image information, this study enables quantitative pavement distress detection without additional field experiments. The approach provides a practical solution for processing large volumes of vehicle-camera data, particularly as connected vehicles generate increasing amounts of uncalibrated roadway imagery.

</details>


### [114] [SUPERVISE: A Unified Framework for Standardized and Reproducible Superpixel Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27753)
*Julien Walther,Rémi Giraud,Michaël Clément*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SUPERVISE——一个统一、可复现的超像素分割评测基准，基于按实际生成超像素数插值的尺度归一化协议，并对20个指标×7个数据集×31种方法做了首个大规模统计分析，揭示指标间高度相关与冗余，主张报告紧凑的代表性指标集，同时开源代码、标签图与评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超像素分割评测长期缺乏标准化：不同方法在异质协议、不一致的尺度区间下比较，所用指标子集存在冗余与错配，导致跨方法对比不公平、结论不可复现。

Method: 构建SUPERVISE框架：采用基于实际生成超像素数量做插值的尺度归一化协议，统一尺度范围与指标集合，支持公平一致的跨方法比较；框架轻量、模块化，基于预计算的共享标签图，便于大规模可复现评测。

Result: 首次对20个评测指标在7个数据集、31种方法上进行大规模统计分析，发现指标间存在强相关性与显著冗余，对常见评测实践构成挑战，为报告紧凑的代表性指标子集提供实证依据。

Conclusion: SUPERVISE统一了超像素评测协议并量化了指标冗余，建议采用精简的代表性指标进行报告；代码、标签图与评测结果已公开，降低社区开展大规模可复现评测的门槛。

Abstract: Image segmentation into superpixels is a widely used technique in computer vision, with a large body of work developed over the years. Yet, its evaluation remains poorly standardized, as methods are compared under heterogeneous protocols, across inconsistent scale ranges, and with redundant or misaligned metric subsets. In this work, we introduce SUPERVISE (SUperpixel PERformance VISualization and Evaluation), a unified and reproducible benchmark that addresses these longstanding inconsistencies. Built on a scale-normalized protocol based on interpolation over the actual number of generated superpixels, SUPERVISE enables fair and consistent cross-method comparisons. We leverage this standardized framework to conduct the first large-scale statistical analysis of 20 evaluation metrics across 7 datasets and 31 methods, revealing strong inter-metric correlations and significant redundancy that challenge common evaluation practices. These findings provide empirical support for reporting a compact set of representative metrics. SUPERVISE is released as a lightweight, modular framework built on precomputed shared label maps, making large-scale reproducible evaluation accessible to the community. All code, label maps, and evaluation results are publicly available at: https://github.com/waldo-j/supervise

</details>


### [115] [Ref-YOLO-World: Decoupling Text-Side Computation for Efficient Referring Expression Comprehension on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27756)
*Yuki Aoki,Hiroo Bekku,Tadashi Okoshi,Jin Nakazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Ref-YOLO-World，将仅依赖指代表达的文本侧计算与图像处理解耦并跨帧复用，在 RefCOCOg val 达 81.29% Acc@0.5，推理加速 18.5%（47.0 FPS），iPhone 15 Pro 上 15.73 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高精度 REC 方法产生图像相关的多模态表征，即使指代表达不变也需逐帧重算；而 YOLO-World 类开放词汇检测器虽可预编码文本并复用，但将整个表达编码为单一嵌入，缺乏显式的 token 级对应关系。

Method: Ref-YOLO-World：把文本编码与图像处理解耦，跨帧缓存并复用仅依赖指代表达的文本侧输出；通过两个轻量模块将缓存的逐 token 文本特征注入 YOLO-World 检测路径，实现显式 token 级对应。

Result: RefCOCOg val 上 Acc@0.5 为 81.29%，在采用解耦文本分支的 REC 模型中最高，并稳定优于微调后的 YOLO-World；预计算使单图推理时间减少 18.5%，达到 47.0 FPS；Core ML 版本在 iPhone 15 Pro 上达 15.73 FPS。

Conclusion: 文本侧预计算与 token 级特征注入可在保持高精度的同时显著降低逐帧开销，验证了端侧实时 REC 的可行性。

Abstract: Many high-accuracy referring expression comprehension (REC) methods produce image-dependent multimodal representations. These representations must be recomputed for every video frame, even when the referring expression remains unchanged. Fast open-vocabulary detectors such as YOLO-World instead pre-encode text once and reuse it throughout the stream. However, encoding each referring expression as a single embedding prevents explicit token-level correspondence. We propose Ref-YOLO-World, which separates text-side computation that depends only on the referring expression from image processing and reuses its outputs across frames. Two lightweight modules inject cached per-token text features into YOLO-World's detection path. Ref-YOLO-World achieves 81.29% Acc@0.5 on RefCOCOg val, the highest among REC models with a decoupled text branch, and consistently outperforms a fine-tuned YOLO-World. Precomputation reduces per-image inference time by 18.5%, yielding 47.0 FPS. A Core ML version runs at 15.73 FPS on an iPhone 15 Pro, demonstrating efficient on-device REC. Code is available at https://github.com/sakusaku3939/Ref-YOLO-World.

</details>


### [116] [Search-RSI: Expanding Search Worlds Along Multimodal Agents' Capability Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27780)
*Chengchao Lv,Yin Wang,Chunzheng Zhu,Wanli Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Search-RSI：一种随策略能力前沿同步扩展检索世界的递归自我改进框架，解决多模态搜索智能体中"证据不可达负样本"累积问题，在九个基准上显著超越静态任务增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳任务合成配方（基于知识图谱构建、在线增长）把可检索世界视为固定不变：新问题本身可能合法且可验证，但其证据并不在检索后端中。智能体发出正确查询却检索不到任何内容，随后被当作策略错误而受罚，导致"不可达负样本"随每轮增长不断累积。此外，静态增长会饱和，冻结的改进器无法跟上策略演化。

Method: 提出 Search-RSI 递归自我改进框架，每轮由当前策略在由跳数深度、关系稀有度、干扰项密度张成的任务空间中诊断自身"部分掌握"区域；在该区域生成新任务及其证据；并由同一策略决定其用途——探针结果分歧的候选用于强化学习，超出能力范围的候选转为自身提示引导的示范。整个过程不依赖外部模型，仅使用训练前即移除的特权任务结构。

Result: 在九个多模态搜索基准上，Search-RSI 在静态任务增长已饱和处仍持续提升：9B 与 27B 模型上相比所依托的配方平均准确率分别提升 9.8 与 9.5 点，其中 7.0 点是"仅使世界保持一致"的做法所无法达到的；若改进器在训练初期冻结，则损失 3.2 点；计算开销为静态增长的 1.27 倍。

Conclusion: 关键在于让策略保持在自我改进循环内：能力前沿会随训练移动，改进器必须与之共同演化。将检索世界的扩展与策略能力对齐，可消除不可达负样本带来的虚假惩罚，从而突破静态任务增长的饱和，且无需外部模型即可实现持续提升。

Abstract: Multimodal search agents answer knowledge intensive visual questions by grounding them in retrieved evidence, and recent recipes train them on multi hop tasks that are synthesized from knowledge graphs and grown online to keep pace with the agent. These recipes, however, treat the searchable world as fixed: a new question can be well formed and verifiable while its evidence is absent from the retrieval backend, so the agent issues the right query, retrieves nothing, and is penalized as if its policy were at fault. Such unreachable negatives accumulate with every round of growth. We propose Search-RSI, a recursive self improvement framework that expands the search world along the agent's capability frontier. In each round, the current policy diagnoses its partially mastered regions of a task space spanned by hop depth, relation rarity, and distractor density; new tasks there are grown together with their evidence; and the same policy decides how they are used: candidates on which its probes disagree feed reinforcement learning, while those beyond its reach become hint guided demonstrations of its own. Keeping the policy inside this loop matters: an improver frozen at the start of training loses 3.2 points. On nine multimodal search benchmarks, Search-RSI keeps improving where static task growth saturates, raising the average accuracy of the recipe it builds on by 9.8 and 9.5 points at 9B and 27B, of which 7.0 points are out of reach for a world that is merely made consistent. The loop uses no external model and only privileged task structure that is removed before training, at 1.27x the compute of static growth.

</details>


### [117] [When Does Looking Closer Pay Off? Simple Foveated ViTs for Fine-Grained Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27788)
*Boris Kriuk,Mohamed Sobhy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FoVT（foveated Vision Transformer）：用224像素"glance"最后一层class attention选出需细化的区域，再从448像素图像取细patch替换被选中的粗网格，同一backbone直接分类混合分辨率输入；在5个细粒度基准上系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度识别需要高分辨率，但并非所有图像区域都需要。作者想验证：能否仅依靠注意力信号做区域选择、在无需特征复用/蒸馏损失/选择调度的情况下，以更少计算获得接近全高分辨率模型的精度。

Method: FoVT：224像素glance的末块class attention指导区域选择；细patch（448）替换选中粗单元，同一backbone分类混合分辨率输入。5个基准、2个随机种子、每数据集26种配置；对比随机选择、CF-ViT移植版、均匀336/448推理、门控版本；并做特征复用、跳过高置信样本、运行时间等消融。

Result: 注意力选择在全部5个基准优于随机，整体最高+2.45pp，最小目标+4.18pp。等Transformer开销下，Holm校正检验未发现FoVT相对CF-ViT移植版有劣化，但也没有数据集在两个种子上显示校正后的优势。复用粗特征无显著收益，复用模块用head学习率训练时反掉1–3pp。跳过最自信的19–24%图像，精度两位小数内不变。在336像素均匀推理的Transformer开销下，门控FoVT在4个基准提升精度（NABirds、FGVC-Aircraft最大），Stanford Dogs不显著。相对同辅助glance损失的均匀448模型，非门控FoVT减少44% Transformer运算，4个基准无显著差异，FGVC-Aircraft掉0.53pp；实际墙钟节省小于运算量降幅。

Conclusion: FoVT的主要价值在于节省计算而非提升精度；简洁的注意力驱动foveation无需特征复用或蒸馏即可匹配均匀高分辨率模型，但墙钟收益有限，且缺乏相对CF-ViT的稳定优势证据。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition benefits from high resolution, but not every image region needs it. We study a foveated Vision Transformer (FoVT) that uses last-block class attention from a 224-pixel glance to select regions for refinement. Fine patches from the 448-pixel image replace the selected coarse cells, and the same backbone classifies the mixed-resolution input. We evaluate FoVT on five benchmarks with two seeds and 26 configurations per dataset. Attention beats random selection on all five benchmarks, by up to 2.45 percentage points overall and 4.18 points on the smallest objects. At equal transformer cost, Holm-corrected tests detect no FoVT deficit relative to a port of the official CF-ViT, but no dataset shows a corrected FoVT advantage in both seeds. FoVT requires no feature reuse, distillation loss, or selection schedule. Reusing coarse features shows no significant benefit and costs 1 to 3 points when the reuse module is trained at the head learning rate. Skipping refinement for the most confident 19 to 24 percent of images leaves accuracy unchanged to two decimal places. At the transformer cost of uniform 336-pixel inference, gated FoVT improves accuracy on four benchmarks, with the largest gains on NABirds and FGVC-Aircraft and no significant gain on Stanford Dogs. Foveation primarily saves computation: relative to a uniform 448-pixel model trained with the same auxiliary glance loss, ungated FoVT uses 44 percent fewer transformer operations, with no significant accuracy difference on four benchmarks and a 0.53-point deficit on FGVC-Aircraft. Wall-clock savings are smaller than the reduction in transformer operations.

</details>


### [118] [$\varepsilon$-SegFormer: Multi-Resolution Vision Transformers for Sparsely Supervised Segmentation in Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27796)
*Erfan Amidi,Sheida RahnamaiKordasiabi,Edoardo Giacomello,Florian Jug*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ε-SegFormer，一种用于稀疏监督语义分割的多分辨率Vision Transformer，基于MuViT架构与ε-Seg训练策略，在EM体数据中以约1万标注像素实现细胞器分割，未见细胞切片平均Dice 0.89（后处理0.91）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EM体数据中的细胞器分割需要密集标注，成本极高（专家数月工作量），且成像分辨率提升会进一步加剧成本。稀疏监督方法虽能降低成本，但其依赖的卷积网络仅能感知像素邻域的有限范围，而细胞器身份往往依赖于远超此范围的上下文信息。

Method: ε-SegFormer构建于MuViT架构之上并扩展ε-Seg的训练方法。对每个标注像素，采样以该像素为中心的三种物理分辨率视图，将各视图的图像块嵌入为单一token序列，并对每个token编码其在图像坐标系中的位置，使注意力机制可在统一坐标框架下关联所有视图。分割头依据像素周围token对其分类；训练时隐藏该像素自身的token，迫使模型依赖上下文而非局部外观进行预测。模型单阶段端到端训练，无需预训练，并引入辅助重建损失。

Result: 在BetaSeg FIB-SEM数据集上，模型从三个细胞内6.7亿体素中的约1万个标注像素学习；在给定细胞掩码条件下，于第四个未见细胞切片上分割细胞核、线粒体和胰岛素颗粒，平均Dice为0.89（后处理后0.91）。消融实验表明：引入更粗尺度视图可提升精度；在局部token上使用紧凑分割头效果最佳，更广的上下文通过编码器中的注意力机制引入。

Conclusion: 多分辨率视图采样与统一坐标位置编码使ViT能够高效利用大范围上下文，在极稀疏标注下实现高精度细胞器分割；关键在于粗尺度视图与"局部紧凑分割头＋编码器长程注意力"的分工设计。

Abstract: Segmenting organelles in electron microscopy (EM) volumes requires dense annotations that take experts months to produce, and every gain in imaging resolution adds to this cost. Sparsely supervised methods reduce this cost to a small fraction of labeled pixels, but the convolutional networks they build on see a limited neighborhood around each pixel, while the identity of an organelle often depends on context far beyond it. We introduce $\varepsilon$-SegFormer, a multi-resolution Vision Transformer for sparsely supervised semantic segmentation, which builds on the architecture of MuViT and extends the training method of $\varepsilon$-Seg. For each labeled pixel, $\varepsilon$-SegFormer samples views at three physical resolutions centered on it, embeds their patches into a single token sequence, and encodes every token's position in image coordinates, so that attention relates all views in one shared frame. A segmentation head classifies the pixel from the tokens around it; during training, the pixel's own token is hidden, so the label must be predicted from context rather than local appearance. The model is trained in a single stage, without pretraining, with an auxiliary reconstruction loss. On the BetaSeg FIB-SEM dataset, $\varepsilon$-SegFormer learns from about 10,000 labeled pixels among the 670 million voxels inside three cells, and, given the cell mask, segments nuclei, mitochondria and insulin granules in a slice of a fourth, unseen cell with an average Dice of 0.89 (0.91 after post-processing). Ablations show that coarser views improve accuracy and that a compact head on local tokens works best, with the wider context entering through attention in the encoder.

</details>


### [119] [Refrain: Learning Multi-Shot Consistency from Inconsistent References for A$^2$O Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27806)
*Hongru Yan,Yue Wu,Jian-Wei Zhang,Liangbin Xie,Mingzhe Zheng,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一模型 Refrain，用自监督重建任务学习跨镜头一致性，实现"任意参考、任意时长、单时间线"的多镜头视频生成，并提出 Shot-Debiased Consistency (SDC) 评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多镜头视频生成需在统一视觉风格的同时保持主体身份与场景内容，但现有方法依赖额外注意力控制或记忆处理，难以在同一框架内处理各镜头风格差异大、参考与目标时长不匹配的问题；同时现有 CLIP/DINO 指标无法识别风格冲突或误判叙事切换为不一致。

Method: 构建自监督重建任务：取同一原始视频的参考片段，对其施加不同的视觉编辑扰动，模型在共享风格提示下恢复未编辑源素材；据此构建 DurMix-300K 数据集（不同时长、独立编辑的参考与原视频配对）。模型按 A²O 原则将图像与视频参考映射到共享时间画布，无需辅助条件模块。评估方面提出 SDC：利用大规模真实视频库建立镜头内内容变化引起的风格变化基线，扣除该基线后以残差衡量跨镜头风格连续性。

Result: Refrain 在跨镜头一致性上达到 SOTA，并在镜头时序、参考遵循度与文本对齐方面表现优异；实验表明 CLIP/DINO 指标常忽略风格冲突或误判叙事切换。

Conclusion: 以自监督重建统一建模跨镜头一致性，可在单一框架内解决风格差异与时长不匹配问题；SDC 提供了更贴合真实分布的跨镜头风格连续性评估方式。

Abstract: A comic-book character, a photorealistic cityscape, an oil-painted coffee cup -- such stylistically diverse references provide visual guidance across shots and frames, yet generating a coherent multi-shot video that unifies their visual styles while preserving subject identities and scene content remains challenging. Existing methods maintain cross-shot continuity through additional attention control or memory processing. However, they struggle to resolve disparate per-shot styles or handle reference-target duration mismatches within a single framework. We introduce Refrain, a unified model that learns cross-shot consistency through a self-supervised reconstruction task. Reference clips from the same original video are deliberately perturbed with distinct visual edits, and the model recovers the unedited source footage under a shared style prompt. The resulting pipeline yields DurMix-300K, which pairs independently edited references of varying durations with their original videos. Operating on the A$^2$O principle (Any Reference, Any Duration, One Timeline), Refrain maps image and video references onto a shared temporal canvas without auxiliary conditioning modules. For evaluation, experiments show that standard CLIP or DINO metrics frequently overlook stylistic clashes or misinterpret intended narrative cuts as inconsistency. We propose Shot-Debiased Consistency (SDC), which uses an extensive real-video bank to establish a baseline for style variation associated with content changes within shots. By discounting this baseline divergence, SDC evaluates cross-shot style shifts against real-world distributions, where smaller residual differences indicate higher stylistic continuity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Refrain achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-shot consistency, while excelling at shot timing, reference adherence, and text alignment.

</details>


### [120] [Redesigning Neural Networks to Not be Confidently Wrong even to Strong Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27811)
*Michael W. Spratling,Heiko H. Schütt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出，以皮层简单细胞（增强选择性）与复杂细胞（增强容差）交替构成的浅层神经网络架构，配合新的学习组件，可实现准确稳健的图像分类，并在多项行为指标上更接近人类视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期深度学习模型曾用"简单细胞层—复杂细胞层"交替的神经层级模拟视觉感知，但标准深度网络存在明显与人脑不一致之处：层数过深、对未知类别与纯噪声图像仍给出高置信预测、易被对抗样本欺骗。作者希望验证改进后的此类架构能否兼具准确性与类人鲁棒性。

Method: 构建浅层（少于十组层对）的简单细胞—复杂细胞交替网络，并引入新的组件以更好地学习选择性表征与容差表征；随后评估其在正常样本、未知类别样本、纯噪声图像与对抗样本上的表现及泛化能力。

Result: 模型在图像分类上准确且稳健：a) 浅层结构更符合生物视觉的快速前馈物体归类；b) 对未知类别样本与纯噪声图像很少给出高置信预测，不易"看到不存在的物体"；c) 对抗样本只引发低置信预测，从而可被有效识别（虽仍不能正确分类）；d) 在训练类别的新样本上保持有竞争力的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该类网络在多个评价指标与基准任务上可与最先进DNN竞争，同时在对抗与未知样本的鲁棒性上显著更接近人类行为，是迈向类人视觉模型的重要一步。

Abstract: Early deep learning models attempted to simulate visual perception using a neural hierarchy that alternated between two types of layer: one modelled on cortical simple cells (that was designed to increase selectivity), and one modelled on cortical complex cells (that aimed to increase tolerance). We show that such a neural network (NN) architecture, when implemented with novel components that are better able to learn selective and tolerant representations, is capable of accurate and robust image classification and improved alignment between the behaviour of the model and the perceptual abilities of humans. Firstly, the proposed models are shallow, fewer than ten layer-pairs, which is more consistent with the rapid feedforward object categorisation performed by the biological visual system. Secondly, they are far less prone to producing classification predictions with high confidence for samples from unknown classes or for image consisting of pure noise, and hence, like humans are not easily fooled into perceiving objects that are not present in the image. Thirdly, for the proposed NNs adversarial samples cause predictions with low confidence, enabling them to be accurately identified. In contrast to humans, our networks still do not correctly classify adversarial samples. However, the behaviour of our networks is a significant step towards human-like behaviour compared to standard DNNs that produce highly confident, but incorrect, classification predictions for adversarial samples. The increased robustness to adversarial and unknown samples is achieved while maintaining competitive performance in terms of generalisation to novel samples from the training categories. This enables models of this type to be competitive with state-of-the-art DNNs across multiple different evaluation metrics and benchmark tasks.

</details>


### [121] [DGC-Net: A Depth-Geometric Conditioning Network for Projection-Supervised OCTA Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27854)
*Lucas Santiago,Bisheng Wang,Jaime S. Cardoso,Tomás Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DGC-Net，将3D-to-2D OCTA分割重新表述为"深度几何条件化预测"，通过深度矩条件化(DM)与整体深度几何调制(HDM)注入轴向血管证据，在OCTA-500 6mm/3mm上取得83.58%/88.62% mIoU的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCTA分割通常采用投影监督范式：模型输入3D体数据但仅受2D en-face语义图监督。现有2D方法丢弃轴向信息，3D-to-2D方法将深度响应压缩为潜在投影，导致投影歧义，使血管交叉、弥漫毛细血管区和FAZ边界易出错。

Method: DGC-Net由两个模块构成：(1) Depth-Moment Conditioning (DM) 将轴向定位与离散度线索注入投影流，保留局部深度几何；(2) Holistic Depth-Geometry Modulation (HDM) 从原始体数据导出轴向先验，捕捉全局深度布局并用于最终2D解码。

Result: 在OCTA-500 6mm和3mm子集上分别达到83.58%与88.62% mIoU，取得当时最优性能。

Conclusion: 以紧凑的轴向血管证据描述子对投影特征进行解码，可有效缓解投影监督带来的深度歧义，验证了深度几何条件化范式在3D-to-2D OCTA分割中的有效性。

Abstract: OCTA segmentation is commonly formulated as a projection supervised task: models observe 3D volumetric scans but are supervised by 2D en-face semantic maps. Existing 2D and 3D-to-2D methods either discard axial information or compress depth responses into latent projections, leaving vessel crossings, diffuse capillary regions, and FAZ boundaries vulnerable to projection-induced ambiguity. We propose DGC-Net, a Depth-Geometric Conditioning Network that recasts 3D-to-2D OCTA segmentation as depth-geometrically conditioned prediction, where projected features are decoded with compact descriptors of axial vascular evidence. DGC-Net preserves local depth geometry through Depth-Moment Conditioning (DM), which injects axial localization and dispersion cues into the projection stream, and captures global depth layout through Holistic Depth-Geometry Modulation (HDM), which derives a raw-volume axial prior for final 2D decoding. Experiments on the OCTA-500 6mm and 3mm subsets show that DGC-Net achieves 83.58% and 88.62% mIoU, respectively, reaching state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [122] [Anchors That Move Without Collapsing: Exclusive-Region Anchor Routing for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27855)
*Aadi Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在共享骨干上验证：软性锚点路由（Perceiver、AnchorFormer、Representative Attention）会退化/冗余；硬性互斥 Voronoi 划分不会坍缩且更准确；提出 focus anchors（多锚点置于学习重要性图峰值、共用同一互斥划分），在杂乱画布上优于固定划分，全高清上两者以约1/5 FLOPs 匹配局部窗口密集注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锚点（anchor）token 能让 ViT 通过少量摘要交换全局信息，但缺乏约束保证不同锚点捕获不同内容。作者希望系统检验各类锚点路由是否存在"坍缩为单一摘要"的问题，并寻找既稳定又准确的区域分配方式。

Method: 在共享骨干上逐组件对比（同一 block、同样接口）：(1) 定量比较 Perceiver 式通路、AnchorFormer（含其质量归一化）、Representative Attention 等软路由的池化图相似度；(2) 采用 patch 网格的互斥硬划分（Voronoi 不可坍缩）作为基线，逐组件匹配比较精度；(3) 提出 focus anchors：多数锚点置于学习到的 importance map 峰值，所有锚点共享一个互斥 Voronoi 划分；(4) 对比 softmax、Sinkhorn、Gumbel-softmax 等松弛分配；测量 FLOPs、显存、单步训练速度；并在 CIFAR-100、杂乱画布、全高清交通标志识别等任务上评测，另设 ResNet-18 与下采样 ViT 对照。

Result: Perceiver 式与 AnchorFormer 通路坍缩为单一摘要（池化图相似度 0.99，后者即便有质量归一化仍如此），其他软路由部分冗余（0.59–0.79）。互斥划分在 CIFAR-100 上逐组件匹配后比 Representative Attention 高 2.4–3.6 个百分点。Focus anchors 在杂乱画布上比固定划分高 1.5 个百分点（95% CI [0.5, 2.6]），但全高清交通标志识别上无优势。全高清任务中两种变体以约 1/5 FLOPs 匹配带局部窗口的密集注意力，固定划分单步训练比 FlashAttention 密集注意力快 1.5 倍，但两者显存占用更高；下采样输入的 ViT 平均精度下降一半。硬性互斥分配是唯一既稳定又最准确的分配方式；softmax 与 Sinkhorn 松弛发散，Gumbel-softmax 可训练但精度较低。局限：从头训练设定下 ResNet-18 在所有任务上更准确；锚点仅在约 1536×1536 像素以上训练快于 FlashAttention，且从不省显存。

Conclusion: 结论指向"硬性互斥区域分配"是稳定且准确的关键设计；focus anchors 的自适应峰值放置只在杂乱场景带来增益，并不普适。整体上锚点式稀疏全局交互在极小 FLOPs 下可达密集注意力精度，但相对卷积网络在精度上仍无优势，且速度/显存收益只在极高分辨率下出现。作者强调结论限于其 block 内的路由模块而非已发表完整模型。

Abstract: Anchor tokens let a vision transformer exchange global information through a few summaries, but nothing forces different anchors to summarise different things. On one shared backbone we show that a Perceiver-style pathway and an AnchorFormer pathway collapse to a single summary, the latter despite its mass normalisation (pooling-map similarity 0.99), while other soft pathways, including Representative Attention, stay partly redundant (0.59-0.79). These results concern the routing modules inside our block, not the full published models. An exclusive partition of the patch grid cannot collapse by construction, and matched component by component it is 2.4-3.6 points more accurate than Representative Attention on CIFAR-100. We then propose focus anchors, which place most anchors at the peaks of a learned importance map while all anchors keep one exclusive Voronoi partition. Focus anchors beat a fixed partition on a cluttered canvas (+1.5 points, 95% CI [0.5, 2.6]) but not on full-HD traffic-sign recognition. There, both variants match dense attention with local windows at about a fifth of its FLOPs, and the fixed partition also trains 1.5x faster per step than dense attention with FlashAttention, though both use more memory; a ViT on a downsampled input loses half its mean average precision. Hard exclusive assignment is the only region assignment we tested that is both stable and most accurate: softmax and Sinkhorn relaxations diverge, and Gumbel-softmax trains but is less accurate. We also report the limits: a ResNet-18 is more accurate on every task we trained it on in this from-scratch regime, and anchors train faster than FlashAttention only from about 1536x1536 pixels and never use less memory.

</details>


### [123] [ENC-QA: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Vision-Language Models on Electronic Navigational Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27858)
*Jiyoon Pyo,Jacob Arndt,Yao-Yi Chiang,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ENC-QA 诊断性基准（77,312 问，源自 6,612 幅 NOAA 电子海图），系统评测视觉语言模型在海图要素识别、空间推理与航次变更关键性判断上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海图通过标准化符号、属性、文本与异构几何编码安全攸关的航海信息；解读海图并判断连续海图更新的航行意义，需要识别航海要素、确定空间关系并运用航海知识。VLM 是有前景的途径，但对其细粒度要素识别、基于海图的空间推理、以及将航海知识用于评估海图变更的系统性评测仍很有限。

Method: 构建 ENC-QA 基准：基于 6,612 幅 NOAA 电子海图生成 77,312 道题，覆盖四项互补能力——ENC 感知、空间推理、感知与空间联合推理、应用；其中 8,227 道题构成递进式子集，将选定的海图变更案例分解为从变更检测到关键性评估的链式问题，支持案例级分析；对 10 个开放权重 VLM 进行评测，并开展受控证据干预实验。

Result: 10 个开源 VLM 评测显示，其在细粒度航海要素识别与基于要素的空间推理上存在显著不足；受控证据干预表明，提供相关感知与空间信息可提升变更关键性判断的准确率。

Conclusion: ENC-QA 可作为海图理解与安全攸关航行决策的诊断性资源；基准与评测代码将于论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Nautical charts encode safety-critical maritime information through standardized symbols, attributes, text, and heterogeneous geometries. Interpreting these charts and assessing the navigational significance of successive chart updates requires recognizing nautical features, determining their spatial relationships, and applying navigational knowledge. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising approach to nautical-chart interpretation, yet systematic evaluation of their fine-grained nautical feature recognition, chart-based spatial reasoning, and application of navigational knowledge to assessing chart changes remains limited. We introduce ENC-QA, a diagnostic benchmark comprising 77,312 questions derived from 6,612 National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA) Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs). The benchmark evaluates four complementary capabilities: ENC Perception, Spatial Reasoning, Joint Perception and Spatial Reasoning, and Application. Of these, 8,227 questions form a progressive subset that decomposes selected chart-change cases into linked questions progressing from change detection to criticality assessment, enabling case-level analysis of how models use perceptual and spatial evidence to determine the navigational significance of chart changes. Evaluations of 10 open-weight VLMs reveal substantial limitations in fine-grained nautical feature recognition and feature-grounded spatial reasoning. Controlled evidence interventions show that relevant perceptual and spatial information improves change-criticality accuracy. Our findings establish ENC-QA as a diagnostic resource for nautical-chart understanding and safety-critical navigational decisions. We will release the benchmark and evaluation code upon publication.

</details>


### [124] [OpenAHBU: Open-sourced Automatic Human Behaviour Understanding Toolkit Through Facial Behaviour Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27862)
*Zijian Wu,Yupeng Huo,Cheng Luo,Yichen Huang,Junan Long,Haijun Lin,Lu Liu,Hatice Gunes,Bernard Ghanem,Siyang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenAHBU 是首个完全开源、可泛化的自动面部行为理解工具包，涵盖 11 项功能、三个语义层级，每项功能提供至少 4 个不同复杂度模型，并通过多数据集聚合训练与协同多任务增强模块提升跨数据集泛化与预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术可用的面部行为理解工具包功能有限、训练流程封闭、泛化能力弱，难以在资源受限与高吞吐等多样场景下灵活部署，也缺乏可复现与可扩展性。

Method: 构建统一工具包 OpenAHBU，整合 11 项 AFBU 功能，分三个语义层级：短时外部面部属性（landmarks、blendshapes、AU、表情）、短时心理状态（valence/arousal、疼痛）、长期心理特质（抑郁、人格）。重新开发 8 项功能，每项功能至少提供 4 个不同计算复杂度的深度学习模型；多数功能模型在多来源学术数据集聚合语料上训练以实现跨数据集泛化；并引入协同多任务增强模块（SMTE），利用最相关支撑功能的预测线索自适应增强目标任务。

Result: 大量实验表明，OpenAHBU 在功能覆盖度和预测性能上均优于现有开源工具包，并在不同计算场景下具备更优的部署灵活性。

Conclusion: OpenAHBU 提供公开的训练与推理脚本、预训练权重、GUI、Python 库及跨平台兼容性，构成可复现、可扩展、可伸缩的开源工具包（已发布于 GitHub）。

Abstract: Automatic facial behaviour understanding (AFBU) is fundamental to various human-centred applications, yet existing academically available toolkits offer limited functionality, closed training pipelines, and weak generalisation. This paper presents OpenAHBU, a fully open-sourced, generalisable toolkit integrating 11 AFBU functions across three semantic levels: (i) short-term external facial attributes, including landmarks, blendshapes, Action Units, and expressions; (ii) short-term facial behaviour-related human psychological states, including valence/arousal and pain; and (iii) long-term facial behaviour-related psychological traits, including depression and personality. Particularly, we re-developed eight functions, where at least four deep learning models covering different computational complexities are developed for each function, providing flexible deployment on resource-constrained and high-throughput computing scenarios. More importantly, models of most functions are trained on a comprehensive corpus aggregating multiple academically available datasets, ensuring robust cross-dataset generalisability.In addition, we include a Synergistic Multi-task Enhancement (SMTE) module to efficiently and adaptively enhance each OpenAHBU function by leveraging the complementary cues contained in its most-related support function's predictions.Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our OpenAHBU outperformed existing open-source toolkits not only in functional coverage but also the predictive performances, while providing superior deployment flexibility across diverse computational scenarios.With fully publicly available training and inference scripts, pre-trained weights, user-friendly GUI, Python library, and cross-platform compatibility, our OpenAHBU provides a reproducible, scalable and extensible toolkit at https://github.com/OpenAHBU/OpenAHBU.

</details>


### [125] [LesionAgent: Training-Free Agentic Tumor Tracking and Response Assessment in Longitudinal CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27876)
*Maximilian Rokuss,Jeya Maria Jose Valanarasu,Alejandro Lozano,Benjamin Hamm,Yannick Kirchhoff,Fabian Isensee,Klaus Maier-Hein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LesionAgent 是一个无需任务级训练、以冻结3D模型为工具的LLM智能体，端到端模拟放射科医生的纵向CT阅读流程，在病灶跟踪、测量与疗效判读上全面超越自动基线，并能显著降低假进展判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型虽能检测、分割、配准与跟踪病灶，但缺乏放射科医生式的"读片"能力：无法判断哪些发现是病灶、哪两个病灶是随访中的同一病灶、新出现的斑点是否为进展，也无法依临床指南规则逐项核对决策。现有自动流程依靠固定规则串联模型，误差累积导致假进展频发。

Method: 提出 LesionAgent：(1) 将冻结的3D模型作为工具调用，不做任务特定训练；(2) 借助带坐标标注的视图在3D空间中行动，实现空间定位与推理；(3) 用病灶登记表（registry）跨随访记忆每个病灶以维持纵向一致性；(4) 对其依赖的每个模型输出进行验证（verification）；(5) 所有数值判定交给确定性指南引擎，避免LLM直接生成数字。并配套提供可运行任意LLM的 napari 阅片应用。

Result: 在四个公开纵向CT数据集上，病灶跟踪数量与测量精度均优于全部自动基线；固定规则串联同一批3D模型在56次随访中误判33次进展，LesionAgent仅2次。临床随访CT上23次随访中20次与放射科医生疗效反应一致，且包含全部反应变化病例；未在DeepLesion上训练的情况下，病灶检索率达89.0%，超过所有在该数据集上训练过的跟踪器（87.4%）。消融显示增益来自harness而非LLM本身：同一LLM与3D模型下，Claude Code检测F1为41%，LesionAgent为78%；可院内本地部署的开源权重LLM由6%提升至73%。

Conclusion: 纵向CT的智能阅片关键在于系统级harness（工具编排、跨访记忆、输出验证与确定性指南计算），而非模型规模或LLM能力本身；该设计可让冻结3D模型与中小型开源LLM达到接近专家的纵向判读水平，并具备院内落地可行性。代码与napari应用已开源。

Abstract: Every few months, a cancer patient's CT scans decide whether treatment continues. 3D foundation models can now detect, segment, register and track lesions, yet none of them reads the scans as a radiologist does. A radiologist decides which findings are lesions, which are the same lesion months later and whether a new spot means progression, and checks each decision against the extensive rules of a clinical guideline. We introduce LesionAgent, an LLM agent that performs this read end to end without task-specific training. It operates frozen 3D models as tools, acts in 3D through coordinate-labelled views, remembers every lesion across visits in a registry, verifies every model output it relies on, and leaves every number to a deterministic guideline engine. On four public longitudinal CT datasets, LesionAgent tracks more lesions and measures them more accurately than every automatic baseline, and where the same 3D models chained by fixed rules call progression falsely at 33 of 56 follow-ups, it does so twice. On clinical follow-up CTs, it agrees with the radiologists' response at 20 of 23 follow-ups, including every change in response, and without training on DeepLesion, it retrieves more of its lesions than every tracker trained on it (89.0% vs. 87.4%). Its harness, not its LLM, makes the difference: given the same LLM and 3D models but not the harness, Claude Code reaches a detection F1 of 41% and LesionAgent 78%, and an open-weight LLM that can run inside the hospital rises from 6% to 73%. LesionAgent and a napari reading app for any LLM are available at https://github.com/MIC-DKFZ/LesionAgent.

</details>


### [126] [Interpretable Tissue Architecture Complements Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27881)
*Zeeshan Shahid,Hammam M. AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出139维“细胞—群落”组织架构表征，并验证其可作为病理基础模型表征的可解释性互补信息，在BCNB的ER/PR分类与LungHist700的四分类任务上通过特征级融合提升MIL预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织架构对组织病理学评估有重要价值，但其与病理基础模型（如CTransPath、PLIP）表征之间的互补预测价值尚不清楚；同时基础模型表征缺乏可解释性。

Method: 构建139维细胞到群落的多尺度表征，含九个特征组（核形态、细胞组成、空间连通性、群落组织、细胞间相互作用）；在多种MIL架构下将架构特征独立使用及与CTransPath/PLIP嵌入做特征级融合；BCNB上以五折患者不相交交叉验证评估ER/PR分类，LungHist700上评估四项分类任务（含腺癌vs鳞癌）。

Result: BCNB上CTransPath融合配置ER/PR平均ROC-AUC为0.9144/0.8360，PLIP融合为0.8791/0.8015；患者级分析显示ER相关差异体现在核形态、图连通性、细胞群落及细胞间相互作用。LungHist700上融合配置在四项任务上平均ROC-AUC更高，如腺癌vs鳞癌0.7967（CTransPath单独为0.7137）。

Conclusion: 可解释的组织架构描述子能够补充基础模型表征，并同时提供可量化的组织病理学组织层面洞见。

Abstract: Tissue architecture provides important information for histopathological assessment, yet its complementary predictive value alongside pathology foundation-model representations remains insufficiently understood. This study investigates whether interpretable measurements of cellular and multicellular tissue organization provide additional predictive information beyond learned representations. We introduce a 139-dimensional cellular-to-community representation comprising nine feature groups characterizing nuclear morphology, cellular composition, spatial connectivity, community organization, and cell-cell interactions. Their predictive contributions are systematically evaluated independently and through feature-level fusion with CTransPath and PLIP embeddings using multiple-instance learning (MIL). On the BCNB dataset, estrogen receptor (ER) and progesterone receptor (PR) classification is evaluated across four MIL architectures using five-fold patient-disjoint cross-validation. Selected CTransPath fusion configurations achieved mean ROC-AUC values of 0.9144 (ER) and 0.8360 (PR), while PLIP fusion achieved 0.8791 and 0.8015, respectively. Patient-level analyses identified ER-associated differences in nuclear morphology, graph connectivity, cellular communities, and cell-cell interactions. On LungHist700, selected fusion configurations achieved higher mean ROC-AUC across four classification tasks, including adenocarcinoma versus squamous cell carcinoma (0.7967 versus 0.7137 for CTransPath alone). Overall, these findings suggest that interpretable tissue-architecture descriptors can complement foundation-model representations while providing measurable insights into histopathological organization.

</details>


### [127] [QuantStream3D: Geometry-Aligned Low-Bit KV Cache Quantization for Streaming Visual Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27901)
*Zunhai Su,Keyu Fan,Ruishan Guo,Zhongzhu Zhou,Jingzehua Xu,Bohan Sun,Hansen Feng,Ruihan Hu,Yik-Chung Wu,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向流式视觉几何Transformer的KV量化框架QuantStream3D，以几何引导混合精度KV量化与几何对齐量化感知蒸馏实现近无损（多以2-bit缓存）的几何精度，并在内存、延迟、批大小与吞吐上获显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视觉几何Transformer将历史KV状态作为跨视图几何记忆，缓存随序列增长而膨胀，制约长序列推理。作者系统刻画流式几何中的KV量化，发现两个关联问题：非均匀几何敏感性与缓存-几何失配——量化敏感性随精度选择与缓存位置变化，检索扰动会改变几何头输入并沿后续预测与KV写入传播。

Method: QuantStream3D包含两部分：几何引导的混合精度KV量化（Geometry-Guided Mixed-Precision KV Quantization），保护敏感的跨视图证据；几何对齐的量化感知蒸馏（Geometry-Aligned Quantization-Aware Distillation），在学生模型生成的量化历史上联合适配Transformer块与几何头。可与已有原生缓存剪枝方法结合。

Result: 在5个基准、2种骨干网络上，多数设置下以主要为2-bit的KV缓存取得近无损几何精度，许多配置甚至超过未量化推理；相比面向LLM的KV量化器，TUM绝对轨迹误差与NeuralRGBD完整度误差分别最多降低57.4%与69.7%。与原生缓存剪枝结合可进一步压缩并提升NeuralRGBD重建质量。优化后的StreamVGGT后端在batch size为1时实现5.05倍物理KV压缩、延迟降低23.1%；同一GPU固定内存预算下支持5.4倍最大批大小与最高1.67倍峰值吞吐。

Conclusion: KV量化是缓解流式视觉几何Transformer缓存瓶颈的有效途径，但需针对几何敏感性与缓存-几何耦合进行专门设计；QuantStream3D以混合精度量化与几何对齐蒸馏实现近无损甚至超越未量化的几何精度，并带来显著的效率收益。代码与预训练权重将公开。

Abstract: Streaming visual geometry transformers retain historical key-value (KV) states as cross-view geometric memory, but growing caches constrain long-sequence inference. We systematically characterize KV quantization in streaming geometry and uncover two linked challenges: Nonuniform Geometric Sensitivity and Cache-Geometry Mismatch. Quantization sensitivity varies across precision choices and cache locations, while perturbed retrieval changes geometry-head inputs and propagates through subsequent predictions and KV writes. Guided by these findings, we propose QuantStream3D, to our knowledge the first dedicated KV quantization framework for streaming visual geometry transformers. Geometry-Guided Mixed-Precision KV Quantization protects sensitive cross-view evidence, while Geometry-Aligned Quantization-Aware Distillation jointly adapts transformer blocks and geometry heads on student-generated quantized history. Across five benchmarks and two backbones, QuantStream3D achieves near-lossless geometric accuracy in most settings with predominantly 2-bit KV caches, and many configurations surpass unquantized inference. Compared with LLM-oriented KV quantizers, it reduces TUM absolute trajectory error and NeuralRGBD completeness error by up to 57.4% and 69.7%, respectively. Integration with existing native cache-pruning methods further pushes compression while improving reconstruction quality on NeuralRGBD. Our optimized StreamVGGT backend achieves $5.05\times$ physical KV compression and 23.1% lower latency at batch size one. Under a fixed memory budget on the same GPU, it supports $5.4\times$ the maximum batch size and up to $1.67\times$ the peak throughput of unquantized inference. Code and pretrained checkpoints will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [128] [AnimalFarm: Auditing the Generation and Detection of Hateful Images Featuring Anthropomorphic Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27905)
*Junjie Chu,Ye Leng,Mingjie Li,Yiting Qu,Michael Backes,Yun Shen,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个系统研究"拟人化非人角色"涉仇恨图像生成与检测的安全风险：构建基准（5类角色×3类生成任务），14,250次查询中92.1%成功出图、其中95.9%含仇恨内容；5个主流检测器最强者GPT-5.5仅识别73.9%，多数低于58%；提出免训练映射回人类表征的流水线与微调检测器，F1分别达0.962与0.979。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟人化非人角色长期被用于承载针对人类群体的仇恨信息（从历史宣传到近期社交媒体AI生成图像），但此类图像带来的安全风险几乎未被研究：生成端是否易被利用、检测端是否失效，均缺乏系统实证。

Method: 从保守筛选的源场景构建基准，覆盖5种角色类型与3类生成任务（文生图、图像编辑、对话式图像生成）；执行14,250条预定义查询评估生成系统；收集4,370张仇恨图像，评测5个流行仇恨内容检测器；并探究两种缓解方案：(1) 免训练流水线，在分类前将非人角色映射回人类表征；(2) 针对该类内容微调的检测器。

Result: 生成端：92.1%查询能产出图像，其中95.9%含仇恨内容。检测端：最强检测器GPT-5.5仅识别73.9%仇恨图像，多数检测器低于58%；误判主要发生在主体为非人角色时被判定为良性。缓解方案：映射流水线F1=0.962，微调检测器F1=0.979。

Conclusion: 拟人化非人角色构成图像生成与仇恨内容检测的双重安全缺口：现有生成系统易被诱导产出仇恨图像，主流检测器对该形态存在系统性盲区；通过表征映射或领域微调可显著修复，揭示该方向亟需纳入安全对齐与检测体系。

Abstract: Anthropomorphic non-human characters have long been used to convey hateful messages targeting human groups, from Nazi-era propaganda to recent AI-generated imagery on social media. However, the safety risks posed by such imagery remain largely unexplored. We thus conduct the first systematic study of hateful image generation and detection involving anthropomorphic non-human characters. We construct a benchmark from conservatively screened source scenes, covering five character types and three image generation tasks: text-to-image generation, image editing, and conversational image generation. Across 14,250 scheduled queries, image generation systems produce images for 92.1\% of queries, of which 95.9\% contain hateful content. We further collect 4,370 hateful images and evaluate five popular hateful-content detectors. Even the strongest detector, GPT-5.5, identifies only 73.9\% of hateful images, while most detect fewer than 58\%. Our analysis reveals that existing detectors often misclassify hateful images as benign when their main subjects are non-human characters. We explore two effective mitigation approaches: a training-free pipeline that maps non-human characters back to human representations before classification and a detector fine-tuned for this type of hateful content. These approaches achieve F1 scores of 0.962 and 0.979, respectively. Our findings expose significant safety gaps in both image generation and hateful-content detection.
  Disclaimer: This paper contains hateful text and imagery.

</details>


### [129] [Star Panoptic Quality: Selectively Evaluating Split and Merged Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27913)
*Erik Großkopf,Paula Tamara Buzduga,Hendrik Möller,Florian Kofler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Star Panoptic Quality（StarPQ）评估框架：先在二分图上构造“星森林”，用clean/merge/split星分别隔离不同失败模式，再按失败模式分别加权计算指标，从而在参考标注存在偏置时区分有效与无效的过分割/欠分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体实例检测对许多分割任务至关重要，但专家实例标注常不可得，只能启发式近似（如取语义图的连通域），易合并相接物体或切碎碎片化物体；即便有专家标注，专家分歧或结构性勾画偏差也会产生拆分或合并的参考实例，分别对应过分割和欠分割。PQ及相关指标虽同时衡量分割与检测质量，却对所有实例等权、与尺寸无关，因此在参考有偏时既无法识别拆分/合并实例，也无法区分有效与无效的过/欠分割。

Method: StarPQ分两步：第一步，在预测与参考的二分图中构造“星森林”（star forest），其中clean、merge、split三类星结构分别隔离不同的失败模式；第二步，对每种失败模式单独加权后计算最终指标，使从业者能够显式考虑实例勾画偏差。

Result: 多项实验表明，StarPQ不仅能针对特定失败模式施加定向惩罚或宽容，还可作为诊断工具揭示各失败模式的普遍程度。

Conclusion: StarPQ弥补了PQ类指标在参考偏置情形下的评估空白，提供了可定制、可诊断的实例分割评估手段。

Abstract: Detecting individual instances is crucial for many segmentation problems. However, expert instance annotations are often unavailable, so instances are approximated heuristically (e.g., as connected components of semantic maps), which can merge touching objects or split fragmented ones. Even when expert annotations exist, expert disagreement or structural delineation biases can yield split or merged reference instances, corresponding to over- or under-segmentation, respectively. Panoptic Quality (PQ) and related metrics capture both segmentation and detection quality while weighting all instances equally, regardless of size. Yet when the references are biased, these metrics can neither detect split or merged instances nor distinguish valid from invalid overor under-segmentation. We present Star Panoptic Quality (StarPQ), a novel evaluation ramework, which closes this gap in two steps: First, we construct a so-called star forest in the bipartite graph whose clean, merge, and split stars isolate different failure modes. Second, we calculate the final metric by individually weighting each failure mode, allowing practitioners to account for instance delineation biases. Across various experiments, we demonstrate that StarPQ not only enables targeted penalization or eniency towards specific failure modes, but also serves as a diagnostic tool to reveal their prevalence.

</details>


### [130] [ESSI: Computationally Efficient Semantic Search for Sparse Autoencoder Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27921)
*James Baker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ESSI方法，用分割掩码标注SDXL激活并借此高效地为语义概念筛选稀疏自编码器特征，同时复用线性分类器校准引导强度，在概念插入与物体遗忘任务上以更低成本取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）可将扩散模型激活分解为可解释特征，但用其实现引导需先确定哪些特征对应某一语义概念。现有"概念→特征"任务方法昂贵，依赖网格搜索或对冻结UNet的反复前向传播。

Method: ESSI（Efficient Semantic Sparse Identification）：利用分割掩码将SDXL激活标注为概念/非概念样本，对SAE特征排序，并通过二分搜索寻找最小的特征集合，使其线性分类器仍能区分正负样本且不低于性能阈值；随后复用该分类器校准引导强度。

Result: 将同一组选出的特征同时用于概念插入与物体遗忘两类任务。与先前工作相比，ESSI成本相当或更低，且在插入与移除两个方向均表现良好。

Conclusion: 以掩码监督+排序+二分搜索替代网格搜索，可低成本、可复用地完成概念到特征的映射，并借同一线性分类器统一实现引导强度标定与插入/遗忘双向控制。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose diffusion model activations into interpretable features, but steering a model with them requires identifying which features map to a given semantic concept, if any. Existing methods for this concept to feature task are expensive, relying on grid searches or repeated passes through the frozen UNet. We propose Efficient Semantic Sparse Identification (ESSI). Using segmentation masks to label SDXL activations as concept or non-concept, ESSI ranks SAE features and binary-searches for the smallest set on which a linear classifier still separates positive from negative samples while not falling below a performance threshold, then reuses that classifier to calibrate the steering strength. We evaluate the same selected features on both concept insertion and object unlearning. Compared with prior work, ESSI is as cheap or cheaper, and performs well on both insertion and removal.

</details>


### [131] [DiTraR: Diverse Scorers and Transcript-Guided Re-ranking for Video Corpus Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27931)
*Mohammad Alijanpour Shalmani,Alale Rezvani Boroujeni,Ali Amini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiTraR 针对视频语料级时刻检索中的“选错视频”瓶颈，从精确区间打分、多打分器能量集成、冻结语言模型转写验证三个互补方向改进视频选择，在 TVR 验证集上以单次评测取得 24.36（IoU 0.5）与 13.55（IoU 0.7）的 R@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现强语料级打分器的 Top-1 错误主要源于选错视频而非边界不精确，且仅对打分器已有证据重新加权无法修复该问题，因此需要专门针对视频选择环节设计方法。

Method: 提出 DiTraR，包含三个组件：(1) witness-interval 打分器，将查询分解为多个探针，对语料中每段视频的每个候选区间进行精确打分；(2) 多打分器能量集成，在一次穷举搜索前对多个独立训练打分器的能量取平均；(3) 转写验证器，使用冻结语言模型阅读排名靠前视频的带时间戳对白，在不改变区间边界的前提下重排时刻。

Result: 所有设置在开发集上冻结、仅做一次评测，在 TVR 验证集 IoU 0.5 与 0.7 下 R@1 分别达到 24.36 与 13.55。集成主要提升视觉内容类查询，验证器主要提升对白类查询，二者收益基本可叠加。

Conclusion: 视频选择是语料级时刻检索的关键瓶颈；面向候选区间的精确打分、能量集成与基于对白的验证器构成互补且可叠加的解决方案，且无需针对测试集调参即可获得稳定增益。

Abstract: Video corpus moment retrieval asks a system to find, in a large collection of untrimmed videos with subtitles, the video and the time interval that a natural-language query describes. We find that most top-ranked errors of a strong corpus-level scorer come from choosing the wrong video, not from imprecise boundaries, and that re-weighting the evidence the scorer already uses does not fix them. We present DiTraR, which addresses video selection from two complementary directions. A witness-interval scorer decomposes the query into probes and scores every candidate interval of every corpus video exactly; a multi-scorer energy ensemble averages the energies of independently trained scorers before one exhaustive search; and a transcript verifier, a frozen language model that reads the time-stamped dialogue of the highest-ranked videos, re-orders the moments without changing their boundaries. With all settings frozen on development data and a single evaluation, DiTraR reaches 24.36 and 13.55 R@1 at IoU 0.5 and 0.7 on the TVR validation split. The ensemble improves queries about visual content, the verifier improves queries about dialogue, and their gains largely add up.

</details>


### [132] [Non-penetrating World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27963)
*Yuhang Dai,Yinwei Wu,Ziyue Lin,Han Ji,Xingyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究交互式视频世界模型中的物体穿透（物理违规）问题，提出基准 NoPeBench 与轻量框架 NoPeWorld，在冻结 DiT 隐状态上训练小型预测头判定候选动作安全性并替换不安全动作，使六个世界模型的平均穿透率从 74% 降至 16%，并具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型在生成过程中常出现物体穿过障碍物等物理违规，缺乏系统评估手段，且现有开源模型无法可靠避免此类失败，因此需要先量化问题再提出干预方法。

Method: 构建 NoPeBench 基准评估世界模型对物理障碍的响应；发现 DiT 隐状态已蕴含预测穿透的信息；据此提出 NoPeWorld——在冻结 DiT 的隐状态之上训练小型预测头，判别候选动作是否安全，并将不安全动作替换为最保留原始意图的安全动作。

Result: 在六个视频世界模型上平均物理违规率由 74% 降至 16%；并展现对开放域图像、第三人称视角及移动障碍物的零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 现有世界模型虽无法可靠防止穿透，但隐状态已编码足够信息；简单的轻量读写式干预即可显著降低违规率，说明"表示内含先验 + 轻量外挂模块"是提升物理一致性的有效路径。代码、基准与权重已开源。

Abstract: We study a new problem of preventing object penetration in interactive video world models. To understand how existing models handle this, we introduce NoPeBench, a benchmark that evaluates how world models respond to physical obstacles. Our evaluation reveals that none of the open-source models can reliably prevent such failures. Surprisingly, however, these models already encode the information needed to predict such violations. We find that the hidden states of diffusion transformers (DiTs) can predict whether a candidate action will penetrate an obstacle. Building on this, we introduce NoPeWorld, a lightweight framework that detects and prevents penetration. Specifically, we train a small prediction head on top of a frozen DiT's hidden states to read out if a candidate action is safe or not. Unsafe actions are then replaced with safe ones that best preserve the original intent. Despite its simplicity, NoPeWorld substantially reduces the physical violation rate, averaged over six video world models, from 74% to 16%. Remarkably, our method shows zero-shot generalization to open-domain imagery, third-person viewpoints, and moving obstacles. We publicly release our code, the NoPeBench benchmark, and model weights. Project Page: https://nopeworld.github.io .

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [On the loss landscape of the linear neural network: beyond critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26671)
*Antoine Lejay,Lionel Lenotre,Sa{ï}d Toubra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"临界谷线(critical thalweg)"这一中间尺度结构，将线性神经网络损失景观的所有临界点统一纳入其中，并给出二维平面上的全局可视化，实证表明梯度下降被吸引至该谷线并沿其下降到全局最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Baldi & Hornik以来线性网络损失景观的临界点已被充分研究，但缺乏中尺度/全局尺度的刻画；而文献表明梯度下降会绕开临界点，因此需要一个能解释这一现象的整体几何框架，并以更弱假设重证经典结论。

Method: 引入"临界谷线"结构——包含全部临界点并具备关键几何性质的中尺度流形；用该框架给出Baldi & Hornik结果的几何证明（假设比现有文献更简单），并将损失景观投影到二维平面进行全局可视化；辅以实验观察梯度下降轨迹与该结构的关系。

Result: 以更简单的假设完成了Baldi & Hornik临界点结果的几何证明；获得可在二维平面上直观呈现的损失景观全局视图；实验显示梯度下降被吸引到临界谷线，并沿其单调下降直至全局最小。

Conclusion: 临界谷线为线性网络损失景观提供了统一的中尺度刻画，既复现并简化了经典临界点理论，又解释了梯度下降规避临界点的机制，为优化动力学分析提供新的几何视角。

Abstract: While the critical points of the loss landscape in linear neural networks have been extensively studied since the seminal work of Baldi \& Hornik in the late 1980s, a description of this landscape at an intermediate or global scale is still lacking. Such a characterization is crucial, particularly as a substantial body of literature indicates that gradient descent circumvents critical points. The present article introduces an intermediate structure, termed the critical thalweg, which encompasses all critical points and exhibits key geometric properties. Beyond providing a geometric proof of Baldi \& Hornik's results using simpler assumptions than the existing literature, this framework offers a global view of the loss landscape that can be easily visualized in a two-dimensional plane. Empirical observations suggest that gradient descent is attracted toward the critical thalweg, along which the loss continues to decrease to its global minimum.

</details>


### [134] [Parasite Features: Causal Abstraction Through Spurious Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26711)
*Denis Sutter,Qing Yao,Sasha Boguraev,Julian Minder,Antonio Di Maio,Thomas Hofmann,Kyle Mahowald,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因果抽象（Causal Abstraction）类方法存在一类"寄生特征"（parasite feature）：即使模型并未真正实现某特征，DAS 等基于因果抽象的等价性检验仍能通过线性对齐映射"虚假地"模拟出两个方向（判别方向与导向方向）的联合行为，从而误判模型实现了该特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果抽象为机制可解释性提供了有力框架，用于判定神经网络是否与某算法等价；但作者希望检验这类等价性检验是否可靠，即是否存在能被"顺带"通过检验的伪特征，从而揭示该框架的潜在失效模式，并为机制可解释性的最佳实践讨论提供依据。

Method: 作者从理论上刻画一类伪特征：表示空间中存在两个方向，其一依据某特征区分输入，其二将模型导向特定输出；基于因果抽象的方法（如 Distributed Alignment Search, DAS）可通过经过这两个方向的路径组合，虚假地模拟其联合行为。实验上，在句法一致性与两位数字加法两个任务中，使用线性对齐映射的 DAS 被发现确实会找到此类寄生特征。

Result: 在句法一致性与两位数加法两项任务中，DAS 借助线性对齐映射均成功"发现"了寄生特征，即在不真实实现该特征的情况下通过了等价性检验，实证了该失效模式的存在性与普遍性。

Conclusion: 因果抽象框架及其派生方法（如 DAS）的等价性检验可能被寄生特征蒙蔽，因此解读此类可解释性结果时需格外谨慎；本文强调了因果抽象应用中的重要考量，并为机制可解释性最佳实践的持续讨论提供贡献。

Abstract: Causal Abstraction defines a notion of \emph{equivalence} between a neural network and an algorithm, thus serving as a powerful framework for mechanistic interpretability. In this paper, however, we show the existence of a class of \emph{spurious features} which straightforwardly pass such equivalence tests. Suppose there are two directions in a model's representation space where: (i) one discriminates the input based on some feature, and (ii) the other steers the model towards a certain output. Interpretability methods based on Causal Abstraction (e.g., Distributed Alignment Search) can \emph{spuriously} simulate these directions' combined behavior by leveraging pathways passing through them, giving the impression that the model implements this feature. In such cases, we say the method discovered a \defn{parasite feature}. Experimentally, we show that Distributed Alignment Search finds parasite features using linear alignment maps in two tasks: syntactic agreement and two-digit addition. Our paper highlights an important consideration for Causal Abstraction and contributes to the ongoing debate about best practices for mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [135] [Cross-Attack Robust Aggregation for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26731)
*Eyhab Al-Masri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTether通过将客户端更新相对坐标中位数的偏差映射为带死区的鲁棒得分进行重加权，并以服务器端优化步骤实现聚合，在多种攻击与拜占庭比例下达40%时均保持最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习使资源受限的IoT与边缘设备无需交换原始数据即可训练共享模型，但被攻陷或故障设备可投毒全局模型。现有鲁棒聚合器各有失效区间：FedAvg在高幅值攻击下崩溃，Krum、中位数、截尾均值等基于距离/统计的防御在非IID数据上损失精度且难以抵御隐蔽攻击。

Method: 提出FedTether：以坐标中位数为参照，计算各客户端更新的偏差，经带死区（dead-zone）的鲁棒得分函数转化为权重，对客户端更新重新加权，并将其作为服务器端优化步骤执行聚合。

Result: 在三个数据集、三类攻击、拜占庭比例最高40%的设置下，FedTether是唯一在所有区间均达顶级的方法：隐蔽攻击下与FedAvg相当（无鲁棒性代价），较鲁棒基线最高提升42个精度点，在高幅值攻击使FedAvg接近随机时仍保持精度；在50客户端部分参与、无共享数据与验证集条件下性质依旧成立。

Conclusion: FedTether以坐标中位数偏差加死区重加权的轻量服务器端机制，兼顾鲁棒性与非IID下的精度，克服了现有聚合器在精度—鲁棒性之间的权衡缺陷，且适用于部分参与的实际部署场景。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) lets resource-constrained IoT and edge devices train a shared model without exchanging raw data, but compromised or faulty devices can poison the global model. Existing robust aggregators each fail in some regime: naive averaging (FedAvg) collapses under high-magnitude attacks, whereas distance- and statistics-based defenses (Krum, median, trimmed mean) sacrifice accuracy on non-IID data and remain vulnerable to stealthy attacks. We present FedTether, which reweights client updates by their deviation from a coordinatewise median through a robust, dead-zoned score applied as a server-side optimizer step. Across three datasets, three attack types, and Byzantine fractions up to 40%, FedTether is the only method top-tier in every regime: it matches FedAvg under stealthy attacks with no robustness tax, exceeds robust baselines by up to 42 accuracy points, and stays accurate where high-magnitude attacks reduce FedAvg to near-random. These properties persist at 50 clients under partial participation, with no shared data or validation set.

</details>


### [136] [Depth-Linked Residual Tokens for Multimodal Retrieval and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26752)
*Jiangfan Zhang,Wentao Bao,Xian Wu,Xiangjun Fan,Jianpeng Cheng,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DepThink：在单次 pre-filling 前向中串联 K 个可学习残差 token，其隐状态通过轻量可学习残差递推耦合，兼顾循环架构的序列精炼能力与并行 token 的单次推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式（looped）Transformer 虽提升参数效率与推理能力，但骨干网络的串行迭代带来显著计算延迟；而可并行学习的特殊 token（如 ViT 的 register token）虽可单次推理，却缺乏序列精炼、且 token 间表征冗余。作者希望同时获得两者的优势。

Method: DepThink 在单次 pre-filling 前向中追加 K 个可学习残差 token，其隐状态经由轻量、可学习的残差递推相互耦合，使每个残差 token 逐步捕捉前序 token 遗漏的互补信息。理论上证明其相比标准可学习 token 具有严格扩展的几何方向可达性，并保证可证的一步下降优势。

Result: 在多模态检索与生成任务上，DepThink 稳定优于可学习 token 基线，并在稠密（2B、8B）与稀疏 MoE（30B-A3B）骨干、LoRA 与全参数微调下均匹配或超过循环架构；训练加速最高 11.9×，推理加速最高 4.3×。

Conclusion: DepThink 以残差递推耦合的可学习 token 替代串行骨干迭代，在保持甚至超越循环架构性能的同时大幅降低训练与推理开销，为多模态检索与生成提供了高效框架。

Abstract: Iterative architectures such as looped language models improve parameter efficiency and reasoning capacity of transformers, yet their sequential backbone iterations introduce substantial computational latency. Conversely, parallelizable learnable special tokens (such as the register tokens in Vision Transformer) enable efficient single-pass inference, but often suffer from the absence of sequential refinement and exhibit redundant representations across tokens. To address these challenges, we introduce Depth-Linked Residual Tokens (DepThink), an efficient framework designed for multimodal retrieval and generation. Operating within a single pre-filling forward pass, DepThink appends a sequence of K learnable residual tokens whose hidden states are coupled through a lightweight, learnable residual recurrence. This mechanism enables each residual token to progressively capture complementary information missed by preceding tokens. Theoretically, we prove that DepThink provides strictly expanded geometric directional reachability over standard learnable tokens and guarantees a provable one-step descent advantage. We evaluate DepThink across multimodal retrieval and generation tasks.
