Motivation: 长尾图分类（LTGC）中，少数大头图含丰富结构信息，大量小尾图结构线索稀少，导致尾图难以学到判别性表示，核心问题是基于图规模的不平衡。

Method: DMPRP：以图表示为query，引入受Knowledge-diverse Experts（KDEX）启发的动态专家记忆模块生成残差向量增强原表示；在节点、根子图、随机游走邻域三个层级从头图采样共现三元组训练该模块，实现头到尾的结构模式迁移；并在PTC上设计受控实验考察小图更稠密时的模式迁移。

Result: 在五个图分类基准上提升尾部类别性能；消融实验表明专家模块与随机游走监督均有贡献；PTC受控实验进一步分析了小图更稠密情形下的模式迁移效果。

Conclusion: 通过动态记忆增强与多层级共现三元组监督可有效实现头到尾的结构模式迁移，改善长尾图分类的尾部性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-tailed graph classification (LTGC) is common in real-world graph datasets, where a few large head graphs provide rich structural information while many smaller tail graphs offer fewer structural cues, making it harder to learn discriminative representations for tail graphs. To address this size-based imbalance, we propose Dynamic Memory-enhanced Pattern Relevance Prediction (DMPRP), which transfers structural patterns from head graphs to tail graphs. Given a graph representation as a query, a dynamic expert memory module inspired by Knowledge-diverse Experts (KDEX) generates a residual vector that augments the original representation. We train the module using co-occurrence triplets sampled from head graphs at the node, rooted subgraph, and random-walk neighborhood levels. Experiments on five graph classification benchmarks show that DMPRP improves tail performance, and ablation studies show that both the expert module and random-walk supervision contribute to these gains. A controlled experiment on PTC further examines pattern transfer when smaller graphs are denser than larger ones. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DMPRP-73A5.

</details>


### [171] [GaLF-ST: Gaussian Latent Fields for Spatial Transcriptomics Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27143)
*Baichuan Yang,Yifan Luo,Yong Liu,Bing Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GaLF-ST：用可学习高斯中心与各向异性协方差对空间位置加权，单阶段联合优化高斯几何、隐向量与共享非线性解码器，实现空间转录组基因表达预测，在七组数据集上取得领先重建性能并显著降低时间与显存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组数据存在空间采样有限、表达观测稀疏的问题；现有方法多用坐标网络隐式建模，缺乏显式空间参数化。部分方法采用"先拟合中间嵌入、再训练表达预测器"的两阶段策略，但适合表达重建的隐表示未必能从空间坐标中预测出来，两阶段对隐表示的要求不一致。

Method: 提出 GaLF-ST（高斯隐场方法）：以可学习的高斯中心与各向异性协方差决定每个位置上隐向量的权重，再由共享非线性解码器将加权平均映射为多基因表达；观测表达误差联合更新高斯几何、隐向量与解码器参数，实现端到端单阶段训练。同时构建统一基准：7个数据组、3种留出比例，与4个代表性方法在相同位置、划分、基因面板与评分规则下对比。

Result: 在跨组织与不同观测密度的实验中，GaLF-ST 取得领先的整体重建性能；在有限观测下能够恢复空间基因表达模式并保持组织域结构，同时大幅降低运行时间与 GPU 显存占用。

Conclusion: 显式高斯隐场参数化结合单阶段联合优化，可有效缓解空间转录组稀疏观测带来的建模困难，并兼顾重建精度与计算效率；统一基准也为后续方法比较提供了可比实验设置。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) measures gene expression at known tissue locations to reveal tissue organization and cellular function. Expression prediction requires modeling both local spatial relationships and associations among genes. Existing methods typically model expression using coordinate networks without explicit spatial parameterization. To address limited spatial sampling and sparse expression observations of ST, some methods firstly fit intermediate embeddings and then train expression predictors, but a latent representation suited to expression reconstruction may be difficult to predict from spatial coordinates. The two training stages can impose different demands on the latent representation. In this paper, we propose GaLF-ST, a Gaussian latent field method in which learnable Gaussian centers and anisotropic covariances determine the weights of latent vectors at each location. A shared nonlinear decoder maps their weighted average to multigene expression. In GaLF-ST, observed expression error jointly updates the Gaussian geometry, latent vectors, and decoder parameters. We further establish a unified benchmark across seven dataset groups and three held-out fractions, comparing GaLF-ST with four representative methods using the same locations, splits, gene panels, and scoring rules. Across tissues and observation densities, GaLF-ST achieves leading overall reconstruction performance, recovering spatial gene expression patterns and preserving tissue domains from limited observations while substantially reducing runtime and GPU memory usage.

</details>


### [172] [Structure Over Scale: Rethinking Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27148)
*Allan Kazakov,Abdurrahman Javat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在RLVR中，FFT与LoRA的选择忽略了"更新几何"这一关键变量；LoRA在高秩下出现rank collapse，而DoRA/QuanTA等结构化适配器更稳定且更新参数<0.6%，作者提出"模型成熟度假设"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作把RLVR的适配方式简化为FFT表达力与LoRA效率的权衡，但作者认为这一框架遗漏了可达更新流形的几何结构，且名义秩与参数量不足以刻画适配效果。

Method: 采用统一的冷启动GRPO协议，在DeepMath-Hard等任务上对比FFT、LoRA（不同秩）、DoRA与QuanTA；通过谱分析考察更新能量在有效方向上的分布；并在三个8B基础模型上关联适配器表现与冻结权重谱结构及预训练规模。

Result: LoRA出现秩崩塌：低秩时性能稳定，秩升高后骤降（DeepMath-Hard上r=64准确率73.1%，r=128降至4.7%）；DoRA与QuanTA在更宽的优化区域内保持稳定，仅更新不到0.6%参数即可媲美FFT；谱分析显示崩塌的LoRA将更新能量集中于少数有效方向，结构化适配器分布更分散。

Conclusion: 名义秩与参数量不足以刻画RLVR适配，施加在可达更新流形上的结构同样重要；作者提出可证伪的"模型成熟度假设"：最优适配几何可能依赖预训练阶段已编码的权重谱结构与规模，而非因果结论。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is often framed as a choice between the expressivity of Full Fine-Tuning (FFT) and the efficiency of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). We find that this framing misses an important variable: the geometry of the update. Under a common cold-start GRPO protocol, LoRA exhibits rank collapse: performance remains broadly stable at low rank, then fails abruptly as rank increases. On DeepMath-Hard, for example, accuracy drops from 73.1% at r=64 to 4.7% at r=128. In contrast, Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA) and Quantum-Informed Tensor Adaptation (QuanTA) remain stable over a wider tested optimization region and are competitive with FFT while updating less than 0.6% of the model parameters. Spectral analysis reveals a corresponding difference in learned update structure: collapsed LoRA concentrates its update energy into relatively few effective directions, whereas structured adapters produce substantially more distributed updates. These results suggest that nominal rank and parameter count alone are insufficient to characterize adaptation in RLVR - the structure imposed on the reachable update manifold also matters. Finally, across three 8B base models, adapter behavior aligns with both frozen-weight spectral structure and reported pre-training scale, motivating the Model Maturity Hypothesis: the adaptation geometry that works best may depend on properties already encoded during pre-training. We present this as a falsifiable hypothesis rather than a causal conclusion. The code can be found here https://github.com/Shrimpstanot/structure-over-scale

</details>


### [173] [The Vulnerability Window: Decision Instability in Quantized Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27155)
*Omkar Shewale,Deepak Kumar,Divakar Kumar Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出,用"基准准确率几乎不变"来验证4-bit量化的奖励模型/LLM评判器是近乎失明的检查:量化在RewardBench上改写了1.1–12.3%的成对偏好判定,而聚合准确率仅变化0.03个点(p=1.00),因为修正与引入的错误相互抵消。翻转集中在全精度margin相对量化噪声较小处,扰动在下游改变的是"选谁"(best-of-64)而非"答什么"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型和LLM评判器常被量化为4-bit以降低大规模打分开销,而通行验收标准是"基准准确率基本不动"。作者认为该检查无法反映量化对偏好判定的实际改写(决策churn),因此需要刻画量化误差的决策层面效应及其下游后果。

Method: 覆盖两个家族8个奖励模型(0.6B–8B)、3个生成式评判器和4种4-bit量化器;用成对偏好翻转率(而非聚合准确率)度量churn;提出以margin/σ为指标的高斯近似 Φ(-|m|/σ) 刻画聚合翻转率,并定义"脆弱窗口占用率";在良性的best-of-16与对抗压力下检验churn稳定性;在六个(模型,量化器)组合上评估best-of-64选择与最终答案的一致性变化;用留出审计比较margin类预测器与高斯近似、直接测量churn率、top-k路由信号的有效性。

Result: 1) 聚合准确率变化最小可至0.03点(p=1.00),但翻转率1.1–12.3%;2) 高斯近似在全部30个标量设置中高估聚合率,且对生成式评判器失效;3) 脆弱窗口占用率随模型规模由25%降至7%,解释小模型更脆弱;4) churn在良性best-of-16下不显著,在对抗压力下明显(某家族146:54有害:有益翻转,另一家族符号反转);5) 下游14.6–65.7%的问题best-of-64选择改变,仅2.6–9.6%最终答案改变,选择变化是答案变化的5–8倍;基准认为"等同"(0.03点)的组合仍有34.5%选择改变;6) 基准churn对下游变化排序ρ=0.89,基准准确率无法可靠排序;7) 所有margin类预测器翻转排序一致(AUC 0.927),分布内直接测得的churn率在水平估计上更优,作为路由信号与朴素top-k相当。

Conclusion: 量化评判器的主要风险不是准确率下降,而是决策churn及其对候选选择的影响;应以margin(相对σ)与实测churn率替代聚合准确率作为验收与诊断指标。作者强调贡献在于对这一现象的系统刻画而非新模型。

Abstract: Reward models and LLM judges are routinely quantized to 4 bits so that scoring many candidates stays affordable, and the usual check is that benchmark accuracy barely moves. That check is close to blind. Across eight reward models from two families (0.6B-8B), three generative judges and four 4-bit quantizers, quantization rewrites 1.1-12.3% of pairwise preference decisions on RewardBench while aggregate accuracy moves by as little as 0.03 points (p = 1.00), because the errors it fixes and introduces cancel. Flips concentrate where the full-precision margin is small relative to the quantization noise scale sigma: a Gaussian approximation Phi(-|m|/sigma) describes the aggregate rate, over-predicting it in all 30 scalar settings and failing for generative judges, and occupancy of the resulting vulnerability window falls from 25% to 7% with model size, which is what makes small judges fragile. The churn stays within noise under benign best-of-16 and becomes clear under adversarial pressure for some quantizers (146:54 harmful:helpful flips on one family, reversing sign on another). Downstream it changes what ships: across six (model, quantizer) pairs from two families, 14.6-65.7% of problems get a different best-of-64 selection while only 2.6-9.6% get a different final answer, with selections changing 5-8x more often than answers in every pair, and the pair the benchmark calls identical (0.03 points, p = 1.00) still changes 34.5% of them. Benchmark churn ranks that downstream change (rho = 0.89); benchmark accuracy does not order it reliably. Held-out audits then locate the useful diagnostic in the margin rather than the Gaussian: every margin-based predictor orders flips identically (AUC 0.927), and on the level a directly measured churn rate wins in-distribution. As a routing signal it is matched by plain top-k. The contribution is the characterization, not the model.

</details>


### [174] [Hierarchical Hybrid-Coordinate Flow Matching for Accurate and Physically Plausible Apo-to-Holo Protein Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27162)
*Minjong Kim,Jaemin Sim,Dongwoo Kim,Myunghyun Jang,Eun-Sol Kim,Juyong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层流匹配模型 CrypticFlow，融合笛卡尔空间与内坐标表示，实现从 apo 结构预测 holo 构象，在保持全局精度的同时显著提升局部骨架几何有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 apo-to-holo 模型多在笛卡尔空间建模，全局精度高但局部主链几何可能物理不合理；内坐标模型可保证局部有效性，但回归生成会放大角度误差（杠杆臂效应）。因此需要融合两种互补表示。

Method: CrypticFlow 采用分层策略：先由 ESM-3 条件化的 E(3) 等变图网络在笛卡尔空间生成稀疏 Cα 锚点；再由条件 midpoint Transformer 递归地在局部内坐标中填充空隙。递归生成时，解码器约束每个中点与序列相邻父锚点的距离等于对应 apo Cα–Cα 距离。

Result: 在 D3PM 测试集上，同等基线容量下取得最低的中位与平均 Cα RMSD；五个独立训练模型的中位/平均直接 RMSD 为 1.333±0.056 Å 与 2.766±0.041 Å。相邻距离保真度 99.58±0.02%（0.2 Å 内），伪角保真度 95.87±0.25%（20° 内）。经 cg2all 重建全原子结构后，MolProbity 分数为 3.034±0.054，优于所评估基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，采用混合坐标的分层流匹配模型能够同时恢复全局精度并保持局部保真度，为 apo-to-holo 构象预测提供了兼顾全局与局部几何有效性的方案。

Abstract: Predicting holo-like protein conformations from apo structures is essential in structure-based drug design. Most existing apo-to-holo models operate in Cartesian space and achieve strong global accuracy. However, high global accuracy does not ensure physically valid local backbone geometry. Internal-coordinate models offer local control and validity, but regressive generation can amplify angular errors, known as the lever-arm effect. We introduce CrypticFlow, a hierarchical flow-matching model that combines these two complementary representations. An ESM-3-conditioned E(3)-equivariant graph network first generates sparse C$α$ anchors in Cartesian space. A conditional midpoint Transformer then recursively fills the gaps in local internal coordinates. During recursive generation, the decoder constrains the distance between each midpoint and any sequence-adjacent parent anchor to the corresponding apo C$α$-C$α$ distance. On the D3PM test split, CrypticFlow achieves the lowest median and mean C$α$ RMSD at published baseline capacities. Across five independent training models, the median and mean direct RMSD are 1.333 $\pm$ 0.056 angstroms and 2.766 $\pm$ 0.041 angstroms, respectively. The quality of local geometry also improves substantially. Our model achieves 99.58 $\pm$ 0.02% adjacent-distance fidelity and 95.87 $\pm$ 0.25% pseudo-angle fidelity, measured within 0.2 angstroms and 20° of the holo reference. Then, we reconstruct the predicted C$α$-only traces into all-atom structures using the cg2all coarse-to-all-atom backmapping method and evaluate their physical and geometric quality with MolProbity. Our model achieves a MolProbity score of 3.034 $\pm$ 0.054, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results show that a hierarchical flow-matching model with hybrid coordinates can recover global accuracy while retaining local fidelity.

</details>


### [175] [BusemannFormer: Horospherical Self-Attention for Hyperbolic Graph Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27167)
*Youheng Yao,Ziyao Zeng,Wenbo Liao,Tianqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Horospherical Self-Attention（HSA）：查询选取理想边界方向、以Busemann深度给键打分，构成token到token的双曲注意力核，并集成于全双曲图Transformer——BusemannFormer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双曲Transformer基本是对常规注意力的适配，未显式利用与树层级紧密相关的几何结构——理想边界。

Method: HSA中每个查询选择一个理想边界方向，按键沿该方向的Busemann深度打分；建立与点积注意力的平坦极限联系，刻画打分的水平集为horosphere（极限球），并对键侧梯度给出界；构建全双曲图Transformer BusemannFormer，含用查询深度调制注意力集中度的log-depth变体，以及支持线性复杂度全局注意力的有限边界字典扩展。

Result: 在层级图与引文图上，BusemannFormer-LD在若干节点分类基准上优于所评估基线，并在多种图结构上取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 显式利用理想边界几何的HSA是有效的双曲注意力设计，兼具理论性质与实用性，并可扩展至线性复杂度全局注意力。

Abstract: Hyperbolic neural networks excel on hierarchical graph data, yet existing hyperbolic Transformers largely adapt conventional attention without explicitly exploiting the ideal boundary, a geometric structure closely tied to tree hierarchy. We propose Horospherical Self-Attention (HSA): each query selects an ideal-boundary direction and scores keys by their Busemann depth along that direction, yielding a token-to-token hyperbolic attention kernel. We establish a flat-limit connection to dot-product attention, characterize score level sets as horospheres, and bound key-side gradients. HSA is integrated into BusemannFormer, a fully hyperbolic graph Transformer, with a log-depth variant that uses query depth to modulate attention concentration. We further introduce a finite-boundary-dictionary extension supporting linear-complexity global attention. Experiments on hierarchical and citation graphs show that BusemannFormer-LD outperforms the evaluated baselines on several node-classification benchmarks and achieves competitive performance across diverse graph structures.

</details>


### [176] [Lift Out, Kick In: Decoupling Event Effects for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27191)
*Yunying Zhu,Zichun Cao,Siwei Sun,Xiaocen Guo,Shengsheng Lin,Jing Hu,Enyun Yu,Zhichao Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Loki事件感知预测框架，用共享的事件响应函数（ERF）先"剥离"历史观测中的事件偏差、再"注入"未来已知事件的响应，在5个基准上均取得最优标准化MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列受外生事件影响，事件效应叠加在系统内在时序动态之上，导致历史观测混杂了持续性动态与事件诱发的偏离，难以判断哪些模式应外推、哪些应归因于特定事件情境。已有事件感知预测方法虽能引入事件信息或建模事件相关变化，但通常将历史事件归因与未来事件生成割裂处理。

Method: 提出Loki（Lift Out, Kick In）通用事件感知预测框架，核心为共享的事件响应函数ERF：按事件类型与相对时间位置建模事件效应，并在历史窗口与预测窗口间共享。流程上先"Lift Out"——从历史观测中剥离估计的事件诱发的偏差，再进行时序预测；随后用同一ERF对已知未来事件"Kick In"相应响应。该形式将事件响应建模与时序预测解耦，同时通过共享机制耦合历史归因与未来生成。

Result: 在五个预测基准上，Loki在全部数据集上取得最优标准化MAE，相较最强非Loki基线误差降低约2.2%–11.7%。消融与受控分析进一步验证了Lift Out、Kick In、共享响应建模的作用，以及与不同预测骨干网络的兼容性。

Conclusion: 共享ERF的解耦式"先剥离后注入"设计能有效分离事件响应与时序动态，提升事件感知预测精度，且具备骨干无关的通用性与良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often shaped by exogenous events whose effects are superimposed on the system's underlying temporal dynamics. Historical observations therefore entangle persistent dynamics with event-induced deviations, making it difficult to determine which patterns should be extrapolated and which should be attributed to specific event contexts. Existing event-aware forecasting methods can incorporate event information or model event-related variation, but typically treat historical event attribution and future event generation separately. We propose Loki (Lift Out, Kick In), a general event-aware forecasting framework built on a shared Event Response Function (ERF). The ERF models event effects according to event type and relative temporal position and is shared across the historical and forecast windows. Loki lifts out estimated event-induced deviations from historical observations before forecasting, then uses the same ERF to kick in responses to known future events. This formulation separates event-response modeling from temporal forecasting while coupling historical attribution and future generation through a shared mechanism. Across five forecasting benchmarks, Loki achieves the best standardized MAE on all five datasets, reducing error by approximately 2.2%-11.7% over the strongest non-Loki baseline. Ablations and controlled analyses further support the roles of Lift Out, Kick In, shared response modeling, and compatibility across forecasting backbones.

</details>


### [177] [cmHC: Balancing Stream Mixing in Hyper-Connections via Geometric Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27193)
*Haowei He,Ziwen Zhao,Chenshuo Pan,Gang Lin,Zhihao Yang,Chao Wang,Xinzhang Liu,Kaidong Yu,Han Gao,Dongfang Li,Menglin Yang,Shuangyong Song,Yongxiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究流形约束超连接（mHC）中的残差流混合问题，揭示"欠混合—过混合"权衡，并提出几何收缩方法 cmHC，在 Birkhoff 多面体内部实现精确双随机混合，提升超大规模预训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mHC 将残差连接扩展为多流并用双随机矩阵（Birkhoff 多面体）约束残差混合以稳定传播，但如何在多面体内"有效"混合残差流尚不明确，缺乏对混合程度影响的理论刻画与实践方案。

Method: 1) 揭示混合权衡：混合不足时残差矩阵贴近 Birkhoff 多面体顶点，超连接退化为近乎独立的多条流；混合过度时矩阵聚集于均匀重心，各流难以区分。2) 理论分析：对跨层混合系数的累积残差混合、流间差异与梯度差异给出界。3) 提出 cmHC：一种精确双随机方法，将 Birkhoff 多面体几何收缩至其内部，迫使残差矩阵深入多面体内部，缓解欠混合并具备可证明的流混合界，且不引入新参数化。4) 在浅层施加适度收缩以避免过混合。5) 在 Ascend NPU 上开发融合算子。

Result: 稠密模型与 MoE 模型在多种参数量级上的大量实验验证了 cmHC 的有效性与可扩展性；可在极大规模预训练中实现稳定梯度传播；Ascend NPU 融合算子使超连接执行延迟最多降低 44.8%。

Conclusion: 残差流的混合程度存在最优区间，mHC 需在欠混合与过混合之间取得平衡；通过对 Birkhoff 多面体进行几何收缩（cmHC），可在不增加参数的前提下同时缓解欠混合、抑制浅层过混合，兼顾理论保证、训练稳定性和硬件执行效率。

Abstract: Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) extends residual connections to multiple streams and stabilizes residual propagation by constraining stream-mixing residual matrices to the Birkhoff polytope, the set of doubly stochastic matrices. In this work, we investigate how to effectively mix residual streams within the Birkhoff polytope. We reveal a mixing trade-off: when under-mixed residual matrices stay close to the vertices of the Birkhoff polytope, hyper-connections will degenerate into a set of nearly independent streams; when over-mixed residual matrices are concentrated around the uniform barycenter of the polytope, streams will become less distinguishable from each other. We theoretically validate our findings by bounding the cumulative residual mixing, stream difference and gradient difference of mixing coefficients across layers. Motivated by these findings, we propose Contraction for Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (cmHC), an exact doubly stochastic method which geometrically contracts the Birkhoff polytope toward its interior. This contraction mitigates under-mixing by forcing the residual matrices to explore deeper into the polytope, with a provable stream mixing bound and no new parameterization. Moreover, we apply a moderate contraction at the shallow layers to prevent over-mixing, enabling stable gradient propagation on extremely large-scale model pretraining. Extensive experiments across dense and mixture-of-experts models at various parameter scales demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of cmHC. We also develop fused operators for cmHC on Ascend NPUs, reducing hyper-connection execution latency by up to 44.8%.

</details>


### [178] [Improved Accuracy Scaling with Problem Complexity in LLM Arithmetic Reasoning Using Executable Symbolic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27203)
*Yu-Bin Liu,Shi-Ju Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出受控基准 GSM-AB，独立调节操作数位数与运算个数，考察可执行符号表示（Lean）相对自然语言推理对算术准确率随复杂度变化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学基准上的高准确率无法说明推理在问题复杂度上升时是否仍可靠，需要区分"数值规模"与"组合复杂度"两个维度来检验准确率随复杂度的衰减规律。

Method: 构建 GSM Arithmetic Benchmark（GSM-AB），对操作数数字长度和运算个数分别进行独立控制变化；在多个模型规模上对比 Lean 可执行表示与自然语言推理，并设置 Python 程序化思维（Python-PoT）作为对照。

Result: 可执行表示能提升准确率并减缓其随复杂度上升的下降；Lean 在所有测试模型规模下均降低了对操作数位数的平均敏感度；而对运算个数增加时衰减更慢的现象仅出现在较大模型规模上，且 Python-PoT 对照呈现类似的运算轴模式。

Conclusion: 推理表示形式决定了算术准确率的缩放规律，支持采用可执行符号表示以在复杂算术任务上获得更可靠的推理。

Abstract: High accuracy on mathematical benchmarks does not establish whether reasoning remains reliable as problem complexity increases. We examine how executable symbolic representations change accuracy scaling in large language models compared with natural-language reasoning. To address this limitation, we introduce the GSM Arithmetic Benchmark (GSM-AB), a controlled benchmark that independently varies operand digit length and operation count to probe numerical scale and compositional complexity. We find that executable representations can improve accuracy and slow its decline as complexity increases. Lean reduces average sensitivity to operand digit length at every tested model size. By contrast, slower degradation with increasing operation count emerges at the larger model sizes. A Python program-of-thought (Python-PoT) control shows a similar operation-axis pattern, suggesting that this finding is not specific to Lean. These findings show that reasoning representation shapes arithmetic accuracy scaling and support executable symbolic representations for more reliable reasoning on complex arithmetic tasks.

</details>


### [179] [LingSante-Pulse: Unified Physiological Pretraining through Random-Feature Prediction and Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27206)
*Linxiao Yang,Wenwei Wang,Tian Zhou,Bingqing Peng,Zihao Zhang,Zuyi Zhu,Jinjie Gu,Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LingSante-Pulse：一个统一、通用的生理信号表征框架，用共享的信号条件编码器独立处理各物理通道，结合跨模态对齐、结构化随机特征预测与掩码波形重建，在 PPG/ECG/EEG/EMG 等 38 项任务上取得域内最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床监护与可穿戴设备采集的生理信号互补，但模态覆盖仅部分重叠，导致信号内信息与跨模态关系难以被联合利用；同时实际场景中传感器数量与组合多变，需要可适配多模态评估与单传感器推理的通用表征。

Method: 1) 共享的信号条件编码器逐物理通道独立处理，支持不同数量与组合的信号输入；2) 联合三项目标：成对跨模态对齐、结构化随机特征预测（RFP）、掩码波形重建，以同时学习生理关系并保留信号内结构信息。

Result: 在覆盖 PPG、ECG、EEG、EMG 的 38 种任务配置下，冻结编码器在各域均取得数据集平衡的平均排名最优；HMC 多模态睡眠分期 Macro-F1=0.7784，Sleep-EDF 仅 EEG 分期 Macro-F1=0.7941，单导联 ECG 呼吸暂停筛查 AUROC=0.9183。对照实验表明功能预测与波形重建提供互补监督，持续刷新的随机监督可泛化到未见教师实现，正确的辅助模态对应关系能改善仅呼吸信号的表征。

Conclusion: 单一编码器可从部分重叠的异构记录中学习跨信号类型、预测目标与传感器配置可复用的生理特征，验证了统一生理表征框架的可行性与通用性。

Abstract: Clinical monitoring and wearable devices capture complementary physiological signals, but their partially overlapping modality coverage makes within-signal information and cross-modal relationships difficult to exploit jointly. We introduce LingSante-Pulse, a unified, general-purpose and flexible physiological representation framework that learns from these heterogeneous recordings. A shared, signal-conditioned encoder processes each physical channel independently and supports different numbers and combinations of signals for multimodal assessment and single-sensor inference. Pairwise cross-modal alignment, structured random-feature prediction (RFP) and masked waveform reconstruction jointly learn physiological relationships and preserve informative within-signal structure. Across 38 general physiological task configurations spanning PPG, ECG, EEG and EMG, the frozen encoder achieves the best dataset-balanced mean rank in each domain. In sleep assessment, multimodal staging on the HMC dataset and EEG-only staging on the Sleep-EDF dataset reach Macro-F1 scores of 0.7784 and 0.7941, respectively, while single-lead ECG apnea screening achieves an AUROC of 0.9183. Controlled experiments show that function prediction and waveform reconstruction provide complementary supervision for retaining temporal and rhythmic information. Representations learned under continually refreshed random supervision generalize to unseen teacher realizations, and correct auxiliary modality correspondence improves respiration-only representations. These results demonstrate that a single encoder can learn reusable physiological features across signal types, prediction targets and sensor configurations from partially overlapping recordings.

</details>


### [180] [A Stationarity-Based Regret Framework for Time-Varying Nonconvex Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27218)
*Nadav Hallak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究时变非凸复合优化，提出基于平稳性（一阶最优性条件）的局部后悔度框架，并以近端梯度残差为核心实例，在梯度变化预算模型下给出在线近端梯度法的次线性后悔保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变非凸复合优化中决策序贯产生，经典基于值的后悔度概念通常不适定（well-posedness 缺失），因此需要一套适用于非凸情形的性能评价准则及对应的可分析算法框架。

Method: 1) 提出基于平稳性的性能框架：由一阶最优性条件构造局部后悔度，并刻画使其成为可靠性能准则的最小结构要求；该抽象涵盖近端梯度残差等经典最优性度量，并在凸情形退化为经典动态后悔。2) 以近端梯度残差作为具体且具算法意义的实例，分析在线近端梯度方法，区分嵌套多调用（multi-call）与完全增量单调用（single-call）两种 oracle 机制；3) 引入梯度变化预算，刻画非凸下次线性平稳性后悔的必要条件；4) 将框架推广到非凸多人博弈，以平稳性后悔解释均衡寻求动态。

Result: 凸问题中，所得局部后悔与标准动态后悔等价；非凸问题中证明若无时间正则性则次线性平稳性后悔不可达，并给出梯度变化预算刻画该必要性；在该模型下为多调用与单调用两种机制证明了次线性后悔保证，揭示每轮计算量与可达性能之间的权衡；数值实验验证理论结论。

Conclusion: 基于平稳性的后悔框架为非凸时变复合优化及非凸多人博弈提供了统一且适定的性能评价与算法分析工具，明确了时间正则性的必要作用，并量化了计算—性能权衡。

Abstract: This paper studies time-varying nonconvex composite optimization, where decisions are made sequentially and classical value-based regret notions are generally ill-posed. We introduce a stationarity-based performance framework defined through local regret measures built from first-order optimality conditions, and identify minimal structural requirements under which such measures constitute sound performance criteria. This abstraction encompasses canonical optimality measures, including the proximal-gradient residual, and collapses to classical dynamic regret in convex settings. We then focus on the proximal-gradient residual as a concrete and algorithmically meaningful instantiation for general composite problems. In convex problems, we show that the resulting local regret is equivalent to standard dynamic regret. In nonconvex settings, we establish that sublinear stationarity-based regret is unattainable without temporal regularity, and introduce a gradient-variation budget that captures this necessity. Under this model, we analyze online proximal-gradient methods and prove sublinear regret guarantees in both nested multi-call and fully incremental single-call oracle regimes, highlighting the tradeoff between per-round computational effort and achievable performance. Finally, we demonstrate how the proposed framework extends to nonconvex multiplayer games by interpreting equilibrium-seeking dynamics through stationarity-based regret, and corroborate the theoretical findings with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [181] [CreditFilter: Calibrated Token Weights over the Within-Response Entropy Rank for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27227)
*Hanqing Zhao,Liuyue Xie,Rong-Cheng Tu,Xu Zhang,Davide Modolo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将无评论家RLVR中"每个token对结果的真实影响"形式化为forking value（下一token选择上成功概率的方差），证明其期望和恰等于奖励方差，据此提出仅依赖softmax概率的CreditFilter：按响应内熵排名加指数窗口重分配token信用，无需额外前向，在MATH500/AIME上显著优于DAPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无评论家RLVR（如GRPO/DAPO类）对响应内所有token赋予统一优势，无法度量单个token对最终结果的实际影响；80/20 mask等高熵启发式虽偏好高熵token，却只关注熵大小而非因果影响，缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 提出forking value：定义为策略在下一token各选择上成功概率的方差，其沿响应的期望和精确等于奖励方差，因而token信用可视为在"分叉点"上花费的固定预算。forking value可分解为fork consequence（需昂贵续写估计）与fork probability（softmax直接给出）两部分；在Qwen3-8B-Base上观察到fork probability随响应内熵秩指数衰减且衰减率按类别不同。CreditFilter据此按响应内（而非批内）熵秩施加指数窗口加权，窗口匹配fork-probability包络（近似假设fork consequence平坦），并以难度阻尼和熵门控回退增强稳定性，无需额外前向。

Result: 三个seed平均下，MATH500上较DAPO提升7.7分，AIME24提升10.5分，AIME25提升9.1分；动态自适应变体性能相当；消融表明两组分均必要——响应内排序（而非批内）与窗口衰减率匹配包络均带来收益。

Conclusion: token级信用分配应以对结果的实际影响（分叉值）而非熵大小为准则；由于forking value主要由softmax可得的fork probability主导且呈指数衰减，指数窗口秩加权即可高效实现近似的预算重分配，在无额外计算成本下显著提升RLVR效果。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically assigns a uniform advantage to every token. Token-reweighting heuristics like the 80/20 mask favor high-entropy tokens, but fail to measure a token's actual impact on the outcome. We formalize this impact as the forking value: the variance of the success probability over the policy's next-token choices. The expected forking values along a response sum exactly to the reward variance. Token credit is thus, in expectation over a prompt's responses, a fixed budget spent at the forks. This motivates weights that redistribute credit within a response. The forking value factorizes into a fork consequence, which requires costly continuations to estimate, and a fork probability, which the softmax provides. On Qwen3-8B-Base, this fork probability decays exponentially with a token's within-response entropy rank at class-specific rates, revealing that most tokens carry negligible forking value. CreditFilter weights each token by an exponential window over the same rank. The window is calibrated to this fork-probability envelope, under an approximately flat fork consequence. CreditFilter needs no extra forward passes and is stabilized by difficulty damping and an entropy-gated fallback. Averaged over three seeds, it outperforms DAPO by 7.7 points on MATH500 and by 10.5 and 9.1 points on AIME24 and AIME25. Its dynamically adaptive variant matches it. The ablations show that both parts matter: ranking tokens within each response rather than across the batch, and matching the window's decay rate to the envelope. Code is available at https://github.com/yoctta/CreditFilter.

</details>


### [182] [More Bits Won't Fix It: Diagnosing and Treating an Ill-Conditioned Backward Pass (A Case Study on Gemma-4)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27243)
*Jeong Hyun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究Gemma-4（12B/31B）LoRA微调在长提示下的梯度数值稳定性，指出图像提示下模型下层梯度近正交源于病态反向路径，并通过反向传播中切断特定MLP-图像token路径以稳定梯度、提升CT病灶定位任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长提示（含CT图像、自然照片或约2000 token文本）上进行LoRA微调时，bf16与fp32梯度在语言模型上层高度一致，但在下层近乎正交；作者希望查明该差异的来源（是精度噪声还是结构性的病态反向路径），并寻找一种低成本、无需改变前向计算的缓解手段。

Method: 以fp32梯度作为参考（在1e-6相对噪声下不变，验证其有效性），施加1e-4噪声观察余弦相似度崩溃；通过状态交换（state swaps）与仅反向干预（backward-only interventions）定位敏感位置；在Gemma-4-12B中定位到第19–21层MLP在图像token位置；随后在反向传播中对该路径做detach（前向保持不变），并在CT病灶定位任务上以四个随机种子与基线比较。

Result: 1e-4噪声下，10次测量中7次余弦相似度低于0.9，而bf16前向状态与fp32每token约差1%。反向detach后，1e-4噪声下余弦相似度在全部5次测量中≥0.97；裁剪前梯度范数中位数由8.5降至0.63；CT病灶定位验证损失由0.336±0.019降至0.296±0.003（四种子，所有改进运行均优于所有基线运行）。冻结下层时增益消失；纯fp32运行仍出现梯度尖峰（最大范数39,862）且未匹配该改进。

Conclusion: 下层梯度近正交并非单纯精度噪声所致，而是图像token位置处病态反向路径（梯度可被单个低信息patch主导）造成的敏感性；仅在反向切断该路径即可同时稳定梯度与改善下游训练。结论受限于单一任务与单一模型，泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: During LoRA fine-tuning of Gemma-4 (12B and 31B) on long prompts, whether they contain CT images, natural photos, or about 2,000 tokens of text, bf16 and fp32 gradients agree in the upper third of the language model (cosine similarity 0.96 or above) but are nearly orthogonal in the lower third. On image prompts, we trace the effect to an ill-conditioned backward path. The fp32 gradient is unchanged by element-wise relative noise of $10^{-6}$ on hidden states, confirming that fp32 is a valid reference. At $10^{-4}$, however, its cosine similarity with the noise-free gradient falls below 0.9 in 7 of 10 measurements, whereas bf16 forward states differ from fp32 states by about 1% per token. State swaps and backward-only interventions localize this sensitivity in Gemma-4-12B to the MLPs of layers 19-21 at image-token positions, where the gradient can be dominated by a single low-information patch. Detaching that path in the backward pass (while leaving the forward pass unchanged) keeps the gradient stable at $10^{-4}$ (cosine similarity 0.97 or above across all five measurements), lowers the median pre-clipping gradient norm from 8.5 to 0.63, and improves validation loss on a CT lesion-grounding task from 0.336 $\pm$ 0.019 to 0.296 $\pm$ 0.003 (four seeds; every modified run beats every baseline run). The gain disappears when the lower layers are frozen, and an fp32 run still exhibits gradient spikes (maximum norm 39,862) and fails to match this improvement. These training results cover one task and one model.

</details>


### [183] [A preference for local interaction between layers emerges during training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27258)
*Jiheon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过“扰动一层并测量扰动向其他层传播量”的实验，发现训练早期层间响应急剧弱且与距离无稳定次序，而随着训练进行响应增强并呈现“邻近层响应强于远层”的局部性，尽管训练在结构上连接了所有层。基于512个五层网络在模加任务上用AdamW与SGLD两种优化器的实验支持这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络各层共享同一误差并联合训练，理论上任一层的变化可影响所有其他层；由此人们预期各层对某一层变化的响应应当相近，而与该层距离远近无关。作者希望检验这一直觉是否成立，即层间响应是否随层间距离呈现结构性差异，以及这种结构是否随训练演化。

Method: 采用“推动-测量”的受控扰动法：在训练的多个阶段复制网络，对复制网络中的某一层施加扰动（push），测量扰动传递到其余各层的幅度，从而刻画层间响应及其与层间距离的关系。实验设置：512个五层网络，任务为模加（modular addition），分别使用AdamW与随机梯度Langevin动力学（SGLD）两种优化器进行训练与对照。

Result: 训练早期，层间响应较弱，且其按距离排序的次序不稳定；随着训练推进，响应整体增强；在几乎所有网络中，相邻层对彼此变化的响应强于相隔较远的层，即出现按距离衰减的局部性。该局部性是在训练过程中形成的，尽管训练的梯度路径在结构上将每一层与所有其他层相连。两种优化器下均观察到该现象。

Conclusion: 层间耦合并非由结构连通性直接决定，而是训练涌现出的性质：共享误差与全连接式梯度传播并不导致各层响应均等，反而在训练中自发形成以距离为序的局部响应结构。这提示深度网络的训练动力学具有可测量的空间（层序）局部化特征，为理解层间协同、扰动传播与训练稳定性提供了新的经验刻画。

Abstract: A deep neural network is a stack of layers, and all layers are trained to lower one shared error. A change in one layer can therefore reach every other layer. Because the error is shared by all layers, one would expect the other layers to respond alike, whether they are next to the changed layer or far from it. We find that early in training the response is weak and its order by distance is not stable. As training proceeds, the response grows, and neighboring layers come to respond to changes in each other more strongly than layers further apart, in almost every network. This locality forms during training, although training links every layer to every other. We show this by pushing one layer of a copy of the network at many stages of training and measuring how much of the push reaches each of the other layers. The experiments use 512 five-layer networks trained on modular addition with each of two optimizers, AdamW and stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics.

</details>


### [184] [Preserving Pretrained CFG Structure in RL for Diffusion Models with Conditional Contrast Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27288)
*Yunfei Zheng,Xiaolong Wang,Dandan Zheng,Xianjun Deng,Shenghao Liu,Wei Xiang,Yuqian Li,Laurence Tianruo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角统一解释CFG与RL，指出预训练CFG方向蕴含生成质量信息，并提出条件对比正则化（CCR）以在RL后训练中保持生成质量、改善奖励-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型后训练中，标准强化学习往往只提升目标奖励而无法可靠保持整体生成质量，导致奖励-质量权衡不稳定，作者将其视为一种奖励黑客（reward hacking）现象。由于CFG已知能改善视觉清晰度、提示对齐与感知质量，作者希望探究CFG的内在结构能否作为RL过程中的有效先验。

Method: 提出统一几何视角，将CFG与RL都解释为共同预测空间中的修正向量；定义RL诱导修正向量在预训练CFG修正向量上的投影，作为衡量质量相关结构保持程度的诊断指标；通过几何分析与旋转干预实验，验证预训练CFG方向携带奖励驱动RL更新未必保留的质量信息；据此提出条件对比正则化（CCR），利用冻结参考模型的条件对比作为RL中的局部结构先验。

Result: 在多种奖励设置与多种骨干网络上，CCR在保持有竞争力奖励的同时更一致地维持生成质量，相比标准RL和通用正则化方法取得更优的奖励-质量权衡。

Conclusion: 预训练CFG修正方向可作为扩散模型RL后训练中的有效结构先验；以投影诊断量化结构保持度、并用CCR约束该结构，可缓解奖励黑客问题，实现更稳定的奖励-质量权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become an important component in the post-training of diffusion models. In practice, however, standard RL often improves the target reward without reliably preserving holistic generation quality, leading to unstable reward-quality trade-offs that we view as a form of reward hacking. Since Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is known to improve visual clarity, prompt alignment, and perceptual quality in generation, we investigate whether its underlying structure can serve as a useful prior during RL. We present a unified geometric view that interprets both CFG and RL as correction vectors in a common prediction space, and identify the projection of the RL-induced correction onto the pretrained CFG correction as a useful diagnostic of quality-related structural preservation. Geometric analysis and rotation intervention further show that the pretrained CFG direction carries information useful for generation quality that reward-driven RL updates do not necessarily preserve. Based on this insight, we propose Conditional Contrast Regularization (CCR), which uses the frozen reference model's conditional contrast as a local structural prior during RL. Experiments across multiple reward settings and backbones show that CCR more consistently preserves generation quality while maintaining competitive reward, yielding a better reward-quality trade-off than standard RL and generic regularization methods.

</details>


### [185] [Gumbel Substitution: Removing Sampler Drift from RL Without Trusting the Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27375)
*Romain Fanucci,Malik El Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"Gumbel substitution"：协调方固定一套跨GPU位级一致的公开Gumbel噪声，采样方按 y=argmax(ℓ^q+G) 采样，训练方用同一噪声重算 v*=argmax(ℓ^p+G) 并在采样方上下文下给 v* 打分，从而在不需要采样方上报 q(y) 的情况下消除分布漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL rollout 来自任意硬件上的不可信采样器时，采样分布 q 与训练策略 p 不一致（精度、checkpoint 滞后），直接用采样器 token 训练会引入等于向 q 做SFT的漂移项。现有修正方法（TIS、MIS、IcePop、score centering、GSPO、CISPO）都依赖 q(y)，而无需许可的采样器上报的 q(y) 可能被误报或造假，缺乏可信性保障。

Method: 1) 协调方为每个token位置固定公开Gumbel噪声，保证跨GPU位级一致；2) 采样器以 y=argmax(ℓ^q+G) 产生token；3) 训练方计算 v*=argmax(ℓ^p+G)，即自身策略在相同噪声下本会抽到的token，并在采样方上下文（前缀）下对 v* 打分；4) 对遵守协议的采样器，v* 在给定前缀下是 p 的精确样本，故对任意 q 漂移期望为零，且不依赖采样方上报概率；5) 分析部署head带来的残差、准入闸门引入的额外偏差，以及自适应采样器仍可做的事；6) 同一比较给出逐token匹配率，用于准入与结算采样方报酬。

Result: 9B模型、两侧同一head：未修正训练的期望漂移是替换法的约170–4,800倍；2B模型强失配下未修正训练崩溃，替换法稳定，且与给定真实 q 的 MIS+score centering 相当；无需许可网络中，9B模型在来自异构采样器的15.75B token上训练158个优化步，无跳步，1.21% token被替换，在全部8个留出基准上均有提升。

Conclusion: Gumbel substitution 用共享随机噪声把"分布修正"转化为"同噪声下的argmax比较"，摆脱了对采样方自报 q(y) 的依赖，理论上消除漂移、实践中稳定可扩展，并顺带提供可结算的逐token匹配率；残留偏差来自head失配、准入闸门与自适应采样，需进一步刻画与限制。

Abstract: When RL rollouts come from untrusted samplers on arbitrary hardware, precision and checkpoint lag, the sampling distribution $q$ differs from the trainer's policy $p$, and training on the sampler's tokens adds a drift term equal to supervised fine-tuning toward $q$. Published corrections (TIS, MIS, IcePop, score centering, GSPO, CISPO) all need $q(y)$, which a permissionless sampler reports and can misreport. We introduce Gumbel substitution. The coordinator fixes public Gumbel noise for every token position, bit-identical across GPUs, and the sampler draws $y=\arg\max(\ell^q+G)$. The trainer recomputes $v^*=\arg\max(\ell^p+G)$, the token its own policy would have drawn with the same noise, and scores $v^*$ in the sampler's context. For a sampler that follows the protocol, $v^*$ is an exact sample from $p$ given the prefix, so the drift vanishes in expectation for any $q$, without sampler-reported probabilities. We measure the residual left by the deployed heads, bound the one added by admission gates, and state what an adaptive sampler can still do. The same comparison gives a per-token match rate that admits and pays samplers. In the lab, on a 9B model with the same head on both sides, the expected drift of uncorrected training is at least 170 to 4,800 times that of substitution. On a 2B model under strong mismatch, uncorrected training collapses while substitution stays stable, comparable to MIS with score centering given the true $q$. On a permissionless network, a 9B model was trained for 158 optimizer steps on 15.75B tokens from heterogeneous samplers, with no skipped step and 1.21% of tokens substituted; it improved on all eight held-out benchmarks.

</details>


### [186] [Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation of RL Experts: Toward Continual Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27376)
*Zhen Zhang,Changyi Yang,Xuehai He,Chengzhi Liu,Yutong Yin,Ziyue Liu,Zhuoran Yin,Yangmin Li,Zhihan Liu,Jisen Li,Mingyu Jin,Chuhan Li,Zhaotian Weng,Ruoyu He,Wenyao Gao,Jiaxin Deng,Chenwei Wu,Hui Shen,Wenhao Chai,Chenyang Zhao,Zhuofan Ying,Kieran Liu,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma,Zhaoran Wang,Alkesh Patel,Zhe Gan,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可组合软提示蒸馏(CSPD)：冻结RL前模型，将每个RL专家独立蒸馏为软提示，通过拼接提示实现专家组合，无需联合训练，性能与MOPD持平且推理开销低，指向固定骨干上的持续后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练产生的领域专家需合并为单一模型，且新专家不断到来使合并成为持续过程。权重合并与多教师在线策略蒸馏(MOPD)在每新增一个专家时都必须对整个模型重新整合，并且可能侵蚀目标领域之外的能力。

Method: 保持RL前模型冻结；将每个RL专家独立蒸馏进一个软提示；通过拼接各专家提示实现组合，无需联合训练。

Result: 单个软提示即可蒸馏一个RL专家，在23个数学、代码与智能体设置中有22个恢复教师至少90%的增益；独立训练的提示可良好组合，多专家设置下平均值随专家增加而上升，完整组合与MOPD相当(67.83 vs. 67.79)；在六个留出基准上泛化最佳；输入空间适配服务开销小，吞吐为未适配骨干的1.5%以内，达rank-4 LoRA的2.40倍。

Conclusion: CSPD为持续式后训练提供可行路径：在固定骨干上以组合软提示方式累积专家能力，兼顾性能、泛化与推理效率。

Abstract: RL post-training typically produces domain experts that must then be consolidated into one model, and as new experts keep arriving, this consolidation becomes a continual process. Weight merging and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) must re-consolidate the whole model whenever an expert is added, and can erode capabilities outside the target domains. We propose Composable Soft-Prompt Distillation (CSPD), which keeps the pre-RL model frozen, distills each RL expert independently into a soft prompt, and composes experts by concatenating their prompts without joint training. A single soft prompt suffices to distill an RL expert, recovering at least 90% of the teacher's gain in 22 of 23 math, code, and agent settings. Independently trained prompts compose well. In a multi-expert setting, the average rises with every expert added, and the full composition matches MOPD (67.83 vs. 67.79). The composition generalizes best across six held-out benchmarks. Input-space adaptation adds little serving overhead, keeping throughput within 1.5% of the unadapted backbone and reaching 2.40x that of rank-4 LoRA. These results point toward continual post-training on a fixed backbone.

</details>


### [187] [HR-Merge: Model Merging with Headroom-Weighted Residual Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27377)
*Jiakang Huang,Xueyan Zhang,Renyi Cai,Jingcheng Niu,Jiaru Li,Yining Wang,Josh Zhang,Jiahe Jiang,Jinman Zhao,Gerald Penn,Baharan Mirzasoleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HR-Merge，一种无需训练的模型合并包装方法：按各任务"验证余量"加权任务残差并重新合并，将结果加回基础合并方案，在NLI与视觉任务上普遍优于现有合并基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（如TIES）对所有任务施加同一合并规则，但精度损失在各任务间并不均匀，存在可恢复空间；作者希望定位"哪里值得恢复"并加以利用。

Method: 关键观察：(1) 丢失的精度可从任务残差（任务向量减合并更新）中恢复；(2) 任务"验证余量"（该任务专家验证精度中被合并损失的比例）可指示恢复收益；(3) 不同任务残差相互干扰，须合并而非简单相加。据此提出HR-Merge：对每个残差按所属任务的余量加权，用原合并算子重新合并加权残差，再以在验证集上选定的强度加回；若无增益则回退至基础合并。

Result: LLaMA-3 8B 的六个NLI适配器上，将TIES的归一化精度从93.29提升至94.29，超过所有测试基线，主要收益来自最弱任务的恢复；视觉方面在三个CLIP骨干、最多20个任务及LoRA合并设置下，整体提升或保持基础精度，并在每种设置中取得最佳模型。

Conclusion: 任务残差中的精度损失可被结构化恢复，且按验证余量加权、重新合并后加回的策略是通用、免训练且可插拔的，可稳定增强现有模型合并方法。

Abstract: Model merging combines experts fine-tuned from a shared backbone, such as LoRA adapters of a large language model, into one multi-task model without further training. Existing methods apply one rule to every task, yet the accuracy they lose is uneven across tasks. We show that the lost accuracy remains recoverable from each task's residual, its task vector minus the merged update, and that a task's validation headroom, the share of its expert validation accuracy the merge loses, indicates where recovery pays off. Residuals from different tasks interfere, so they must be merged rather than summed. We propose HR-Merge, a training-free wrapper around any merging method. It weights each residual by its task's headroom, re-merges the weighted residuals with the base operator, and adds the result back at a strength selected on validation, falling back to the base merge when no strength helps. On six NLI adapters of LLaMA-3 8B, HR-Merge lifts TIES from 93.29 to 94.29 normalized accuracy, above all tested bases, mainly by recovering its weakest task. In vision, on three CLIP backbones, up to 20 tasks, and LoRA merging, it generally improves or preserves base accuracy and yields the best model in every setting.

</details>


### [188] [Degradation of Language Models under Controlled Load: Synchronization, Rule Execution and Misfires on the Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27404)
*Hennadii Kutomanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将大模型在受控负载下的性能退化分解为同步、规则执行与误触发三个成分，为每个成分拟合其对各类负载的依赖规律，构成模型"护照"，并给出七个模型的护照结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着负载增加，语言模型错误增多，但其背后是不同性质的失效过程：可能与源文本失去对应、可能位置保持但规则执行错误、也可能改动了与任何规则无关的词。已有研究通常以整体准确率衡量退化，无法区分这些异质成分，因此需要一种能对退化进行细粒度归因的方法。

Method: 构造无意义文本上的词替换规则任务以施加受控负载，并将负载类型（文本长度、词在答案中的位置、替换密度、规则数量、规则在列表中的位置）分别独立设置；通过将答案与参考文本对齐以追踪每个词的处理结果，进而把退化拆分为同步、规则执行与误触发三类成分；采用考虑"请求间难度离散度本身随负载变化"的统计模型，为各成分分别拟合其对各负载类型的依赖规律。

Result: 所提分离方法可行：可对每一成分恢复出其对每一种负载的依赖规律，这些规律汇总构成模型的"护照"；论文按此方法构建并报告了七个模型的护照，从而刻画不同模型在三类失效成分上的差异。

Conclusion: 对退化的成分化分解为研究神经网络行为的细粒度结构提供了途径，使模型失效模式可在统一框架下被定量描述与比较。

Abstract: As the load grows, a language model makes more and more errors, but different processes stand behind this decline in quality. The model may lose the correspondence with the source text, it may keep its place in the text but execute a rule wrongly, or it may change a word that no rule concerns. In this work the degradation of a model under controlled load is separated into these three components: synchronization, rule execution and misfires. The load is set by rules that replace one word with another in a meaningless text, and each kind of load (the text length, the position of a word in the answer, the replacement density, the number of rules and the place of a rule in the list) is set separately. The alignment of the answer with the reference makes it possible to follow the outcome of every word, and a statistical model that takes into account that the spread of difficulty between requests itself depends on the load restores, for each component, the laws of its dependence on every kind of load. These laws make up the passport of a model. The paper presents the method of the separation, the principles of constructing the laws and the passports of seven models built by this method; the separation opens the way to studying the fine structure of the behavior of a neural network.

</details>


### [189] [Learned Network Dynamics for Model Predictive Traffic Signal Control under Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27435)
*Dang Viet Anh Nguyen,Carlos Lima Azevedo,Tomer Toledo,Filipe Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GNN-MPC，一种用图神经网络学习动作条件化网络动力学的滚动时域交通信号控制器，通过近似最佳响应协调交叉口相位序列，在非周期性事故场景下显著优于所有对比控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通信号MPC依赖排队响应模型，但异构路网中该模型难以指定与标定，事故导致通行能力与转向比例变化时尤甚；同时受控停止线车道短，上游排队溢出车道的测量之外，构成第二重限制。

Method: 构建GNN-MPC：以图神经网络学习动作条件化的路网动力学预测，用于滚动时域规划；交叉口间相位序列通过近似最佳响应协调，并设接受检验保证预测代价不高于初始方案；在微观仿真中设计受控对比实验，分离观测（上游表示）与预测（学习动力学vs标定store-and-forward）的作用。

Result: 在均匀网格与21交叉口异构网络的非周期性事故场景下，GNN-MPC相对所有测试控制器降低行程时间并提高完成行程数；异构网络中，相同上游测量下行程时间比MaxPressure低13%，而去除上游表示的GNN-MPC无法复现该性能；相同测量、规划器与目标下，用标定store-and-forward预测器替换学习动力学使行程时间增加31–34%。

Conclusion: 观测隐藏需求与预测其对控制的响应是网络级预测控制的两个互补必要条件；学习式动力学与上游需求表示共同构成GNN-MPC性能优势的来源。

Abstract: Model predictive control (MPC) of urban traffic signals relies on a model of how queues respond to signal decisions, which is hard to specify and calibrate in heterogeneous networks, particularly when incidents change capacities and turning shares. Measurements add a second limitation: the controlled lanes at the stop line are often short, so queues extending upstream of them fall outside lane-level measurements. We propose GNN-MPC, a receding-horizon controller that plans with action-conditioned network dynamics learned by graph neural networks. Phase sequences are coordinated across intersections by approximate best responses, with an acceptance test that keeps the predicted cost no higher than that of the initial plan. In microscopic simulation under non-recurrent incidents, GNN-MPC reduces travel time and increases completed trips relative to all tested controllers, on a homogeneous grid and a heterogeneous 21-intersection network. Controlled comparisons on the heterogeneous network distinguish the roles of observation and prediction. With nearly as many vehicles entering the network, GNN-MPC reduces travel time by 13% relative to MaxPressure with the same upstream measurement, and GNN-MPC without the upstream representation does not reproduce its performance. Under the same traffic measurements, planner and objective, replacing the learned dynamics with calibrated store-and-forward predictors increases travel time by 31-34%. Observing hidden demand and predicting its response to control are thus complementary requirements of network-level predictive control.

</details>


### [190] [THRIFTY: Cost-Constrained Reasoning-Data Synthesis for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27436)
*Aasheesh Singh,Vishal Vaddina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出THRIFTY——一个基于Thompson采样的bandit控制器，在固定生成token预算下为小语言模型蒸馏收集更多样的正确推理轨迹，在数学与代码基准上每token多获至多22%的多样正确轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM通常依赖大型教师LLM蒸馏出的推理轨迹训练，而合成数据生成是流水线的主要成本。现有采集方法缺乏成本意识，或仅将固定采样预算均匀分配到各查询，忽视了题目难度的巨大差异，导致战略性过采样或欠采样。

Method: 将数据采集建模为带背包约束的bandit问题（bandits-with-knapsacks），并提出多样性感知目标；用在线Beta后验跟踪每题的成功概率，基于Thompson采样逐token分配到预期能产生最多新异正确解的问题上；由于重复解价值递减，自适应停止对已饱和题目的重采样、把预算集中于覆盖不足的题目，并以机会成本淘汰不可解题。

Result: 在数学（AIME、MATH-500）与代码（LiveCodeBench）基准上，相较标准均匀采样配方与最强自适应基线，THRIFTY在每个生成token上多收集至多22%的多样正确轨迹，且在稀缺预算与困难问题场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: 成本感知、多样性驱动的自适应预算分配能显著提升教师蒸馏数据采集效率，尤其适用于预算紧张与高难度任务，为SLM高效训练数据构建提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are increasingly attractive for efficient, specialized deployment, and are typically trained on reasoning trajectories distilled from a larger teacher LLM. Generating this synthetic data is a major cost of the pipeline, especially when the teacher is a frontier model. Existing collection methods, however, are largely cost-unaware, or at best spread a fixed sampling budget uniformly across queries. Such allocation ignores the wide variation in problem difficulty and the strategic over- or under-sampling it invites. Thus, we propose THRIFTY, a Thompson-sampling bandit controller that spends a fixed generation-token budget to collect diverse correct reasoning trajectories, casting the task as a bandits-with-knapsacks problem with a novel diversity-aware objective. It tracks each problem's success probability with an online Beta posterior and spends each token where it expects the most new distinct correct solutions. Because repeated solutions add progressively less value, THRIFTY adaptively stops resampling saturated problems, concentrates budget on under-covered ones, and retires the unsolvable at the budget's opportunity cost. On math (AIME, MATH-500) and code (LiveCodeBench) benchmarks, THRIFTY gathers up to 22% more diverse correct trajectories per generation token than both the standard uniform-sampling recipe and the strongest adaptive baseline, with the largest gains in the scarce-budget and hard-problem regimes.

</details>


### [191] [Learning When to Ask: Advisor-Aware Post-Training for SLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27443)
*Aasheesh Singh,Vishal Vaddina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADAPT多阶段后训练方法，教会小模型以成本感知方式向强模型求助并跨步骤遵循建议，在TextWorld、LiveCodeBench、GAIA上大幅提升成功率且求助稀疏、可泛化至未见advisor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型（SLM）适合低成本高吞吐的智能体部署，但在多步推理与长程任务所需的工具使用上较弱。仅把SLM接到强模型（advisor-executor模式）的"harness设计"在开源与闭源小模型族上开箱即失败，因此需要后训练来教会模型"何时求助"以及"如何跨步执行建议"。

Method: ADAPT（ADvisor-Aware Post-Training）为多阶段后训练配方：仅使用策略自身rollout、以环境最终结果为监督信号，无需学习奖励模型或人工标注，从而习得成本感知的求助时机与建议遵循行为。

Result: 相对未训练harness基线，TextWorld成功率24.5%→77.3%，LiveCodeBench 21.8%→56.0%，GAIA 17.5%→49.3%；每任务仅约1.0–2.1次 advisor 调用。两个长程任务上缩小了与"完美择时求助oracle"差距的70–85%，代码生成上甚至超过该oracle；替换为训练中未见的advisor时增益仍保持。

Conclusion: 单纯连接advisor不足以让小模型胜任长程任务，需通过无标注、基于自身rollout的结果监督后训练来习得求助策略；该策略稀疏、有效且对advisor变化鲁棒。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are increasingly attractive for cheap, high-throughput agentic deployment, but they remain weak at multi-step reasoning and tool use that long-horizon tasks demand. We study how to post-train an SLM to seek advice from a stronger model and act on that advice itself, following an advisor-executor pattern. Out of the box, this pattern fails across open- and closed-source small-model families: wiring an SLM to an advisor is a harness-design matter that is not enough on its own. A post-training recipe is needed to teach the model when to ask, in a cost-aware fashion, and how to follow advice across steps. Our method, ADAPT (ADvisor-Aware Post-Training), is a multi-stage recipe that instills this behavior, learning entirely from the policy's own rollouts supervised by the environment's outcome, with no learned reward model or human annotation. Across three domains, compared to an untrained harness baseline, ADAPT improves success rate in embodied text reasoning (TextWorld) from 24.5% to 77.3%, code generation (LiveCodeBench) from 21.8% to 56.0%, and deep web search (GAIA) from 17.5% to 49.3%, while querying the advisor sparingly (~1.0-2.1 calls per task). On the two long-horizon domains it closes 70-85% of the gap to an oracle that perfectly times when to ask for advice, and on code generation it exceeds that oracle. The gains persist when the advisor is replaced by one unseen in training, showing that the learned help-seeking policy is robust to a changing advisor rather than tied to the one it was trained with.

</details>


### [192] [Cannot Wait to Verify: Parallel Drafting to Eliminate Verifier Idle Time in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27477)
*Divya Jyoti Bajpai,Arun Verma,Manjesh Kumar Hanawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种即插即用的"层并行"草稿生成方法，在投机解码的草稿阶段并行评估多个Transformer层，用隐藏状态空间散度做一致性校验，无需改结构或重训练，草稿阶段最高加速1.65×且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归推理受限于逐token串行执行，延迟高、GPU利用率低；投机解码虽引入草稿-验证范式缓解该瓶颈，但草稿阶段本身仍是串行的，导致验证器空闲、算力闲置。作者希望在不改动模型架构、不重新训练的前提下加速草稿生成。

Method: 从共享隐藏状态出发，对多个Transformer层并行进行前向评估以生成候选草稿状态；通过在隐藏状态空间度量偏差来验证层间一致性——偏差较小时接受对应的层草稿状态，偏差较大时施加校正步骤。方法即插即用，利用层间冗余，不修改架构、不需重训练。

Result: 在NLP任务上，草稿阶段最高加速1.65×，精度下降极小，同时整体性能与标准投机解码（SD）持平。

Conclusion: 层间冗余可被有效利用来并行化草稿生成，从而提升硬件利用率、降低延迟；该方案作为通用插件可与现有投机解码流程兼容，为串行草稿瓶颈提供了低成本改进路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive inference in large transformer models is limited by token-level sequential execution, resulting in high latency and low GPU utilization. Speculative decoding (SD) mitigates this bottleneck by introducing a draft-and-verifier model. However, the drafting stage remains sequential, leaving the verifier idle and underutilizing compute. To address this, we propose a plug-and-play drafting adaptive drafting method that parallelizes inference across transformer layers during draft generation. Starting from a shared hidden state, our approach evaluates multiple layers in parallel and verifies consistency by measuring divergence in hidden-state space. Layer-wise draft states are accepted when deviations are small; otherwise, a correction step is applied. By exploiting redundancy across transformer layers, our method accelerates draft generation and improves hardware utilization without modifying architectures or requiring retraining. Experimental results on NLP tasks show that our method speeds up the drafting stage by up to $1.65\times$ with minimal degradation in accuracy, while maintaining the same performance as SD.

</details>


### [193] [Verifying and Strengthening Neural Tangent Kernel Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27487)
*Hao Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文给出两层神经网络神经切核（NTK）惰性训练收敛定理的 Lean 4 端到端形式化证明，并对标准分析做出两点改进：采用行协方差集中代理使宽度证书缩小 36–121 倍，以及通过 L² 参数几何化解 Hessian 范数 Θ(√p) 导致的线性化障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NTK 理论解释了超宽网络在惰性训练下的收敛性，其标准证明组合了矩阵集中、随机 Gram 矩阵、连续时间动力学、谱扰动与自举论证，但此前缺乏机器可检验的验证。作者希望提供可机器检验的端到端形式化证明，并顺带修正标准分析中的技术松弛。

Method: 在 Lean 4 中形式化两层网络：光滑有界激活（实例化为 tanh）、冻结偏置、成对 ±1 初始化及显式宽度条件下，证明梯度流的存在唯一性与残差指数衰减界。形式化覆盖初始强制性、核漂移、移动自举、残差 ODE 及流的存在唯一性；仅假设总体 NTK 非退化 λ₀>0。集中不等式方面用经典行协方差代理替代矩阵 Bernstein 方差代理；参数几何上采用 L² 度量而非 sup 范数 Taylor 估计。

Result: 得到端到端定理：以概率 1−δ 梯度流存在唯一且 ‖r(t)‖² ≤ ‖y‖² e^{−λ₀t/2}。集中宽度证书在 (m,d)=(16,8) 时缩小 36–53 倍，在 (32,16) 时缩小 94–121 倍（Lean 验证不等式，比值为数值计算）。同时给出网络 Hessian 在 sup 范数参数球上算子范数为 Θ(√p) 的纸面计算，说明 sup 范数 Taylor 估计无法给出 O(1/√p) 线性化界，需改用 L² 几何。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了 NTK 惰性训练理论的机器检验端到端证明，并在集中界与参数几何两方面改进了标准分析；但所需宽度虽为多项式量级，仍远超实际可用规模，限制其适用性。

Abstract: Writing
  Neural tangent kernel (NTK) theory explains why very wide neural networks trained by gradient methods converge in the lazy-training regime. Its standard proof combines matrix concentration, random Gram matrices, continuous-time dynamics, spectral perturbation, and a bootstrap argument, but has lacked machine-checked verification. We present a Lean\~4 formalisation for two-layer networks, proving an end-to-end theorem: for a smooth bounded activation (instantiated with $σ=\tanh$), frozen biases, paired $\pm 1$ initialisation, and explicit width conditions, gradient flow exists uniquely with probability at least $1-δ$, and
  $$ \|r(t)\|^2 \leq \|y\|^2 e^{-λ_0 t/2}. $$
  The sole data assumption is population NTK non-degeneracy, $λ_0>0$. Initial coercivity, kernel drift, the movement bootstrap, the residual ODE, and flow existence and uniqueness are formally proved.
  The formalisation also sharpens the standard analysis in two ways. First, replacing the conventional matrix-Bernstein variance proxy with the classical row-covariance proxy yields estimated concentration-width certificates for $\tanh$ that are $36$--$53\times$ smaller at $(m,d)=(16,8)$ and $94$--$121\times$ smaller at $(32,16)$. Lean certifies the inequality; the ratios are numerical. Second, the network Hessian has operator norm $Θ(\sqrt{p})$ on the sup-norm parameter ball (a paper calculation), preventing the expected $O(1/\sqrt{p})$ linearisation bound via sup-norm Taylor estimates. An $L^2$ parameter geometry resolves this issue. Required widths remain polynomial but far beyond practical scales, limiting applicability.

</details>


### [194] [Learning Unknown Oscillators From Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27492)
*Priyank Jayraj,Poonam Goyal,Navneet Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究由未知振荡器驱动的半汤普森（semi-bandit）问题：每轮从4个奖励通道中选2个观测各自奖励，频率误差会改变未来奖励排序。作者提出"检验式commitment"控制策略，在连续置信集上界定后悔，后悔呈平方根速率增长；实验表明检验式commitment仅需49次拟合（对比1,023次），平均后悔高出1.5%，并获得4.69倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典半bandit/振荡器驱动奖励的算法通常假设频率已知或每轮重新拟合，计算代价高、缺乏对频率估计误差如何影响未来排序与观测的理论刻画。本文动机是：明确在何种条件下"只拟合一次振荡器并采用贪心选择"就能暴露预测误差，从而用更少拟合获得可控后悔。

Method: 1) 设定每轮选2个（共4个）奖励通道、观测个体奖励的半bandit框架；2) 证明当承诺于单一拟合振荡器时，其贪心选择能够暴露预测误差；3) 设计控制器在连续置信集上约束后悔，并在预算内保持最长区块；4) 当检验失败时，用在线回归策略采集观测；5) 将结果扩展到两个竞争振荡器情形，并用置信集认证轨迹后悔。

Result: 后悔以平方根速率增长（至多对数因子），对所有频率成立，并延伸至双振荡器竞争场景。在96个全新合成实例上，检验式commitment使用49次拟合而非1,023次，平均后悔仅比每轮拟合高1.5%；匹配的孤立计时下实现4.69倍加速；置信集亦可认证轨迹后悔。

Conclusion: 承诺于单个拟合振荡器并结合失效检验与按需在线回归，可在保证平方根级后悔的同时大幅降低拟合与计算开销；理论上的置信集既支撑后悔界，也提供轨迹后悔的认证，为振荡器驱动半bandit提供高效且可证的策略。

Abstract: We study semi-bandits driven by unknown oscillators. Each round, a learner selects two of four reward channels and observes their individual rewards. Frequency errors change future reward rankings and the resulting measurements. We show when committing to one fitted oscillator lets its greedy choices expose prediction errors. Our controller bounds reward loss (regret) over a continuous confidence set and holds the longest block within budget. An online regression policy gathers observations when the check fails. Regret grows at a square-root rate, up to logarithmic factors, across all frequencies and extends to two competing oscillators. On 96 fresh synthetic instances, checked commitment uses 49 fits instead of 1,023, with 1.5% higher mean regret than fitting every round. Matched isolated timings give a 4.69-fold speedup. The confidence sets also certify trajectory regret.

</details>


### [195] [Turnout: Adaptive Attention Switching for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27500)
*Anna Kuzina,Mart van Baalen,Paul N. Whatmough,Babak Ehteshami Bejnordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Turnout通过事后线性化+蒸馏训练线性路径，并用强化学习策略在生成时逐层动态决定全注意力与线性注意力的切换，从而在推理任务上取得更优的精度-内存Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后Transformer线性化用固定大小的状态替代随生成长度线性增长的KV缓存，可降低内存，但常带来精度损失；在需要长生成的推理任务中尤为严重。工作动机是在保留内存收益的同时缓解线性化导致的精度下降。

Method: 对预训练全注意力模型做事后线性化：通过蒸馏训练一条线性路径，同时保持原全注意力路径不变，形成混合模型；生成时保留两条路径，用学习到的策略控制每层何时从全注意力切换为线性注意力；该策略以强化学习训练，奖励同时权衡答案正确性与内存占用。

Result: 在推理任务的实证评估中，学习到的自适应策略相比固定切换与静态混合基线，取得更优的精度-效率Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 动态、逐层自适应的切换策略优于静态混合方案，能够在维持线性化内存优势的同时有效缓解精度损失。

Abstract: Post-hoc transformer linearization replaces the key-value cache of full attention, which grows linearly with generation length, with a fixed size state. This change reduces memory consumption but often hurts accuracy. In reasoning tasks, which require long generations, this effect is especially pronounced. In this work we introduce Turnout, a method that mitigates accuracy loss as a result of linearization, while maintaining its memory-reduction benefits. We apply post-hoc linearization to a pretrained full-attention model, training a linear path through distillation while leaving the original full-attention path unchanged. During response generation, the resulting hybrid model retains both paths and uses a learned policy to control when each layer switches from full to linear attention. We train the policy with reinforcement learning using a reward that balances answer correctness and memory use. Empirical evaluation of our method shows that the learned adaptive policy achieves a better accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier on reasoning tasks compared to fixed switching and static hybrid baselines.

</details>


### [196] [Breaking the Barrier of Hard Samples: A Data-Centric Approach to Synthetic Data for Medical Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27511)
*Maynara Donato de Souza,Cleber Zanchettin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Profile2Gen，一个以数据为中心的框架，用于指导和精炼合成数据生成，重点解决回归任务中的难学样本问题；在 6 个医学数据集、7 种先进生成模型上进行了约 18,000 次实验，表明精炼后的合成样本可降低预测误差、提升模型可靠性，并将 DataIQ 框架推广至回归任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学研究中数据稀缺与数据质量问题仍是构建稳健预测模型的主要障碍。依赖真实世界数据易导致模型存在偏差、跨人群泛化性差；合成数据虽被视为有前景的解决方案，但其样本的代表性与有效利用仍存在挑战，尤其缺乏对合成数据生成过程的引导与筛选机制。

Method: 提出 Profile2Gen 数据为中心的框架，用于引导合成数据的生成与精炼，聚焦回归任务中难学样本（hard-to-learn samples）的处理；同时将 DataIQ 框架推广至回归任务场景。实证方面在 6 个医学数据集上，结合 7 种最先进生成模型，开展约 18,000 次实验。

Result: 精炼后的合成样本能够降低预测误差并提升模型可靠性；经统计分析验证，该方法在性能上达到或优于仅使用真实数据训练的模型。

Conclusion: 以数据为中心的合成数据引导与精炼策略可有效缓解医学预测建模中的数据稀缺与偏差问题；DataIQ 向回归任务的推广拓展了其适用范围，为合成数据在更广泛场景下的可靠应用提供了依据。

Abstract: Data scarcity and quality issues remain significant barriers to developing robust predictive models in medical research. Traditional reliance on real-world data often leads to biased models with poor generalizability across diverse patient populations. Synthetic data generation has emerged as a promising solution, yet challenges related to these samples' representativeness and effective utilization persist. This paper introduces Profile2Gen, a novel data-centric framework designed to guide the generation and refinement of synthetic data, focusing on addressing hard-to-learn samples in regression tasks. We conducted approximately 18,000 experiments to validate its effectiveness across six medical datasets, utilizing seven state-of-the-art generative models. Results demonstrate that refined synthetic samples can reduce predictive errors and enhance model reliability. Additionally, we generalize the DataIQ framework to support regression tasks, enabling its application in broader contexts. Statistical analyses confirm that our approach achieves equal or superior performance compared to models trained exclusively on real data.

</details>


### [197] [On the Limits of Observability for Evaluating Coverage in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27543)
*Ziluowen Luo,Jun Yin,Songyang Liu,Ji Qi,Chuang Hu,Kyrie You Wu,Chaozhuo Li,Senzhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有机器遗忘评估因只关注选定遗忘集而忽略"覆盖度"（方法可满足的请求范围）；作者从可观测性与表示秩下界分析出发，提出CEO框架，用组合式颜色-形状基准、留一重训练oracle与SAE激活扩展来估计覆盖度，发现20个基线中仅从头重训练能覆盖全部删除目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前遗忘评估依赖预测准确率与成员推断攻击（MIA），且通常在人工选定的遗忘集上评测，因而忽视了一个关键维度——Coverage，即方法实际能可靠响应的请求范围。这种选择性评估可能掩盖方法在其它删除请求上的失效，需要系统性刻画可观测性限制与容量限制对覆盖度的影响。

Method: 1）用502个重训练模型做可观测性压力测试，比较预测输出与隐藏嵌入对删除的可见性；2）理论分析：证明用线性打分区分全部n个单例删除需共享表示秩为Ω(log n)，并推广到任意子集，给出部分删除表现为完全删除的条件；3）提出CEO框架：构建组合式颜色-形状合成基准，使遗忘效果与保留效用可从完整输出分布测量，并为每个目标提供留一重训练oracle；4）以固定数量的oracle可观测样本估计经验目标覆盖度（被删除时可靠标记、在任何其它删除下极少被标记的目标比例）；5）用原始模型训练的SAE冻结编码器将各可观测表示扩展到高维激活空间，以恢复被漏检的删除。

Result: 压力测试显示隐藏嵌入能揭示在预测输出中被漏检的已知删除；理论给出秩Ω(log n)的容量下界及部分删除伪完全的条件；在CEO基准上对20个基线方法的广泛实验表明，即使在该简单合成任务上，也只有从头重训练能覆盖所有删除目标，其余方法均存在覆盖缺口。

Conclusion: 仅以选定遗忘集上的准确率与MIA评估会系统性高估遗忘方法的有效性；可观测性与表示容量共同限制覆盖度。CEO提供了可度量的覆盖度评估范式，并表明当前遗忘算法在覆盖全删除请求方面与精确重训练存在显著差距，未来评估应报告覆盖度并借助激活空间探测弥补可观测性不足。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of any requested subset of training data while preserving utility on the remaining data. Empirical evaluations measure predictive accuracy and infer training-set membership (MIAs) from model outputs or intermediate activations. However, by focusing on selected forget sets, these evaluations can overlook Coverage, the range of requests a method can fulfill. To assess this coverage, we first conduct a stress test of observability with 502 retrained models, finding that hidden embeddings reveal known deletions missed in predictions. To characterize capacity limits on coverage, we show that distinguishing all $n$ singleton deletions with linear scores can require a shared representation of rank $Ω(\log n)$. We further extend the bound to arbitrary subsets and show when partial deletion can appear complete. Building on this analysis, we propose CEO, a framework for evaluating unlearning coverage under limited observability. Its compositional color-shape benchmark makes forgetting and retained utility measurable from the full output distribution and provides a leave-one-out retraining oracle for each target. From a fixed number of oracle observables, we estimate empirical target coverage, the fraction of targets reliably flagged when deleted and rarely flagged under any other deletions. To recover missed deletions, CEO further expands each observable representation into high-dimensional activation space with the frozen encoder of a SAE trained on the original model. Extensive experiments of 20 baselines shows that even on this simple synthetic task, only retraining from scratch covers all deletion targets.

</details>


### [198] [WEIR: Watershed Exploration and Drift-Aware Prefix Shrinkage for Multi-Turn Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27570)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WEIR：基于Doob轨迹方差守恒的actor-critic框架，通过分水岭探索与内在漂移收缩实现多轮LLM智能体的信用分配与高效 rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮语言模型智能体通常只在长交互轨迹末端获得单一二值奖励，难以区分关键决策与常规步骤，也难以估计准确的中间价值。前缀树方法虽通过合并共享动作历史提升样本效率，但仍存在两个结构缺陷：(1) 朴素池化以固定权重将过时的off-policy结果混入critic目标；(2) 均匀或基于熵的分支策略把续采样浪费在结局已定的死胡同状态上。

Method: 提出WEIR框架，理论基础为Doob turn-variance conservation identity。具体包括：维护跨迭代的规范动作前缀树，配以指数衰减的访问计数；训练前缀条件化的critic，其目标为极小极大（minimax）漂移感知收缩目标——在稀疏节点由单轨迹回报主导、在密集节点向池化经验均值收缩；复用更新前的同一critic进行两级预算分配：任务间按Neyman分层 B_q ∝ sqrt(p̂_q(1-p̂_q)) 分配 rollout 预算，任务内将分支导向"分水岭分叉"——即高生存余量不确定性与陡峭价值悬崖同时出现的位置。

Result: 在四个多跳QA基准、ALFWorld与WebShop上，基于Qwen2.5-3B/7B-Instruct：QA平均精确匹配分别为32.18%与37.85%，ALFWorld成功率分别为84.83%与87.81%；较CAPO提升1.50–3.74个百分点，同时相较链式采样减少28–29%的rollout token。

Conclusion: WEIR通过漂移感知的收缩目标缓解前缀树中的off-policy污染，并利用同一critic引导的分水岭探索集中采样预算于信息量最高的决策点，在多项多轮任务上兼顾性能提升与采样效率，验证了方差守恒视角下信用分配与探索分配的统一设计。

Abstract: Multi-turn language model agents often receive only a single binary reward at the end of a long interaction trajectory, making it difficult to separate decisive decisions from routine steps or estimate accurate intermediate values. Prefix-tree methods improve sample efficiency by merging trajectories that share action histories, yet two structural flaws remain: naive pooling mixes stale off-policy outcomes into critic targets with fixed weights, and uniform or entropy-driven branching wastes continuations on dead-end states whose outcome is already settled. We introduce WEIR (Watershed Exploration and Intrinsic-Drift Shrinkage), an actor-critic reinforcement learning framework grounded in a Doob turn-variance conservation identity. WEIR maintains a cross-iteration canonical action-prefix tree with exponentially discounted visit counts and trains a prefix-conditioned critic toward a minimax drift-aware shrinkage target that adapts from single-trajectory returns at rare nodes to pooled empirical means at dense nodes. Using the same pre-update critic, WEIR allocates rollout budgets across tasks via Neyman stratification $B_q \propto \sqrt{\hat{p}_q(1-\hat{p}_q)}$ and directs within-task branches to watershed forks where high survival-headroom uncertainty coincides with steep value cliffs. Across four multi-hop QA benchmarks, ALFWorld, and WebShop on Qwen2.5-3B/7B-Instruct, WEIR achieves $32.18\%$ and $37.85\%$ average QA exact match and $84.83\%$ and $87.81\%$ ALFWorld success rates, outperforming CAPO by $1.50$--$3.74$ points while generating $28$--$29\%$ fewer rollout tokens than chain sampling.

</details>


### [199] [Minimax-Optimal Small-Loss Regret in Online Convex Optimization and Applications to Contextual Search and Online Inverse Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27572)
*Shinsaku Sakaue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为在线凸优化（OCO）给出基于小损失的遗憾界，仅需每轮一个次梯度，达到 $O(1+\sqrt{\Delta_T^\star})$ 的最优界，并应用于上下文搜索与在线逆优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 OCO 遗憾界以时间 $T$ 为尺度，无法刻画"好情况"下损失很小时应更优的表现。作者希望在不假设光滑性、不假设梯度范数被损失值约束、也不知道 $\Delta_T^\star$ 与时间跨度的情况下，取得小损失（small-loss）型最优遗憾界。

Method: 以 FTRL（跟随正则化领导者）为框架，采用多基学习器混合，并把 Gupta 等（2026）为在线逆优化设计的"度量更新撤销"（metric-update revocation）机制推广到一般 OCO；每轮仅使用一个次梯度，且无需损失值或时间跨度先验。

Result: 在 $\ell_p$ 球（$1\le p\le\infty$）上取得 $O(1+\sqrt{\Delta_T^\star})$ 且对定义域依赖也是极小极大最优的界；$d$ 维、比较翻转偏差 $\kappa$ 的上下文搜索中，欧氏归一化下达到 $\Theta(\sqrt d/\kappa^2)$ 的极小极大期望累积绝对误差，仅用多项式次算术运算，解决 Liu 等（2021）的开放问题；在线逆优化在欧氏单位球效用域上相对观测动作的遗憾为 $O(\sqrt d+d^{1/4}\sqrt{D_T})$，$D_T=0$ 时与 Gupta 等（2026）一致，概率单纯形上改进为 $O(\log(d+1)+\sqrt{D_T\log(d+1)})$。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了简洁、无参数的小损失 OCO 算法，兼具最优性、广泛适用性与计算可行性，并在上下文搜索（回答开放问题）与在线逆优化两个下游任务中给出改进或匹配最优的保证。

Abstract: We give small-loss regret bounds for online convex optimization (OCO), using one subgradient per round. Let $Δ_T^\star$ denote the cumulative loss of the best fixed decision in hindsight, measured relative to the per-round minima. Our algorithm achieves the regret bound $O(1+\sqrt{Δ_T^\star})$ without knowing $Δ_T^\star$, up to domain and gradient-scale factors. The bound is minimax optimal, including its domain dependence, on $\ell_p$ balls for every $1\le p\le\infty$. No smoothness or condition bounding gradient norms by loss values is assumed, and neither loss values nor the time horizon are needed. Algorithmically, we extend the metric-update revocation of Gupta et al. (2026), originally developed for online inverse optimization, to general OCO using the follow-the-regularized-leader framework and a mixture of multiple base learners. We also apply our algorithm to contextual search and online inverse optimization. In $d$-dimensional contextual search with comparison flips of unknown bias $κ>0$, we obtain minimax expected cumulative absolute error $Θ(\sqrt{d}/κ^2)$ under Euclidean normalization using polynomially many arithmetic operations, resolving an open question of Liu et al. (2021). For online inverse optimization with the Euclidean unit-ball utility domain, our algorithm bounds the regret relative to observed actions by $O(\sqrt{d}+d^{1/4}\sqrt{D_T})$, where $D_T$ is their cumulative suboptimality; this matches the regret rate of Gupta et al. (2026) when $D_T=0$. Our guarantee extends to other utility domains; for example, on the probability simplex, the bound improves to $O(\log(d+1)+\sqrt{D_T\log(d+1)})$.

</details>


### [200] [PRISM: Validation-Free Online Batch Selection for LLM Fine-Tuning via Preconditioned Conic Ghost Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27578)
*Lin Tang,Wei Zhang,Jing Li,Hongyu Chen,Ming Zhao,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PRISM：一种无验证集的在线批选择器，在因子化二阶矩度量下对齐梯度，并将子集构建建模为正射线投影上的锥形设施选址问题，实现高效且可证明近优的 LLM 微调数据选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线批选择可降低 LLM 微调成本，但现有选择器依赖验证集/代理集，或使用未预条件的欧氏设施选址目标；后者受重尾梯度范数与跨层各向异性影响，直径偏移的相似度被扭曲。

Method: PRISM 用因子化二阶矩度量（预条件）对齐候选梯度，将子集构建构造为正射线投影上的锥形设施选址；通过因子化预条件 ghost 内积计算两两几何量，避免物化逐样本参数梯度；锥目标保持归一化、非负、单调子模，保证 (1-1/e) 贪心近似，且加性误差随总批梯度能量而非最坏离群直径缩放；按最佳对齐射线分配候选样本，得到闭式径向投影权重，自动将正交或噪声样本权重收缩至零。

Result: 推导出非凸收敛界，表明优化误差直接由锥表示代价控制；在 1.5B–8B 参数的四种 LLM 微调配置中，PRISM 优于全批训练、欧氏设施选址与零阶选择器，在无留出数据情况下媲美或超过验证引导基线。

Conclusion: PRISM 提供了一种无需验证集、理论可证且实用的在线批选择方案，兼顾计算效率、鲁棒性与下游性能。

Abstract: Online batch selection lowers the cost of fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) by replacing each candidate batch with a smaller informative subset. Existing selectors typically depend on a held-out validation or proxy set, or else rely on Euclidean facility-location objectives over unpreconditioned gradients whose diameter-shifted similarities are distorted by heavy-tailed gradient norms and cross-layer anisotropy. We introduce PRISM (Preconditioned Ray-Isotropic Submodular Matching), a validation-free online batch selector that aligns candidate gradients in a factored second-moment metric and casts subset construction as conic facility location over positive ray projections. Computing the required pairwise geometry through factored preconditioned ghost inner products avoids materializing per-example parameter gradients, while the conic objective remains normalized, nonnegative, and monotone submodular---guaranteeing a $(1-1/e)$ greedy approximation whose additive error scales with total batch gradient energy rather than the worst-case outlier diameter. Assigning each candidate to its best-aligned selected ray induces closed-form radial projection weights that automatically shrink orthogonal or noisy examples toward zero. We derive a nonconvex convergence bound showing that optimization error is governed directly by the conic representation cost. Across four LLM fine-tuning configurations spanning 1.5B to 8B parameters, PRISM outperforms full-batch training, Euclidean facility location, and zeroth-order selectors, and rivals or surpasses validation-guided baselines without held-out data.

</details>


### [201] [Adapting AI Weather Foundation Models to a Changing Climate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27605)
*Xinlei Xiong,Wenbo Hu,Shuxun Zhou,Lingxi Xie,Richang Hong,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向AI气象基础模型的在线持续适应框架，利用"地理持久偏差+流依赖误差"两类误差结构，在解码器低维敏感子空间内做流依赖校正，并以残差记忆与变量级在线门控处理持久偏差；严格时间顺序在线评测下，69个输出场、5个预报时效平均MSE/MAE较最强基线δ-Adapter降低6.90%/6.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI气象基础模型从历史档案中学习，而部署期气候分布持续演变，形成"模型—气候失配"；反复全模型重训代价过高，因此需要持续在线适应。同时，预报误差本身的结构（空间持久性 vs. 随大气状态变化的流依赖性）决定了适应应如何设计，而现有工作缺乏对该结构的针对性利用。

Method: 构建在线适应框架，显式分解并利用两种互补误差结构：(1) 对Aurora-1.3B的预报损失敏感性分析显示其高度集中于解码器，据此在固定的低维敏感子空间中施加流依赖校正；(2) 用残差记忆模块跟踪校正后仍残留的持久性误差，并由变量级在线门控控制持久分量的贡献强度。实验以ERA5 1959–1968做离线初始化，1969–2022按严格时间顺序流式在线评测，且更新仅使用此前已获得的验证目标，避免信息泄漏。

Result: 以2米温度为例，由ERA5直接度量的相对历史参考的分布漂移越大，冻结模型误差越大、适应增益越高，且适应后模型在各评测时段技能更稳定。在69个输出场与6–72小时五个时效上，相较六种在线适应基线中最强的δ-Adapter，MSE与MAE分别降低6.90%与6.45%。

Conclusion: 预报误差的可分解结构（持久偏差与流依赖误差）为在线持续适应提供了有效归纳偏置；仅更新解码器低维子空间并辅以持久误差记忆与门控，即可在严格无未来信息的流式评测中获得稳定且显著优于现有在线适应基线的性能，为AI气象模型应对气候漂移提供低成本的持续学习方案。

Abstract: AI weather foundation models learn from historical archives, while the climate distribution encountered during deployment continues to evolve. This growing model--climate mismatch motivates continual adaptation without repeated full-model retraining, but the structure of weather forecast errors determines how such adaptation should be performed. We develop an online adaptation framework that exploits two complementary error structures: geographically persistent biases and flow-dependent errors that vary with the atmospheric state. Analysis of Aurora-1.3B reveals strongly concentrated forecast-loss sensitivity in its decoder, motivating flow-dependent corrections within fixed low-dimensional sensitivity subspaces. A residual memory tracks persistent errors remaining after this correction, while a variable-wise online gate controls the contribution of the persistent component. We evaluate Aurora-1.3B using ERA5 from 1959--1968 for offline initialization and a strictly chronological 1969--2022 stream for online evaluation, with updates restricted to previously available verification targets. For 2-metre temperature, distributional departure from the historical reference, measured directly from ERA5, is associated with larger frozen-model errors and greater adaptation gains, while the adapted model maintains more stable skill across evaluation periods. Across 69 output fields and five lead times from 6 to 72 hours, our method reduces MSE and MAE by 6.90\% and 6.45\%, respectively, relative to $δ$-Adapter, the strongest of six online adaptation baselines.

</details>


### [202] [Quantum-Informed Regularized Poisson Learning for Graph-Based Semi-Supervised Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27609)
*Hamed Gholipour,Farid Bozorgnia,Hamzeh Mohammadigheymasi,Joao Neves,Moharram Challenger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 IPQSSL——量子启发的正则化 Poisson 图半监督分类框架：对局部图特征做 QR 分解、提升为酉编码，经共享变分电路测得节点级对角权重以稳定对称化 Poisson 系统，并给出正定性与 Hermitian 块编码兼容形式；七个基准上正则化效果依赖数据集与标签预算，增益主要来自对角稳定而非变分训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签稀缺条件下图半监督分类的 Poisson 型方法（如 Poisson 学习）数值稳定性与算子条件数受限，同时缺乏通往量子线性代数的清晰接口。作者希望在经典 NISQ 实现与容错量子计算之间建立资源透明的桥梁，并厘清对角正则化何时真正有益。

Method: 1) 对局部图特征上下文做 QR 因子分解，并提升为酉（unitary）编码；2) 用共享变分量子电路处理编码态，测量结果生成节点级对角权重；3) 该对角权重用于稳定一个对称化的 Poisson 系统（对称化正则化算子）；4) 在显式谱条件下证明对称化正则化算子的正定性；5) 推导 Hermitian、块编码兼容的形式，将经典 NISQ 导向实现与未来容错路径分离；6) 在 7 个基准（表格数据 + 引用网络 Cora）上做对比与消融。

Result: 在 Iris 与 Wine 上，除单标签预算外 IPQSSL 均优于 Poisson 基线，Iris 在每类 10 标签时达 0.946 准确率；German Credit 上所有预算均提升，但所有方法仍低于多数类基线率。在 Breast Cancer、Heart Disease、Optdigits 与 Cora 上原始 Poisson 更优，且在两个最大图上差距随标签预算增加而缩小。消融显示增益归因于对角稳定化而非变分训练。

Conclusion: IPQSSL 提供了 Poisson 图学习与量子线性代数之间资源透明的衔接，并经验性地明确刻画了对角正则化产生收益的边界条件；其核心贡献是稳定化机制与可块编码的 Hermitian 形式，而非变分电路本身。

Abstract: We introduce IPQSSL, a quantum-informed regularized Poisson framework for graph-based semi-supervised classification under scarce labels. Local graph feature contexts are QR factorized, lifted to unitary encodings, and processed by a shared variational circuit whose measurements yield node wise diagonal weights that stabilize a symmetrized Poisson system. We establish positive definiteness of the symmetrized, regularized operator under explicit spectral conditions and derive a Hermitian, block encoding compatible formulation that separates the classical NISQ oriented implementation from a future fault-tolerant pathway. Across seven benchmarks spanning tabular data and a citation network, the effect of regularization is dataset- and budget-dependent: IPQSSL improves over the Poisson baselines on Iris and Wine at all but the single-label budget, reaching 0.946 accuracy on Iris at ten labels per class and at every budget on German Credit, where, however, all methods remain below the majority class rate. Original Poisson remains preferable on Breast Cancer, Heart Disease, Optdigits, and Cora, with the deficit narrowing as the label budget grows on the two largest graphs. Ablations attribute the gains to diagonal stabilization rather than variational training. The result is a resource transparent bridge between Poisson graph learning and quantum linear algebra, with an explicit empirical delineation of when diagonal regularization helps.

</details>


### [203] [Circular Predictive Coding Unlocks Effective Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27610)
*Tommaso Salvatori,Matteo Forasassi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出循环预测编码网络（circular PC networks），通过能量最小化本身获取深度而非堆叠层，理论证明其平衡点收敛等价于深度平衡模型的定点迭代，并在图像分类、数独约束求解与自回归语言建模上取得有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测编码网络虽能通过能量最小化完成信用分配并支持任意拓扑，但此前主要用于训练前馈架构；当模型过深时，误差信号在多层传播中变得不稳定或消失，导致性能无法匹敌反向传播。需要一种不依赖层数堆叠即可获得有效深度的方案。

Method: 提出循环网络（circular networks），其深度直接来源于能量最小化过程而非堆叠层。理论上证明循环预测编码网络能量泛函的平衡点收敛可逼近深度平衡模型（DEQ）的定点迭代过程；实验上构建循环预测编码模型，并在数独任务中使用嵌套能量最小化循环，进一步将循环结构引入Transformer以支持自回归语言建模。
