Result: （i）在同规模下优于其他预测编码模型，以显著更少参数量达到与SOTA预测编码ResNet和VGG相当的图像分类精度；（ii）可求解需多步迭代推理的约束满足问题，前馈模型完全无法胜任，在Sudoku-hard上通过嵌套能量最小化循环达到99.3%测试准确率；（iii）在TinyStories和WikiText-103上训练循环Transformer，实现预测编码模型此前难以企及的自回归语言建模。

Conclusion: 循环性本身即可解锁有效深度，使预测编码网络在参数效率、迭代推理与序列建模上具备优势；该结果对物理神经网络具有直接意义，即可以固定尺寸电路上的递归换取计算深度。

Abstract: Predictive coding networks perform credit assignment through an energy minimization process. Despite their ability to train models with arbitrary network topologies, they have mostly been used to train feedforward architectures to match the performance of backpropagation. However, they fail to do so when the models become too deep, because error signals become unstable or vanish as they travel through many layers. We address this limitation with circular networks, which get their depth directly from the energy minimization, rather than from stacking layers. Formally, we prove that convergence to an equilibrium of the energy functional of a circular predictive coding network approximates the fixed-point iteration process of a deep equilibrium model, showing that circularity alone suffices to unlock effective depth. Empirically, we show that circular models (i) outperform other predictive coding models of the same size on image classification tasks, matching the accuracy of state-of-the-art predictive coding ResNets and VGG networks despite having substantially fewer parameters; (ii) solve constraint satisfaction problems that require many steps of iterative reasoning, a task not feasible with feedforward models at all, reaching 99.3% test accuracy on Sudoku-hard via nested energy minimization loops; and (iii) scale to autoregressive language modeling by training a circular transformer on TinyStories and WikiText-103, before out of reach for predictive coding models. These results have direct implications for physical neural networks, where computational depth can be traded for recursion on a fixed-size circuit.

</details>


### [204] [Potential emergence in LLM agents: a statistical physics perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27618)
*Zhuo-Yang Song,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出把"智能体状态"作为中间变量，用于衡量和预测LLM智能体后训练过程；并从统计物理视角引入配分函数刻画全局势能与状态转移，在Qwen3.5-9B的两个任务上验证了后训练中势能结构增强、奖励与状态熵协同变化的一致性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多从人类偏好或语料统计角度理解LLM智能体行为，但缺乏能够量化并预测后训练如何组织智能体行为的中间刻画；作者希望寻找一种中介变量，使后训练效果既可测量又可预测。

Method: 提出以智能体状态（agent states）作为中间变量；从统计物理视角研究支配LLM智能体推理中状态转移的全局势能涌现；引入配分函数对KL正则化的理想转移分布建模，并跟踪势能结构、平均奖励与状态熵这三类量。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B的两个任务（安全测试选择、物理论证审计）上，观测到后训练过程中势能结构按照框架预测的方式增强，同时奖励与状态熵的联合变化提供了内部一致性证据。

Conclusion: 状态为"后训练如何组织智能体行为"提供了定量描述方式，配分函数/势能框架可作为分析与预测后训练效果的实用工具。

Abstract: There has been significant recent interest in understanding the behaviour of LLM agents from the perspective of human preferences or corpus statistics. In this paper we propose agent states as intermediate variables through which post-training can be measured and predicted. From a statistical physics perspective, we study the emergence of a global potential which governs the transition of state in LLM agent reasoning. We introduce a partition function to model the ideal KL-regularised transition distribution and track potential structure, mean reward and state entropy. We demonstrate our approach in two Qwen3.5-9B tasks: safety-test selection and physics-reasoning auditing. We observe that the potential structure strengthens during post-training as predicted from our framework, while joint changes in reward and state entropy provide consistency evidence. States thus provide a quantitative description of how post-training organises agent behaviour.

</details>


### [205] [Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Subchunk Rollouts in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27645)
*Nan Tang,Han-Tao Zhang,Jing-Cheng Pang,Hao-Nan Wang,Hao-Xiang Ren,Yi-Fei Sheng,Jun-Yin Ye,Tian Xu,Fan-Ming Luo,Hao-Xin Lin,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LS-MBPO，一种轻量级残差模型强化学习框架，用隐空间世界模型改进预训练VLA策略，在LIBERO、RoboTwin及真实ALOHA机器人上取得更优任务性能与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）虽能通过想象经验提升预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，但多依赖大规模图像/视频生成世界模型，计算开销巨大。隐空间世界模型更高效，却面临两大难题：递归预测中的误差累积，以及与VLA视觉输入接口不兼容。

Method: 提出LS-MBPO轻量级残差MBRL框架，包含三项技术：(1) 分层强制（Hierarchical Forcing），将稠密因果监督与自回归反馈结合，缓解复合预测误差；(2) 隐空间子块推演（Latent Subchunk Rollouts），利用冻结VLA策略输出的动作块，配合隐空间残差策略做逐子块精炼，弥合隐空间想象与VLA策略执行间的差距；(3) 进度条件分支起点课程（Progress-Conditioned Branch-Start Curriculum），使推演起点由任务后期逐步前移，从而从短程想象推演中有效学习。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin基准以及真实ALOHA机器人上的大量实验表明，LS-MBPO相较现有方法具有更优的任务性能与训练效率。

Conclusion: 以隐空间世界模型为核心的轻量级残差MBRL框架，可在低计算开销下有效增强预训练VLA策略，验证了隐空间想象与VLA执行接口结合的可行性。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) can improve pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies through imagined experiences. However, existing VLA-MBRL methods primarily rely on large-scale image or video generation world models, incurring large computational overhead. Latent world models offer a more efficient alternative but face two challenges: compounding errors during recursive prediction and incompatibility with VLAs' visual input interfaces. In this work, we propose LS-MBPO, a lightweight residual MBRL framework that leverages latent world models to improve upon pretrained VLAs. LS-MBPO introduces two key techniques: (1) Hierarchical Forcing, which combines dense causal supervision with autoregressive feedback to mitigate compounding prediction errors; and (2) Latent Subchunk Rollouts, which leverage action chunks from a frozen VLA policy and a latent residual policy for successive subchunk refinement, bridging the gap between latent imagination and VLA policy execution. We further introduce a Progress-Conditioned Branch-Start Curriculum that progressively shifts rollout starting points from later to earlier task stages, enabling effective policy learning from short imagined rollouts. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and a real-world ALOHA robot show that LS-MBPO achieves superior task performance and training efficiency compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [206] [Late-Time Dynamics of Flow Matching: Distance Geometry and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27653)
*Umberto Biccari,Junqing Huang,Kang Liu,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用线性插值（独立高斯与经验样本之间）的流匹配模型，刻画有限训练集的几何如何决定终末动力学与样本概率分配，证明精确轨迹在晚期重启后以指数小误差逼近“向最近样本指数收缩”的显式路径，并给出时间一致、关于重启噪声尺度的平方根界（在双点例中为紧），进而提出两种受几何启发的学习方法并以MNIST实验说明各误差项的独立作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有限训练集情形，核心问题是：训练集几何如何塑造生成过程的终末动力学（terminal dynamics）以及各样本被赋予的概率。作者希望将流匹配与score-based diffusion在共同极限几何下统一起来，并把渐近分析转化为可指导模型设计与评估的工具。

Method: 1）建模：采用独立高斯先验与经验样本间的线性插值流匹配；以到训练集的平方距离构造显式收缩路径（指向最近样本，指数收缩）。2）理论刻画：证明从指定晚期状态重启的精确轨迹，在所有经过时间上、在远离Voronoi界面的紧集上一致逼近参考路径，误差关于重启噪声尺度平方的倒数指数小；界面附近的初始质量控制精确律与参考律之间的二次Wasserstein误差；对高斯重启律与固定高斯管重启律给出关于噪声尺度一致于时间的平方根速率，并用双点例说明其紧性；在并列（tie）点用Clarke次微分刻画重标速度场的图形极限。3）方法：提出(a)带值与梯度监督的距离势拟合，(b)沿显式收缩路径的速度拟合；并用传输估计将误差分解为初始化失配、速度误差、收缩不完全与端点分配四项。

Result: 获得指数小的逐轨迹一致逼近误差、由界面初始质量控制的二次Wasserstein界，以及关于重启噪声尺度平方根、时间一致的收敛速率（双点例显示该速率一般不可改进）；Clarke次微分给出tie点处重标速度场图形极限的刻画；MNIST实验显示支撑邻近性、最近站点保持性与特征保真度三者的不同作用，传输估计可分离各误差来源。

Conclusion: 有限训练集下的流匹配终末行为由数据集的Voronoi几何（含界面质量分配）主导，并可与score-based diffusion通过共同极限几何相连接；该渐近分析既能导出有紧性保证的定量误差界，也能直接指导学习目标的选择与生成模型的评估指标设计。

Abstract: Generative models transform simple random inputs into samples that resemble observed data. For a finite training set, a central question is how its geometry shapes the terminal dynamics and the probabilities assigned to individual samples. We address this question for flow matching with linear interpolation between independent Gaussian and empirical samples. The squared distance to the training set generates explicit paths contracting exponentially toward the nearest sample. We prove that exact trajectories restarted at prescribed late-time states approximate these paths uniformly over all elapsed times and compact sets of starting states away from Voronoi interfaces, with an error exponentially small in the inverse squared restart noise scale. The initial mass near interfaces controls the quadratic Wasserstein error between the exact and reference laws. For Gaussian and fixed Gaussian-tube restart laws, this gives a square-root rate in the restart noise scale, uniform over time, which a two-site example shows to be sharp in general. At ties, the Clarke subdifferential characterizes the graphical limit of the rescaled velocity field. This geometry motivates two learning methods: fitting the distance potential with value and gradient supervision, and fitting velocities along explicit contraction paths. A transport estimate separates initialization mismatch, velocity error, incomplete contraction, and endpoint allocation. MNIST experiments illustrate the distinct roles of support proximity, nearest-site retention, and feature fidelity. These results connect flow matching and score-based diffusion through a common limiting geometry and show how asymptotic analysis can inform the design and evaluation of generative models.

</details>


### [207] [Beyond Sample Hardness: Adaptive Data Selection through Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27656)
*Hai Gan,Suorong Yang,Guang Li,Baile Xu,Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LeDAS：一个端到端、感知学习动态的数据选择框架，联合刻画样本硬度、学习进度与退化三类信号，并用上下文老虎机自适应融合，在视觉与语言任务上以50%选择率匹配或超越全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据选择可降低训练成本，但现有方法过度依赖样本硬度（sample hardness），忽略样本学习动态的差异，导致选择次优。

Method: 1) 用指数移动平均（EMA）跟踪逐样本损失动态，得到三个互补信号：当前学习难度（硬度）、持续学习提升（学习进度）、潜在退化（deterioration）；2) 提出Adaptive Metric Fusion，基于上下文老虎机（contextual bandit）机制，依据训练反馈动态调整各信号相对权重；3) 引入有界陈旧度补偿（bounded staleness compensation），对长期未被选中的样本提升选择优先级；整体为端到端框架，额外计算开销边际化。

Result: 在多种视觉与语言任务上验证有效性与通用性；相较SOTA基线持续取得更优性能，在ImageNet-1K与LLM监督微调中于50%选择率下匹配或超过全量数据训练；模块化、任务无关设计可集成互补选择信号，对噪声与损坏数据具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以学习动态为核心的多信号自适应融合优于单一硬度驱动的选择策略，LeDAS具备高效、通用、可扩展的数据选择能力。

Abstract: Data selection has emerged as an effective approach to reducing the model training costs. However, most existing methods often rely heavily on sample hardness, which may overlook differences in sample learning dynamics and lead to suboptimal sample selection. In this paper, we propose LeDAS, an end-to-end Learning Dynamics-Aware Selection framework for dynamic data selection, which jointly characterizes sample importance through hardness, learning progress, and deterioration, enabling adaptive sample prioritization throughout training with marginal additional computational overhead. Specifically, we track per-sample loss dynamics using exponential moving averages (EMA) to obtain three complementary signals that capture current learning difficulty, ongoing learning improvements, and potential degradation. To accommodate evolving training dynamics, we introduce Adaptive Metric Fusion, a contextual bandit-based mechanism that dynamically adjusts the relative importance of these signals based on training feedback. Furthermore, a bounded staleness compensation mechanism accounts for historical selection patterns by increasing the selection priority of samples that have not been selected for extended periods. Extensive experiments across diverse vision and language tasks demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of LeDAS. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, LeDAS consistently achieves superior performance, matching or surpassing full-dataset training at a 50\% selection ratio on ImageNet-1K and in LLM supervised fine-tuning. Moreover, its modular and task-agnostic design enables the integration of complementary selection signals, demonstrating strong robustness to noisy and corrupted datasets.

</details>


### [208] [SMSD: A Semantic Misinformation Spreader Detection Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27661)
*Zeeshan Bin Siddique,Michael Bewong,Md Geaur Rahman,Sajal Halder,Selasi Kwashie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMSD多模态框架，将虚假信息传播者检测建模为时序、以用户为中心、行为驱动的学习问题，在FibVid COVID-19基准上显著提升召回率、F1与MCC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体虚假信息传播者会放大错误叙事，早期检测至关重要；现有方法忽视用户行为的动态演化、忽略用户立场而引入语义噪声、且难以有效处理新出现的用户。

Method: SMSD采用多模态架构，融合用户级、网络级与会话传播三类特征；引入立场感知标注策略，将用户区分为支持、反对、质疑与中立四类；并提出月度分位数分析以模拟真实且演化的用户参与模式。

Result: 在FibVid COVID-19基准数据集上，立场感知标注下召回率提升45.0%、F1提升49.5%、MCC提升74.6%；阈值标注下分别提升17.7%、7.4%、11.4%，且在两种设置下均稳定优于所有基线。

Conclusion: SMSD通过显式建模用户立场与行为演化，有效缓解语义噪声与新用户冷启动问题，验证了多模态特征与时序参与模拟对传播者早期检测的有效性；后续需在更多数据集与更长时间跨度上进一步验证泛化性。

Abstract: Misinformation spreaders on social media significantly amplify false narratives, making early detection critical to mitigate their spread. Existing approaches often overlook evolving user behavior, introduce semantic noise by ignoring user stance, and fail to handle newly emerging users effectively. To address these limitations, we propose a \underline{S}emantic \underline{M}isinformation \underline{S}preader \underline{D}etection approach (SMSD) that models misinformation propagation as a temporal, user-centric, and behavior-driven learning problem. SMSD adopts a multimodal architecture that incorporates three categories of features: user-level, network-level, and conversation propagation. A stance-aware labeling strategy is also introduced to differentiate supportive, opposing, questioning, and neutral users towards misinformation claims. Additionally, a monthly quantile analysis is introduced to simulate realistic, evolving user participation. Initial experimental results on the FibVid COVID-19 benchmark dataset demonstrate that SMSD improves recall by 45.0\%, F1 by 49.5\%, and MCC by 74.6\% under stance-aware labeling and by 17.7\%, 7.4\%, and 11.4\%, respectively, under threshold-based labeling. It consistently outperforms all baselines in both settings.

</details>


### [209] [Geometrically Constrained Learning II: Agnostic Proper Learning of Lipschitz Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27700)
*Jane Lange,Cassandra Marcussen,Arsen Vasilyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上高斯分布下，给出 Lipschitz 函数的 proper learner：时间复杂度、样本复杂度与表示规模均为 $(d+1)^{\mathrm{poly}(\lambda/\varepsilon)}$；在已知标签方差界时可推广为 agnostic 学习，复杂度 $(d+1)^{\mathrm{poly}(V\lambda/\varepsilon)}$，误差为 $\mathsf{opt}+\varepsilon$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Lipschitz 函数学习多依赖 improper 方法或仅给出近似 Lipschitz 的假设，缺乏在 Gaussian 分布下的真 proper learner。本文希望学习器输出的假设本身全局 $\lambda$-Lipschitz，同时在样本、时间与表示上仅以 $d$ 的多项式指数、对精度 $\varepsilon$ 与 Lipschitz 常数 $\lambda$ 呈多项式依赖，并在标签尺度受控时达到 agnostic 保证。

Method: 方法分为两步：(1) 在凸梯度约束下求解 $L_1$ 多项式回归，得到一个多项式近似；(2) 对该多项式进行"局部纠正"：先平滑并截断其值域，再与自身的下 Lipschitz 包络（canonical Lipschitz correction）比较。关键技巧是用高斯采样代理量替换下包络中"到次水平集的距离"，使得纠正算子可由样本局部、稳定地估计，从而保证输出严格全局 Lipschitz 且误差可控。

Result: 对 $\lambda$-Lipschitz 标签，学习器返回全局 $\lambda$-Lipschitz 的 $h$，绝对损失或平方损失不超过 $\varepsilon$，样本复杂度、运行时间与表示规模均为 $(d+1)^{\mathrm{poly}(\lambda/\varepsilon)}$。Agnostic 情形下，标签方差 $\le V^2$（$V\ge 1$）时复杂度为 $(d+1)^{\mathrm{poly}(V\lambda/\varepsilon)}$，误差为 $\mathsf{opt}+\varepsilon$；并指出对标签尺度的某种控制是信息论上必要的。

Conclusion: 该工作首次（在所述设定下）给出高斯分布下 Lipschitz 函数的高效 proper learner，通过凸梯度约束回归加局部 Lipschitz 纠正的组合实现，并自然扩展到 agnostic 情形；标签尺度的可控性是实现该保证的本质前提。

Abstract: We give a proper learner for Lipschitz functions over the Gaussian distribution on $\mathbb{R}^d$. Given random Gaussian examples labeled by a $λ$-Lipschitz function, $λ\ge 1$, the learner returns a function $h:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ that is globally $λ$-Lipschitz and has loss (absolute or squared) at most $\varepsilon$. The sample complexity, running time, and representation size are \[
  (d+1)^{\mathsf{poly}(λ/\varepsilon)}. \] The learning algorithm can be made \emph{agnostic}, given a known bound on the variance of the label marginal distribution. Some known control of label scale is information-theoretically necessary. For arbitrary labels with variance at most $V^2 \ge 1$, the complexity is \[
  (d+1)^{\mathsf{poly}(Vλ/\varepsilon)}, \] and the error is $\mathsf{opt} + \varepsilon$, where $\mathsf{opt}$ is the minimal error among Lipschitz approximators.
  Our approach combines $L_1$ polynomial regression under a convex gradient constraint with local correction of the Lipschitz property. The learner finds a polynomial that, after smoothing and clipping its range, is very close to a canonical Lipschitz correction of itself, the lower Lipschitz envelope. Our local corrector modifies the lower envelope by replacing distances to sublevel sets with a Gaussian sampling proxy, allowing the construction to be locally and stably estimated from samples.

</details>


### [210] [Solving the pre-image problem in kernel PCA: The reproducing property is all you need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27718)
*Joel-Pascal Ntwali N'konzi,Stefan Klus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文利用RKHS的再生性质，为核PCA导出一个可证明一致的显式重构映射（pre-image map），并扩展到RFF近似，兼顾计算效率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核PCA的投影映射已被充分理解，但从RKHS回到输入空间的重构（pre-image问题）长期缺乏良好定义：现有方法要么依赖数值不稳定的非线性优化且对初始化敏感，要么需要额外训练一个监督学习模型而增加开销；更重要的是，多数方法在采用线性核时无法退化回标准PCA的重构映射，缺失了自然应成立的一致性。

Method: 基于RKHS再生性质（reproducing property）解析地推导核PCA的重构映射；推导仅依赖温和假设——RKHS包含线性函数或核为universal核（标准核均满足）；并进一步引入"RFF再生性质"概念，将该构造推广到随机傅里叶特征（RFF）近似下的核PCA。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的数值实验表明：所提方法在多种核选择下与当前最优方法性能相当，计算效率更高，且在这些基线方法严重失效的场景中仍保持稳定一致的表现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了闭式、可证明一致的核PCA重构映射，消除了对优化或额外监督学习的依赖，为核PCA及RFF近似提供了实用且理论完备的pre-image解决方案。

Abstract: Kernel PCA extends PCA to nonlinear dimensionality reduction by implicitly mapping data into a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) via the kernel trick, with applications ranging from image denoising to out-of-distribution detection. While its projection map is well understood, appropriately defining the associated reconstruction map is a longstanding challenge since projecting back from the RKHS to the input space is non-trivial. This is known as the pre-image problem. Existing approaches have tackled this problem by either relying on nonlinear optimization, which is prone to numerical instabilities and sensitive to initialization, or by solving a separate supervised learning problem, incurring additional computational overhead. Furthermore, although the projection map of kernel PCA reduces to that of standard PCA when a linear kernel is used, most of these approaches are not guaranteed to recover PCA's reconstruction map in this case, a consistency property that one would naturally expect to hold. In this paper, we leverage the reproducing property of the RKHS to derive a reconstruction map for kernel PCA that is provably consistent. Our derivations rely on the mild assumption that the RKHS either contains linear functions or the kernel is universal, which holds for standard kernels. We further extend the method to the random Fourier features (RFFs) approximation of kernel PCA by introducing a corresponding notion of RFF reproducing property. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method performs comparably with state-of-the-art methods across kernel choices, while remaining more computationally efficient and maintaining consistent performance in settings where these baseline methods drastically fail.

</details>


### [211] [Beyond Math, Code, and Logic: Do Reasoning Models Transfer to Scientific Argumentation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27722)
*Buse Sibel Korkmaz,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统评测语言模型在科学论证（论证挖掘、反驳生成、篇章级推理等七项任务）上的能力，发现即便是前沿通用推理模型也存在明显能力缺口，并提出显式"脚手架式"论证推理训练框架，使小模型接近或超越大型专有系统并具备跨场景泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学话语依赖论证性推理（识别主张、评估证据、构建连贯回应）。现有具备推理能力的语言模型虽在数学、编程、逻辑基准上表现强劲，但其科学论证能力尚不清楚，需要系统性评估以揭示能力边界与改进路径。

Method: 基于研究论文、同行评审意见与基金申请书数据，构建覆盖论证挖掘、反驳生成与篇章级推理的七项任务评测体系；在揭示缺口后，提出一种显式搭建（scaffold）模型论证推理过程的训练框架，对模型进行论证感知的推理训练。

Result: 前沿通用推理模型在领域特定论证任务上表现不佳，暴露出基础性能力缺口；经该训练框架得到的紧凑模型在科学论证任务上接近或超过规模更大的专有系统，并能泛化到未见过的对话场景，体现出超出任务特定监督的推理迁移。

Conclusion: 有效的科学论证能力并非规模带来的涌现属性，而需显式的推理感知训练；这为构建能实质性参与科学话语的AI系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scientific discourse depends on argumentative reasoning: identifying claims, evaluating evidence, and constructing coherent responses. While recent advances in reasoning-capable language models have demonstrated strong performance on mathematical, coding, and logical benchmarks, their ability to engage in scientific argumentation remains unclear. We present the first systematic evaluation of language models across seven tasks spanning argument mining, rebuttal generation, and discourse-level reasoning using research papers, peer reviews, and grant proposals. Our study reveals that even frontier models with strong general reasoning skills struggle with domain-specific argumentative tasks, highlighting a fundamental capability gap. To address this, we introduce a training framework that explicitly scaffolds the argumentative reasoning process in language models, substantially improving their competence in scientific discourse. The resulting compact models approach or exceed the performance of much larger proprietary systems and generalize to unseen conversational settings, demonstrating reasoning transfer beyond task-specific supervision. These findings underscore that effective scientific argumentation is not an emergent property of scale, but requires explicit reasoning-aware training, and they point toward practical pathways for building AI systems that can contribute meaningfully to scientific discourse.

</details>


### [212] [A Switching Mechanism for Selecting Environment-Adaptive Personalized Gains in Hearing Aids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27726)
*Nafisa Zarrin Tasnim,Nasser Kehtarnavaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向助听器的自动切换机制：通过单类语音活动检测与一致性检验排除前景语音干扰，结合贝叶斯变点检测与历史环境记忆的在线发现方法，在无需预设环境数量的情况下识别环境变化、复现回归环境并发现新环境，从而为当前背景音频环境选择个性化增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化放大增益较处方增益更受助听器使用者偏好，但偏好设置随背景音频环境变化，因此需要能根据当前背景环境自动选择个性化增益的切换系统。

Method: 1) 设计单类语音活动检测器，从含噪语音中识别语音活动，避免前景语音干扰背景环境分析；2) 进行一致性检验以区分前景语音与背景语音，同时保留对助听器用户理解困难的背景babble作为关注环境；3) 对无前景语音的音频段采用在线发现方法，融合贝叶斯变点检测与已遇环境记忆，以检测变化、识别回归环境并发现新环境，且无需预设环境数量。

Result: 在覆盖36种背景噪声类型的8组语音/背景语料对上，三随机种子平均宏平均F1为85.53%–91.98%；发现方法在两族测试流上的调整Rand指数分别为0.91和0.96；在结合四种常见背景音频环境的同录音回归条件中，切换机制在105次环境变化后均选择正确的个性化增益配置，每次回归均召回已存增益配置，且未出现切换到错误增益配置的情况。

Conclusion: 该切换机制能够可靠地感知背景环境变化并匹配个性化增益配置，验证了单类语音活动检测、一致性检验与在线环境发现在助听器个性化增益自动切换中的有效性。

Abstract: Personalized amplification gains for hearing aids have been shown to be preferred over prescriptive gains. Since the preferred settings can vary depending on the background audio environment, this paper presents a switching mechanism or system that automatically selects personalized gains for a current background audio environment. To prevent foreground speech from interfering with background environment analysis, a one-class voice activity detector is designed to identify speech activity from speech mixed with noise. A consistency check is conducted to enable distinguishing foreground speech from background speech, preserving babble as a background environment of interest because hearing aid users often struggle to understand speech in background babble. Sound segments without foreground speech are then analyzed by an online discovery method combining Bayesian change-point detection with a memory of previously encountered environments. This discovery method detects changes, recognizes returning environments, and discovers new ones without a predefined number of environments. Experimental results show that three-seed mean macro-averaged F1 scores of 85.53%-91.98% across eight speech/background-corpus pairs covering 36 background noise types were achieved. Furthermore, the discovery method achieved adjusted Rand indices of 0.91 and 0.96 on two families of test streams. In the same-recording-return condition of audio streams combining speech with four background audio environments often encountered by hearing aid users, the switching mechanism selected the correct personalized gain profile after 105 environment changes, recalled the stored gain profile at every return, and made no switches to an incorrect gain profile.

</details>


### [213] [DPMA: Learning to Compress Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27759)
*Jon Irureta,Jon Imaz,Philipp Wiesner,Gorka Azkune,Javier Fernandez-Marques,Alex Iacob,Andrej Jovanović,Aizea Lojo,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免重训练的MoE压缩框架DPMA，在全局专家预算下通过梯度下降联合学习"剪枝/合并/吸收"三种压缩操作及逐层专家分配，在4个MoE模型、8个选择题基准、5种压缩率上平均精度最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE虽计算高效，但需存储全部专家，显存开销大，难以部署在资源受限硬件上。现有免重训练压缩方法多局限于"剪枝"或"合并"单一策略，且各层采用统一压缩率，未考虑层间重要性差异。

Method: DPMA为每个专家设置独立可学习系数，使其可被剪枝、参数合并，或将路由贡献"吸收"——把路由权重前传给保留专家。系数与专家分配在全局专家预算约束下经梯度下降联合优化，目标是在校准数据上匹配原模型输出，同时冻结原权重。

Result: 在4个MoE模型、8个多选题基准、5种压缩率上，DPMA在各模型上按基准与压缩率平均的精度均为最优；在75%专家缩减下，每个模型均优于最强基线，最大提升6.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 将剪枝、合并与路由吸收统一为可微的连续优化问题，并配合全局预算下的逐层分配，可在免重训练条件下显著优于单一策略的MoE压缩方法。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer high computational efficiency, but storing all experts requires substantial memory and limits deployment on resource-constrained hardware. Most retraining-free MoE compression methods commit to either pruning or merging experts, often with a uniform compression ratio across layers. We introduce DPMA (Differentiable Prune Merge Absorb), which jointly learns compression operations and layer-wise expert allocation through gradient descent under a global expert budget. Separate learnable coefficients allow DPMA to prune experts, merge their parameters, and absorb their routing contributions by forwarding routing weights to retained experts. These coefficients and expert assignments are optimised to match the original model's outputs on calibration data while its weights remain frozen. Across four MoE models, eight multiple-choice benchmarks, and five compression ratios, DPMA achieves the highest accuracy averaged over benchmarks and compression ratios on each model among the evaluated methods. At $75\%$ expert reduction, it outperforms the strongest baseline on every model, with gains of up to $6.7$ percentage points.

</details>


### [214] [$\mathtt{DistP}$: Scaling Local-Update Training with Zero-Shot Hyperparameter Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27808)
*Andrej Jovanović,Alex Iacob,Philipp Wiesner,Christophe Roux,Max Zimmer,Lorenzo Sani,Sebastian Pokutta,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DistP理论框架，将DDP中的零样本超参数迁移规则扩展到local-update训练，支持跨宽度/深度的学习率迁移并给出批大小扩展规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: local-update（本地更新）方法比DDP更通信高效，但引入了额外超参数需要昂贵调参；DDP可通过零样本超参数迁移复用小模型调好的设置，而该迁移对local-update训练是否适用尚不清楚。

Method: 构建DistP理论框架，将既有迁移规则从DDP推广到不同worker数、本地步数与外层优化器的local-update训练。外层为averaging时，推导出支持宽度与深度方向的学习率迁移及内层学习率跨同步间隔迁移的规则，并分析全局批大小增大时的两种机制：本地批增大采用平方根缩放，增加worker时采用固定参考学习率；进一步将宽度/深度迁移推广到其他外层优化器（如Adam），并分析带动量外层优化器时内层—外层学习率需联合调参的情形。

Result: 在823M参数语言模型实验中，DistP在更大规模下无需重新调参即相较基线降低8.1%验证损失、提升10%平均下游准确率；拟合的缩放律表明该优势随计算预算增长而扩大。

Conclusion: DistP为通信高效的local-update训练提供了可迁移的超参数规则，支持稳定扩展到Adam等外层优化器，并在大规模下展现出随计算预算增长的优势。

Abstract: Local-update methods offer a communication-efficient alternative to Distributed Data Parallelism ($\mathtt{DDP}$), but introduce additional hyperparameters that require costly tuning. For $\mathtt{DDP}$, zero-shot hyperparameter transfer reduces this cost by reusing settings tuned on smaller models, but its applicability to local-update training remains unclear. We propose $\mathtt{DistP}$, a theoretical framework extending existing transfer rules from $\mathtt{DDP}$ to local-update training with varying worker counts, local steps, and outer optimizers. With averaging as the outer optimizer, $\mathtt{DistP}$ supports learning-rate transfer across width and depth while improving model quality. The tuned inner learning rate also directly transfers across synchronization intervals, but follows two regimes when increasing the global batch size: square-root learning-rate scaling for larger local batches and a fixed reference rate for additional workers. We then generalize width and depth transfer to other outer optimizers, enabling stable scaling with $\mathtt{Adam}$. However, with momentum in the outer optimizer, changing batch size or local steps can require inner--outer learning-rate tuning, consistent with prior work. In language-modeling experiments at $823$M parameters, $\mathtt{DistP}$ reduces validation loss by $8.1\%$ and improves mean downstream accuracy by $10\%$ relative to baselines without retuning at the larger scale. Fitted scaling laws suggest that this advantage grows with the compute budget, reinforcing the benefits of $\mathtt{DistP}$ for communication-efficient training.

</details>


### [215] [Mixture of Latent Recursions for Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27823)
*Weixun Luo,Ye Mao,Ranran Huang,Junpeng Jing,Krystian Mikolajczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoLR：通过对低秩潜表示进行自适应递归精炼，以近乎零的额外参数提升LoRA的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA采用固定低秩结构做任务适配，其表达能力通常需靠扩大或增加可学习模块来提升，带来成比例的参数开销。

Method: 递归地用共享变换精炼每个输入的下投影潜表示；轻量阈值路由器逐token独立决定是否继续精炼；动态调整阈值以鼓励各递归深度的均衡利用。

Result: 在多种语言与视觉任务上稳定优于代表性LoRA变体，且相较LoRA参数开销可忽略；分析显示MoLR学到输入相关的递归深度分配，并支持无需重训的推理期算力-性能可控权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应递归为参数高效微调提供了新的表达力扩展维度，兼顾参数效率与推理灵活性。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) performs task-specific adaptation using a fixed low-rank architecture, whose expressiveness is typically increased by enlarging or adding learnable modules with a proportional parameter overhead. We introduce Mixture of Latent Recursions (MoLR), an adaptive recursion framework that enhances adaptation expressiveness while preserving parameter efficiency. MoLR recursively refines the down-projected latent representation of each input using a shared transformation. A lightweight threshold-based router independently determines whether each token should undergo further refinement, while dynamically adjusted thresholds encourage balanced utilization across recursion depths. Extensive experiments across diverse language and vision tasks demonstrate that MoLR consistently outperforms representative LoRA variants with negligible parameter overhead over LoRA. Further analyses show that MoLR learns input-dependent recursion-depth allocation and enables a controllable inference-time compute-performance trade-off without requiring retraining. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [216] [Graph Signal Generation via Graph-Aware Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27826)
*Vimal K B,Sergio Rozada,Antonio G. Marques,Elvin Isufi,Hadi J. Rad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图感知扩散模型GAD，将前向过程改为图上的热扩散并由高斯噪声驱动；在谱域中每个频率为标量高斯信道，进而提出MASS（每个图频率独立扩散率、统一SNR）以均衡去噪误差并改善反向SDE刚性，在推荐、交通插补与预测任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型对图节点信号建模时，前向噪声在各节点间独立且与图结构无关，忽略了节点间相关性，导致对图结构化数据的分布学习不充分、生成/插补效率低。

Method: 1) 设计前向过程为图上的热扩散，由高斯噪声驱动，使噪声传播依赖图拓扑；2) 在图傅里叶基下，前向转移退化为各频率独立的标量高斯信道；3) 共享扩散率会使高频衰减快于低频，故提出Mode-Adaptive SNR Scheduling (MASS)，为每个图频率分配独立扩散率并使各频率SNR保持一致；4) 对图平稳信号证明MASS可使最优线性去噪器的归一化误差在各频率上相等；5) 以（移位）拉普拉斯条件数刻画反向SDE刚性，给出MASS的对数上界与共享扩散率的线性下界。

Result: GAD+MASS在推荐准确率上提升5–19%；交通插补RMSE最多降低7.8%，且反向步数减半；预测任务仅用3步反向过程即改善MAE与RMSE。

Conclusion: 将前向扩散与图结构耦合、并在谱域进行频率自适应SNR调度，可同时提升图信号生成式建模的精度与采样效率；理论与实验共同表明MASS在误差均衡与降低反向SDE刚性方面优于共享扩散率方案。

Abstract: Many signals are defined on the nodes of a graph, such as traffic flow at road sensors or user purchases of related products. They are often partly observed, and learning their distribution enables imputing missing values and forecasting future ones. Diffusion models learn such distributions, but their forward noise is independent across nodes and does not depend on the graph. We introduce graph-aware diffusion (GAD), whose forward process is a heat diffusion on the graph driven by Gaussian noise. In the graph Fourier basis, the forward transition is a scalar Gaussian channel at each frequency. With a shared diffusion rate, high graph frequencies are attenuated faster than low ones. We propose Mode-Adaptive SNR Scheduling (MASS), which assigns each graph frequency its own diffusion rate and keeps all of them at the same SNR. For graph-stationary signals, MASS equalizes the normalized error of the optimal linear denoiser across frequencies. We bound reverse-SDE stiffness as a function of the (shifted) Laplacian's condition number, with a logarithmic upper bound for MASS and a linear lower bound for shared rates. GAD with MASS improves recommendation accuracy by 5-19%, reduces traffic-imputation RMSE by up to 7.8% with half as many reverse steps, and improves forecasting MAE and RMSE using only 3 reverse steps.

</details>


### [217] [DistDNA: Language Model Fingerprinting under Stochastic Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27831)
*Lifan Hu,Qian Yu,Zhaomin Wu,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DistDNA：用提示条件下的响应分布刻画模型，以逐提示 MMD 比较指纹，在多个解码设置下无需训练分类器即可识别模型身份，115 个 checkpoint 上识别准确率较 LLM DNA 提升 10.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为指纹（如 LLM DNA）对随机解码（未知温度与 top-p）带来的响应变异刻画不足；且解码设置变化造成的响应差异可能与换 checkpoint 相当，单一指纹距离不足以判定模型身份，通常需训练联合分类器。

Method: 将模型表示为提示条件响应分布，用逐提示最大均值差异（MMD）度量模型间距离；对被测模型仅采样少量响应估计分布，并与多个解码设置下采集的已知 checkpoint 参考指纹比较，取最近者完成分配，不训练分类器；理论分析有限采样对期望平方指纹距离引入与方差相关的项，在等响应数下以 O(1/R) 衰减（R 为每提示响应数）。

Result: 在 115 个模型 checkpoint 条目上的实验表明，DistDNA 相比最强基线 LLM DNA 识别准确率提升 10.36%。

Conclusion: 基于响应分布的指纹表示配合多解码设置参考集与最近邻分配，可在随机解码下稳健识别模型身份，优于确定性指纹表示，且无需分类器训练。

Abstract: Behavioral fingerprints identify a large language model (LLM) from its responses to a shared set of prompts, enabling applications such as verifying whether a deployed model corresponds to the checkpoint it claims to be. Deployed models, however, are commonly served with stochastic decoding, under which the same model may produce different responses to an identical prompt, depending on an unknown temperature and top-$p$ setting. Existing representations such as LLM DNA provide limited treatment of this variation. A natural remedy is to characterize models by their response distributions, which, however, raises two challenges. First, each distribution must be estimated from only a small number of sampled responses. Second, a change in decoding setting can alter responses to the same extent as a change of checkpoint. Consequently, fingerprint distances alone provide an incomplete basis for model identity, and identification typically relies on a classifier trained jointly on fingerprints from many checkpoints. We propose DistDNA, which represents a model by its prompt-conditional response distributions and compares models by promptwise maximum mean discrepancy (MMD). The model under examination is assigned to the known checkpoint whose reference fingerprints, collected at several decoding settings, lie nearest to its own, without training a classifier. We show that finite sampling adds variance-dependent terms to the expected squared fingerprint distance, with these terms decreasing as $O(1/R)$ for equal response counts, where $R$ is the number of responses per prompt. Experiments across 115 model checkpoint entries demonstrate that DistDNA improves identification accuracy over the strongest baseline, LLM DNA, by 10.36%.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Visual Fidelity: Comparing Categorical and Continuous World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27870)
*Sarah Parisot,Melanie Fernandez Pradier,David Bignell,Sergio Valcarcel Macua,Katja Hofmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了离散（类别）预测与连续预测，以及下一token生成与整帧迭代生成两种范式对世界模型动力学学习的影响；在可控2D/3D环境中发现类别模型以更少算力获得更高的rollout有效性与长时程表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉真实感不等于动力学忠实性。现有方法（自回归token模型 vs. 整帧flow matching）常将"预测表示形式（类别/连续）"与"生成范式（逐token/整帧）"捆绑在一起，导致难以判断究竟是哪一因素决定了所学动力学的可靠性。作者希望在可控环境中解耦这些变量。

Method: 构建受控的2D与3D环境，在已知动力学的前提下，分别考察类别预测与连续预测在下一token生成与整帧迭代生成下的表现；以动力学有效性（rollout validity）、校准度（calibration）和视觉质量（如FVD、下一帧RGB精度、语义准确率）为指标，并在2D中使用无损视觉编码以排除编码器干扰。

Result: 2D无损编码下，类别模型以更少训练算力取得更高rollout有效性，随机实体尤为明显；类别模型中贪心解码rollout有效性最高，而下一token模型的采样给出校准最好的随机预测；连续模型出现"游戏角色消失"等失效，与复杂环境中的现象一致。3D中类别模型同样以更少算力获得更强长时程性能；即使使用有损tokenization，在语义准确率与FVD上仍具竞争力，尽管下一帧RGB精度较低。

Conclusion: 类别预测是学习世界动力学的一条算力高效路径；视觉保真度指标（如RGB精度）不足以反映动力学正确性，评估应关注rollout有效性与校准。

Abstract: Visual world models can now generate compelling interactive worlds, but visual realism does not guarantee faithful dynamics. We systematically compare how categorical and continuous prediction shape learned dynamics under both next-token and iterative whole-frame generation. These choices are often bundled in approaches such as autoregressive token models and whole-frame flow matching. We design controlled 2D and 3D environments and evaluate dynamics validity, calibration, and visual quality. In 2D with lossless visual encoding, categorical models achieve higher rollout validity with less training compute, particularly for stochastic entities. Among categorical models, greedy decoding produces the highest rollout validity, while sampling from a next-token model yields the best-calibrated stochastic predictions. Failures in continuous models, such as disappearing game characters, echo those seen in more complex environments. Here, known dynamics let us measure their impact. In 3D, categorical models also achieve stronger long-horizon performance with less training compute. Even models with lossy tokenization remain competitive on semantic accuracy and FVD, despite lower next-frame RGB accuracy. Together, these findings make a strong case for categorical prediction as a compute-efficient route to learning world dynamics.

</details>


### [219] [From Gist to Verbatim: The Controllable Path of One Image Through a Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27875)
*Luca Saglietti,Luca Giammanco,Marc Mezard,Chiara Plizzari,Luca Biggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将记忆化重构为控制问题：用可学习条件信号引导冻结扩散模型复现单张目标图像，发现记忆在达到逐像素精确复现之前会先经历"抽象化"阶段，此时样本保持语义主旨而外观多样，该阶段形成的记忆比精确复现阶段更接近文本条件、更适合编辑/单样本识别/检索，因而记忆可作为探测生成模型内在相关性结构的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流研究把生成模型的泛化能力视为核心、把记忆化（memorization）视为失败模式，因而缺乏对"模型如何记住单个数据"这一过程本身的系统刻画。作者反转视角，追问记忆是如何形成、如何随时间演化的，以及记忆化能否反过来成为理解生成模型内部知识组织方式的探针。

Method: 把记忆化建模为控制问题：冻结扩散模型参数，仅学习一个条件信号（conditioning signal），使其将生成过程导向目标图像；在训练全程追踪该记忆的形成轨迹，并分析不同阶段样本在语义内容与外观多样性上的表现，同时与普通文本条件进行比较，测试编辑、单样本识别与检索等下游能力。

Result: 记忆的形成并非一步到位：在收敛到逐字复现（verbatim reproduction）之前，会先经过一个"抽象化"区间——样本保留目标的语义主旨（gist），但在外观上保持多样，将可预测的细节交由生成先验补全；这种语义组织在无标签、无caption、无显式语义目标的情况下自发涌现。在抽象化阶段，学到的记忆在表征上接近普通文本条件，且在图像编辑、单样本识别和检索任务上优于近乎完美重建阶段的记忆。

Conclusion: 记忆化不应仅被视为泛化的失败，而可作为探测生成模型所编码"相关性结构"的手段：它揭示了关于一个数据点哪些信息必须被存储、哪些可由先验知识重建，以及学到的记忆如何在"重建式回忆"与"字面数据存储"之间连续插值。

Abstract: Modern generative models are typically studied for their ability to generalize, while memorization is treated as a failure mode. We invert this perspective and ask how a generative model can recall a single datum. We frame memorization as a control problem: a learned conditioning signal steers a frozen diffusion model toward a target image, and we follow the formation of this memory throughout training. Before converging to exact reproduction (verbatim), memory passes through a regime of abstraction: samples preserve the target's semantic content (gist) while remaining diverse in appearance, leaving predictable detail to the generative prior. This semantic organization emerges despite the absence of labels, captions, or any explicit semantic objective. In the abstraction regime, the learned memory approaches ordinary text conditioning and supports editing, one-shot recognition, and retrieval more effectively than in the near-perfect reconstruction regime. These results suggest that memorization can serve as a probe of the relevance structure encoded by a generative model: what must be stored about a datum, what can be reconstructed from prior knowledge, and how learned memories interpolate between reconstructive recall and literal data storage.

</details>


### [220] [Renormalized Entropy from Symmetries: Simplicity Bias and Neural Heat Death](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27889)
*Vasco Portilheiro,Samuel Liebana,Andrew Saxe,Peter Orbanz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究神经网络参数对称性如何通过Langevin动力学（高斯正则化）下的平衡分布产生隐式偏置，并在零正则化极限下推导出一个新的"熵"项，用以解释对称性对网络所学函数的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数对称性（不改变所表示函数的参数变换）普遍存在于神经网络中，但其对最终所学函数分布的影响尚不清楚。作者希望把对称性带来的隐式偏置从其他因素中分离出来，定量刻画这种对称性诱导的偏好。

Method: 计算高斯正则化下Langevin动力学的表示函数平衡分布；取正则化强度趋于零的极限（并证明在若干情形下零正则化极限与无穷时间极限可交换）；在正温度下引入重整化（renormalization）程序以导出新的熵泛函；分别对线性网络和齐次激活网络作解析推导；并用神经正切核（NTK）视角进行解读。

Result: 低温极限下可恢复已知的有效正则化结果；正温度下重整化给出预测对称性偏好函数的新熵。线性网络得到显式熵项，倾向于奇异、低秩函数；齐次激活网络得到偏好"不活跃神经元"的熵项，可能导致"神经热寂"——平衡质量集中于输出趋零的网络。NTK视角下，该效应表现为一个简单性偏置，选择在参数扰动下变化缓慢的函数。

Conclusion: 参数对称性本身即可产生可计算的隐式偏置，其形式可由对称性诱导熵刻画；该偏置在低温下退化为经典有效正则化，在正温度下则偏好低秩、奇异或输出趋零的函数，可理解为NTK意义下的简单性偏置。

Abstract: Neural networks have parameter symmetries: parameter transformations that do not affect the represented function. How do these symmetries affect the functions that neural networks ultimately learn? We study the implicit bias arising from these symmetries by calculating the equilibrium distribution over represented functions under Langevin dynamics with Gaussian regularization. To isolate the effect of symmetries on this distribution, we study the limit in which the strength of the regularization is taken to zero. In certain cases, we show that the zero-regularization and infinite-time limits commute. In the low temperature limit, we recover known effective regularizations. At positive temperature, a renormalization procedure allows us to derive a novel entropy which predicts symmetry-induced preference for certain functions. For linear networks, we obtain explicit entropy terms that favor singular and low-rank functions. For networks with homogeneous activations, we find entropy terms that favor inactive neurons, which can lead to neural "heat death" in which equilibrium mass concentrates on networks with vanishing output. We interpret these results through the neural tangent kernel, as a simplicity bias selecting for functions that vary slowly under variations in the parameters.

</details>


### [221] [FairITR: Fairness-Aware Learning of Multicategory Individualized Treatment Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27894)
*Zeyu Chen,Wei Qian,Bintong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出面向多类别治疗的公平性个体化治疗方案（FairITR）框架，要求治疗决策与敏感属性独立，通过角度基分类、对抗去偏与协方差正则联合优化治疗收益与公平性，实验表明可显著降低不公平性且保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化治疗方案（ITR）依据个体特征给出治疗决策，效果优于“一刀切”策略，但若不考虑模型公平性，可能在种族、性别等敏感属性划分的群体间产生不公平决策。现有ITR估计方法多只优化治疗结果，公平性导向的ITR研究有限，且很少涉及多类别治疗的现实场景。

Method: 定义多类别ITR下的公平性概念——治疗决策需与敏感属性独立，并据此形式化公平ITR估计问题；提出FairITR学习框架，以角度基分类（angle-based classification）为多类别ITR学习基础，融合对抗学习与基于协方差的正则化以缓解治疗决策中的不公平，并给出对抗去偏机制所诱导公平性的理论论证。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的大量实验显示，FairITR相较现有基线显著降低了ITR中的不公平性，同时保持具有竞争力的治疗价值（treatment value）。

Conclusion: FairITR为多类别治疗场景提供了兼顾收益与公平的可行方案，其对抗去偏加协方差正则的设计在理论上具备公平性保障，实证上实现了公平-收益的较好权衡；未来可进一步拓展公平性定义与更复杂决策场景。

Abstract: Individualized treatment rules (ITRs) recommend treatment decisions based on individual characteristics and can improve treatment outcomes beyond traditional one-size-fits-all strategies. However, similar to other commonly used algorithmic decision-making methods, ITRs without considering model fairness may result in unfair decisions across groups defined by sensitive attributes, such as race or gender. Although extensive methods have been developed for ITR estimation, most focus solely on optimizing treatment outcomes, while research on fairness-aware ITRs remains limited. Moreover, the practical scenario of multicategory treatments is rarely considered in existing fair ITR studies. To address these gaps, we introduce a fairness notion for multicategory ITRs that requires treatment decisions to be independent of sensitive attributes, and then formulate the corresponding problem of estimating fair ITRs. To solve this problem, we further propose FairITR, a fairness-aware learning framework that jointly optimizes treatment outcomes and fairness. FairITR builds on angle-based classification for multicategory ITR learning and integrates adversarial learning with covariance-based regularization to mitigate unfairness in treatment decisions. A theoretical justification for the fairness properties induced by the adversarial debiasing mechanism is also provided. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that FairITR substantially reduces unfairness in ITRs while maintaining competitive treatment values relative to existing baselines.

</details>


### [222] [SUAF: Spectral Universal Advantage Features for Zero-Shot Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27919)
*Huaze Tang,Runqing Miao,Tianren Peng,Lam Nguyen,Chao Wang,Jairong Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: (1) 提出以“动作中心化、正滞后”的规范依赖矩阵(CDM)分析表征应捕捉的统计依赖； (2) 其SVD给出谱通用优势特征(SUAF)，谱值刻画当前动作与未来状态-动作访问的条件互信息并决定奖励相关的近似误差； (3) 截断谱模态在等向平均与奖励一致minimax准则下均为最优秩约束近似； (4) 用动作中心化Soft-HGR目标+双向TD估计从无奖励数据中学习SUAF； (5) 将谱近似误差与策略改进的充分条件相联系，实验验证谱预测、特征学习与跨奖励零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与奖励无关的表征可使RL智能体跨任务复用知识，但表征容量有限，由此引出三个基本问题：这类表征应捕捉何种统计依赖；该依赖中哪些成分应被保留以改进策略；丢弃其余成分会如何影响决策。论文旨在为这三个问题给出统一且有理论保证的答案。

Method: 1) 对给定参考策略构造动作中心化、正滞后的规范依赖矩阵CDM，剥离当前状态后可分离出当前动作的额外预测贡献；2) 对CDM做SVD得到成对的谱通用优势特征SUAF，通过线性奖励投影恢复延迟优势；3) 理论分析：谱值量化“给定当前状态下当前动作与未来状态-动作访问”的互信息，并决定奖励相关近似误差，证明保留主导模态在等向平均情形与奖励一致minimax准则下均为最优秩约束近似；4) 将即时奖励对比项分离，避免直接压缩完整优势算子产生的谱底；5) 用动作中心化Soft-HGR目标配合双向时序差分估计，从无奖励数据学习SUAF；6) 将谱近似误差与策略改进的充分条件建立联系。

Result: 理论层面给出CDM谱的互信息解释与奖励相关误差刻画，证明截断SUAF的最优秩逼近性质，并导出由谱误差控制的策略改进充分条件；有限状态实验验证谱预测与特征学习方法；在离散任务与连续控制基准上的零样本评测显示所学表征可跨奖励复用。

Conclusion: 以规范依赖分析为框架，可将“应捕捉何种依赖、保留哪些成分、丢弃代价如何”统一为CDM谱分析问题：主导谱模态即应保留的最优子空间，其截断误差可量化并转译为策略改进的充分条件；SUAF可由无奖励数据经Soft-HGR+双向TD估计获得，从而支持跨奖励的知识复用。

Abstract: Reward-independent representations enable reinforcement learning agents to reuse knowledge across tasks, but limited representation capacity raises three fundamental questions: what statistical dependence should these representations capture, which components of that dependence should be retained for policy improvement, and how does discarding the remaining components affect decision-making? We address these questions through canonical dependence analysis. For a reference policy, we construct an action-centered, positive-lag canonical dependence matrix (CDM) that isolates the additional predictive contribution of the current action beyond the current state. Its singular value decomposition yields Spectral Universal Advantage Features (SUAF), paired features that recover delayed advantage through linear reward projections. We show that the spectrum quantifies mutual information between current action and future state-action visitation conditioned on current state and determines reward specific approximation errors. Retaining the leading modes gives optimal rank-constrained approximations under both isotropic average-case and reward-uniform minimax criteria. Separating the immediate reward contrast also avoids the spectral floor arising from direct compression of the full advantage operator. To learn SUAF from reward-free data, we develop an action-centered Soft-HGR objective with bidirectional temporal-difference estimation. We further connect spectral approximation errors to sufficient conditions for policy improvement. Finite-state experiments validate the spectral predictions and feature-learning method, while zero-shot evaluations on discrete tasks and continuous-control benchmarks demonstrate the utility of the learned representations across rewards.

</details>


### [223] [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Agnostic PAC Learning for Partial Concept Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27946)
*Liang-Yu Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对有限VC维d的偏概念类，构造了确定性agnostic PAC学习器，样本复杂度O((d+log(1/δ))/ε²)，达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏概念类（partial concept class）允许概念在部分样本上未定义（⋆），其agnostic PAC学习的样本复杂度下界已由Alon等（FOCS 2021）给出，但匹配该下界的上界（尤其确定性学习器）此前未解决，本文填补这一空白。

Method: 构造了一个确定性agnostic PAC学习算法：从任意分布D中抽取O((d+log(1/δ))/ε²)个独立同分布样本，输出一个全域概念ĥ:X→{-1,+1}（而非偏概念），并以至少1-δ的概率使ĥ的误差不超过H中任一偏概念最小经验/真实误差加ε。技术核心在于处理偏概念在样本上未定义（⋆）的情形，并将其转化为全域预测。

Result: 所构造学习器的样本复杂度为O((d+log(1/δ))/ε²)，与Alon等人给出的下界相符，从而确立了偏概念类agnostic PAC学习的最优样本复杂度，且该结果由确定性算法实现。

Conclusion: 偏概念类的agnostic PAC学习样本复杂度最优阶为Θ((d+log(1/δ))/ε²)，本文给出匹配的确定性上界，完整刻画了该问题的样本复杂度。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal H\subseteq\{-1,+1,\star\}^{\mathcal X}$ be a partial concept class of finite VC dimension $d$. We construct a deterministic agnostic PAC learner that, for every $\varepsilon,δ\in(0,1)$, uses $O((d+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon^2)$ i.i.d. examples from an arbitrary distribution $\mathcal D$ over $\mathcal X\times\{-1,+1\}$ and returns a total concept $\hat h:\mathcal X\to\{-1,+1\}$ satisfying $\text{er}_{\mathcal D}(\hat h)\leq \min_{h\in\mathcal H}\text{er}_{\mathcal D}(h)+\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, where $\text{er}_{\mathcal D}(h)=\mathbb P_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal D}[h(x)\neq y]$. This establishes the optimal sample complexity, matching the lower bound of Alon et al. (FOCS 2021).

</details>


### [224] [Topology Hub Optimal Transport: Eliminating Long Context Dilution at Flash Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27951)
*Dylan Forde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: THOT 将 hub-and-spoke 最优传输稀疏注意力中的容量门控改为有界支撑的 simplex sparsemax 或阈值 ReLU，并配合三段式闭行 Sinkhorn 调度与精确秩-(D+2R+1) adjoint Triton 核，使长上下文检索在 262K 长度下保持 97–99% 零样本召回，同时获得 2.2x/3.3x 级别的核级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏/结构化注意力虽规避了二次成本，但在数十万干扰项中检索少量相关事实时，归一化质量随长度扩散导致性能退化；已有的 hub-and-spoke OT（块级尾部精化、固定支撑传输拓扑）仍存在干扰项质量泄漏与残差、算子不够高效的问题。

Method: 四项设计：(1) 通过 simplex sparsemax（O(L log L)）或免排序的 token 局部阈值 ReLU（O(L)）实现有界支撑容量门控，使超额干扰质量归零；(2) 三次扫描 1+2 退火行闭合 Sinkhorn 调度以抑制二部残差；(3) 在批量 split-factor Triton 核中实现精确秩-(D+2R+1) One-Reference-Tile adjoint（BF16 Tensor Core，FP32 potentials）；(4) 分块独占前缀因果变体，经验证无未来信息泄漏。

Result: NVIDIA L4 上，L=128 训练、L=262,144 零样本：sparsemax THOT 在 α=0 下 97.40%、α=0.05 下 98.83%（O(L) 阈值 ReLU 在 α=0.05 处持平，α=0 时仅 80.60%），softmax 门控 hub OT 降至 3.52%。双向算子微基准：H=8, D=64, L=262K 时前向+VJP 100.1 ms（比 latent softmax 快 2.2x、编译临时量小 4.0x）；L=4,096 时 1.34 ms vs cuDNN FlashAttention 4.37 ms。WikiText-103 多针检索：L=65K 时 100.00%（128/128），短训练 RoPE FlashAttention 仅 0.78%；4 层 WikiText-4K 上因果/掩码 LM 增益温和。

Conclusion: 有界支撑容量门控 + 退火闭行 Sinkhorn + 精确低秩 adjoint 的组合，可在近线性复杂度下显著提升超长上下文精确检索鲁棒性并保持核级效率；但大规模多层的实际收益仍有限（需走未融合路径），LM 端提升幅度温和。

Abstract: Long-context models use sparse or structured attention to avoid quadratic cost, yet retrieving a small set of relevant facts embedded among hundreds of thousands of distractors degrades as normalization mass disperses with length. Hub-and-spoke optimal transport (OT) replaces the dense $L \times L$ matrix with a linear-size graph over local windows and $K$ shared hubs balanced by Sinkhorn scaling. Building on block-wise tail refinement and fixed-support transport topology (arXiv:2605.08123, arXiv:2609.07954), we introduce Topology Hub Optimal Transport (THOT): (1) bounded-support capacity gating via simplex sparsemax ($O(L\log L)$) or sorting-free token-local thresholded ReLU ($O(L)$), driving excess distractor mass to zero; (2) a three-sweep $1+2$ annealed row-closed Sinkhorn schedule that damps bipartite residuals; (3) an exact rank-$(D+2R+1)$ One-Reference-Tile adjoint in batched split-factor Triton kernels (BF16 Tensor Cores, FP32 potentials); and (4) a chunked exclusive-prefix causal variant with verified zero future leakage. On NVIDIA L4, sparsemax THOT trained at $L=128$ reaches 97.40% zero-shot recall of fixed bindings at $L=262{,}144$ under pure task loss ($α=0$) and 98.83% at $α=0.05$ (matched by $O(L)$ thresholded ReLU at $α=0.05$, vs. 80.60% at $α=0$), while softmax-gated hub OT drops to 3.52%. In bidirectional operator microbenchmarks, our kernel runs forward+VJP in 100.1 ms at $H=8, D=64, L=262\mathrm{K}$ (2.2x faster with 4.0x smaller compiler temporaries than latent softmax) and 1.34 ms vs. 4.37 ms for cuDNN FlashAttention at $L=4{,}096$; multi-layer LM runs use the unfused path. Zero-shot WikiText-103 multi-needle retrieval hits 100.00% (128/128 queries at $L=65\mathrm{K}$) vs. 0.78% for short-trained RoPE FlashAttention, and compact 4-layer WikiText-4K runs show modest causal and masked LM gains.

</details>


### [225] [Ranking-Preserving Coreset Selection for Efficient Evolutionary Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27959)
*Mamoona Tasadduq,Maryam Bashir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RPC通过进化优化构造能保持配置相对排名的紧凑核心集，在维持精度的同时将HPO阶段加速约1.14x–8.80x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数优化（HPO）对预测性能至关重要，但候选配置需在大数据集上反复训练，计算代价高昂，亟需降低评估成本的方法。

Method: 提出保序核心集（ranking-preserving coreset, RPC）选择框架：构造一个紧凑子集，使各超参数配置在其上的相对排名与全量数据一致。核心集选择被建模为进化优化问题，目标为最大化全量数据排名与子集排名之间的Spearman秩相关，并对子集规模施加惩罚。随后在所选核心集上执行进化式HPO，采用GA、CHC与PSO三种算法。

Result: 在7个表格、2个文本、2个图像基准数据集上，RPC显著缩短HPO时间，同时决策树、文本分类与图像分类任务的测试精度与全量数据HPO相当。HPO阶段加速约1.14x–8.80x；核心集构建的一次性开销通过端到端运行时间与盈亏平衡分析单独核算。在未参与构建的20组留出配置上，RPC在11个数据集中的8个上排名相关性高于同规模随机采样（数据集级Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p = 0.014）。

Conclusion: 保序性可泛化到选择阶段未使用的配置，说明RPC并非仅拟合构建样本；核心集一次构建后可在多次HPO运行中复用以摊薄开销，RPC是加速进化HPO的有效方案。

Abstract: Hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is essential for high predictive performance, but its computational cost becomes prohibitive when candidate configurations require repeated training on large datasets. This paper proposes a ranking-preserving coreset (RPC) selection framework for accelerating evolutionary HPO. The method constructs a compact coreset that preserves the relative ranking of hyperparameter configurations observed on the full training data. Coreset selection is formulated as an evolutionary optimization problem that maximizes Spearman rank correlation between full-data and subset-based configuration rankings while penalizing subset size. The selected coreset is then used for HPO with three evolutionary algorithms: Genetic Algorithm (GA), Cross-generational elitist Selection, Heterogeneous Recombination and Cataclysmic Mutation (CHC), and Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO). Experiments on seven tabular, two text, and two image benchmark datasets show that RPC substantially reduces HPO time while maintaining test accuracy comparable to full-dataset HPO across decision tree, text classification, and image classification tasks. Across datasets and optimizers, HPO-stage speedups range from approximately 1.14x to 8.80x, while the one-time coreset construction cost is accounted for separately through end-to-end runtime and break-even analysis. On 20 held-out configurations excluded from coreset construction, RPC achieves higher ranking correlation than same-size random sampling on 8 of 11 datasets (dataset-level Wilcoxon signed-rank test, p = 0.014). These results show that ranking preservation extends beyond the configurations used during selection. The one-time construction cost can be amortized when the coreset is reused across HPO runs.

</details>


### [226] [Model Carving: Training Transformers That Optimize Their Own Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27962)
*Kaden Zheng,Pratyusha Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Model Carving：在单次训练中通过可学习标量门同时学习 Transformer 的权重与架构，训练损失可剪除子层，从而得到更小、异构、高效的"雕刻"模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法将架构选择与训练过程分离，如经典 Transformer 在每层以固定顺序堆叠相同的注意力和 MLP 子层，这种统一性是惯例而非有依据的设计选择，可能并非最优；为特定任务而言，最优架构应更小、更高效、且远非均匀。作者主张训练本身应塑造模型拓扑。

Method: Model Carving 在一次训练中同时学习权重与架构：为每个子层施加可学习的标量门（scalar gates），使训练损失在优化过程中直接移除模型自身组件，从而"释放"出内部更小的异构网络。实验在 140M–2.7B 的标准 Transformer 及 140M–1.3B 的 looped Transformer 上进行预训练评估，并与同等算力的稠密模型、剪枝与蒸馏得到的同尺寸模型对比。

Result: 同等训练算力下，carved 模型的验证损失低于使用至多 1.7 倍算力训练的稠密模型；同时优于剪枝或蒸馏得到的同尺寸模型。该优势随规模增大而增强，并可迁移到少样本评估与生成等下游任务。由于被移除的子层不再执行，carved 模型每 token FLOPs 最多降低 34%，解码速度最高提升 1.6 倍且无质量损失。

Conclusion: 架构与权重一样应当被学习而非预先固定；获得边缘设备小模型的最佳途径是训练中"雕刻"，而非事后压缩大模型；每个大型 Transformer 内部都藏着一个更小、更快、更强的网络等待被雕刻出来。

Abstract: Machine learning has largely kept separate the choice of architecture and the process of training. A classic Transformer, for instance, trains a fixed set of attention and MLP sublayers at every layer, in the same order, and optimizes the weights within. This uniformity is a convention rather than a principled design choice, and it is likely suboptimal: the best architecture for a given task may be smaller, more efficient, and far from uniform. We believe that, ideally, training itself should shape the model's topology. To this end, we introduce Model Carving, which learns both a Transformer's weights and its architecture in a single run. By applying learnable, scalar gates to each sublayer, we allow the training loss to remove the model's own components during training -- freeing a smaller heterogeneous network inside. We evaluate Model Carving by pre-training standard Transformers from 140M to 2.7B parameters and looped Transformers from 140M to 1.3B. At equal training compute, carved models reach a lower validation loss than dense models trained with up to 1.7 times more compute. Furthermore, carved models also outperform size-matched models produced by pruning or distillation, suggesting that the best way to obtain small models for edge devices is to carve them during training, not to compress a large one afterwards. This advantage both grows with scale and carries over to downstream tasks such as few-shot evaluation and generation. Because the removed sublayers no longer run, carved models also cost up to 34% fewer FLOPs per token and decode up to 1.6 times faster without cost in quality. Our results show that architecture, like weights, should be learned and not fixed in advance, and that inside every large transformer is a smaller, faster, stronger one waiting to be carved out.

</details>


### [227] [On the Complete Feature Set for ADHD Classification in Children and Adolescents Based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27970)
*Javier Sanchis,Álvaro Navarro,Jorge García-Carrasco,Tahar Kechadi,Juan Trujillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发布BALLADEER脑电数据集：整合156名儿童青少年的669段既有EEG记录，覆盖五种任务条件，提供统一时间窗下的7类EEG特征（来自115个特征定义/模板）及质量决策记录，并给出XGBoost参与人留出评估示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意缺陷多动障碍（ADHD）涉及注意、活动与冲动控制，EEG可记录儿童青少年执行认知任务时的脑活动；但跨记录比较特征需要一致的信号预处理、共享分析窗口和可追溯的质量决策，现有数据分散、流程不统一，难以复现和横向比较。

Method: 对156名参与者、五种任务条件下的669段既有BALLADEER记录进行整理与策展：将连续信号与115个特征定义/模板预计算得到的7类特征族在同一时间区间上对齐，记录每段记录的质量决策；技术验证涵盖信号完整性、特征估计器数值检查与发布文件完整性；并以参与人留出（participant-held-out）方式，用全部7类特征的XGBoost（8秒窗口）与仅基于训练折招募组先验的基线预测器比较。

Result: 技术验证通过；各任务中预处理后保留记录时间的中位比例为100%；示例评估显示Robots任务中招募组判别能力强于三种Slackline条件；作者强调这些重叠队列的描述性结果不构成诊断效度证据。

Conclusion: 该数据集为在同一时间窗上比较7类EEG特征族提供了统一、可追溯的策展资源，并公开数据、策展软件与预测示例的聚合结果；但后续分析仍需处理参与人内重复测量、残余伪迹和采集系统差异。

Abstract: Attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder is a neurodevelopmental condition that can affect attention, activity and impulse control. Electroencephalography (EEG) records brain activity as children and adolescents perform cognitive tasks. However, comparing features derived from these recordings requires consistent signal preparation, shared analysis windows and documented quality decisions. We curated 669 existing BALLADEER recordings from 156 participants across five task conditions so that researchers can compare seven EEG feature families on the same time intervals for each participant and task. The dataset links continuous signals to precomputed measurements from 115 feature definitions or templates and records the quality decisions made for each recording. Analyses must still account for repeated measurements within participants, residual artefacts and differences between acquisition systems. Technical validation covered signal integrity, numerical checks on feature estimators and completeness of the released files. In every task, the median proportion of recording time retained after preprocessing was 100%. To illustrate reuse, we report saved participant-held-out evaluations comparing XGBoost using all seven families on eight-second windows with a predictor based only on recruitment-group prevalence in the training fold. Recruitment-group discrimination was stronger in Robots than in the three Slackline conditions. These descriptive results from overlapping cohorts do not establish diagnostic validity. The dataset, curation software and aggregate results for the predictive example are publicly available.

</details>


### [228] [A Methodologically Rigorous Benchmark of Linear Regression,RNN, and LSTM for S&P 500 Forecasting:Identifying Sources of Performance Inflation in Financial DeepLearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27978)
*Hari Sitaula,Joseph Opitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在统一预处理与时间序列评估流程下，对S&P 500次日收盘价预测比较线性回归、普通RNN与LSTM三类模型，并系统审计深度学习股价预测文献中的三类结果虚高来源（小波去噪评估目标、缺失朴素持续性基线、best-of-N重复最优报告），随后在纠正后的实验协议下重新评估各模型，以朴素持续性预测作为主基线进行准确性与稳定性比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习股价预测文献普遍存在评估方法失真问题：评估目标被小波去噪处理、缺少朴素持续性基线、以及从多次重复实验中只报告最优结果，导致性能被系统性高估，缺少可比、可信的基准对照。

Method: 使用2006–2025年包含基本面价格数据、宏观指标与工程化技术特征的数据集预测S&P 500次日收盘水平；在统一预处理与按时间顺序的评估流程下，基准比较线性回归、vanilla RNN、LSTM三种模型族；提出方法学审计，指出并控制三类结果虚高来源，构建纠正后的评估协议；每种配置进行N=30次独立重复实验；采用RMSE、MAPE、Pearson相关系数作为指标，并以朴素持续性预测（以当日收盘价预测次日收盘价）为主要基线。

Result: 性能以RMSE、MAPE和Pearson相关系数衡量，并取N=30次独立重复的均值；在准确率、跨重复稳定性以及架构深度敏感性三个维度比较模型族。摘要未给出具体数值结果，重点在于纠正协议下各模型相对于朴素持续性基线是否体现出真实改进。

Conclusion: 深度学习股价预测文献中广泛存在的结果虚高实践会显著扭曲模型优劣判断；需在统一预处理、时间序列划分、强制朴素持续性基线以及多次重复统计报告的规范协议下评估模型，才能获得可信的预测性能结论。

Abstract: This study examines one-day-ahead forecasting of the S&P 500 closing level using a 2006-2025 dataset that includes fundamental price data, macroeconomic indicators, and engineered technical features. We benchmark three model families, namely linear regression, vanilla recurrent neural networks (RNN), and long short-term memory networks (LSTM), within a unified preprocessing and chronological evaluation pipeline. A central contribution of this work is a methodological audit of common practices in the deep-learning-for-stock-prediction literature. We identify three sources of result inflation that frequently appear in published methodologies: wavelet-denoised evaluation targets, the absence of a naive persistence baseline, and best-of-N replicate reporting. We then re-evaluate all model families under a corrected protocol that controls for each. Performance is reported using root mean squared error, mean absolute percentage error, and the Pearson correlation coefficient, averaged across N = 30 independent replicates per configuration. We compare models on accuracy, stability across replicates, and architectural depth sensitivity, using the naive persistence forecast (predicting today's close as tomorrow's close) as a primary baseline against which all candidate models must demonstrate improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Adding One More Agent: How AI Team Size and Formation Shape Productivity and Diversity in Human-AI Brainstorming Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26663)
*Rong-Ching Chang,Hao-Chuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在一个N=168的头脑风暴实验中，将"AI伙伴数量（1个 vs 2个）"与"行为形成方式（校准至人类平均水平 vs 参与者定制）"交叉设计，并以人-人双人组为参照。结果发现：双AI房间在共享板上发布的想法更多，但人类撰写的想法大幅下降；单AI时行为形成方式有效（平均校准条件下人类贡献最高），双AI时该效应消失；AI的数量与形成方式共同重塑人类贡献，单智能体结论无法推广到多智能体团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已提升个体生产力，但"增加多个AI伙伴究竟有益还是有害"仍无定论；同时，现有研究多基于单智能体设置，缺乏对AI数量与AI行为形成方式交互作用的检验，也缺少与人类-人类双人组的直接参照，因此无法判断单智能体结论能否推广到多智能体团队。

Method: 采用2×2被试间实验设计（N=168），交叉"AI伙伴数量（一个 vs 两个）"与"行为形成（校准至平均人类行为 vs 由参与者定制）"，并设置人-人双人组作为参照条件。以头脑风暴任务为场景，测量共享板上的想法发布数量、人类-authored想法数量、发言与转化行为、AI社交性话语与AI发布量、人类想法的全局多样性与会话内差异化等指标。

Result: 双AI房间在共享板上发布的想法总数更多，但人类-authored想法显著减少。单AI条件下行为形成方式起作用：与平均校准AI配对的被试发布的想法多于其他所有条件；双AI条件下该效应消失。人类持续发言但转化为板上帖子的想法更少。AI的社交性话语正向预测人类发帖，而AI的发帖量负向预测人类发帖。AI在场提升了人类-authored想法的全局多样性，但只有第二个AI提升了会话内差异化。

Conclusion: AI伙伴的数量及其行为形成方式会重塑人类实际贡献的内容；单智能体研究结果不能直接推广到多智能体团队，多AI并不等同于更高的人机协作产出。

Abstract: Generative AI has improved individual productivity, but whether adding a plurality of AI partners helps or hurts remains open. We crossed AI partnership (one versus two) with behavioral formation (calibrated to average human behavior versus customized by the participant) in a brainstorming experiment ($N$=168) with human-human dyads as reference. Two-AI rooms posted more ideas to the shared board, but human-authored ideas dropped substantially. With one AI partner, formation mattered: humans paired with an average-calibrated AI posted more than any other condition. With two, this effect vanished. Humans continued talking but converted fewer ideas into board posts. AI social talk predicted more human posts, while AI posting volume predicted fewer. AI presence increased global diversity of human-authored ideas, but only a second AI increased within-session differentiation. The number of AI partners and their formation can reshape what the human contributes; single-agent results do not generalize to multi-agent teams.

</details>


### [230] [Coherence by Construction: Synthesizing CTI Knowledge Graphs and the Formal Limits of Coherence Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26717)
*Tanzimul Alam Fahim,Mohammad Arif Hossain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"仿真式生成"范式：由语言模型智能体提出意图、确定性裁判依据显式世界模型裁决事件，产物作为事件的因果记录。在STIX 2.1网络威胁情报知识图谱上实例化，并证明：任何只读取"结构匹配随机图可复现"特征的验证器，对"构造即一致"的生成器均无信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流做法让大语言模型逐个输出结构化对象（如知识图谱三元组），虽保证单对象合法，但对象间关系缺乏落地依据（ungrounded），一致性只能事后校验、无法由构造保证；现有评测基准也无法区分"表面结构相似"与"语义一致"。

Method: 1) 形式化"生成即仿真"（generation by simulation）：LLM智能体提出意图，确定性裁判（referee）在显式世界模型上裁决结果，事件发生才发射关系；2) 在STIX 2.1上以三个引擎实例化（含对结构化图基因组施加LLM遗传算子的进化引擎），共用确定性编译器使畸形/悬空输出不可表示；3) 证明引用闭包、因果落地、标识符稳定性三性质，并用消融定位其来源；4) 双LLM评审复现评分排序；5) 提出并证明"无用性定理"，用Erdős–Rényi对照构造四种检验（结构分离、嵌入迁移、归纳链接预测、真实数据增广）。

Result: 三性质得证；消融表明其源于裁判与世界模型而非策略；两独立LLM评审复现引擎排序。核心结果为警示性：定理证明结构匹配随机图可复现的图特征对一致性生成器无判别力；四种评估场景（结构分离、嵌入迁移、归纳链接预测、真实数据增广）均奖励平凡性而非一致性，并给出各自机制与有效基准须满足的条件。

Conclusion: 一致性应作为构造性质而非事后度量；本文既提供可证明正确性的仿真式生成框架，也揭示主流图/知识图谱生成评测的失效模式，为设计有效基准给出判据。

Abstract: Synthesizing relational training data such as knowledge graphs with a large language model typically emits structured objects one at a time, which keeps each object valid but leaves the relationships between them ungrounded. We study an alternative paradigm, generation by simulation: rather than describing a relational artifact, we simulate a process that produces it and take the artifact to be its causal record. Language-model agents propose intent and a deterministic referee resolves outcomes against an explicit world model, so a relation is emitted only when the event it represents occurs, making coherence a property of construction. We instantiate the paradigm for cyber threat intelligence knowledge graphs in the STIX 2.1 standard with three engines, among them an evolutionary engine applying language-model genetic operators to structured graph genomes, sharing a deterministic compiler that makes malformed and dangling output unrepresentable. We prove referential closure, causal grounding, and identifier stability, an ablation localizes each to the referee and world model rather than the policy, and two independent LLM judges reproduce the rubric's engine ranking. Our central result is cautionary, and we prove it: a validator reading any graph feature set a structure-matched random graph can reproduce is uninformative about coherence for a coherence-by-construction generator. Using Erdős-Rényi controls we realize the theorem four times, as structural separation, embedding transfer, inductive link prediction, and real-data augmentation each reward a triviality rather than coherence; we give the mechanism behind each and the conditions a valid benchmark must meet.

</details>


### [231] [TOUMAII: A 4.5M-Pair Tool-Calling Preference Dataset and Its Source-Agnostic Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26722)
*Moritz Pötzsch,Andrea Gentilini,Lisa Rossgoderer,Tom Osterfeld,Kesava Prasad Sanjivi Arul,Swanand Ravindra Kadhe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TOUMAII 大规模工具调用偏好数据集（约450万条验证过的偏好对）及 TOUMAII-Gen 合成偏好数据生成流水线，用 DPO 提升企业智能体的工具调用可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用是企业智能体的核心能力；SFT 只优化"生成正确的调用"，从不对比"看似合理但错误的调用"，而现实中细微格式错误即可使调用失效、错误决策会中断交互。DPO 恰好针对这种对比学习，但工具调用场景（尤其真实多轮）的偏好对数据极度稀缺。

Method: 从 Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M（apache-2.0）对话中构造约4.5M条经校验的偏好对，覆盖多轮与单轮交互、并行与串行函数调用；并发布 TOUMAII-Gen 流水线，输入工具调用 SFT 数据集即可生成含"自然出现的模型生成失败模式"的偏好数据集，具备源无关性（另发布基于 ToolACE 的第二份小规模偏好集）。

Result: 在3B参数模型上做 DPO 微调，效果与其它高质量工具调用偏好数据集持平（performance parity）。

Conclusion: TOUMAII 与 TOUMAII-Gen 缓解了工具调用偏好数据稀缺问题，生成流水线可跨数据源复用，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: To autonomously execute multi-step tasks, enterprise agents must reliably interact with tools, call APIs, or query databases, making tool-calling a fundamental capability of AI models. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) optimises for producing the correct call, but never contrasts it with a plausible incorrect one. In an environment where even a minor formatting error can invalidate a call and an incorrect decision can derail the interaction, direct preference optimisation (DPO) targets exactly this contrast. DPO, therefore, requires preference pairs rather than plain demonstrations. For tool-calling, such data, especially in realistic multi-turn settings, remains extremely scarce. To address this scarcity, we introduce TOUMAII, a large-scale tool-calling preference dataset containing ~4.5M validated preference pairs, derived from Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M (apache-2.0) conversations. The dataset consists of multi- and single-turn interactions and includes parallel and sequential function calls. Additionally, we release TOUMAII-Gen, a synthetic data generation pipeline that consumes tool-calling SFT datasets and generates a preference dataset with naturally occurring model-generated tool-calling failure modes. To validate the effectiveness of our DPO data, we fine-tune a 3-billion-parameter model, achieving performance parity with other high-quality tool-calling preference datasets. Furthermore, we release a second small preference dataset derived from ToolACE (apache-2.0), showcasing the source-agnostic nature of our pipeline.
  We release the complete code and datasets: https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen and https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets

</details>


### [232] [ChatAtom: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Molecular Dynamics Simulation and Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26782)
*Trupti Mohanty,Ming Du,Mitchell A. Wood,Brad L. Boyce,Remi Dingreville,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChatAtom——一个多智能体大语言模型系统，可将自然语言请求转化为完整的分子动力学（MD）工作流，用户无需编写输入脚本或分析代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MD 模拟能预测材料的结构、力学与动态性质，是材料设计与发现的核心工具；但其流程高度依赖专家：需要设计模拟、编写可靠输入脚本、执行作业并用独立工具分析结果。这一门槛限制了 MD 的普及，因此需要自动化、可审查、可观测的一体化工作流。

Method: 采用职责分工的多智能体架构：编排器（orchestrator）负责研究规划；LAMMPS 智能体与 OVITO 智能体各采用“生成器-审查器（generator-reviewer）”配对，在执行前生成并审查模拟与可视化脚本；高性能计算智能体负责将作业分发到远程资源；分析智能体解读已完成的计算。系统提供浏览器界面，在本地执行时流式传输热力学数据与渲染帧；并支持以可加载技能（skills）扩展新能力而无需修改智能体，从而实现自主假设生成与检验。

Result: 通过脚本生成基准测试、端到端裂纹扩展研究以及闭环自动研究循环（closed-loop campaign）进行评估，验证了系统的脚本生成质量与全流程闭环能力。

Conclusion: ChatAtom 将脚本生成、执行、可视化与分析整合进一条经过审查且可观测的流水线，降低了开展 MD 研究的门槛，拓宽了先进模拟工作流的可及性。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics (MD) simulation resolves atomic-scale motion and predicts the structural, mechanical, and dynamic properties of materials, making it cen- tral to materials design and discovery. Yet an MD study requires an expert-driven workflow: designing the simulation, writing reliable input scripts, executing them, and analyzing the results with separate tools. Here we present ChatAtom, a multi-agent large language model system that translates a natural-language request into a complete MD workflow without the user writing input scripts or analysis code. Each agent has a defined responsibility within the workflow. An orchestrator plans the study; the LAMMPS and OVITO agents each use a generator-reviewer pair to prepare and review simulation and visualization scripts before execution; a high-performance computing agent dispatches jobs to remote resources; and an analysis agent interprets the completed run. The work- flow is accessible through a browser interface that streams thermodynamic data and rendered frames during local execution, allowing users to follow the simu- lation as it progresses. The architecture also supports new capabilities through loadable skills without modifying the agents, enabling autonomous hypothesis generation and testing. We evaluate ChatAtom through script-generation bench- marks, an end-to-end study of crack propagation, and a closed-loop campaign. By bringing script generation, execution, visualization, and analysis into one reviewed and observable pipeline, ChatAtom lowers the barrier to conducting MD studies and broadens access to advanced simulation workflows.

</details>


### [233] [Beyond Final Answers: Interpretable Tumor Immune Microenvironment Reasoning from H&E and mIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26794)
*Yixuan Duan,Kexin Geng,Hanwen Xu,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HERMES：一种联合读取H&E与mIF、按临床指南步骤推理并用过程奖励校验中间量的R1式多模态大模型，在结直肠癌TIME四项任务上达84.5%，显著超越12个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理MLLM存在两个缺口：其一，只读H&E，无法获得mIF提供的细胞表型；其二，其推理缺乏步骤级真值，R1式强化学习仅奖励最终答案，推理过程不受校验。同切片H&E-mIF配对数据可同时弥补这两点——mIF无需人工标注即可给出每步（分区、计数、归一化）的细胞级真值。

Method: 提出HERMES（H&E-mIF Reasoning with Multimodal Evidence Supervision）：以R1式强化学习训练的多模态大模型（4B），对配对H&E与mIF联合推理，推理按临床指南分解为步骤；并引入过程奖励（process reward），将模型陈述的每一个中间量与mIF细胞级真值逐项核对。

Result: 四项目野级TIME任务平均准确率84.5%，高于12个商用/通用/医学/病理MLLM中最优者的74.7%；中间量正确率亦高于最强开源权重基线。去掉过程奖励后，强化学习相对监督微调提升甚微（80.0% vs 78.8%）且正确中间量更少；对决定性中间量进行编辑，885道测试题中答案均如预期改变。零样本外推：CODEX成像外部队列76.4%（对比67.3%），训练中未出现的头颈癌76.7%（对比72.5%）。

Conclusion: 同切片H&E-mIF配对所提供的细胞级过程监督是真值可核验推理的关键；过程奖励对性能与推理可解释性均有实质贡献，模型具备跨成像平台与跨癌种的迁移能力。

Abstract: Readouts of the tumor immune microenvironment (TIME), such as CD8+ T-cell density, intra-epithelial infiltration and the Ki67 index, combine tissue architecture from H&E with cell phenotypes from multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF). Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) could automate these readouts with an explanation, but two gaps remain. Pathology MLLMs read H&E alone, while these readouts need the cell phenotypes that mIF measures. And their reasoning has no step-level truth to be checked against, so R1-style reinforcement learning rewards only the final answer and leaves the reasoning unverified. Same-section H&E and mIF provide what both gaps lack: mIF measures every cell without manual annotation, which gives cell-level ground truth for each step that clinical guidelines prescribe, from delineating a tissue compartment to counting and normalizing the marker-positive cells within it. We therefore present HERMES (H&E-mIF Reasoning with Multimodal Evidence Supervision), an R1-style MLLM that reasons jointly over paired H&E and mIF in guideline steps and is trained with a process reward that checks every stated quantity against this truth. Across four field-level TIME tasks in colorectal cancer, HERMES (4B) reaches an average accuracy of 84.5%, compared with 74.7% for the strongest of twelve proprietary, general, medical and pathology MLLMs, and a higher share of its intermediate quantities is correct than for the strongest open-weight baseline. Without the process reward, reinforcement learning barely improves on supervised fine-tuning (80.0% vs. 78.8%) and fewer stated quantities are correct; editing a decisive stated quantity changes the answer as implied in all 885 test questions. Without retraining, it keeps the highest average accuracy on an external cohort imaged with CODEX (76.4% vs. 67.3%) and on head and neck cancer, a cancer type absent from training (76.7% vs. 72.5%).

</details>


### [234] [Memorization and Generalization in Generative Models: A Perspective From Energy-based Modeling and Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26797)
*Bao Pham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以联想记忆（AM）的吸引子/能量盆视角重新解释生成模型的智能机制，提出"记忆与泛化可共存于同一检索过程"，并通过扩散模型（含Stable Diffusion）与均匀态离散扩散语言模型实证刻画从记忆到创造（伪状态作为生成能力起点）的相变过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型已能写代码、生成逼真图像、进行长对话，但其智能背后的机制仍不清楚；同时，AM中的"伪状态（spurious states）"长期被视为记忆失败的缺陷。作者希望将这一"失败"重构为创造性的起点，并统一解释记忆与泛化。

Method: (1) 论证扩散模型等价于联想记忆：小数据时每个训练样本周围形成独立吸引盆，数据增多后盆地收缩、伪状态（混合样本但不匹配任何样本的吸引子）出现，并以盆地体积、能量、曲率跨数据集与架构（含Stable Diffusion）度量该转变；(2) 在语言领域超越能量视角，证明均匀态离散扩散模型是能检索未见过数据的AM，其吸引盆仅由条件似然最大化产生；(3) 用条件熵作为低成本诊断指标，定位模型处于转变过程中的位置。

Result: 随训练集增大，训练样本周围的盆地收缩、未见过样本周围的盆地扩张，直至被污染的测试句子能被与训练样本同等可靠地恢复；条件熵可在单条序列内部揭示记忆与泛化的共存，并廉价地诊断模型所处的转变阶段。

Conclusion: AM是一个由吸引盆定义的普遍原理，记忆与泛化可在单一模型中并存；AM为判断生成模型何时"回忆"、何时"创造"提供了统一的分析框架。

Abstract: Generative models now write working code, produce realistic images, and hold long conversations, yet the mechanisms behind their intelligence still evade our understanding. This thesis explores that puzzle through Associative Memory (AM), in which patterns are stored as energy minima and retrieved through attractor dynamics. Beyond their capacity, AMs form new minima known as spurious states, long regarded as a failure of memory. We argue that this failure is where creativity begins, and that memorization and generalization can coexist as outcomes of one retrieval process. First, we show that diffusion models behave as AMs. With little data, a diffusion model forms a distinct basin of attraction around each training sample. As the data grows, these basins shrink, and spurious states emerge as attractors that blend stored samples yet match none of them. They are the first signs of generative capability. We characterize this transition by measuring the volume, energy, and curvature of these basins across datasets and architectures, including Stable Diffusion. We then turn to language and move beyond the energy-based view of AM. We show that uniform-state discrete diffusion models are AMs capable of retrieving unseen data, with basins of attraction that arise from conditional likelihood maximization alone. As the training set grows, basins around training examples contract while basins around unseen examples expand, until corrupted test sentences are recovered as reliably as training ones. Conditional entropy exposes this coexistence within a single sequence and cheaply diagnoses where a model lies along the transition. Together, these results suggest that AM is a general principle defined by basins of attraction, and that memorization and generalization can coexist within one model. AM thus offers a framework for asking when a generative model recalls and when it creates.

</details>


### [235] [Beyond Demographic Discrimination: Benchmarking Normative-Descriptive Calibration in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26870)
*Radhika Bhargava,Shinibali Bhattacharyya,George Mihaila,Jay Prajapati,Anwesan Pal,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"规范性—描述性校准"概念并构建NDCB基准，检验同一模型在"预测结果"与"做出决定"两种框架下对同一对候选对象的差异化处理是否一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性基准多在单一推理目标下评估群体歧视，几乎没有基准检验模型在"描述"与"决策"框架下是否对同一项目给出不同处理；而LLM已广泛部署于社会性后果场景，需区分规范性推理（应然）与描述性推理（或然）。

Method: 构建NDCB：697个匹配对场景，覆盖6个领域，每场景在两种框架下各提问一次；定义三个指标——规范性参与率(NER)、规范性泄漏率(NLR)、规范性—描述性分离度(NDS)。在6个LLM上评测，并以BBQ准确率、BOLD作对比基线；另做简化模板下的触发词消融和候选顺序反转实验。

Result: NDS可区分15个模型对中的13对，而BBQ准确率仅7对、BOLD为0对（作者亦提示该计数受样本量影响）。Claude Sonnet 4.6与Opus 4.6分离最强（平均NDS分别为+0.55、+0.52，置信区间重叠）；GPT-5.5在单pick指令下平均NDS为负(-0.14)，去掉后接近零(+0.02)。简化模板中删除单个触发词不改变NDS符号，但可使其偏移达0.46并改变模型排序。顺序反转实验显示：面对完全相同的候选，六个模型均偏好排在前面的选项；平均顺序效应后，人口属性相关倾向指向边缘化候选。

Conclusion: "描述差异"与"规定差异"是可分离的两种行为，且跨模型表现不同；单目标公平性评测会掩盖框架依赖的偏差，需要双框架校准式评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in socially consequential settings, where they must distinguish between normative reasoning (what should happen) and descriptive reasoning (what is likely to happen). Most fairness benchmarks evaluate demographic discrimination under a single reasoning objective, and none we know of tests whether a model treats the same item differently when asked to describe an outcome versus decide one.
