Conclusion: 性能损失主要经由输入相关的旋转相位传导，而非符号信息缺失；该机制在未见程序族上稳定复现。然而基于此机制的致性训练目标虽减小了内部差异，却未能带来优于常规数据增强的效果，说明该内部差异并非性能瓶颈的全部。

Abstract: Visual features can retain every symbol a task needs while a model built on them still changes its answer when irrelevant parts of the image change. We study this gap in visual state tracking, where a recurrent executor reads a sequence of symbol tiles through a frozen ViT encoder and decides whether a displayed candidate equals the final state. Our diagnostic pairs each input with a donor view that preserves every queried tile, checks with a symbol-supervised decoder that the full symbol chain is recoverable from both feature sequences, and swaps individual internal channels of the same fitted executor in both directions. On 3,526 pairs in which every symbol is recovered in both views, changing the surrounding context lowers the balanced accuracy of a rotary recurrent head by 17.13 points. The loss runs mainly through the input-dependent rotation phase: restoring the original phase repairs 351 of 391 new errors and brings accuracy back to 88.54%, yet 142 individual predictions still differ from the original ones. A locked test on 192 unseen program families reproduces the pathway (28 of 29 new errors repaired; 382 of 384 predictions unchanged), and the effect partly carries over to single-image canvases. Consistency objectives derived from this analysis reduce the internal discrepancy they target but do not outperform ordinary data augmentation.

</details>


### [302] [Average-Depth Variable Execution via Request-Local Token Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27738)
*Pengjie Zheng,Mengwei Xie,Huan Yang,Si Wu,Pengfei Hu,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AVERT 提出按请求预算自适应分配每 token 的执行层数，通过路由器逐层决策跳过 Transformer 块并调整残差与 K/V 写入，单检查点支持混合预算批处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层跳过可降低大模型推理成本，但请求级预算未说明如何在生成的不同 token 间分配层执行量，且不同预算请求难以同批处理。

Method: 在每个路由层，路由器读取当前 token 状态与该请求预算，决定是否执行 Transformer 块；跳过时经轻量残差适配器传递且不写入该层 K/V；单一检查点即可在同一批次服务不同预算请求，并使每请求的平均执行深度贴合连续预算。

Result: 在 3 个 LLaMA 骨干、6 项质量指标、13 个预算共 234 组对比中，AVERT 有 206 组最优或并列最优；单预算与混合预算批次下每请求预算的平均绝对误差不超过 1.15 个百分点；混合预算使 TPOT 降低 4.3%–21.2%。

Conclusion: AVERT 实现了细粒度、预算可控的层跳过，在保持质量竞争力的同时提高批处理填充率并降低解码延迟。

Abstract: Layer skipping can reduce large language model inference costs, but a request budget does not specify how to allocate layer execution across generated tokens. We introduce \method{}, which allows tokens to execute different numbers of layers while matching each request's mean executed depth to a continuous budget. At each routed layer, a router reads the current token state and its request's budget and decides whether to execute the Transformer block. Tokens that skip the block pass through a lightweight residual adapter and write no key/value (K/V) entry at that layer. A single checkpoint serves requests with different budgets in the same batch. Across three LLaMA backbones, six quality metrics, and 13 budgets, AVERT is best or tied for best in 206 of 234 comparisons. Across single- and mixed-budget batches, the mean absolute per-request budget error is at most 1.15 percentage points. When grouping requests by budget leaves batches underfilled, mixing budgets reduces decode time per output token (TPOT) by 4.3--21.2%.

</details>


### [303] [Can Vision-Language Models Synthesize Executable Game-Playing Programs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27741)
*Yao Tong,Jiaxin Qi,Dingyu Zhang,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PlayScriptBench，将 VLM 游戏智能体评测从“逐步交互选动作”转为“从游戏状态合成可执行游戏程序（脚本）”，并设计 Explore–Perception–API Construction–Strategy Composition 四阶段流水线；覆盖 4 类 16 款游戏，强 VLM 仍表现不佳，揭示被动视觉理解与可执行闭环控制间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 游戏基准多采用逐步交互范式：每次动作前都把当前视觉观测送入模型。该范式在真实部署中存在两大缺陷：①游戏循环需等待模型推理，无法实时交互；②反复调用 VLM 成本高昂。同时，现有基准只评测“依据当前视觉状态选择动作”，难以刻画长程策略与程序化控制的综合能力。

Method: 提出基于脚本的游戏基准 PlayScriptBench：要求 VLM 从游戏状态出发，合成可直接执行的游戏程序（脚本化控制器），从而联合评测视觉定位、游戏动态理解、长程策略推理与可执行程序合成。为便于当前通用 VLM 完成评测，设计四阶段流水线：Explore（游戏探索）、Perception（状态感知）、API Construction（接口构建）、Strategy Composition（策略组合），将控制器合成解耦，使智能体逐步构建可执行程序。

Result: 在涵盖 4 种类型、共 16 款游戏上的实验表明，即便强 VLM 在该基准上依然表现吃力，说明被动式视觉游戏理解与可执行闭环游戏控制之间存在实质性差距。

Conclusion: 脚本化控制器合成可作为 VLM 游戏智能体评测的互补方向：它规避逐步推理带来的实时性与成本问题，并更全面地检验视觉感知、动态理解、长程规划与程序生成的综合能力。当前模型能力仍不足以胜任该设定，为该方向留下明确研究空间。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have endowed multimodal agents with increasingly strong visual perception and long-horizon reasoning capabilities, making VLM-based autonomous game playing feasible. Existing game benchmarks primarily evaluate whether an agent can select appropriate actions from the current visual state across different scenarios and games. However, most of them adopt a step-by-step interaction paradigm where the current visual observation is sent to the agent before each action. In practical deployment, this paradigm introduces two fundamental limitations: the game loop must wait for model inference, making real-time interaction impractical, and repeated VLM calls incur substantial cost. Therefore, we propose PlayScriptBench, a script-based game benchmark that evaluates whether VLMs can synthesize an executable game-playing program from game states. This setting jointly evaluates visual grounding, understanding of game dynamics, long-horizon strategic reasoning, and executable program synthesis, providing a more comprehensive assessment of game-playing agents. To support more tractable evaluation for current general-purpose VLMs, we further introduce a four-stage pipeline --- Explore, Perception, API Construction, and Strategy Composition --- that decomposes controller synthesis into game exploration, state perception, interface construction, and strategic control, enabling agents to progressively construct an executable program. Experiments on 16 games spanning 4 genres show that even strong VLMs struggle on our benchmark, revealing a substantial gap between passive visual game understanding and executable closed-loop game control. These results establish script-based controller synthesis as a complementary direction for benchmarking VLM-based game-playing agents.

</details>


### [304] [QuASAR: Toward an LLM-Assisted Framework for Screening, Prototyping, and Assessing Quantum-Advantage Opportunities in Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27778)
*Mark L. Carroll,Jordan A. Caraballo-Vega,Brian P. Powell,John L. Schnase*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuASAR是一个双智能体LLM流水线，从天体物理文献中筛选可量子化的计算内核，经引用核验后自动重构算法、构造量子映射、实现并基准测试原型；十个候选中九个完成小规模原型，三个案例（组合优化、随机模拟、贝叶斯推断）均未在测试规模上取得量子优势，但证明流水线能把已发表想法转化为可运行原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别哪些科学计算适合使用量子算法，需要同时具备领域知识与量子算法设计能力，而这两种专长很少集中于同一人。作者希望用LLM智能体流水线弥合这一鸿沟，降低领域科学家使用量子计算的门槛。

Method: 实验性双智能体LLM驱动流水线：智能体一在科学文献中筛查具有可行量子算法对应物的计算内核，提出带引用的候选；智能体二独立核验这些引用。对通过核验的候选，重构其源计算结构、形式化量子映射、构建并执行原型，并与经典基线进行基准测试。应用于取自天体物理文献的十个候选计算。

Result: 十个候选中有九个被推进到小规模、带基准测试的原型；详细报告了三个冗余度最低的清晰案例，覆盖组合优化、随机模拟与贝叶斯推断。流水线在存在源算法时重构之，在无源算法时构建原创且已披露的映射。三个案例在测试规模上均未实现量子优势，其天体物理设定也尚未经过专家验证。

Conclusion: 实验表明该流水线能将已发表的想法转化为可运行原型，供科学家检视、与经典方法对比并决定是否继续推进。QuASAR作为探索性框架开放给社区评估、批评与进一步发展，其思路有望拓宽量子计算在科研中的使用，并可推广至更一般的计算改进。

Abstract: Identifying scientific computations that could benefit from quantum algorithms requires both domain knowledge and fluency in quantum algorithm design, expertise that rarely resides in one person. We report results from QuASAR, an experimental, two-agent, LLM-driven pipeline intended to bridge this gap. One agent screens scientific literature for computational kernels with a plausible quantum-algorithmic analogue and proposes candidates, each backed by citations. A second agent independently verifies those citations. For candidates that survive verification, it reconstructs the source's computational structure, formulates a quantum mapping, builds and executes a prototype, and benchmarks it against classical baselines. We applied this pipeline to ten candidate computations drawn from the astrophysics literature. Nine candidates were carried through to a small-scale, benchmarked prototype. We report three in detail, chosen as the clearest examples with the least redundancy across the set. They span combinatorial optimization, stochastic simulation, and Bayesian inference. The pipeline attempted to supply quantum-design expertise a domain scientist might lack, reconstructing source algorithms where one existed and building original, disclosed mappings where none did. None of the three case studies achieved quantum advantage at the tested scales, and their astrophysical framing has not yet undergone expert validation. What the experiment does show is that the pipeline can turn a published idea into a runnable prototype, which a scientist can then inspect, test against classical methods, and decide whether to pursue. QuASAR is an exploratory framework, offered to the community for evaluation, critique, and further development, with an approach that could broaden the use of quantum computing in scientific research and extend to computational improvements more generally.

</details>


### [305] [A Benchmark Protocol and Fairness Analysis Towards Open-Set EEG Seizure Type Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27783)
*Targol Bakhtiarvand,Terrance E. Boult,Jugal Kalita,Adham Atyabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次为EEG癫痫发作类型分类建立开放集识别协议，基于TUSZ v2.0.3按临床分类学划分已知/未知类别，评估7种事后开放集打分器，结果显示最佳AUROC仅0.66，开放集识别仍是难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动EEG癫痫发作类型分类系统普遍假设闭集：部署时出现的发作类型均在训练中出现过。但临床中患者会出现罕见或非典型发作类型，落在训练标签空间之外；强制输出错误标签比拒识更有害。因此需要开放集识别协议来评估系统检测未知类型的能力。

Method: 在TUSZ v2.0.3上构建首个开放集识别协议：以临床分类学定义已知/未知类（≥12名受试者的发作类型为已知集，罕见类型为未知集）。将7种事后开放集打分器（MSP、MaxLogit、energy、Mahalanobis、kNN、GHOST、COSTARR）应用于部分微调的EEG基础模型（EFM），采用受试者不相交的留一类出（LOCO）协议，3个随机种子，检验方法能否检测被排除出微调的发作类型。

Result: 真实未知类上，最佳打分器（Mahalanobis）平均AUROC为0.66，且顶尖打分器间差异处于种子间波动范围内。来自未见受试者的留出已知发作类型仅略高于随机水平（所有打分器AUROC 0.54-0.58）。各打分器的受试者级拒识率高度不均；GHOST相对energy在受试者级公平性上无显著提升（双侧Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p≥0.25）。

Conclusion: 作者公开协议、受试者划分与代码作为初始基线，结论表明开放集癫痫发作类型识别仍是困难且未解决的问题。

Abstract: Automated EEG seizure type classification systems are typically trained and evaluated under the closed-set assumption, where all seizure types encountered at deployment are seen during training. In clinical practice, this assumption fails: patients present with rare or atypical seizure types that fall outside the training label space, and a system that forces a wrong label is worse than one that abstains. We introduce the first open-set recognition protocol for EEG seizure type classification on the Temple University Hospital Seizure Corpus (TUSZ v2.0.3), defining known and unknown classes using the clinical taxonomy itself: seizure types with sufficient subject representation (at least 12 subjects) form the known set, and rare types form the unknown set. We evaluate seven post-hoc open-set scorers (MSP, MaxLogit, energy, Mahalanobis, kNN, GHOST, and COSTARR) applied to a partially fine-tuned EEG Foundation Model (EFM), using a subject-disjoint leave-one-class-out (LOCO) protocol over three seeds that tests whether methods can detect seizure types excluded from fine-tuning. On true unknown classes, the best scorer (Mahalanobis) reaches a mean AUROC of 0.66, and differences among the top scorers are within seed-to-seed variation. Held-out known seizure types from unseen subjects are detected only slightly above chance (AUROC 0.54-0.58 for all scorers). Per-subject rejection rates are highly uneven for all scorers, and GHOST does not significantly improve per-subject fairness over energy scoring (two-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank, p >= 0.25). We release the protocol, subject splits, and code as an initial baseline; our results indicate that open-set seizure type recognition remains a hard, open problem.

</details>


### [306] [ChildAI-Gov: An Evidence-Linked AI System for Assessing EdTech Policies Against Children's Digital Rights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27799)
*Naveen Sigalapalli,Huizhi Liang,Clara Crivellaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可解释AI系统ChildAI-Gov，基于“儿童权利影响分数”（CRIS）五维度评估教育科技政策对儿童数字权利的尊重程度，结合大模型判断、确定性规则与RAG证据溯源，并构建57条真实政策数据集AIPolicy-EdTech。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI进入儿童教育的速度快于治理速度，而现有对教育科技工具是否尊重儿童权利的核查仍依赖人工与专家，耗时且难以规模化，缺乏可追溯、可解释的自动化评估手段。

Method: 定义CRIS指标，涵盖安全、隐私、透明度、自主性与非歧视五个维度；系统读取教育科技工具的隐私或AI政策，将gpt-4o-mini的LLM判断与确定性规则校验相结合进行打分；引入RAG组件，从自建儿童权利框架知识库（EU AI Act、UNICEF指南等）中检索治理条款，使每个分数均绑定证据来源；同时构建AIPolicy-EdTech数据集（57条真实政策）。

Result: 五个维度聚合为单一测量的内部一致性良好（Cronbach's alpha = 0.79）；证据接地使评分更严格但保持排序不变；无接地基线明显更宽松，在ChildAI-Gov未通过任何政策的情况下通过了近一半政策。

Conclusion: ChildAI-Gov提供基于书面承诺的证据关联式决策支持，而非对真实世界行为的观测或法律合规性的裁定，定位为审计辅助工具。

Abstract: We present ChildAI-Gov, an explainable AI system for assessing whether education-technology (EdTech) policies respect children's digital rights. Generative AI is reaching children's education faster than its governance can keep pace, yet checking a tool against children's rights remains manual, expert-dependent, and slow. We propose the Child Rights Impact Score (CRIS), an evidence-linked measure across five dimensions: safety, privacy, transparency, autonomy, and non-discrimination. ChildAI-Gov reads an EdTech tool's privacy or AI policy and scores each dimension by combining a large-language-model judgement (gpt-4o-mini) with a deterministic rule check. A retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) component grounds every score in the governing provisions, retrieved from a curated knowledge base of children's rights frameworks (the EU AI Act, UNICEF guidance, and others), so each score is tied to the evidence behind it and an auditor can trace which provision it rests on. We also construct AIPolicy-EdTech, a dataset of 57 real EdTech policies. On it, the five dimensions cohered as a single measure (Cronbach's alpha = 0.79), and grounding made scoring stricter while preserving rankings; an ungrounded baseline was far more lenient, passing nearly half the policies where ChildAI-Gov passed none. It offers evidence-linked decision support on documented commitments, not observation of real-world behaviour or determinations of legal compliance.

</details>


### [307] [SLP-Bench: Evaluating and Improving Acoustic Claims of Reasoning Audio Language Models on Clinical Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27810)
*Côme Hosxe,Rachid Riad,Quentin Spinat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SLP-Bench，将推理型音频语言模型思维链中的每条声学论断与临床医生的感知评分逐条比对，评估事实性、重采样稳定性与改写一致性；十个零样本模型论断平衡准确率仅 0.52–0.58，用 GRPO 微调 7B 模型可提升至 0.60、平均 kappa 从 0.39 升至 0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型音频语言模型在回答临床语音问题时会生成涉及说话人声音属性的思维链，但每条声学论断都需要核实。现有临床语音评测只打分最终答案，无法检验中间论断是否正确，因此缺少对论断级忠实性与稳健性的评估手段。

Method: 构建 SLP-Bench 基准：覆盖 5 个由临床医生评分的语音数据集、25 个感知维度，把思维链中的每条声学论断与同一录音的临床医生感知评分对齐，从三个角度评测——事实性（论断与医生评分是否一致）、重采样下的稳定性、以及对改写问题和不同问题的跨问题一致性。对 10 个开放权重推理型 ALM 做零样本评测，并用 GRPO 以"论断与医生一致"为奖励对 7B 模型微调。

Result: 十个零样本模型论断级平衡准确率 0.52–0.58；论断在重采样与问题改写下会发生变化；一致性最好的模型并非事实性最好。GRPO 微调后，7B 模型论断平衡准确率由 0.54 提升至 0.60，平均 kappa 由 0.39 提升至 0.61，同时保持逐维度问答的答案准确率不降。

Conclusion: 仅看最终答案的评测会掩盖推理型 ALM 在声学论断上的不可靠性；论断级评测（事实性、稳定性、一致性）是必要维度。以临床医生一致性为奖励的强化学习可显著提升论断忠实度而不牺牲答题性能。SLP-Bench 需经各数据提供方审批后开放申请。

Abstract: Audio language models (ALMs) can answer clinical questions from speech, outperforming baselines built on handcrafted acoustic features. Reasoning ALMs generate a chain of thought that refers to properties of the speaker's voice before predicting. Each statement the model makes about the voice needs to be checked against clinicians' perceptual ratings of the same recording. On clinical speech, existing evaluations score only the final answer. We introduce Speech Language Pathologist Bench (SLP-Bench), a benchmark grading each acoustic claim in the chain of thought against clinicians' perceptual ratings of the same recording. It covers five clinician-rated datasets and 25 perceptual dimensions, and assesses claims for factuality, stability under resampling, and consistency across reworded and different questions. Across ten open-weight reasoning ALMs evaluated zero-shot, claim balanced accuracy ranges from 0.52 to 0.58, claims change under resampling and rewording, and the most consistent models are not the most factual. Fine-tuning a 7B ALM with GRPO on a reward for claims that agree with clinicians raises its claim balanced accuracy from 0.54 to 0.60 and its mean kappa from 0.39 to 0.61, while preserving answer accuracy on per-dimension questions. SLP-Bench is available upon request, subject to approval by the providers of each dataset.

</details>


### [308] [Auditable Financial Knowledge Graphs: Schema-Driven Construction and Intrinsic Quality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27835)
*Samarth Sarin,Lovepreet Singh,Dhagash Mehta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可审计、模式驱动的财务时序知识图谱构建方法：用通用文档智能服务解析页面级文档，通过重叠窗口与分阶段LLM抽取结构实体、定量观测与领域关系，经模式校验、多策略实体消解、时序合并约束与细粒度溯源构建图谱，并给出七维加权+两道硬门槛的内在质量评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 财务文档同时包含叙述性披露、会计指标、公司结构、管理层评述与时间相关事实，传统基于文本块（chunk）的RAG难以有效表示；同时缺乏与下游检索/生成解耦的、可诊断的图谱质量评估手段。

Method: 1) 以页面为边界的文档经通用文档智能服务解析；2) 重叠文档窗口上分阶段LLM抽取：结构实体、定量观测、领域特定关系；3) 候选图元素按预定义模式校验；4) 范围感知的实体消解，融合精确、规范化、模糊、嵌入与LLM辅助匹配；5) 时序合并约束、确定性边去重、冲突感知属性管理与细粒度溯源，以保持发行主体身份、报告期有效性与证据可追溯性。

Result: 构建了来自异构财务文档的、证据可溯源（evidence-grounded）的时序知识图谱，并提出内在评估框架：7个加权维度、2个硬性校验门槛及结构诊断指标，将图质量评估与下游检索及答案生成分离。

Conclusion: 该方法为财务知识图谱的构建提供了可审计、可复现的流程与内在质量评估体系，支持系统性诊断与迭代优化；解耦评估使图谱质量可独立衡量，便于后续改进与下游应用。

Abstract: Financial documents combine narrative disclosures, accounting metrics, corporate structures, management commentary, and time-dependent facts, making them difficult to represent using conventional chunk-based retrieval-augmented generation systems. This paper presents an auditable, schema-driven methodology for constructing temporal financial knowledge graphs from page-delimited documents parsed using a general purpose document intelligence service. Overlapping document windows undergo phased large language model extraction of structural entities, quantitative observations, and domain-specific relationships. Candidate graph elements are validated against predefined schemas and consolidated through scope-aware exact, normalized, fuzzy, embedding-based, and LLM-assisted entity resolution. Temporal merge constraints, deterministic edge deduplication, conflict-aware property management, and fine-grained provenance preserve issuer identity, reporting-period validity, and traceability to source evidence. The contributions of this work comprise the proposed methodology for financial knowledge graph construction, the creation of an evidence-grounded temporal knowledge graph from heterogeneous financial documents, and an intrinsic framework for evaluating the quality of the resulting graph. The evaluation framework assesses seven weighted dimensions, two hard validation gates, and structural diagnostics. By separating graph-quality assessment from downstream retrieval and answer generation, the framework supports systematic diagnosis and iterative refinement of the constructed graph.

</details>


### [309] [Beyond Self-Critique: Epistemic Convergence Control for Multi-Agent Research Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27839)
*Chrisantus Eze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体科研验证框架 \sys{}：用非对称角色架构（Advocate/Skeptic/Arbitrator）降低角色表征同质化，用 Belief-Delta 收敛准则自适应停止辩论，并将每条被支持的论断锚定到可审计的原文片段，在提升争议性主张检出率的同时降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于角色分工的 LLM 辩论式科学主张验证存在两大缺陷：(1) 辩论智能体由同一基座模型实例化，共享相同潜在表征，导致自我批评偏差在结构上不可避免；(2) 固定迭代协议要么在证据稳定前过早终止，要么在收敛后继续浪费算力。论文旨在同时解决这两个失效模式。

Method: 三点设计：(1) 非对称角色架构——Advocate、Skeptic、Arbitrator 具备对立的认知指令（opposing epistemic directives），以降低角色嵌入相似度；(2) Belief-Delta（\DB{}）收敛判据——基于证据的停止规则，在信念增量低于阈值时终止迭代；(3) 证据锚定与审计——每条被支持的主张绑定逐字文献片段，并由独立的 NLI 分类器核查。

Result: 角色嵌入相似度从对称 MAD 的 0.918 降至 0.852（p = 5.82×10⁻⁸）；争议性主张暴露率 22.0% vs. 15.4%；过度迭代率 0.0% vs. Fixed-3 的 62.5%，同时 LLM 调用量减少 2.4 倍；幻觉率 21.7%，与 Self-Refine 批评者的 25.9% 相当（p = 0.61），且具备可审计的证据溯源。实验规模为 40 个研究假设、200 次运行，基座模型 DeepSeek-R1-32B。

Conclusion: \sys{} 在推理计算效率与严格的认知验证之间取得平衡：非对称角色分工缓解自评偏差，自适应收敛判据消除过度迭代，证据锚定提供可审计性。局限在于幻觉率并未显著优于 Self-Refine 批评者，其优势主要体现在计算效率与可审计性维度。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems for scientific claim verification increasingly rely on role-structured LLM debate to pressure-test hypotheses. However, when debating agents are instantiated from the same underlying base model, they share identical latent representations, making self-critique bias structurally inevitable. Static fixed-iteration protocols compound this by either halting prematurely before evidence stabilizes or wasting inference compute after convergence. We present \sys{}, a multi-agent research verification framework that addresses both failure modes. First, we introduce an asymmetric role architecture comprising an Advocate, Skeptic, and Arbitrator with opposing epistemic directives, producing significantly lower role-embedding similarity (0.852 vs.\ 0.918 for symmetric Multi-Agent Debate [MAD], $p = 5.82 \times 10^{-8}$) and surfacing more contested claims (22.0\% vs.\ 15.4\%). Second, we propose the Belief-Delta ($\DB$) convergence criterion, an evidence-grounded stopping rule that eliminates over-iteration (0.0\% vs.\ 62.5\% for Fixed-$3$) while cutting LLM calls by 2.4$\times$. Third, each supported claim is anchored to a verbatim literature snippet audited by an independent NLI classifier, achieving a 21.7\% hallucination rate (comparable to 25.9\% for a Self-Refine critic, $p = 0.61$) while establishing auditable evidence grounding. Across 200 runs on 40 research hypotheses evaluated with DeepSeek-R1-32B, \sys{} balances compute efficiency with rigorous epistemic verification.

</details>


### [310] [SLICR: Strategy Logic-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27865)
*Arshia Rafieioskouie,Matthew Lucas,Tzu-Han Hsu,Borzoo Bonakdarpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SLICR 将策略逻辑（Strategy Logic, SL）与 LTL 结合，把形式化规范转化为整形奖励，用于部分可观测对抗环境下的规范引导强化学习，并在 Predator-Prey、Google Research Football 与自建 SMAC-HARD 扩展基准上验证了有效性、可扩展性与对多样对抗策略的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范引导 RL 多依赖 LTL 等对单一智能体行为进行约束的规范，难以在对抗、部分可观测场景中表达"对其他智能体策略的响应"这一需求，因此需要更强的规范语言支撑。

Method: 采用 Strategy Logic（SL）对智能体策略进行显式量化，并用 LTL 刻画目标与约束，从而同时推理"应如何行动"与"应如何回应他者策略与行为"；再将此类规范转换为整形奖励，驱动 RL 训练（SLICR）。

Result: 在 Predator-Prey、Google Research Football 以及自研的 SMAC-HARD 扩展三个基准上进行评测，结果表明 SLICR 具备有效性、可扩展性，并能适应多样化的对抗策略。

Conclusion: SLICR 将规范引导 RL 推进到对抗性与部分可观测设定，说明基于策略量化的规范形式在复杂多智能体任务中具有实用价值；摘要未给出具体性能提升幅度与消融细节。

Abstract: Specification-guided reinforcement learning (RL) translates formal specifications of desired behavior of agents into shaped rewards. This paper introduces SLICR, a significant paradigm shift that advances the state of the art to specification-guided RL in adversarial settings under partial observability. We employ Strategy Logic (SL), which allows explicit quantification over agents' strategies and uses the linear temporal logic (LTL) to specify the agents' objectives and constraints. This enables reasoning not only about how agents should act to satisfy a specification, but also about how they should respond to the strategies and behaviors of other agents. We evaluate SLICR on three popular benchmarks: Predator-Prey, Google Research Football, and our home-grown extension of SMAC-HARD, demonstrating its effectiveness, scalability, and ability to adapt to diverse adversarial strategies.

</details>


### [311] [Integrating Generative Artificial Intelligence with Learning Style Models: Opportunities, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27868)
*Malithi Nawarathne,Prasanna Haddela,Nuwan Kodagoda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述遵循PRISMA 2020指南，检索Scopus、IEEE Xplore与Google Scholar（2015–2026），纳入41项研究，将生成式AI能力映射到VARK、Kolb、Felder-Silverman及Honey & Mumford等学习风格模型，以支持大规模在线课堂中的学生投入，并总结实施现状、益处、挑战与未来研究路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线课堂中学生投入度不足，缺乏个性化支持；生成式AI具备灵活、多模态与实时自适应能力，有望与既有学习风格模型结合以改进学习者中心的教学设计，但目前缺乏系统性的证据梳理。

Method: 系统性文献综述：依据PRISMA 2020流程，检索Scopus、IEEE Xplore、Google Scholar（2015–2026年），筛选出41篇符合纳入标准的研究，进行生成式AI能力与学习风格偏好的映射、现有实施案例的综合分析，并归纳优势与挑战。

Result: 在纳入的41项研究中，生成式AI的多模态内容生成、对话式交互与实时反馈等能力可与四类学习风格模型的偏好维度形成对应；现有实施显示出个性化内容与即时适应的潜力，同时存在学习风格测量效度争议、技术可靠性与教学整合等方面的挑战。

Conclusion: 尽管学习风格的实证效度仍存争议，生成式AI仍能实现灵活、多模态、实时的学习适应；本研究为教育者与开发者构建更有效、以学习者为中心的学习系统提供实践启示，并提出未来研究与实践路径。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (Generative AI) has emerged as a powerful tool for transforming learning. This review examines how Generative AI can be integrated with established learning style models (VARK, Kolb, Felder-Silverman, and Honey & Mumford) to support student engagement in large online classes. Following PRISMA 2020 guidelines, we searched Scopus, IEEE Xplore, and Google Scholar (2015 to 2026) and included 41 studies that met the eligibility criteria. The review maps Generative AI capabilities to learning style preferences, synthesizes existing implementations, highlights benefits and challenges, and proposes pathways for future research and practice. Despite ongoing debates regarding the empirical validity of learning styles, Generative AI enables flexible, multimodal, and real-time adaptation. This study offers practical insights for educators and developers seeking to build more effective, learner centered learning systems

</details>


### [312] [Speculative Decoding Is Less Speculative Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27891)
*Guanning Zeng,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DICE通过将采样随机性（预采样并同步的"抽签"）引入草稿构建，使有限草稿计算更集中于目标模型实际会走的路径，在无需额外训练、不改变目标采样分布的前提下实现SOTA解码吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码的效率取决于有限草稿计算能否准确预判目标模型实际采样路径。该路径的不确定性来自两方面：目标模型预测与采样随机性。前者需一次目标前向才能获知，后者却可在草稿前固定。现有方法多只利用草稿器的预测，忽视了可提前获知的采样随机性，造成草稿计算分配不够有针对性。

Method: 提出DICE（Draw-Informed Candidate Selection）：在草稿与验证阶段预采样并同步验证随机数；将该已知抽签与现有草稿器预测结合，评估候选续写在实际采样中被采用的概率，并据此分配计算。该原则用于草稿树构建中的节点选择，并扩展至异步预取，用于预测下一轮应准备的草稿。方法不增加草稿器训练，也不改变目标采样分布。

Result: 在多种设置下取得SOTA解码吞吐，相较强基线最高带来24.6个百分点的加速。

Conclusion: 将可提前确定的采样随机性显式纳入草稿候选筛选，是提升投机解码计算效率的有效且正交的途径；DICE为草稿树构建与异步预取提供了统一准则，且保持采样分布不变、无需额外训练。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by preparing candidate continuations before the target model generates them. Its efficiency depends on how well limited drafting computation anticipates the path the target will actually follow. Anticipating this path involves two sources of uncertainty: the target model's predictions and the randomness of sampling. Yet the former requires a target-model forward pass, while the latter can be fixed before drafting. Building on this observation, we introduce DICE (Draw-Informed Candidate Selection), which pre-samples and synchronizes verification randomness across drafting and verification. By combining these known draws with predictions from an existing drafter, DICE allocates computation toward continuations more likely to be used in the current sampling run. This principle guides node selection throughout draft-tree construction and extends to asynchronous prefetching, where DICE predicts which next-round drafts to prepare. Without additional drafter training or changes to the target sampling distribution, DICE achieves state-of-the-art decoding throughput across diverse settings, with speedups of up to 24.6pp over strong baselines.

</details>


### [313] [The Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework: Scalable Multimodal UX Attribution in Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27899)
*Yi Ding,Gaurav Kumar,Rui Min,Isabella Ye,Bhavika Goyal,Emilio Antunez,Huihui Chen,Ke Xu,Xiaowei Sun,Xin Xu,Huijie Feng,Pradyumna Narayana,Golnaz Farhadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework：Tier 1用微调encoder-decoder LLM做可扩展的用户情感回归检测，Tier 2用多模态LLM对页面渲染与布局做细粒度诊断，实现可扩展、准确且可归因的UX评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模网页搜索中维持UX质量需要可扩展的评测。传统问卷速度慢，成为快速A/B测试的瓶颈；自动化指标又缺乏足够粒度，难以诊断视觉-空间层面的UX异常。

Method: 双层架构。Tier 1（宏观系统）：基于Gemini XS微调的encoder-decoder LLM，输入遥测特征，通过多任务正则化与上下文特征工程进行用户情感回归检测。Tier 2（微观系统）：用多模态LLM（Gemini 3.0 Pro）原生评估搜索结果块的视觉渲染与空间布局，以"User Toil"（达到满意结果所需估计努力）量化，并结合RL自动prompt优化与AI judge识别win/loss模式。

Result: Tier 1在50万+留出记录上PR-AUC提升+9.8%，在12个留出实验中与基线召回持平且精确率相对提升约20%；线上将测量时延从数周降至数小时，问卷开销降低40%以上。Tier 2的MLLM F1提升>6.5%。系统每日处理O(100k)线上实验查询。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了可扩展、准确且具诊断能力的UX归因，兼顾宏观回归检测与微观视觉-空间诊断。

Abstract: Sustaining user experience (UX) quality in large-scale web search requires scalable evaluation. Traditional surveys are slow and bottleneck rapid A/B testing, while automated metrics often lack the granularity to diagnose visual-spatial UX anomalies. To address this, we introduce the Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework, a novel industrial architecture for automated UX attribution. Tier 1 (Macro System) acts as a scalable user sentiment regression detector, utilizing a fine-tuned encoder-decoder LLM (based on Gemini XS) on telemetry features. Through multi-task regularization and contextual feature engineering, this tier achieves a +9.8% PR-AUC gain on over 500,000 held-out records, matching baseline recall on all validated regressions with $\sim$20% relative precision gain among the 12 held-out experiments. In production, it reduces measurement latency from weeks to hours and cuts survey overhead by over 40%. Tier 2 (Micro System) provides granular diagnostics by employing a Multimodal LLM (Gemini 3.0 Pro) to natively assess visual renderings and spatial layout of web result blocks, quantifying "User Toil" as the estimated effort required to reach a satisfactory result. Enhanced by RL-based automated prompt optimization and an AI judge for win/loss patterns, the MLLM yields a $>$6.5% F1 score improvement, offering deep insights into content quality and layout friction. Ultimately, this framework enables scalable, accurate, and diagnostic UX attribution, processing O(100k) live experiment queries daily.

</details>


### [314] [Functional Depth Is Learned: Isolating the Computation That Layers Produce Only Together](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27911)
*JaeSeong Kim,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出精确的容斥分解（inclusion-exclusion decomposition）来隔离"连通分量"——即仅在指定层集合在恒等消融（identity ablation）下共同作用时才出现的计算，以刻画Transformer的功能深度。在Pythia、OLMo、Amber的公开检查点上追踪该量，发现：跨层连通协同随预训练增强；连通分量在直接干预下行为上重要；成对表示相似度与成对连通协同负相关但二者不可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer性能随深度扩展，但层数只统计了串行计算的机会；层可能只是近乎独立的细化和，彼此依赖很弱。因此需要区分"架构深度"与"功能深度"，刻画层与层之间的真实依赖关系如何随预训练发展。

Method: 引入精确的容斥分解，在恒等消融条件下隔离出连通分量（connected component），即只有指定层集合共同作用时才显现的计算；以写入相对（write-relative）的方式度量跨层连通协同，并在Pythia、OLMo、Amber的公开检查点上跨多个交互阶数追踪该量；同时做直接干预（删除连通分量对比删除同范数随机方向）以及成对表示相似度与成对连通协同的相关性分析，并比较基于相似度与基于协同的块删除准则。

Result: 三点发现：（1）所有测量交互阶数上的写入相对连通协同都从初始化或最早检查点基线出发，随预训练持续增强；（2）连通分量在直接干预下具有行为后果：在全部35个模型-阶数单元中，删除它们对困惑度的损害均大于删除同位置同范数的随机方向；（3）在七个模型上，成对表示相似度与成对连通协同始终负相关，但两者不可互换——相似度准则与协同准则的块删除在Pythia上常一致，在Amber上则出现分歧。

Conclusion: 架构深度只提供串行计算的机会，而预训练过程逐步在该深度内组织出跨层依赖关系，即功能深度是训练涌现的结果而非层数自动带来的。

Abstract: Transformer performance scales with depth, but layer count merely counts the opportunities for sequential computation: layers could still behave as nearly independent refinements that barely depend on one another. We study how transformers develop functional depth during pretraining, introducing an exact inclusion-exclusion decomposition that isolates the connected component, i.e., the computation that appears only when a specified set of layers acts together under identity ablation. Tracking this quantity across public checkpoints of Pythia, OLMo, and Amber yields three findings. First, write-relative connected coordination strengthens from its initialization or earliest-checkpoint baseline as pretraining proceeds, across all measured interaction orders. Second, these connected components are behaviorally consequential under direct intervention: deleting them damages perplexity more than deleting a norm-matched random direction at the same site in all 35 measured model-order cells. Third, pairwise representation similarity is consistently negatively correlated with pairwise connected coordination across seven models, yet the two signals are not interchangeable: similarity- and coordination-based block-removal criteria often agree in Pythia but diverge on Amber. Together, these results suggest that architectural depth provides the opportunity for sequential computation, while pretraining progressively organizes cross-layer dependencies within that depth.

</details>


### [315] [BioEmu2: Scalable Emulation of Biomolecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27924)
*Michael Gastegger,Adam E. Foster,José Jiménez-Luna,Tim Hempel,Sarah Lewis,Yu Xie,Lixin Sun,Iryna Zaporozhets,J. Thorben Frank,Yaoyi Chen,Michael Plainer,Ludwig Winkler,Pedro B. P. S. Reis,Sven H. Giese,Roberto Sordillo,Victor García Satorras,Eray Inanc,Kacper Kapuśniak,Soojung Yang,Rishwanth Raghu,Rachel Kim,Gabriel Dernbach,Deniz Gunceler,Hannes Schulz,Bonnie Kruft,Marwin Segler,Christopher M. Bishop,Cecilia Clementi,Ryota Tomioka,Frank Noé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出生成模型BioEmu2，统一静态结构预测、平衡采样与时间演化，采样速度比MD快数个数量级，支持蛋白/核酸/修饰残基/配体/离子多组分体系的原子级结构生成与动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构预测已有重大进展，但大规模建模驱动生物分子功能的运动与相互作用仍是核心难题；MD计算代价过高，难以规模化。

Method: 训练于实验与合成结构以及大量MD数据；采用条件生成框架，支持以残基接触为条件、以用户自定义偏置势进行引导；引入上下文并行（context parallel）模式以扩展至超大体系；具备增强采样能力。

Result: 结构预测精度与顶级开源折叠模型相当；可预测兆道尔顿级复合物；平衡分布接近MD参考，测试体系自由能误差约1 kcal/mol；可外推长时间尺度轨迹并预测结合与构象变化的动力学；可计算涉及稀有态的自由能。

Conclusion: BioEmu2使生物分子系综与动力学研究具备可扩展性，将结构预测、平衡采样与时间演化整合于单一生成模型，显著降低动力学建模成本。

Abstract: Despite major advances in structure prediction, modelling the motions and interactions that drive biomolecular function at scale remains a central challenge. We introduce BioEmu2, a generative model for atomically resolved biomolecular structures combining static structure prediction, equilibrium sampling and time evolution, that samples orders of magnitude faster than molecular dynamics (MD). Trained on experimental and synthetic structures and extensive MD data, it supports multi-component systems containing proteins, nucleic acids, modified residues, ligands and ions. Structure-prediction accuracy is comparable to top open-weight biomolecular folding models, and a context parallel mode enables prediction of megadalton complexes. Equilibrium distributions approximate MD references with free-energy errors near 1 kcal/mol across tested proteins and complexes. Time propagation predicts long-timescale dynamical trajectories and kinetics of binding and conformational changes. Generated structures can be controlled by conditioning on residue contacts or steering with user-defined bias potentials. Enhanced sampling enables free energy calculation involving rare states. BioEmu2 makes the study of biomolecular ensembles and dynamics scalable.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [316] [One Behavior, Out of Order: Deterministic Execution and Parallel Simulation of ROS 2 Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26676)
*Chengzi Huang,Behnam Khodabandeloo,Duc Anh Nguyen,Chuanchao Gao,Wang Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种让ROS 2应用确定性的轻量级协调机制，并基于预知依赖关系实现乱序并行仿真算法，在不修改应用与ROS 2的前提下加速测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2异步发布-订阅通信使应用行为依赖调度与平台，同一输入在不同运行、不同机器上表现不同，导致故障难以复现、测试不可靠；同时大规模确定性应用的测试耗时过长。

Method: 1) 为每个组件（含事件驱动组件）规定在预定义时刻读写输入输出，将计算与通信解耦，使应用确定性化；2) 由这些读写时刻在运行前推导激活间依赖，提出乱序并行仿真算法，某个激活的依赖一完成即启动；3) 在仿真器中实现该算法，兼容现有ROS 2应用与ROS 2本身，无需改动。

Result: 在工业用例上比同步并行仿真快至多2.6倍；相比异步并行仿真，消息内存需求低一个数量级。同时返回精确结论（收敛）确定性使测试可重复，乱序并行仿真在保持确定性的同时显著提速并降低内存开销。

Conclusion: 通过时间点协调+依赖预分析的乱序并行仿真，可在零侵入条件下兼顾ROS 2应用的确定性与测试效率。

Abstract: System-level simulation is widely used to validate robotic applications before hardware deployment. However, Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2), a popular middleware for such applications, uses asynchronous publish-subscribe communication, which can make application behavior depend on scheduling and the platform. The behavior can then vary across runs and machines even with the same inputs. This nondeterminism makes faults hard to reproduce and tests unreliable. In this paper, we first give ROS 2 applications a lightweight coordination scheme in which every component, including event-driven ones, reads its inputs and writes its outputs at predefined time instants. By decoupling computation and communication, the scheme makes an application deterministic, that is, it always has one behavior for given inputs. Tests are thus repeatable, but for large applications they still take a long time. To speed up these tests, we then present an out-of-order parallel simulation algorithm. From the predefined read and write instants, it knows before the run which activations of components depend on which. It starts each activation as soon as those it depends on have finished. Finally, we implement the algorithm in a simulator that runs existing ROS 2 applications without any change to them or to ROS 2. We evaluate the simulator on industrial use cases and synthetic graphs. On the industrial use cases, it is up to 2.6 times faster than synchronous parallel simulation and needs an order of magnitude less memory for messages than asynchronous parallel simulation.

</details>


### [317] [Latent Space Planning with Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26706)
*Hrishikesh Viswanath,Anindita Ghosh,Uttaran Bhattacharya,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将潜空间规划表述为由序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）引导的优化，结合梯度优化器更新规划，并引入基于PDE的代价-到达（cost-to-go）引导采样分布演化，在导航与操作任务上显著优于梯度法、采样法与MPPI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习世界模型的规划中，采样类规划器在长时域上表现不佳，梯度类方法易陷入局部最优。MPPI虽为互补方案，但每轮迭代都从高斯提议重新采样，仅靠重要性权重逼近目标分布；且"偏好到达目标轨迹"的目标分布难以直接定义，需借助中间分布序列。SMC天然适合通过重要性加权与重采样演化该分布序列，故以此为基础重构潜空间规划。

Method: 将潜空间规划建模为以SMC引导的优化：维护动作序列种群，通过重要性加权与重采样演化分布，同时用梯度优化器更新规划；并借鉴经典规划思想，引入由PDE推导的cost-to-go，利用潜状态空间结构引导样本分布演化。在具有视觉与本体感受世界模型的导航和操作任务上评估。

Result: 平均成功率上较梯度规划器分别高29与50个百分点，较采样规划器高35个百分点，较MPPI高7个百分点；迭代次数少于梯度规划器，且与MPPI相差在4%以内。

Conclusion: SMC引导的优化框架能有效结合种群演化与梯度更新，PDE导出的cost-to-go进一步改善分布演化，从而在成功率与计算效率上同时取得优势，优于现有梯度、采样和MPPI类规划方法。

Abstract: Planning with learned world models amounts to finding action sequences whose predicted rollouts reach a goal. Sampling-based planners often struggle at longer horizons, while gradient-based methods can become trapped in local minima. Meanwhile, model predictive path integral control (MPPI), a complementary approach, alternates between estimating an expectation under a cost-weighted distribution from samples and updating the plan from this estimate. Its samples, however, are drawn afresh at every iteration from a Gaussian proposal and reach the target distribution only through importance weights. Moreover, a target distribution favoring goal-reaching trajectories can be difficult to define directly, motivating a sequence of intermediate distributions. Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) provides a natural mechanism for evolving such a sequence through importance weighting and resampling. Motivated by this, we formulate latent-space planning as optimization guided by SMC, which moves a population of action sequences while a gradient-based optimizer updates the plan. Inspired by classical planning, we further introduce a PDE-derived cost-to-go that guides the evolution of the sample distribution using the structure of the latent state space. On navigation and manipulation tasks with visual and proprioceptive world models, our formulation outperforms gradient-based planners by $29$ and $50$, sampling-based planners by $35$ and MPPI by $7$ percentage points in average success rate, while succeeding in fewer iterations than gradient-based planners and within 4\% of MPPI.

</details>


### [318] [FORTH: Grounding Tactile VLA Execution via Forward-Only Proxy-Control Pairing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26744)
*Junde Li,Wei-Han Yu,Xiangyi Chu,Yaoxin Li,Jiawei Xu,Ka-Fai Un,Pui-In Mak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文指出“代理损失最小化即意味着物理力接地”的假设不成立，并提出纯前向测量框架FORTH，用触觉编码器、零阶适配器与SlipGuard连续触觉门控在三个层级上把优化目标与物理控制观测量显式配对，在易塑性结构件上以低至1/29的峰值夹持力避免永久形变，并在LIBERO三套件上达到97.6%成功率、峰值激活内存降低7.12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略普遍被认为具备力控能力，但这一判断建立在未经检验的假设之上：代理损失（proxy loss）的下降被视为物理力接地的证据。作者希望检验该假设，并揭示其在部署层面的失效来源。

Method: 先在三层次上论证假设失效：冻结视觉主干丢弃接触几何；下游损失掩盖token级梯度对齐；离散指示量引发优化崩溃。随后提出FORTH——前向测量与执行框架，含三个接地支柱：(1) 层局部、纯前向的触觉编码器，无需反向传播即保留空间接触几何；(2) token并行的零阶适配器，实现激活空间更新；(3) SlipGuard，在初期滑移发生时触发的连续触觉验证门控。

Result: 在弹塑性结构壳体上，FORTH闭环门控的峰值夹持力比单调反射斜坡低29倍，基线造成永久形变而FORTH避免结构压溃；在三个LIBERO评测套件上平均成功率97.6%，与SOTA执行性能持平；同一主干上相比反向传播步，峰值激活内存降低7.12倍。

Conclusion: 代理损失最小化并不等价于物理力接地；将各层优化目标与控制观测量显式配对、并以纯前向方式获取触觉接地信号，可在保持SOTA任务成功率的同时显著降低力与内存成本，即“更强的感知带来更深的物理智能”。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are widely credited with force control, operating on the unverified assumption that proxy loss minimization implies physical force grounding. In this work, we demonstrate that this assumption breaks down at three core deployment levels: frozen visual backbones discard contact geometry, downstream losses mask token-level gradient alignment, and discrete indicators trigger optimization collapse. To address these disconnections, we present FORTH, a forward-only measurement harness and execution framework that explicitly pairs the optimization objective at each level with its physical control observable. FORTH incorporates three grounded pillars: a layer-local, forward-only tactile encoder that preserves spatial contact geometry without backpropagation, a token-parallel zeroth-order adapter enabling activation-space updates, and SlipGuard, a continuous tactile verification gate that fires on incipient slip. On an elasto-plastic structural shell, FORTH's closed-loop gate peaks at 29 times lower grip force than a monotone reflex ramp, preventing structural crush where the baseline leaves permanent deformation. Furthermore, FORTH matches state-of-the-art execution performance with a 97.6% average success rate across three evaluated LIBERO suites, while reducing peak activation memory by 7.12 times compared to a backpropagated step on the same backbone. Enhanced perception enables deeper physical intelligence.

</details>


### [319] [MomentMem: Memorizing Key Moments for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26745)
*Guangyu Zhao,Yongchang Zhang,Ruizhang Jiang,Zhihong Yu,Yuhao Zhang,Tao Wu,Qimin Zhou,Kunsong Shi,Haoran Wen,Zeyu Zhang,Yin Li,Kun Zhan,Yiwu Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出视觉时刻记忆任务与VMBench基准，并设计分层架构MomentMem（VLM记忆器选关键帧+执行器条件动作预测），在VMBench上平均成功率49.9%，超最强基线31.7个百分点，记忆器可零样本迁移并可少样本适配新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作常需记住已不可观测的视觉信息（如物体原始位置）。现有方法依赖完整观测历史（长时序下冗余）或文本摘要（丢失精确视觉细节），缺乏对"关键视觉时刻记忆"的系统研究，也缺少对应评测基准。

Method: 形式化"视觉时刻记忆"（visual moment memory）问题；构建VMBench——需记忆空间位置、视觉外观及其关联的长时序操作任务集；提出MomentMem分层架构：由VLM记忆器选择关键帧，执行器以所选记忆为条件预测动作，从而将记忆选择与闭环任务成功解耦评估，并独立研究记忆器泛化性。

Result: VMBench上平均成功率49.9%，领先最强基线31.7个百分点；记忆器对未见外观可零样本迁移，仅需少量记忆标注即可适配新任务（43.7% vs 全监督44.3%）；在RMBench上具竞争力，真机操作亦有提升。

Conclusion: 保留稀疏视觉证据对依赖记忆的操作任务价值显著；记忆与执行分离的设计兼具可评估性与可迁移性，为长时序记忆型操作提供新范式。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires remembering visual information that is no longer observable, such as the original location of an object. Existing approaches typically rely on full observation histories, which become redundant over long horizons, or text summaries, which may omit precise visual details. We study visual moment memory, the ability to retain such details from key moments for subsequent manipulation. To support this study, we introduce VMBench, a suite of long-horizon manipulation tasks requiring memory of spatial locations, visual appearances, and their associations. We further propose MomentMem, a hierarchical architecture in which a VLM-based memorizer selects keyframes and an executor predicts actions conditioned on the selected memories. This separation enables us to evaluate memory selection alongside closed-loop task success and to study memorizer generalization independently of the executor. On VMBench, MomentMem achieves 49.9% average success, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 31.7 percentage points. Moreover, we find that the memorizer transfers well to unseen appearances zero-shot. It can also adapt to an unseen task with only a few memory annotations, reaching 43.7% success, close to the 44.3% obtained with full memory supervision. Further experiments show competitive performance on RMBench and improvements in real-robot manipulation. These results highlight the value of retaining sparse visual evidence for memory-dependent manipulation. Our code is available at https://github.com/PKU-VaLuE-Lab/MomentMem.

</details>


### [320] [Search-Informed MPPI for Kinodynamic Motion Planning via Monte Carlo Tree Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26763)
*Ihab S. Mohamed,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SI-MPPI统一框架，将MPPI轨迹优化嵌入MCTS树搜索，通过结构化中间目标（baits）引导扩展，在大型杂乱2D占据栅格环境中以更小的搜索树和更平滑的角控制实现运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模杂乱环境中的动力学约束运动规划需在长时域内高效生成动力学可行且无碰撞的轨迹。MCTS擅长长时域策略性探索，但在连续控制空间分支因子大且依赖随机默认策略rollout；MPPI擅长局部轨迹优化但易陷入障碍物导致的局部极小。二者互补需求催生统一框架。

Method: 将MCTS的扩展与默认策略仿真阶段融合为单一MPPI扩展阶段，由结构化中间目标（baits）引导：自适应bait处理规则保留几何有效的baits作为局部目标，驱动GPU批量MPPI优化生成动力学可行的候选运动片段；可行性门控检查碰撞安全与进展充分性，top-k选择将最低代价的通过候选作为树边，形成稀疏有向树；ESS（有效样本量）引导的自适应热启动后续扩展，基于代价的反向传播更新树统计量。

Result: 在四个大型杂乱2D占据栅格地图上，SI-MPPI相比三个动力学基线始终生成最小的搜索树和最平滑的角控制曲线，同时规划时间与路径长度保持竞争力。消融实验表明GPU批处理、自适应bait处理及保留多个扩展候选对规划效率与鲁棒性至关重要。

Conclusion: SI-MPPI通过将局部优化（MPPI）与长时域搜索（MCTS）深度耦合，并以bait引导与可行性门控保证稀疏树增长，有效缓解了MCTS分支爆炸与MPPI局部极小两类问题，是一种高效、鲁棒的动力学运动规划方案。

Abstract: Kinodynamic motion planning in large-scale, cluttered environments remains challenging, as dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories must be computed efficiently over long horizons. Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) can strategically explore long-horizon alternatives but faces large branching factors in continuous control spaces and often relies on random default-policy rollouts, whereas Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) efficiently optimizes local trajectories yet remains susceptible to obstacle-induced local minima. To overcome these challenges, we propose Search-Informed MPPI (SI-MPPI), a unified framework that embeds MPPI trajectory optimization within MCTS-based tree search. The core concept is to fuse the expansion and default-policy simulation stages of MCTS into a single MPPI expansion stage guided by structured intermediate targets, termed baits. During each expansion, an adaptive bait-handling rule retains geometrically valid baits as local targets for GPU-batched MPPI optimizations that generate dynamically feasible candidate motion segments. Feasibility gating then checks these candidates for collision safety and sufficient progress, and top-$k$ selection admits the lowest-cost accepted candidates as tree edges, yielding sparse, directed tree growth; thereafter, effective sample size (ESS)-guided adaptation warm-starts subsequent expansions, while cost-based backpropagation updates tree statistics. Extensive simulations across four large-scale, cluttered 2D occupancy-grid maps demonstrate that SI-MPPI consistently produces the smallest search trees and smoothest angular control profiles compared with three kinodynamic baselines, while maintaining competitive planning times and path lengths. Ablation results further highlight the importance of GPU batching, adaptive bait handling, and retaining multiple expansion candidates for planning efficiency and robustness.

</details>


### [321] [TacHOI: Contact-Aware Object Reconstruction and Tracking for Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26764)
*Jackson Codd,Haoyu Li,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacHOI：无需训练，融合触觉接触与单目视觉实现手-物联合重建与跟踪，无需物体模板或深度传感器，显著降低度量尺度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频可为机器人学习提供可扩展的人类演示数据，但单目视频恢复物体度量几何与运动受尺度-深度歧义和手部遮挡影响；仅依赖图像派生的接触与形状先验无法唯一确定度量尺度。

Method: 提出免训练框架TacHOI：以预训练视觉估计为起点，用触觉接触约束物体相对估计手部的深度；利用度量标定手部上空间分离的多点接触作为物体尺寸的"物理标尺"；将上述约束与视觉对齐联合，优化手部位姿、物体轨迹及各向异性物体尺度。

Result: 在100个ARCTIC与HO3D序列（合成触觉观测）上，TacHOI取得所评估基线中最低的中位ADD-S*，ARCTIC误差由15.6 cm降至3.6 cm，HO3D达2.8 cm；在OpenTouch上的额外实验证明其对实测触觉观测同样适用。

Conclusion: 触觉接触可作为物理尺度约束有效消解单目尺度歧义，实现无模板、无深度传感器的手-物联合重建与跟踪，并具备向真实触觉观测迁移的能力。

Abstract: Egocentric videos offer a scalable source of human demonstrations for robot learning. However, recovering metric object geometry and motion from monocular video is hindered by scale-depth ambiguity and hand-induced occlusion, while image-derived contact and shape priors alone cannot uniquely determine metric scale. We introduce TacHOI, a training-free framework that integrates tactile contact with monocular vision for joint hand-object reconstruction and tracking, without requiring object templates or depth sensors. Starting from pretrained visual estimates, TacHOI uses tactile contact to constrain object depth relative to the estimated hand, while spatially separated contacts on a metrically calibrated hand provide a physical ruler for object extent. It incorporates these constraints into a joint optimization of hand poses, object trajectories, and anisotropic object scale alongside visual alignment. Across 100 ARCTIC and HO3D sequences with synthesized tactile observations, TacHOI achieves the lowest median ADD-S* among the evaluated baselines, reducing error from 15.6 to 3.6 cm on ARCTIC and achieving 2.8 cm on HO3D. Additional experiments on OpenTouch demonstrate its applicability to measured tactile observations.

</details>


### [322] [Acoustic Markers of Engagement in Children with Autism Spectrum Disorder and Their Caregivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26810)
*Young Suh Hong,Kaleen Shrestha,Austin Joel Dympep,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用家庭环境下社会辅助机器人(SAR)干预数据，发现儿童与照护者共享三个与投入度显著相关的声学标记（AlphaRatio、HNR、SlopeV500-1500），且儿童的状态依赖性声学变异幅度显著大于照护者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征用户投入度对儿童自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)的人机交互(HCI)应用至关重要；家庭场景中照护者常作为对话引导者，因此需同时分析儿童与照护者的发声以刻画互动动态，而现有工作对此关注不足。

Method: 基于家庭SAR干预数据集，提取儿童与照护者的声学特征，采用线性混合效应模型(LMM)识别能显著区分投入与未投入的声学特征；并构建State × Group交互模型，比较两组标准化状态相关差异的幅度。

Result: 儿童与照护者共享三个显著的投入度声学标记：平均Alpha Ratio、平均谐噪比(HNR)与平均谱斜率500-1500(SlopeV500-1500)，反映投入时发声更清晰平滑；交互模型显示儿童在AlphaRatio、HNR与SlopeV500-1500上的状态相关变异显著大于照护者。

Conclusion: 儿童与照护者的发声模式可用于建模家庭HCI情境下的投入度；二者共享核心声学指标但敏感度不同，提示面向ASD儿童的交互系统应分别考虑两类说话者的发声特性。

Abstract: Characterizing user engagement is essential for developing effective human-computer interaction (HCI) applications, particularly for children with autism spectrum disorder (ASD). Because home-based HCI naturally involves caregivers as conversational facilitators, analyzing both child and caregiver vocalizations can provide critical insights into characterizing interaction dynamics. Using a dataset of home-based socially assistive robot (SAR) interventions, this work demonstrates how child and caregiver vocalization patterns can model engagement in HCI contexts. We employed linear mixed-effects models (LMMs) with child and caregiver acoustic data to identify acoustic features that significantly differentiated engagement from disengagement. The results demonstrated that children and caregivers share three significant acoustic markers of engagement: mean Alpha Ratio (AlphaRatio), mean Harmonics-to-Noise Ratio (HNR), and mean Spectral Slope 500-1500 (SlopeV500-1500). This reflects cleaner and smoother vocalizations during engagement across both groups. Furthermore, we evaluated a State x Group interaction model to compare the magnitudes of standardized state-dependent differences between children and caregivers. The results indicated that children exhibit significantly greater state-related variation than caregivers in three acoustic features: AlphaRatio, HNR, and SlopeV500-1500.

</details>


### [323] [Skill-JEPA: Learning Planner-Aligned Action Spaces for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26825)
*Tomás Couso Coddou,Christ Devia,Rex G. Liu,Rodrigo Toro Icarte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Skill-JEPA 通过一个状态条件 VAE 学习与规划器分布对齐的潜在技能动作空间，使 JEPA 世界模型的预测更可靠，显著提升远距离目标规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 JEPA 的世界模型只在训练数据中结构化、状态相关的动作上学习，而标准规划器从高斯分布中采样候选动作序列，导致候选动作落在分布外、预测不可靠，且目标越远失败越频繁。作者认为部分远距离规划困难源于动作空间本身的不匹配。

Method: 提出 Skill-JEPA：以当前状态为条件的变分自编码器将短动作序列编码为带标准高斯先验的潜在技能并解码回动作；世界模型改为预测技能的效果而非原始动作；规划器直接从同一高斯先验采样技能序列进行搜索。仅新增一个模块，与世界模型端到端联合训练，不改动规划器，无子目标、无层次结构、无手工设计技能。

Result: 在 3 种 JEPA 世界模型、4 个基准上，80 步远目标平均成功率由原始动作的 42% 提升至 62%；收益随目标距离增大而提升，且在 4 种规划器上均成立。在四足导航任务 AntMaze 上，相隔两格的目标成功率从 0.4% 提升至 38%。

Conclusion: 远距离目标规划的部分困难来自动作空间与规划器采样分布的不匹配，通过将动作空间与规划器对齐即可在不修改规划器的前提下消除该困难，表明动作空间设计是 JEPA 世界模型规划的关键因素。

Abstract: World models based on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn from pixels to predict the outcome of actions in a latent space. To reach a goal, a planner samples many candidate action sequences, uses the model to predict where each one leads, and executes the best one. However, standard planners draw their candidates from a Gaussian, while the model has only seen the structured, state-dependent actions of its training data. Its predictions for such candidates are unreliable, and planning fails more often as goals move farther away. We propose Skill-JEPA, which learns an action space aligned with the planner. A variational autoencoder conditioned on the current state encodes short action sequences into latent skills with a standard Gaussian prior, and decodes them back into actions. The world model predicts the effect of skills rather than raw actions, and the planner searches over skills starting from that same Gaussian, so its candidates resemble the model's training data. Skill-JEPA adds a single module, trained end-to-end with the world model, and leaves the planner untouched, with no subgoals, hierarchy, or hand-designed skills. Across three JEPA world models and four benchmarks, average success on goals 80 steps away rises from 42% with raw actions to 62%; the gains grow with goal distance and hold across four planners. On AntMaze, a quadruped locomotion task, success on goals two maze cells away rises from 0.4% to 38%. This suggests that part of the difficulty of reaching distant goals lies in the action space, and can be removed without changing the planner.

</details>


### [324] [LoCo-WAM: Test-Time Training as Long Context for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26853)
*Sheng Cheng,Deqian Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LoCo-WAM 通过测试时训练（更新视频专家中的低秩适配器）使世界-动作模型在保持短观测窗口的同时利用更长的经验流，在四个仿真基准上提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型依赖短观测历史进行决策，延长时间窗口会增加每一步的计算开销，且往往需要单独训练模型。作者希望在不增加历史长度、不使用专家动作标签和额外数据的前提下，让模型从更长的经验流中学习。

Method: 提出 LoCo-WAM：在测试时利用新观测到的帧更新视频专家中的低秩适配器（LoRA），用适配后的预测引导后续动作。更新在单个 episode 内累积、episode 之间重置，使已移出输入窗口的过去观测仍能影响决策。方法无需专家动作标签、额外数据或元训练。

Result: 在两个世界-动作模型、四个仿真基准上任务成功率均有提升。在 RoboCasa GR-1 上，2.4s 历史的模型可匹配 9.6s 历史训练的模型，4.8s 历史的模型则超出后者 4.8 个百分点。分析表明：更新改善了未来帧预测；其作用主要通过适配"想象未来"影响动作；且随 episode 推进对动作的影响逐渐增强。

Conclusion: 通过测试时低秩适配累积经验，LoCo-WAM 以低成本实现长时程经验利用，为世界-动作模型提供了一种无需额外监督的高效改进途径。

Abstract: World-action models guide robot actions by predicting what happens next, but typically condition on a short observation history. Extending this history increases computation at every decision and can require a separately trained model. We introduce LoCo-WAM, which learns from the outcomes of its actions through test-time training while keeping the same short history length. It uses newly observed frames to update a low-rank adapter in the video expert, whose adapted predictions guide subsequent actions. The updates accumulate within each episode and are reset between episodes, allowing past observations to influence decisions after they leave the input window. LoCo-WAM thus learns from a longer stream of experience without expert action labels, additional data or meta-training. Experiments on two world-action models show improved task success across four simulation benchmarks. On RoboCasa GR-1, a model with 2.4,s of history matches one trained with 9.6,s, while a model with 4.8,s exceeds it by 4.8 percentage points. Our analysis shows that the updates improve prediction of upcoming frames, affect the action mainly through adaptation of the imagined future, and have an increasing effect on actions as the episode unfolds.

</details>


### [325] [Coherent Moment Propagation: Epistemic Uncertainty for Pre-trained Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26855)
*Miguel Saavedra-Ruiz,Anqi Li,Claudia D'Arpino,Alperen Degirmenci,Tucker Hermans,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对流匹配策略无法评估动作可信度的问题，本文证明了BayesDiff的不确定性传播在流匹配/扩散ODE中随欧拉步数T以O(1/T)速率衰减（方差塌缩），提出携带状态与权重交叉协方差的CoMP（相干矩传播）递归，并用观测嵌入扩展最后一层后验，实现可靠认知不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略在机器人学习（长程规划、灵巧操作）中表现优异，但无法判断其输出动作是否可信，尤其在训练集外的新情境。认知不确定性可量化这种"无知"，然而在流匹配策略上于推理时计算并不简单：动作由ODE求解器生成，不确定性必须贯穿整个积分过程传播。已有的BayesDiff在扩散模型中通过去噪链传播均值与方差，但在最后一层Laplace后验近似下严重低估认知不确定性。

Method: 1) 理论证明：对流匹配与扩散ODE，BayesDiff的总方差随欧拉步数T以O(1/T)速率衰减；2) 提出Coherent Moment Propagation（CoMP），含两条受扩展卡尔曼滤波与无迹卡尔曼滤波启发的递归，通过携带状态与权重的交叉协方差修正方差塌缩；3) 用策略已计算的观测嵌入扩展最后一层后验，从而在无需重训练的情况下改善CoMP及多种测试时估计器的不确定性估计。

Result: 在三个操作基准上验证，应用于失败检测与用于微调的示范样本选择；CoMP纠正了BayesDiff的方差塌缩，同时匹配或优于多种不确定性估计器。

Conclusion: CoMP为流匹配策略提供了可靠、推理期可计算的认知不确定性估计，并将其成功用于失败检测与示范筛选等实际下游任务。

Abstract: Flow matching policies underpin the state-of-the-art in robot learning, with impressive results ranging from long-horizon planning to dexterous manipulation. However, they provide no measure of whether their predicted commands can be trusted, particularly in novel situations outside their training set. Epistemic uncertainty reflects uncertainty about the model's weights and quantifies this ignorance, but calculating it at inference time from a flow matching policy is not trivial. Actions are produced by an ODE solver, requiring uncertainty to be propagated through the entire integration process. BayesDiff addresses this problem for diffusion models by propagating the mean and variance of the state through the denoising chain under a last-layer Laplace posterior approximation, but it severely underestimates the epistemic uncertainty. In this paper we: i) prove that, for flow matching and diffusion ODEs, BayesDiff's total variance decays at rate $O(1/T)$ as the number of Euler steps $T$ used by the solver increases; ii) introduce Coherent Moment Propagation (CoMP), two recursions inspired by the extended and unscented Kalman filters that fix this problem by carrying the cross-covariance between state and weights; and iii) widen the last-layer posterior with observation embeddings the policy already computes, improving uncertainty estimates without retraining for CoMP and several test-time estimators alike. We demonstrate these benefits on three manipulation benchmarks with applications in failure detection and demonstration selection for fine-tuning, where CoMP corrects BayesDiff's variance collapse while matching or outperforming several uncertainty estimators.

</details>


### [326] [Staleness Deadlines for Motion Plans during Onboard Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26858)
*Hassan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"陈旧性截止时间"(staleness deadlines)机制：用机器人时钟与校准的行人预测区域/机器人偏差管确定每个查询的可容忍推理预算，使语言模型推理延迟转化为可度量的任务结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可为机器人提供通用自主规划能力，但板载推理可能耗时近一分钟，期间机器人与行人持续移动；机器人必须决定在可能被待定答案推翻前继续执行当前计划多久，此前缺乏原则性决策依据。

Method: 以行人校准预测区域与机器人偏差管设定截止时间；LLM返回后由图规划器重建路径，且须通过与墙壁和预测行人的同一 clearance 检查方被采纳。比较两类预设预算策略（固定预算、按"继续当前计划至必须刹车"的时长设置每查询预算），用精确二项检验评估各推理配置下的近距擦碰、错误决策与迟到率。任务为走廊标志导致路线封闭与需 LLM 推断目的地的配送请求。

Result: 81,000 次基于 Qwen3-4B 记录答案的仿真任务中，完整推理使错误决策降低约 20 倍，按截止时间跟踪使迟到减少最多 64%；移动中从未碰撞，但从不避让的行人仍会撞上停住的机器人（离线 23%、实机 32%）。Jetson AGX Orin 感知负载下，短预算未达错误决策目标，完整推理未达迟到目标；固定预算策略对走廊标志通过，逐查询策略对配送通过。32 个至少一方记录损失的实机任务中，24 个离线回放与实机在三项损失上一致。

Conclusion: 截止时间机制将较慢的推理转化为可度量的任务结果，使设计者能测试各推理配置可支撑多少推理量；简单固定预算并非普遍适用，需按任务与推理配置选择预算策略。

Abstract: Language models may enable general autonomy in robot planning, but onboard their reasoning can run for most of a minute while the robot and nearby pedestrians keep moving. The robot must then decide how long to follow a plan that the pending answer may overturn. Here we present staleness deadlines, which hand that decision to the robot's clock. Calibrated prediction regions for pedestrians and deviation tubes for the robot set the deadline. When the language model answers, a graph planner rebuilds the route, which the robot adopts only after it passes the same clearance check against walls and predicted pedestrians. Exact binomial tests determine which prespecified budget-selection policies keep close calls, wrong decisions and late arrivals below their targets at each inference profile. Candidate policies either fix one budget or set each query's budget from how long the robot can continue its plan before it must brake. We study corridor signs that close routes and delivery requests whose destination the language model must infer. In 81,000 simulated missions with recorded Qwen3-4B answers, full reasoning cuts wrong decisions about twentyfold, and tracking until the deadline cuts late arrivals by up to 64%. The robot never collides while it moves, although pedestrians who never yield still walk into the stopped robot in 23% of offline and 32% of live missions. On a Jetson AGX Orin under perception load, short budgets fail the wrong-decision target, and full reasoning the lateness target. Between these extremes, a fixed-budget policy passes for corridor signs and per-query policies pass for deliveries. Live Orin runs match offline replay on all three losses in 24 of the 32 missions where either run records a loss. The deadline turns slower reasoning into measurable mission outcomes, which let designers test how much reasoning each inference profile can support.

</details>


### [327] [RoboCap-10k: A Metrically Accurate Egocentric Dataset for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26916)
*Kheri Hughes,Qasim Ali,Nikash Bhardwaj,Owen Akhibi,Niresh Dravin,Vivek Sharma,Sam Cho,Ethan Lee,Paul Luo,Sunny Yu,Jonathan Victor,Amanda Young,Michael Cho,Ademi Adeniji,Vincent Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboCap-10k：1万小时真人第一视角操作数据集，统一用250克多相机帽子（RoboCap）采集，提供重力对齐6-DoF头部轨迹与厘米级多视角手部姿态等度量3D标注及任务语言描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频是机器人学习可扩展性最好的操作数据来源，但难点在于高质量监督信号。现有大规模数据集或缺少度量3D标签，或依赖异构设备、动捕实验室、头显内置实时追踪器，且其精度未对公开数据报告。

Method: 在11国工人带薪班次中采集1万小时无脚本操作视频，覆盖18个任务族、200种任务类型、2000个任务；全部数据由同一设备RoboCap（250g帽子，6个同步全局快门相机+双IMU）采集；在线标定使每台设备极线误差低于1px；视觉惯性SLAM得到重力对齐6-DoF头部轨迹；多视角手部追踪器输出度量厘米级手部姿态；每段episode配任务与子任务语言描述。

Result: 构建10,000小时、18任务族/200任务类型/2000任务的真实场景操作数据；设备极线误差<1px；提供重力对齐6-DoF头轨迹与厘米级手部姿态标注，以及episode级任务/子任务文本标注。

Conclusion: 以专用统一硬件+标定与SLAM/多视角手部追踪流水线，可在真实工作场景规模化产出带度量3D监督的第一视角操作数据，缓解现有语料缺3D标签与精度不可报告的问题。

Abstract: Egocentric video is the most scalable source of manipulation data for robot learning, but its difficulty lies in producing high-quality supervision. Existing large corpora lack metric 3D labels or rely on heterogeneous devices, motion-capture labs, or a headset's built-in real-time tracker, whose accuracy is not reported for the released data. We introduce RoboCap-10k: 10,000 curated hours of unscripted manipulation recorded during workers' paid shifts in 11 countries, spanning 18 task families, 200 task types, and 2,000 tasks. Every hour is captured on one device, the RoboCap, a 250 g hat with six synchronized global-shutter cameras and dual IMUs. Online calibration brings every device below 1 px of epipolar error, visual-inertial SLAM yields gravity-aligned 6-DoF head trajectories, and a multi-view hand tracker yields metric cm-precise hand poses. Each episode is annotated with task and sub-task language captions. Project page: https://robocap10k.com

</details>


### [328] [Mechanistic Analysis of Unified Proximity-Tactile Observation by a Capacitive Fingertip Array Constrained by a Metal Phalanx](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26950)
*Shun Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文针对金属指骨上的电容式指尖阵列，建立多导体Maxwell电容矩阵模型，分析驱动屏蔽、近/远场算子与TDM建立等机制，并用FEM评估单层与双层阵列方案；结论为方程与仿真仅构成标定基线，性能尚未实测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电极-人体寄生电容比对目标邻近增量大1–3个数量级，且串扰与TDM建立问题使CDC读数无法等同于电极-目标电容，因此需要一个能区分各贡献项的建模框架来判断近距与力感知方案的可行性。

Method: 将阵列建模为含传感电极、驱动屏蔽节点、指骨地与外部目标的多导体系统，由Maxwell电容矩阵推导CDC测量量，并在观测算子中纳入各未激活端接；考察三种机制：驱动屏蔽对屏蔽支路电流的隐藏及增益/相位失配泄漏、远场与近场算子之别、TDM建立受屏蔽负载限制的帧率上限；进而分析单层阵列（ΔC = ΔC_force + ΔC_dielectric(ε_target)）与双阵列堆叠（接触时外阵列作法拉第屏蔽、内阵列读硅胶间隙压缩）；用FEM仿真量化响应与串扰。

Result: FEM给出单调非线性接触响应：0.05 mm时为4.6 fF，0.5 mm时为370 fF，基线1.08 pF；尼龙与金属目标差最大0.3 fF（受矩阵导出分辨率限制、未网格收敛）；驱动屏蔽读出下互项抵消；粗网格串扰约1 fF。近距模式下电极-屏蔽耦合1.47 pF，使10⁻³–10⁻⁴失配成为定量门槛：5 mm处15.9 fF增量在|ε_A|≈10⁻²时即等于泄漏量。

Conclusion: 单层结构无法给出与材料无关的力分布；双阵列堆叠通过外阵列法拉第屏蔽在原理上解耦近距与力。驱动屏蔽失配是近距模式的定量瓶颈。力分布、机械串扰与全部台架量尚未验证，所给方程与FEM证据仅为样机标定的基线，不构成实测性能结论。

Abstract: A capacitive fingertip array on a metal phalanx under thin silicone skin senses centimetre-scale conductive targets by fringing fields and contact force by dielectric compression. Electrode-to-body parasitics exceed the useful proximity increment by one to three orders of magnitude, and crosstalk and time-division-multiplexing (TDM) settling prevent identifying the capacitance-to-digital converter (CDC) reading with the electrode-target capacitance. The array is modelled as a multi-conductor system (sensing electrodes, driven-shield nodes, phalanx ground and an external target). The CDC measurand follows from the Maxwell capacitance matrix, with each inactive termination in the observation operator. Three mechanisms are examined: driven-shield hiding of shield-branch current and gain/phase-mismatch leakage; distinct far- and near-field operators; and TDM settling as a frame-rate limit set by shield load. A single layer used for both proximity and force reads Delta C = Delta C_force + Delta C_dielectric(epsilon_target), so its force map cannot be material-independent. A dual-array stack is analysed: the outer array is grounded as a Faraday screen during contact, and the inner array reads silicone-gap compression. FEM simulations give a monotonic, nonlinear contact response (4.6 fF at 0.05 mm to 370 fF at 0.5 mm on a 1.08 pF base), a nylon-versus-metal difference of at most 0.3 fF at exported-matrix resolution (not mesh-converged), mutual-term cancellation under driven-shield readout, and about 1 fF crosstalk on the coarse mesh. In proximity mode, electrode-shield coupling of 1.47 pF makes 10^{-3}-10^{-4} mismatch a quantitative gate: a 15.9 fF increment at 5 mm equals the leakage at |epsilon_A| ~ 10^{-2}. Force map, mechanical crosstalk and all bench quantities remain unvalidated; the equations and FEM evidence are a baseline for prototype calibration, not measured performance claims.

</details>


### [329] [RoboDyna: A Benchmark for Evaluating Robotic Manipulation under Physically Grounded Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26969)
*Zhiyuan Li,Rui Heng Yang,Xuan Zhao,Amir Rasouli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDyna 是一个面向动态场景下操作策略物理理解与动作能力的基准，含 20 个概念性动态任务与 10 个家居任务、3,500 条专家演示及同步/异步评测协议；评测显示现有 VLA 与世界动作模型成功率均低于 20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态真实世界中的物体操作要求理解环境如何运作与演化并据此行动。现有基准存在三方面不足：(1) 物理类基准只评估预测保真度，不评估闭环动作；(2) 动态机器人操作基准多采用简化、物理上不合理的物体运动，无法检验真实动力学下的推理与行动；(3) 评测通常为同步式，推理期间冻结环境，使计算耗时与任务成败脱钩。

Method: 提出 RoboDyna 基准：①任务设计——20 个概念性动态任务覆盖 5 类能力，采用简单物体与低场景杂乱度，并使用两因素难度设计；另有 10 个家居任务，在视觉与语义更丰富的仿真真实环境中实例化同样的能力；②数据与协议——提供 3,500 条专家演示，配套同步与异步两套评测协议，指标包含任务成功率与部分完成度，并提供交互式工具用于人类基线评测；③实验——在 5.4K 个 episode 上评测多个视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型与一个世界动作模型。

Result: 所有策略成功率均低于 20%，显示动态操作能力存在实质局限。世界动作模型能捕捉简单物理动力学，但在复杂交互上表现不佳，且其预测保真度与任务成功率并不一致对应。同步-异步对比表明：推理延迟会翻转 5–12% 的 episode 结果，但平均成功率变化最多仅 3%。在同等条件下，人类基线显著优于所有策略。

Conclusion: RoboDyna 填补了动态、闭环、真实动力学条件下操作策略评测的空白，揭示了当前 VLA 与世界动作模型在动态操作上的显著不足，并表明"预测精度≠任务成功"以及推理时延对单次结果的影响不可忽略，为后续动态操作研究提供了统一评测平台。

Abstract: Object manipulation in the dynamic real world requires understanding how the environment functions and evolves, and acting accordingly. Existing benchmarks, however, do not adequately address these needs. Physics-oriented benchmarks evaluate predictive fidelity but not closed-loop action. Dynamic robotic manipulation benchmarks often use simplified, physically implausible object motions, limiting evaluation of reasoning and action under realistic dynamics. Evaluation is also typically synchronized, freezing the environment during inference and making computation time irrelevant to task outcomes. To this end, we propose RoboDyna, a benchmark for evaluating the physical understanding and action capabilities of manipulation policies in dynamic scenes. RoboDyna comprises 20 conceptual dynamic tasks spanning five capability categories, with simple objects and minimal scene clutter, under a two-factor difficulty design, and 10 household tasks that instantiate the same capabilities in visually and semantically richer simulated real-world environments. RoboDyna provides 3,500 expert demonstrations, paired synchronous and asynchronous evaluation protocols, metrics for task success and partial completion, and interactive tools for human baseline evaluation. Evaluating vision-language-action models and a world-action model across 5.4K episodes, we find that all policies achieve success rates below 20%, revealing substantial limitations in dynamic manipulation. The world-action model captures simple physical dynamics but struggles with complex interactions, and its prediction fidelity does not consistently align with task success. Sync-async comparisons show that inference latency flips 5-12% of episode outcomes while changing average success rates by at most 3%. Human baselines substantially outperform all policies under matched conditions. Project page: https://robodyna.github.io/robodyna/

</details>


### [330] [RoboPRO: A Perturbation-Resilient and Obstacle-Aware Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26971)
*Zhiyuan Li,Charles Eret,Dingsheng Liu,Xuan Zhao,Joshua Choi,Yangzheng Wu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang,Amir Rasouli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboPRO 是一个面向扰动鲁棒性与避障规划的机器人操作基准，包含 80 个双臂任务、四个室内场景、16,000 条示教与 5.6K 语言指令，并提出两种安全事件指标，系统评估视觉、语言与物体级扰动下的策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准多为干净桌面环境，且仅以成功率作为唯一指标，无法回答两个部署关键问题：策略在真实条件下是否安全操作，以及失败由何导致。因此需要一个统一刻画多种鲁棒性维度、并显式衡量安全性的基准。

Method: 构建 RoboPRO 基准：80 个双臂原子与组合任务、四个布置完备的室内环境、16,000 条示教、5.6K 条多样化语言指令；场景中设置不同数量（非目标）干扰物，从仅含目标物的干净场景到密集拥挤场景，施加视觉与操作双重杂乱度。提出两种安全感知事件指标，用于判定机器人与环境或干扰物之间的非安全接触。评测协议按因子分析三类关键扰动（视觉、语言、物体级）及不同干扰物密度，并在该基准上对当前先进操作策略进行大量实验。

Result: 实验表明：场景杂乱会显著降低成功率并增加非目标碰撞；不同扰动对性能的影响程度各异；训练数据构成会影响整体性能。

Conclusion: RoboPRO 揭示了真实条件下操作任务的关键挑战，强调成功率之外的碰撞类安全指标的必要性，并为后续面向鲁棒性与避障规划的研究提供统一评测平台与方向指引。

Abstract: Object manipulation in the real world requires robotic policies to be robust to contextual perturbations and capable of safe planning in cluttered scenes. However, the majority of existing robotics benchmarks contain clean tabletop environments with success rate as the sole metric, leaving two critical deployment questions unanswered: does the policy manipulate safely under real conditions and what does lead to its failure? To this end, we present RoboPRO, a Robotics benchmark for Perturbation-Resilient and Obstacle-aware planning, which unifies various axes of robustness in manipulation. RoboPRO comprises 80 diverse bimanual atomic and compositional tasks across four furnished indoor environments, with a total of 16,000 demonstrations and 5.6K diverse language instructions. The scenes are populated with different numbers of distractors (non-targets), ranging from clean, involving only target objects, to densely populated to impose both visual and operational clutter. Moreover, we propose two safety-aware event metrics to determine unsafe contact between the robot and the environment or distractors. Our evaluation protocol consists of a per-factor analysis of three key perturbations, namely visual, linguistic, and object-level, with different levels of distractor densities. We conduct extensive experiments on RoboPRO using state-of-the-art manipulation policies to analyze the impact of visual and operational clutter, perturbations, and instructional diversity. Our findings indicate clutter can drastically reduce success and increase non-target collision, different perturbations have different effects on the performance, and that training data composition affects overall performance. Our findings highlight the key challenges for manipulation in real-world conditions and motivate future research in this domain. Project page: https://robopro-bench.github.io/RoboPRO/

</details>


### [331] [Geo-Force: A Theory of Force Sensing from Object Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26992)
*Abdullah Ayad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文形式化定义了"虚拟触觉传感器"所计算的量——Geometric Force（几何接触力），即通过渲染待接触表面、用touch map预测触觉信号、再由信号估计每个taxel受力三步得到，并证明其协变性、零接触消失性与Lipschitz稳定性，同时揭示几何无法推断刚度的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作精细物体需要接触力信息，但触觉传感器脆弱且难以集成；RGB-D等深度传感器则广泛可得。用深度传感器测得的几何来预测触觉信号、保留触觉传感表面而省去电子器件的"虚拟触觉传感器"有望弥合这一差距，但其究竟计算什么、输出何时可信尚不清楚，需要给出形式化回答。

Method: 将Geometric Force定义为虚拟触觉传感器每个taxel上的力，分三步计算：(1)渲染从虚拟传感器视角可见的待接触表面；(2)用学习或仿真的touch map预测触觉信号；(3)由该信号估计力。随后证明三条性质：坐标系变换下力矢量仅发生旋转；渲染表面与传感器不接触时力为零；对Lipschitz映射，误差随三步误差至多线性增长。渲染误差又取决于表面采样/重建质量与传感器位姿精度。进而给出基本极限：几何不包含刚度信息，故对同形状不同刚度物体不存在精确的touch map；在弹性接触模型下，所有物体特异知识都集中于touch map。

Result: 证明了Geometric Force的三个性质——协变性（仅旋转）、零接触时消失、以及关于三步误差的线性Lipschitz稳定性；刻画了渲染误差与表面采样/重建及位姿精度的依赖关系；证明了同形状不同刚度物体下touch map无法精确预测触觉的根本极限；并从弹性接触模型推出物体特异知识全部位于touch map中。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了对虚拟触觉传感器"计算什么、何时可信"的形式化刻画，由此导出了对深度传感器与目标物体的要求、设计规则，以及该虚拟传感器失效的条件。

Abstract: Robots need contact forces to handle delicate objects, but tactile sensors are fragile and hard to integrate, whereas depth sensors, such as RGB-D cameras, are widely available. A virtual touch sensor that keeps the sensing surface of a tactile sensor but replaces its electronics with a touch signal predicted from the target object's geometry, as measured by a depth sensor, could close this gap, yet it is unclear what such a virtual sensor computes and when its output can be trusted. We give a formal answer. We define the Geometric Force as the force on each taxel of a virtual touch sensor, computed in three steps: rendering the surface to be touched as seen from the virtual sensor, predicting the touch signal with a touch map, learned or simulated, and estimating the force from that signal. We prove three properties of the Geometric Force. First, its force vectors only rotate under a change of coordinate frame. Second, it vanishes when the rendered surface does not touch the virtual sensor. Third, it is stable: its error grows at most linearly with the errors of the three steps for Lipschitz maps. The rendering error, in turn, depends on how well the surface is sampled or reconstructed and how accurately the sensor is placed. We then show a fundamental limit: geometry does not reveal the object's stiffness, so no touch map can predict touch exactly for objects of the same shape and different stiffness. Under an elastic contact model, all object-specific knowledge resides in the touch map. From these results, we derive requirements on the depth sensor and the objects, design rules, and the conditions under which such a virtual sensor fails.

</details>


### [332] [PonderPounce-WM: When Does Predicting the Future Help Context-Dependent Robot Control?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26996)
*Jaeyoon Jung,Suhwan Choi,Yunsung Lee,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PonderPounce-WM，在 PonderPounce 基础上加入三个预测视觉特征变化的未来 token 作为辅助监督；实验表明，未来预测通过辅助监督（而非将未来 token 直接输入动作模型）即可提升上下文相关机器人控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究未来预测如何影响上下文相关的机器人控制，并厘清"预测性监督"与"直接未来条件输入"两种机制的作用差异。

Method: 在 PonderPounce 上扩展出 PonderPounce-WM：预训练 MLLM（Ponder）将历史与示教整合为连续认知并供给动作模型 Pounce；新增三个未来 token，监督其预测视觉特征变化；系统考察 token 的排列顺序与传递方式（cognition-first 排序、cognition-only vs. 直接未来 token 传递），并用探针分析认知表示中的未来/过去信息含量。

Result: RoboMME 上，cognition-first 排序 + cognition-only 传递将成功率从 60.83% 提升至 67.42%；同排序下直接传递未来 token 为 64.00%。探针显示认知中未来信息增加，而已测试的过去事件属性仍大体可解码。四个真实机器人任务在异步执行下平均成功率 67.44%（PonderPounce 为 60.98%）。

Conclusion: 未来预测可通过辅助监督改善上下文相关控制，无需将未来 token 传给动作模型；直接未来条件输入收益较小。

Abstract: How does future prediction affect context-dependent robot control? We introduce PonderPounce-WM, extending PonderPounce with three future tokens supervised to predict visual-feature changes. Its pretrained MLLM, Ponder, integrates history and demonstrations into continuous cognition for the action model, Pounce. We investigate token ordering and delivery to distinguish predictive supervision from direct future conditioning. On RoboMME, cognition-first ordering with cognition-only delivery improves success from 60.83% to 67.42%. With the same ordering, direct future-token delivery yields 64.00% success. Probes show increased future information in cognition while the tested past-event attributes remain largely decodable. Across four real-robot tasks, PonderPounce-WM achieves 67.44% mean success under asynchronous execution, compared with 60.98% for PonderPounce. These results suggest that future prediction can improve context-dependent control through auxiliary supervision without delivering future tokens to the action model.

</details>


### [333] [FoRe: A Force-Guided Framework for Autonomous Grasp Recovery in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27011)
*Shuai Peng,Yongkai Liao,Yuxin Cheng,Haoyuan Wang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoRe：利用力反馈将演示分段训练子策略，并以局部强化学习训练专用重试策略，配合推理期控制器检测抓取失败并调用重试，实现局部抓取失败的自主恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在抓取式操作上表现良好，但难以可靠地从局部抓取失败中恢复：拾放任务中抓取失败后策略常继续执行搬运而非重试，反映出对本体感知线索判断阶段推进的过度依赖，以及重试监督信号不足；已有恢复方法多依赖部署时引导或演示增强。

Method: 提出 Force-guided Recovery (FoRe) 框架：(1) 借助力反馈将演示划分为 approach-grasp 与 transport-placement 两阶段，分别训练策略；(2) 以 approach-grasp 策略初始化专用重试策略，并在失败后状态上通过局部 RL 精化，弥补重试监督不足；(3) 推理时控制器结合力反馈与抓取指令状态，协调各阶段策略、检测抓取失败并调用重试策略，避免不当阶段推进。同时构建力增强吸附操作基准 LIBERO-SUCTION（改编自 LIBERO），含完整演示轨迹。

Result: 在 LIBERO-SUCTION、LIBERO 及真实世界任务上，FoRe 一致提升 VLA-Adapter 与 π0.5 的任务成功率。LIBERO-SUCTION 上平均绝对增益分别为 18.4% 和 6.5%，真实世界分别为 25.0% 和 21.7%；在两个仿真基准上每个任务仅用一条演示时仍然有效。

Conclusion: 力反馈驱动的阶段划分与失败触发的局部 RL 重试策略，可在少量重试监督下显著提升 VLA 策略对局部抓取失败的鲁棒性，且具备低演示数据需求与跨模型、跨仿真/真实场景的通用性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong performance in grasp-based manipulation, yet reliable recovery from local grasping failures remains challenging. Specifically, in pick-and-place tasks, policies may continue transport after a failed grasp instead of retrying, potentially reflecting overreliance on proprioceptive cues for stage progression and limited retry supervision. Existing recovery approaches typically rely on deployment-time guidance or demonstration augmentation. We introduce Force-guided Recovery (FoRe), a framework for autonomous recovery from local grasping failures. Using force feedback, FoRe partitions demonstrations into approach-grasp and transport-placement stages to train separate policies. To supplement limited retry supervision, FoRe then initializes a dedicated retry policy from the approach-grasp policy and refines it through local reinforcement learning (RL) from post-failure states. To avoid inappropriate stage progression, a controller combines force feedback with grasp-command status at inference to coordinate the stage policies, detect grasp failures, and invoke the retry policy. We also construct LIBERO-SUCTION, a force-augmented suction manipulation benchmark adapted from LIBERO, with complete demonstration trajectories for policy training. FoRe consistently improves task success rates for VLA-Adapter and $π_{0.5}$ on LIBERO-SUCTION, LIBERO, and real-world tasks. On LIBERO-SUCTION, average absolute gains are 18.4% and 6.5%, respectively. The corresponding real-world absolute gains are 25.0% and 21.7%. FoRe also remains effective with only one demonstration per task on both simulation benchmarks.

</details>


### [334] [RoboColiseum: Co-evolution of Simulation, Evaluation, and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27042)
