*Chenghao Yin,Da Huang,Di Yang,Jichao Wang,Junhui Wu,Nanshu Zhao,Tong Ran,Zhijun Li,Gang Zhao,Linting Guo,Rui Feng,Sheng Zhang,Tianyu Jiang,Wenjun Sun,Yongxin Su,Zhaobo Liu,Zheyuan Xing,Sukai Wang,Yue Zhou,Qihan Jiang,Jianhua Jiang,Chuang Men,Yuxiang Yan,Daizhen Lv,Kunyu Yu,Yongjian Shen,Tianxiang Zhu,Siying Zhang,Wenhao Wang,Jun Zhao,Bowen Jiang,Zhi Zeng,Maoqing Yao,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboColiseum：集成系统辨识、可组合物理/渲染与多维基准的具身模型仿真评测平台，22组真机-仿真配对任务得分皮尔逊相关0.9686，设8条评测赛道与预算感知评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身模型仿真评测需要标定过的交互、多样化任务与显式执行约束，现有平台难以同时满足，故构建统一平台。

Method: 用录制指令回放辨识机器人动力学与物体交互；以共享接口连接刚体与可变形体仿真到渲染；构建8条赛道（指令遵循、空间推理、通用操作、鲁棒性、灵巧操作、精密操作、全身协调、跨本体执行），并采用预算感知评分融合任务进度、资源消耗与完成奖励；Agent2Act将语言模型接入机器人控制执行，VLA采用混合任务监督微调。

Result: 22组配对真机-仿真配置任务得分皮尔逊相关0.9686；报告12个Season I策略与Season II公开榜单全部11个系统（8个Agent系统、3个VLA策略）；结果显示各评测类别内部赛道间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: RoboColiseum将仿真开发与能力导向评测衔接，为扩展机器人交互与模型评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Simulation-based evaluation of embodied models requires calibrated interactions, diverse tasks, and explicit execution constraints. We present RoboColiseum, a platform combining system identification, composable physics and rendering, and multidimensional benchmarking. Recorded-command replay characterizes robot dynamics and object interactions, while a shared interface connects rigid and deformable simulation to rendering. Across 22 paired real--simulation robot--task configurations, task scores have a reported Pearson correlation of 0.9686. Eight evaluation tracks span instruction following, spatial reasoning, general manipulation, robustness, dexterous manipulation, precision manipulation, whole-body coordination, and execution across robot embodiments. Budget-aware scoring combines task progress, resource use, and completion rewards. We report 12 selected Season~I policies and all 11 systems on the public Season~II leaderboard, comprising eight Agent systems and three VLA policies. Agent2Act connects language models to robot-control execution; the VLA study uses mixed-task supervised fine-tuning. The results reveal substantial variation across tracks within each evaluation category. RoboColiseum connects simulation development with capability-oriented evaluation and provides a foundation for extending robot interactions and model assessment.

</details>


### [335] [BeyondEgo: Learning to Act Beyond Egocentric Perception via Humanoid Ego-Exo Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27047)
*Zhaoshan Yang,Chenglei Li,Haoyu Lei,Yutao Zhang,Yuyu Yang,Zhuo Chen,Ling Ding,Zhongxing Ren,Yi Gu,Heyang Zhang,Peng Su,Jin Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BeyondEgo通过引入同步外视角与未来观测特权监督，在空间和时间两个维度上扩展了第一人称VLA策略的感知能力，并构建了首个闭环人形全身移动操作基准HELM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形全身移动操作需要协调全身运动，但仅依赖第一人称视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略会遗漏关键的身体-场景关系；同时单纯的动作模仿缺乏对未来场景演变的显式监督信号。

Method: 空间上：用冻结的DINOv3编码同步外视角，并将得到的token与第一人称VLM token拼接以条件化动作专家。时间上：利用双视角的未来观测作为特权监督，训练任务条件化VLM特征上的残差适配器；一个前瞻专家回归0.6秒后的双视角DINOv3表征。部署时丢弃前瞻专家，仅保留4.2M参数的适配器。

Result: 在HELM基准的五个全身任务上，BeyondEgo将分布内成功率从48.0%提升至92.0%，在200个含布局、资产、材质与光照偏移的留出场景上从23.5%提升至65.0%；消融实验表明双视角DINOv3前瞻带来最佳的迁移效果。

Conclusion: 空间-时间双维度的感知扩展与前瞻式表征监督能显著提升人形VLA策略的成功率与分布外泛化能力，且部署开销极小。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires coordinating whole-body motion to perform manipulation tasks in complex environments. Yet egocentric vision-language-action (VLA) policies can miss critical body--scene relationships, and action imitation alone provides no explicit supervision for future scene evolution. We propose BeyondEgo to extend VLA perception in space and time. Spatially, we encode a synchronized exocentric view with frozen DINOv3 and concatenate the resulting tokens with egocentric VLM tokens to condition the action expert. Temporally, we use future observations from both views as privileged supervision for a residual adapter on task-conditioned VLM features. A foresight expert regresses DINOv3 representations of both views 0.6s ahead. At deployment, the foresight expert is discarded and the 4.2M-parameter adapter is retained. We also introduce HELM, to our knowledge, the first closed-loop humanoid loco-manipulation benchmark with synchronized egocentric and exocentric views across five whole-body tasks.On HELM, BeyondEgo improves success from 48.0% to 92.0% in distribution and from 23.5% to 65.0% on 200 held-out scenes with layout, asset, material, and lighting shifts. Ablations show that dual-view DINOv3 foresight yields the best transfer results.

</details>


### [336] [A Learning-Enhanced Control Scheme for Aerial Manipulator Under Composite Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27048)
*Meng Wang,Jindou Jia,Kexin Guo,Xiang Yu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对空中机械臂强耦合与外扰下的高精度动态跟踪难题，提出"学习逼近+观测器补偿"的复合控制方案：基于多项式分解定理将未知高阶耦合项分解为共享常量基矩阵、状态相关多项式矩阵与任务相关参数向量，用双层优化离线提取任务不变的共享信息，再用自适应律在线更新任务相关参数，并由观测器补偿逼近残差与外扰，实现稳定瞬态与高精度稳态跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂存在强耦合效应与外部扰动，现有方法通常只补偿一阶耦合项，遗留高阶非线性未处理，故需在"学习即控制"思想下同时兼顾跨工况知识复用与残差/扰动鲁棒性。

Method: 依据多项式分解定理，将未知高阶项分解为共享常量基矩阵、状态相关多项式矩阵和任务相关参数向量；建立双层优化公式离线提取任务不变的共享基，实现不同作业条件下的知识复用；利用学得的共享基设计自适应律在线更新任务相关参数向量；再引入观测器补偿不可避免的逼近残差与外部扰动，构成学习与观测互补的复合控制框架。

Result: 在空中机械臂四个代表性场景的实机实验中验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性，并获得兼具稳定瞬态响应与高精度稳态跟踪的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将高阶耦合项的共享结构学习与自适应在线更新、观测器残差补偿相结合，可在保证瞬态稳定性的同时显著提升稳态跟踪精度，并具备跨工况复用与抗扰能力，已由实机实验证实。

Abstract: Achieving high-precision dynamic tracking in aerial manipulators remains fundamentally challenging due to strong coupling effects and external disturbances. Existing approaches are typically limited to compensating first-order coupling term, leaving higher-order nonlinearities unaddressed. Building upon the philosophy of learning-for-control, this paper proposes a composite control scheme that combines learning-based approximation with observer-based compensation in a complementary manner. Based on the polynomial decomposition theorem, the unknown high-order term is decomposed into a shared constant basis matrix, a state-related polynomial matrix, and a task-related parameter vector. A bilevel optimization formula is first established to extract the task-invariant shared information, enabling efficient knowledge reuse across different operation conditions. By leveraging the learned shared basis, an adaptive law is designed to update the task-dependent parameter vector online. Furthermore, an observer is introduced to compensate for the inevitable approximation residual and external disturbances. The resulting framework leads to a concurrent attainment of the stable transient response and high-precision steady-state tracking performance. Extensive real-world experiments on an aerial manipulator across four representative scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [337] [PACE: Policy Augmentation and Compensation With Explicit Disturbance Estimation for Robust Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27061)
*Shixuan Han,Yuyang Wang,Jingting Jia,Zeshuai Chen,Jindou Jia,Kexin Guo,Xiang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACE框架：将载荷变化、质心偏移、执行器退化与外力统一为关节空间扰动，用无需关节加速度测量的非线性观测器在线估计，并将估计同时用于前馈力矩补偿与策略观测增强，另给出无需力传感器的解析接触估计，实现强扰动下的四足运动鲁棒跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足强化学习运动控制通常依赖域随机化来应对未建模动态与外部扰动，但该策略迫使策略对可能遇到的所有条件做保守折中，导致在变化动力学下难以保持精确跟踪，且难以泛化到采样范围之外（OOD）。作者希望缓解这一鲁棒性与跟踪精度之间的权衡。

Method: 1）将载荷变化、质心偏移、执行器退化及外力集总为单一关节空间扰动；2）设计非线性观测器在线重构该扰动，且不依赖关节加速度测量；3）扰动估计一方面作为前馈力矩补偿构成基础控制通路，另一方面增广策略观测，实现扰动感知的动作生成（策略增广与补偿，PACE）；4）针对快速变化的接触效应学习困难，提出基于关节与惯性测量的解析接触估计方法，无需力传感器。训练环境为仿真平地、附加质量不超过3 kg。

Result: 在11种仿真动力学条件下，相较当前最优基线，速度跟踪误差降低18.1%–57.0%；在分布外（OOD）指令下仍保持更优跟踪。实机实验中，机器人承载11.5 kg载荷（接近自身质量），完成未见过的16 cm下台阶及穿越五种室外地形。

Conclusion: 显式扰动估计并反馈至策略与控制器，可在不牺牲鲁棒性的前提下显著提升跟踪精度与泛化能力，缓解域随机化的保守折中；解析接触估计提供了无需力传感器的实用替代；方法在严重载荷与未见地形下具备实机迁移能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has made quadrupedal locomotion remarkably robust. To handle unmodeled dynamics and external disturbances, domain randomization is usually employed to make a policy hedge against every condition it might meet. However, maintaining accurate tracking across varying dynamics and generalizing beyond the sampled range remain challenging. Here we show that explicitly estimating the disturbance and feeding the resulting estimation back into both the policy and the controller can relax this compromise. We introduce Policy Augmentation and Compensation with Explicit disturbance estimation (PACE). The framework lumps payload variations, center-of-mass (CoM) shifts, actuator degradation, and external forces into one joint-space disturbance. A nonlinear observer subsequently recovers this disturbance online without requiring joint acceleration measurements. The disturbance estimation provides feedforward torque compensation as the foundational control pathway and further augments the policy observation to enable disturbance-aware action generation. Considering the learning difficulty of fast-varying contact effects, we further provide an analytical contact estimation approach from joint and inertial measurements, without demanding force sensors. Across eleven simulated dynamics conditions, PACE reduces velocity-tracking error by 18.1% to 57.0% against state-of-the-art baselines, and maintains improved tracking under out-of-distribution (OOD) commands. Notably, in real-world experiments, the deployed real robot carried an 11.5 kg payload, nearly equal to its own mass. It also negotiated unseen 16 cm step-downs and crossed five outdoor terrains. The policy was trained exclusively on flat ground in simulation with at most 3 kg of added mass.

</details>


### [338] [OpenRing: Deadlock-Free Execution of Any Planner on AGV Floors with One-Way Lanes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27127)
*Zhihao Lin,Anh-Tu Nguyen,Hui Zhang,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenRing框架，将"路径规划速度"与"系统持续运行能力"解耦：由规划器给出路线与顺序，由本地守卫只允许不会闭合环路的移动与改道，从而在任意提案或规划器失效下保证不死锁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身多智能体路径规划需持续为重规划车辆分配路线，但实际偏差会使原计划失效；在单向车道下，迟到车辆或规划器失联可能使一环满路段被闭合，导致系统瘫痪。作者希望在规划器可能任意出错甚至丢失的情况下，仍能从结构上排除环路闭合，而不是依赖规划质量。

Method: 将规划与安全保证分离：规划器提出路线与顺序；本地守卫（local guard）仅提交不会闭合"满路段环"的移动与改道；顺序具有有限时限的绑定效力。理论上分析守卫必要性、干净计划的穿越顺序保持性，以及吞吐上界：对两车位路段进行建模与证明，给出"一旦扰动结束即保证推进、入口停止则全部车辆退出"的性质，并推导任何"在任意停车下不把车辆存入交叉口"的规则其每路段吞吐不超过 B/T_s。实证上用有绑定顺序的三规划器在八层场景中对比、126次运行统计死锁率与闭环吞吐，并进行物理仿真验证。

Result: 在带有绑定顺序的基线中，三个规划器在八层场景的126次运行里有80次出现死锁；而在OpenRing内部，126次运行无一死锁。OpenRing的闭环吞吐与最佳基线相当或更高。物理仿真未观察到死锁或车辆重叠。理论上，所有守卫否决都是必要的（在仅于提案时改道的规则类中），干净计划保持其穿越顺序，且存在 B/T_s 的每路段吞吐上界。

Conclusion: 通过把"跑多快"与"能否持续运行"分离，OpenRing以本地守卫的否决机制在任意提案与规划器失联下获得无死锁的结构性保证，代价明确（吞吐上界），且实证吞吐不劣于基线，为终身MAPF的鲁棒部署提供了可证明的安全层。

Abstract: Lifelong multi-agent path finding replans routes for automated guided vehicles, but deviations can invalidate a plan. With one-way lanes, a late vehicle or lost planner can close a ring of full segments. We separate how fast the floor runs from whether it keeps running. In OpenRing a planner proposes routes and an order, a local guard commits only moves and route changes that cannot close such a ring, and the order binds for a bounded time. Despite arbitrary proposals or planner loss, no ring closes. With two-place segments, traffic can continue under disturbances; progress is guaranteed once they end, and all vehicles exit if entries also stop. Among rules that reroute only on a proposal, every guard veto is necessary, and a clean plan keeps its crossing orders. The guarantee has a price: no rule that never stores a vehicle in a crossing under arbitrary stops carries more than $B/T_s$ per segment of $B$ places and traversal time $T_s$. With binding orders, three planners deadlock in 80 of 126 runs across eight floors. Inside OpenRing, none of the 126 runs deadlocks. OpenRing's closed-loop throughput rivals or exceeds the best baseline's. Physics simulations show no deadlocks or overlaps.

</details>


### [339] [Opto-Electronic Hybrid Reinforcement Learning Based on MZI for Robotic Manipulator Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27129)
*Mengting Yu,Shuiying Xiang,Changjian Xie,Yonghang Chen,Haowen Zhao,Xingxing Guo,Zhijian Hao,Yahui Zhang,Yanan Han,Yue Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于马赫-曾德尔干涉仪（MZI）的光电混合强化学习架构，将脉冲神经网络（SNN）Actor 的关键线性层计算迁移至光学 MZI 芯片，用于 Franka Panda 机械臂的点到点到达任务，在保持控制精度的同时实现 1.39 TOPS/W 能效与 120 ps 推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能大规模部署需要机械臂完成到达、抓取等基础操作，对控制精度、实时性与能效要求苛刻。端到端强化学习虽在连续动作空间决策上表现优异，但受电子计算架构限制，存在计算开销大、推理延迟高、能效低的问题，难以低功耗边缘部署；光学计算虽具高速并行优势，但其与强化学习的结合研究仍处于早期阶段。

Method: 构建 MZI 光电混合 RL 架构，采用 DDPG-HER 与 TD3-HER 两种算法结构；将 SNN Actor 的关键层线性计算在光学 MZI 芯片上实现，并在 PyBullet 仿真环境中针对 Franka Emika Panda 机械臂的到达任务进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提架构在保持控制精度的前提下，实现 1.39 TOPS/W 的能效与 120 ps 的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了光学计算与强化学习融合用于机器人精确运动控制的可行性，为机器人低功耗、高精度控制提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Precise motion control for autonomous robots is a core technical enabler for the large-scale deployment of embodied intelligence. In particular, basic manipulation tasks, such as point-to-point reaching and target grasping with robotic manipulators, impose stringent requirements on control accuracy, real-time response, and energy efficiency for edge deployment. As a prevailing end-to-end control paradigm, reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated remarkable capabilities in autonomous learning and generalization for decision-making in continuous action spaces. Nevertheless, constrained by electronic computing architectures, it generally suffers from substantial computing overhead, high inference latency and limited energy efficiency, making low-power edge deployment challenging. Optical computing, benefiting from its high-speed parallelism, offers a promising route to break energy-efficiency bottlenecks; however, research on the integration of optical computing and RL remains at an early stage. In this work, we propose a Mach-Zehnder Interferometer (MZI)-based opto-electronic hybrid RL architecture. We adopted two algorithm structures: Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient combined with Hindsight Experience Replay (DDPG-HER) and Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient combined with HER (TD3-HER). We implemented the key layer linear computation of the spiking neural network (SNN) Actor on an optical MZI chip for the reaching task of a Franka Emika Panda robotic manipulator. The architecture is evaluated in the PyBullet simulation environment. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed architecture achieves an energy efficiency of 1.39 TOPS/W and an inference latency of 120ps while maintaining control accuracy, which provide a feasible pathway toward energy-efficient and precise control for robots.

</details>


### [340] [Learning from Incomplete Actions: Interval Motion Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27171)
*Yunpeng Mei,Chenyu Wang,Xiaowen Zhu,Chenbo Xin,Hongjie Cao,Yiyuan Li,Chengye Zhang,YuAn Wang,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对第一视角演示中动作标注存在连续缺失（gap）的问题，利用缺失区间两端可靠位姿提供的净平移与旋转信息，在保留可靠动作上常规 flow matching 的同时，引入聚合运动组合目标与共享区间分支，实现缺失动作的可靠训练，并在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 及真实机器人任务上取得优于 masking 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉标注的第一视角演示常导致动作监督出现连续缺口，而缺口两端仍存在可靠位姿，蕴含净平移与旋转信息。现有 masking 等方法未能充分利用这类端点约束，浪费了可用监督信号，因此需要一种能在动作缺失条件下仍能稳定训练 VLA 策略的方法。

Method: 1) 对可靠局部动作保留普通 flow matching 训练；2) 设计组合目标（composition objective），约束缺失动作估计的聚合运动与端点净变换一致；3) 当缺口长度超过策略预测视界时，将多个固定窗口组合起来；4) 引入共享区间分支（shared interval branch），从可靠端点学习并输出组合目标。

Result: LIBERO 上仅用 20% 演示、70.5% 动作缺失，主方法三次训练平均成功率 87.9%；在观测暴露与训练开销匹配的成对比较中较 masking 高 10.5%。Ego-informed RoboTwin 缺口设置下成功率 45.4%（masking 40.3%）。五类真实机器人任务平均成功率 59.6%，优于单独 interval flow matching 的 54.8% 与 masking 的 48.8%。

Conclusion: 利用缺失区间的端点净变换作为聚合约束，可有效恢复动作缺口处的监督信号，且与 flow matching 兼容；在仿真与真实机器人上均一致优于 masking 与 interval flow matching，验证了组合目标与共享区间分支设计的有效性。

Abstract: Visual labeling of egocentric demonstrations often leaves contiguous gaps in action supervision. Reliable poses around a gap still provide its net translation and rotation. We use this information to train vision-language-action policies while preserving ordinary flow matching on reliable local actions. A composition objective constrains the aggregate motion of missing-action estimates, combining multiple fixed windows when a gap exceeds the policy horizon. A shared interval branch learns from reliable endpoints and supplies the composition target. On LIBERO with 20% of demonstrations and 70.5% missing actions, the main method achieves 87.9% mean success over three training runs. In one paired comparison with matched observation exposure and training cost, its advantage over masking is 10.5%. On Ego-informed RoboTwin gaps, success is 45.4%, versus 40.3% for masking. On five real-robot tasks, mean success is 59.6%, versus 54.8% for interval flow matching alone and 48.8% for masking.

</details>


### [341] [PACE-WM: Parallel World Modeling with Action History for Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27173)
*Haishan Liu,Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Yao Zhang,Changti Wu,Fanping Liu,Jianzheng Liu,Lei Xie,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PACE-WM 并行世界模型，以已执行动作历史作为时间上下文条件因果 Transformer，并行预测多步未来表征，并配合 RAMP 动作选择策略，在视觉规划任务中实现 98% 平均成功率与约 7–9 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 式世界模型虽能以未来动作条件预测未来表征进行视觉规划，但相似观测可能掩盖不同运动状态，缺乏时间上下文时难以正确解释未来动作效果，因此需要引入动作执行历史来消歧运动状态。

Method: PACE-WM 维护已执行动作的循环记忆，并用该记忆条件化因果 Transformer；在每个预测窗口内并行预测多个未来表征，不进行递归的预测状态反馈。规划阶段提出 Reference-Anchored Minimum Progress (RAMP)，以共享参考为锚点，沿直接路径与分裂路径评估候选动作的预测目标改进量来打分选动作。

Result: 在四个视觉规划任务上，PACE-WM+RAMP 平均成功率达 98%，在相同 CEM 设置下规划速度比自回归 LeWM 快约 7–9 倍；另构建四个受控基准，用于评估运动消歧、目标相对运动与目标自适应能力。

Conclusion: 将执行动作历史作为时间上下文并结合并行非递归未来预测，可有效解决观测歧义下的规划问题；RAMP 的参考锚定打分进一步兼顾目标相对进展与规划效率，整体在成功率与速度上优于自回归基线。

Abstract: JEPA-style world models support visual planning by predicting future representations conditioned on future actions. However, similar observations can conceal different motion states, making temporal context essential for interpreting future action effects. We propose PACE-WM, a parallel world model that uses executed-action history to contextualize future prediction and planning. Specifically, PACE-WM maintains a recurrent memory of executed actions and uses this memory to condition a causal Transformer. PACE-WM predicts multiple future representations in parallel without recursive predicted-state feedback within each prediction window. We further introduce Reference-Anchored Minimum Progress (RAMP) for action selection during planning. RAMP scores candidate actions by their predicted goal improvement along direct and split paths relative to a shared reference. Across four visual planning tasks, PACE-WM with RAMP achieves an average success rate of $98\%$ and plans approximately $7$-$9\times$ faster than autoregressive LeWM under matched CEM settings. We also construct four controlled benchmarks to evaluate PACE-WM's capabilities in motion disambiguation, goal-relative motion, and goal adaptation. Project page is available at https://github.com/LHS180/PACE-WM.

</details>


### [342] [Inject, Encode, Navigate: Feature-Wise Time Injection for Rectified Flow with Bidirectional Feature Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27179)
*Xiaogang Wang,Debasish Mishra,Jia Hao Toh,Wangsheng Liu,Yash Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出三项改进提升视觉机器人导航策略的效率与性能：采用rectified flow将推理步数降至一步；引入双向Mamba视觉编码器建模长序列时序依赖；并提出特征级timestep条件化设计，使视觉嵌入显式受flow timestep调制，修复时序上下文与生成过程耦合不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉模型的机器人导航方法（如diffusion policy）需昂贵的迭代去噪，难以兼顾效率与性能；同时直接在精度与速度间组合高效组件并未带来增益，作者希望找出并解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 1) 使用rectified flow框架把推理步数压缩到1步；2) 采用基于选择性状态空间建模的双向Mamba视觉编码器，捕捉观测帧间双向时序依赖，支持更长输入序列；3) 关键设计——特征级timestep条件化，将flow timestep显式注入视觉嵌入，实现噪声水平感知的轨迹生成，恢复梯度通路并促进rectified flow与时序编码器的协同交互。

Result: 作者分析发现，简单组合上述两个组件不会带来额外收益甚至变差，根源在于Hydra式时序上下文在所有flow timesteps上被无差别共享、timestep信息仅在速度网络内部引入，耦合过弱；采用特征级timestep条件化后，在多个数据集上的定量与定性实验均稳定优于baseline与对比方法。

Conclusion: 效率与精度组件需通过适当的条件化机制耦合才能发挥协同效应；显式噪声水平感知的视觉条件化是单步rectified flow导航策略有效的关键。

Abstract: Vision-model-based robotic navigation has gained increasing popularity, yet existing approaches still struggle to deliver effective and efficient navigation performance, \textit{e.g.}, diffusion-based policies that require costly iterative denoising. In this work, we propose three improvements to bridge these gaps. First, we adopt a rectified flow framework to reduce the required inference steps to one while achieving comparable or even superior performance. Second, we leverage a bidirectional Mamba-based visual encoder that employs selective state-space modeling to more effectively capture bidirectional temporal dependencies across observation frames, even with longer input sequences. Most importantly, we find that naïvely combining these two components to balance accuracy with inference speed yields no further performance gains or even produces inferior results. Upon extensive analysis, we realized that the root cause lies in the fact that the Hydra-derived temporal context is shared unchanged across all flow timesteps, and timestep information is introduced only within the velocity network. This weak coupling limits the model's ability to adaptively exploit temporal visual features at different flow states. To address this, we propose a feature-wise timestep conditioning design that explicitly conditions the visual embedding on the flow timestep, enabling noise-level-aware trajectory generation, thereby restoring a meaningful gradient pathway and enabling coherent interaction between the rectified flow and the temporal encoder. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms both baseline and competitive methods.

</details>


### [343] [HumanoidPose: Monocular 3D Pose Estimation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27181)
*Jinlin Gan,Mingzhe Li,Junsheng Zhang,Zhibo Zhou,Jiahan Geng,Yuhua Luo,Changwang Zhang,Chenglu Wen,Lan Xu,Siqi Shen,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单目3D人形机器人位姿估计基准Humanoids与框架HumanoidPose，结合类人视觉感知、关节建模与显式运动学接地，实现强关节恢复与3D位姿估计并支持零样本真实场景迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人进入真实环境后，单目3D全身位姿恢复对状态感知、动作分析与机器人学习至关重要。但HRPE处于机器人位姿估计(RPE)与人体位姿估计(HPE)的交叉地带：RPE主要面向非人形机器人（多为固定基座机械臂），HPE依赖人类专属骨架拓扑、人体模型与运动先验，两者均未充分覆盖该任务。现有工作多停留在2D关键点检测与HPE适配，缺少人形原生的3D位姿恢复研究，也缺乏配套训练数据与评测协议的专用基准。

Method: 构建Humanoids基准：提供跨多种人形本体的合成3D监督、人工标注的真实2D数据，以及在实体Roban人形机器人上采集的RGB序列与机器人状态配对数据用于3D评测。提出HumanoidPose框架：融合类人视觉感知、机器人关节(articulation)建模与显式运动学接地(kinematic grounding)。

Result: 在Humanoids与DHRP-H1基准上，方法在关节恢复与3D位姿估计上表现强劲，并实现向真实世界2D与3D评测的零样本迁移；消融实验验证了机器人原生关节建模与显式运动学接地的贡献。

Conclusion: 填补了人形机器人原生3D位姿估计的基准与方​​法空白，数据集与代码将公开以推动社区后续研究。

Abstract: As humanoid robots increasingly enter real-world environments, recovering their 3D whole-body pose from monocular imagery is becoming increasingly important for external state perception, motion analysis, and robot learning. However, Humanoid Robot Pose Estimation (HRPE) lies at the intersection of conventional Robot Pose Estimation (RPE) and Human Pose Estimation (HPE), while not being fully addressed by either paradigm: RPE primarily focuses on non-humanoid robots, particularly fixed-base manipulators, whereas HPE is built upon human-specific skeletal topologies, body models, and motion priors. Despite recent progress in humanoid 2D keypoint detection and HPE adaptation, humanoid-native 3D pose recovery remains insufficiently studied, and a dedicated benchmark with suitable training data and evaluation protocols is still lacking. To address this gap, we introduce Humanoids, a benchmark for monocular 3D humanoid robot pose estimation, and propose HumanoidPose, a framework for this task. Humanoids provides synthetic 3D supervision across diverse humanoid embodiments, manually annotated real-world 2D data, and real-world RGB sequences paired with robot states for 3D evaluation on a physical Roban humanoid. HumanoidPose combines human-inspired visual perception with robot articulation modeling and explicit kinematic grounding. Experiments on Humanoids and the DHRP-H1 benchmark demonstrate strong performance in articulation recovery and 3D pose estimation, including zero-shot transfer to real-world 2D and 3D evaluation. Ablation studies further show the contribution of robot-native articulation modeling and explicit kinematic grounding. The dataset and code will be publicly released to support future research in the community.

</details>


### [344] [Tripwire: Robot Agents that Program Their Own Interrupts under Human Disturbance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27182)
*Sunli Chen,Yun Zong,Qinhong Zhou,Xiaowen Qiu,Jiaben Chen,Zeyuan Yang,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TRIP：一种免训练、事件驱动的智能体框架，让智能体在每次调用时自行编写"绊线"（轻量有状态 Python 程序）监控策略运行过程，触发即中断并把控制权交还智能体重新规划，从而在人类干扰下显著提升机器人安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体呈"回合制"：调用预训练机器人策略（VLA/WAM）后须等其返回才能重新决策，形成盲区间。期间人可能走入路径或移动待抓取物体。提高调用频率受限于推理模型每次决策耗时数秒，且多数控制步无需新决策，因此需要在策略执行期间识别"何时需要重新思考"。

Method: TRIP 为免训练的事件驱动框架：每次调用智能体时，智能体写出一条 tripwire——与策略并行运行的有状态轻量 Python 程序，可调用紧凑 VLM（如定位行人并测量距离）判断是继续执行还是交还控制权；触发时框架挂起策略、携原因返回控制，智能体重新评估、调整并在新绊线下继续。评估用 3 个 VLA 与 1 个 WAM，在 RoboHD（168 个 RoboCasa365 场景，模拟人员干扰，任何接触即失败）上测试，并与相同智能体与工具的回合制框架对比。

Result: 最强策略下人类接触率减半，安全性由 61.3% 提升至 81.3%，任务成功率未下降（38.7% vs. 31.0%）。真实机器人实验表明 TRIP 能在人类干扰下完成两项操作任务，而回合制框架与纯策略基本失败。

Conclusion: 让智能体自行编写事件触发条件，可在不重新训练、不频繁调用大模型的前提下弥合"策略连续反应—智能体离散决策"之间的盲区，兼顾安全与任务成功，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: Agent harnesses are turn-based: the agent acts, waits for the result, then reasons. When the action invokes a pretrained robot policy, such as a vision-language-action (VLA) or world action model (WAM), the policy may react continuously to observations, but the agent cannot revisit its decision until the call returns. Meanwhile, a person may step into the robot's path or move the object it is about to grasp. Invoking the agent more often narrows this blind interval, but a reasoning model takes seconds per decision and most control steps need no new one. We propose TRIP, a training-free, event-driven harness in which the agent programs its own interrupts. With each call, the agent writes a tripwire: a lightweight, stateful Python program that runs alongside the policy, can query a compact VLM (for example, to locate a person and measure their distance), and decides whether the call continues or control returns to the agent. When it fires, the harness halts the policy and returns control with the reason; the agent then reassesses, adapts, and continues under a rewritten tripwire. We evaluate TRIP with three VLAs and one WAM on RoboHD, a benchmark of 168 RoboCasa365 scenes in which a simulated person disturbs the robot and any human-robot contact counts as failure. Compared with a turn-based harness using the same agent and tools, TRIP halves the rate of human contact with the strongest policy, raising safety from 61.3% to 81.3%, without sacrificing task success (38.7% vs. 31.0%). Real-robot experiments show that TRIP completes two manipulation tasks under human interference where the turn-based harness and the policy alone largely fail.

</details>


### [345] [NavTTT: Adaptive Parametric Memory via Hierarchical Test-Time Training for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27219)
*Xiaoyu Luo,Chengxing Lin,Jiaxin Huang,Jiale Liu,Jingyu Guo,Sirui Chen,Aolin Li,Shaoli Huang,Cheng-zhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NavTTT：将 fast-weight 测试时训练用于 VLN，通过导航条件引导的自适应记忆形成与分层记忆整合，缓解流式视觉观测的长期历史存储与冗余干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN 智能体需在长回合中累积并推理流式视觉观测，但 Transformer 上下文容量有限，难以维持长期历史。已有 fast-weight TTT 可把流式观测编码进演化参数状态，却不区分信息性观测与重复视觉内容，冗余 token 干扰后续决策所需信息的保留。

Method: NavTTT 含三部分：(1) 利用全局导航上下文对视觉证据做重要性优先化；(2) 将语言指令与空间信息结合，构建空间落地的记忆表示；(3) 以回合局部的非线性 fast weights 存储为自适应参数记忆（APM）；并在多个解码器深度上维护独立的 fast-weight 记忆，解耦记忆形成以减少跨层耦合、避免误差/偏置在不同记忆层级间传播，同时让共享历史在不同抽象层级上独立表示。

Result: 在 VLN-CE 基准上取得较强导航性能，并在真实机器人上实现有效的指令跟随。

Conclusion: 导航条件化、分层解耦的记忆式测试时训练可有效突破 Transformer 上下文限制，为 VLN 提供可扩展的长期记忆机制，并具备真机部署可行性。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) demands that agents accumulate and reason over streaming visual observations throughout long navigation episodes. Existing approaches struggle to maintain long-term history, constrained by the finite context capacity of transformer architectures. To solve the problem, fast-weight test-time training (TTT) provides a mechanism for encoding streaming observations into an evolving parameter state. However, existing TTT does not distinguish informative observations from repetitive visual content, allowing redundant tokens to interfere with the retention of information needed for subsequent decisions. We propose Navigation-Conditioned Test-Time Training (NavTTT), which introduces adaptive memory formation and hierarchical memory integration for vision-and-language navigation. NavTTT leverages global navigation context to prioritize informative visual evidence and integrates language instructions with spatial information to construct spatially grounded memory representations. Episode-local nonlinear fast weights store these representations as Adaptive Parametric Memory (APM), preserving history beyond the visual context for later decisions. To reduce cross-layer coupling in memory formation, NavTTT maintains independent fast-weight memories across multiple decoder depths. This decoupled design prevents errors or biases from spreading across different memory levels. Meanwhile, it enables shared historical information to be independently represented at various levels of abstraction. Experiments demonstrate strong navigation performance on VLN-CE benchmarks and effective instruction following on a real robot.

</details>


### [346] [Trailblazer-F: Speculative Action-Flow Inference for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27226)
*Haibo Hu,Lianming Huang,Qiao Li,Nan Guan,Chun Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Trailblazer-F 是一个用于驾驶世界-动作模型的推测式动作流推理框架：用轻量 drafter 在紧凑的路点空间中预测去噪路径，使目标模型可并行验证多步、减少串行更新，从而在保持规划质量的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界-动作模型通过迭代动作去噪生成规划轨迹，每次更新依赖前一状态，这种串行依赖导致推理延迟高，限制实时规划。推测式推理可缓解延迟，但在连续动作空间中面临高效候选生成与合适验证的挑战。

Method: 提出 Trailblazer-F：一个轻量 drafter 充当"开路者"，在紧凑且结构化的路点（waypoint）空间中规划候选去噪路径，使目标模型能够并行验证多个去噪步并以更少的串行更新推进；预训练目标模型保持冻结，仅靠 drafter 提供提议，兼顾质量与效率。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 navtest 全部 12,146 个场景上，规划性能接近基线：SimWAM 与 DriveWAM 的 PDMS 分别变化 +0.04 和 -0.08 分；NVIDIA L20 GPU 上成对推理测得 2.31× 与 1.55× 加速；NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin 实车实验分别达 1.96× 与 1.37× 加速，且轨迹与原生采样定性一致。

Conclusion: 推测式动作流推理可在不微调目标模型的前提下有效打破去噪串行瓶颈，在保持规划精度的同时带来跨平台（数据中心 GPU 与车载边缘设备）的稳定加速，适用于实时驾驶规划部署。

Abstract: Driving world-action models generate planned trajectories through iterative action denoising, with each update depending on the preceding state. This sequential dependency incurs substantial inference latency, limiting timely planning. Speculative inference offers an opportunity to reduce this latency by predicting future denoising states for parallel target evaluation, but requires efficient proposal generation and suitable verification for continuous actions. We present Trailblazer-F, a speculative action-flow inference framework in which a lightweight drafter acts as a trailblazer, charting a candidate denoising path through the compact, structured waypoint space so that the target model can verify multiple steps in parallel and advance with fewer sequential updates. Our method maintains planning quality while keeping the pretrained target model frozen.Across all 12,146 NAVSIM v1 navtest scenes, Trailblazer-F maintains near-baseline planning performance, with PDMS changes of +0.04 and -0.08 points for SimWAM and DriveWAM, respectively. Paired inference measurements on an NVIDIA L20 GPU yield 2.31x and 1.55x speedups. Real-vehicle experiments on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin further demonstrate 1.96x and 1.37x speedups, together with close qualitative trajectory agreement with native sampling. our code is here https://github.com/DanileHaibo/Trailblazer-F

</details>


### [347] [AELF: Active Terrain-Aware UAV Exploration Using a Lightweight Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27263)
*Mingyang Li,Xiao Cao,Yi Luo,Yuting Tao,Hongjie Huang,Bowen Xu,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于轻量2.5D地图的无人机自主探索方法，无需先验高度信息、自适应地形起伏，将2D路径规划升维到3D评估代价，在仿真与真实森林、山地中实现快速探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于3D体素地图的探索方法计算开销大，且因遮挡和有限视野产生"盲体素"问题；已有方法依赖固定高度尺度，需要先验高度信息，难以适应复杂地形。

Method: 用2.5D地图替代3D体素地图，保留地形信息并降低计算量，同时消除盲体素问题；探索中主动自适应地形高程变化，不依赖先验高度；在2.5D地图上做2D路径规划，再提升到3D，并结合地形可通行性评估视点间代价，以减少频繁高度变化。

Result: 仿真与真实环境实验证明方法优于现有方案；真实测试中快速探索超过2000 m²的杂乱森林及高程变化达10 m的坡地，最大速度5 m/s。

Conclusion: 轻量2.5D表示结合2D规划升维评估，可兼顾3D探索的通用性与效率，实现复杂地形下平滑、快速、无需先验高度的自主空中探索。

Abstract: This paper presents an efficient and robust approach for autonomous aerial exploration in complex environments. Instead of relying on conventional 3D voxel-based maps for exploration, we utilize a lightweight 2.5D map framework that reduces computational overhead while preserving essential topographic information. More importantly, this representation systematically eliminates the blind voxel problem commonly encountered in voxel-based exploration due to occlusions and limited visibility. Unlike previous methods that rely on a fixed altitude scale for exploration, our approach requires no prior height information and actively adapts to terrain elevation changes during exploration. To further enhance exploration efficiency while preserving the generality of 3D exploration, we perform 2D path planning on the 2.5D map framework, which is then lifted to 3D to evaluate the cost between viewpoints by taking into account terrain traversability, thereby reducing frequent altitude changes and enabling smoother exploration. Extensive experiments conducted in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and advantages of our approach over existing solutions. Our approach demonstrates fast exploration of over 2000~$\text{m}^2$ of cluttered forest and sloped mountainous terrain with elevation changes of up to 10~m, reaching a maximum speed of 5~m/s in real-world tests.

</details>


### [348] [HermiteTwin: Bicubic Hermite Shell Digital Twins for Contact-Rich Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27282)
*Kangrui Zhang,Mu Huang,Xing Shen,Tiantian Liu,Bo Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HermiteTwin 用双三次 Hermite 壳表示薄物体的变形、用材料空间稠密采样表示接触，从机器人操作的多视角视频重建物理数字孪生，在重建、重仿真与未来预测上优于弹簧-质点基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体操作重建方法通常用单一粒子集同时承担建模弯曲与解析机器人接触两项职责，二者分辨率需求冲突，因此难以兼顾物理紧凑性与接触精细度。

Method: 将变形与接触分离建模：物体表示为双三次 Hermite 壳（C^1 单元的粗网格，节点位置与导数定义光滑曲面，含 Kirchhoff–Love 弯曲），夹爪通过材料空间中的稠密采样点执行抓取、夹持与释放，并借 Hermite 基函数将附着力求和传递到壳上；用 CMA-ES 结合伴随法精化材料与接触参数；以仿真壳驱动重建的高斯外观模型。

Result: 在真实布料与泡沫操作实验中，HermiteTwin 在重建、重仿真和未来预测三项任务上均优于当前最优的弹簧-质点孪生方法。

Conclusion: 变形与接触可各自以独立分辨率表示，从而在保持物理状态紧凑的同时实现细粒度接触；该解耦表示配合参数辨识与高斯外观模型，可有效提升薄可变形物体数字孪生的精度与预测能力。

Abstract: While existing methods for reconstructing deformable object manipulation have made impressive progress, they typically represent a thin object with a single particle set that must serve two purposes at once: modeling bending, and resolving contact with the robot. To bridge this gap, we present HermiteTwin, a framework that reconstructs a physical digital twin of a thin deformable object from multiview video of a robot manipulating it. Our key idea is to represent deformation and contact separately, each at its own resolution. The object is simulated as a bicubic Hermite shell, a coarse grid of $C^1$ elements whose nodal positions and derivatives define a smooth surface with Kirchhoff--Love bending, while the gripper acts through dense samples in material space that are captured, clamped, and released, and whose attachment forces reach the shell through the Hermite basis. This decoupling keeps the physical state compact while contact stays fine-grained. We identify effective material and contact parameters with CMA-ES followed by adjoint-based refinement, and drive a reconstructed Gaussian appearance model with the simulated shell. In experiments on real cloth and foam manipulation, HermiteTwin outperforms the state-of-the-art spring--mass twin across reconstruction, resimulation, and future prediction.

</details>


### [349] [Re-JEPA: Coupling Representation Prediction and Signal Reconstruction for State-Conditioned EMG Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27299)
*Yijian Yang,Tao Chen,Xiaodong Yang,Zongheng Guo,Heng Liu,Lun Shu,Chen Luo,Junhao Tu,Lei Shu,Mingzhou Wu,Yifan Liu,Zhuanghui Zhang,Zhenqiang Liu,Shilei Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Re-JEPA：将JEPA潜在预测与信号重建耦合、并用状态条件运动解码器实现跨用户/跨动作的连续sEMG手部跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从sEMG进行连续手部跟踪需同时具备跨用户、跨动作类型的泛化能力与对细微运动的敏感性。JEPA类联合嵌入预测架构虽能通过上下文预测学习潜在表征空间，但跟踪效果取决于表征保留哪些运动相关细节、以及这些细节如何随手部状态演化被解读，现有方法对此缺乏约束。

Method: 提出Re-JEPA框架：(1) 预训练阶段无需运动学标签，从可见EMG patch预测多层级连续潜在目标，并基于融合预测重建被掩码的幅值与相位属性，以信号重建作为对上下文预测器的额外约束；(2) 下游跟踪采用Gated Persistent Motion (GPM)解码器，将EMG token读出以循环状态token与运动历史为条件，通过门控状态更新和增量姿态预测生成连续轨迹。

Result: 在emg2pose跟踪协议下，全量微调后角度MAE相对vemg2pose分别降低约10.3%（跨用户）、19.3%（跨阶段）和14.9%（联合跨用户-跨阶段）划分。

Conclusion: 在JEPA式潜在预测中引入幅值/相位信号重建约束，并结合状态条件化的循环增量解码，可有效提升sEMG连续手部跟踪的跨用户与跨阶段泛化精度。

Abstract: Improving continuous hand tracking from surface electromyography (sEMG) requires generalization across users and motion types while maintaining sensitivity to subtle movements. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn latent representation spaces through contextual prediction, but their effectiveness for tracking depends on which motion-relevant details these representations preserve and how those details are interpreted as the hand state evolves. We introduce Re-JEPA, a tracking framework that couples representation prediction with signal reconstruction and incorporates state-conditioned motion decoding. During pretraining without kinematic labels, Re-JEPA predicts continuous latent targets at multiple levels from visible EMG patches and reconstructs masked amplitude and phase attributes from the fused predictions. Signal reconstruction imposes an additional constraint on the contextual predictor, encouraging its outputs to retain information needed for signal recovery. For downstream tracking, the Gated Persistent Motion (GPM) decoder conditions EMG-token readout on recurrent state tokens and motion history, then generates continuous trajectories through gated state updates and incremental pose prediction. Under the emg2pose tracking protocol, Re-JEPA reduces angular MAE after full fine-tuning by approximately 10.3%, 19.3%, and 14.9% relative to vemg2pose on the cross-user, cross-stage, and joint cross-user-stage splits, respectively.

</details>


### [350] [ManipScope: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Instruction Following and Generalization in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27305)
*Jiacheng Li,Yaping Li,Congsheng Xu,Qingchen Liu,Haonan Wang,Zhijie Deng,Jiahu Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ManipScope：仅用于评估的诊断基准，检验机器人在任务要求改变时是否真正遵循指令。基于 RoboTwin 2.0，构建 48 个无示范的留出场景（六大任务族），并对 12 个策略检查点做系统性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操纵任务"成功完成"并不能证明机器人遵循指令，而非执行熟悉的常规动作；现有基准无法区分"技能具备"与"目标遵循"。

Method: 构建任务成功之外的诊断链，每步检验一种失败解释：干扰物位移分析、夹爪记录、在相同初始状态上重分配目标以测指令敏感性、指令无关选择器作下界、GPT-6 智能体作正控制、训练任务复现、同数据检查点对比、agent 选目标+策略执行的分工实验，以及未来视频预测与内部干预探针案例研究。

Result: 11 个选择场景共 13,200 回合中，84.6% 的失败是移动了干扰物，失败回合通常抓取非目标；目标重分配时 10 个检查点在 1,000 个状态上仅 3.9% 完全遵循指令，低于指令盲选择器的 16%，而 GPT-6 正控制达 88%；失败在未修改训练任务上复现且非技能缺失所致；两个同数据检查点仍能遵循颜色指令。

Conclusion: 这些场景中的成功在很大程度上反映与指令无关的偏好；预测与执行可能一致地指向错误目标，动作改变也未必传递目标。评估须分别校验任务成功、一致性与对请求目标的敏感性。

Abstract: Completing a manipulation task does not establish that a robot follows the requested goal rather than a familiar routine. ManipScope is an evaluation-only diagnostic benchmark for robotic instruction following under changed task requirements. Built on RoboTwin 2.0's simulation resources, it specifies 48 held-out scenes across six task families with explicit source-task links; policies are trained on unmodified RoboTwin 2.0 demonstrations, and no demonstrations of the scenes are provided, so success requires reusing skills under a changed request rather than fitting supplied examples. Beyond task success, ManipScope provides a chain of diagnostics in which each step tests one explanation of a failure. For twelve policy checkpoints, 84.6% of failures in 13,200 episodes on eleven selection scenes displace a distractor, and gripper records show that failed episodes usually grasp a non-target. When the target is reassigned among the candidates on identical initial states, ten checkpoints follow every instruction on 3.9% of 1,000 states, below the 16% of an instruction-blind chooser, while a GPT-6 agent run as positive control reaches 88%. The failure recurs on unmodified training tasks and is not explained by missing manipulation skills, so success in these scenes largely reflects instruction-independent preferences; two checkpoints trained on the same data nevertheless follow color instructions. In a feasibility test, letting an agent select the target and the policy execute partly restores instruction following. Case studies with future-video and internal-intervention probes further show that forecasts and execution can agree on the wrong target and that action changes need not transfer the goal. Success, consistency, and sensitivity must each be checked against the requested goal.

</details>


### [351] [Frontier Voltage Boost Laplacian Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27308)
*Vinay Pritamani,Rishabh Jain,Jack Lowry,Joshua Smith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Frontier Voltage Boost Laplacian 路径规划方法，在继承调和势场法无局部极小值、路径平滑等优点的同时，通过分段（piecewise）策略提升大尺寸地图与窄通道场景的求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期调和势场（Harmonic Potential Field）规划方法虽无局部极小值且路径平滑，但难以扩展到大地图，处理窄通道效率低，需在保留优点的同时提升可扩展性与效率。

Method: 提出一种分段式（piecewise）的 Laplacian 路径规划框架，引入"前沿电压提升"（Frontier Voltage Boost）机制改进原始调和势场方法，将大问题分解为局部子问题分段求解。

Result: 该方法可在更大尺寸地图上求解，并比既有调和势场方法更快地通过窄通道；实验刻画了算法正确性、性能与路径平滑度随地图规模变化的规律。

Conclusion: 分段式前沿电压提升 Laplacian 规划在保持无局部极小值与平滑路径特性的基础上，显著改善了调和势场法的可扩展性与效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces Frontier Voltage Boost Laplacian path planning. The method inherits many of the attractive features of earlier Harmonic Potential Field planning methods, including absence of local minima and smooth paths, while improving the original. The piecewise approach we introduce is able to solve larger maps and handle narrow passages more quickly than earlier Harmonic Potential Field methods. After explaining the algorithm, we experimentally characterize its cor- rectness, performance, and path smoothness as a function of map size. Code is available at https://github.com/vpritamani/FrontierVoltageBoostLaplacianPathPlanning

</details>


### [352] [Embodied Interaction Policy: A Unified Model for Full-Duplex Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27327)
*Youqiang Gui,Yunfei Liu,Lijian Lin,Ye Zhu,Jianan Wang,Cheng Peng,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的流式框架 EIP，用逐层联合注意力把音频交互专家与运动专家耦合，实现语音与机器人运动的连续协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时人—机器人社交交互需要语音与肢体行为持续协调；现有系统将交互智能体与动作生成分离，导致对话状态变化时协调困难。

Method: EIP 通过 layer-wise joint attention 将 Audio-Interaction expert 与 motion expert 耦合，使动作生成可直接访问演化的交互上下文；采用 chunk-wise causal mask 避免访问未来信息；引入 vocal token 编码说话状态与回复的语义情绪；基于共语与双人交互数据构建 EI-Data，并分两阶段训练以学习状态自适应与情绪条件化行为。

Result: 运动质量优于代表性手势生成基线；相较代表性级联系统，交互成功率更高，warm-start 延迟降低 64%；物理部署与用户研究显示运动自然性与交互舒适度提升。

Conclusion: 在冻结交互专家的条件下，EIP 支持主动响应期间的连续机器人运动与可感知打断的轮次切换，验证了统一流式交互—动作策略在真实社交交互中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time human-robot social interaction requires continuous coordination between speech and physical behavior. Existing systems often separate interaction agents from motion generation, complicating coordination as conversational states change. We propose \textbf{Embodied Interaction Policy (EIP)}, a unified streaming framework that couples an Audio-Interaction expert with a motion expert through layer-wise joint attention. This allows motion generation to directly access the evolving interaction context. A chunk-wise causal mask prevents access to future context, while a vocal token encodes speaking state and the semantic emotion of the response. We construct \textbf{Embodied Interaction Dataset (EI-Data)} from co-speech and dyadic interaction data and train our model in two stages to learn state-adaptive and emotion-conditioned behavior. Combined with the frozen interaction expert, EIP supports continuous robot motion during proactive responses and interruption-aware turn-taking. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate improved motion quality over representative gesture-generation baselines. Compared with a representative cascaded system, EIP achieves higher interaction success and reduces warm-start latency by 64\%. Physical deployment and user studies further demonstrate improved motion naturalness and interaction comfort.

</details>


### [353] [Latent Jacobian Steer: Eliciting Contact-Rich Out-of-Distribution Behaviors from Pretrained Humanoid Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27338)
*S K Swaminathan,Shivam Sood,Shreyansh Kansal,Chengyang He,Guillaume Adrien Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Latent Jacobian Steer (LJS)，将直接关节动作空间的采样与冻结预训练人形控制器隐空间(z)语义相耦合，通过策略雅可比的阻尼局部逆将任务相关动作扰动映射为隐指令，在仿真中搜索并组合出复杂、接触丰富的分布外行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练通用隐条件全身人形控制器(BFM)难以引出复杂、接触丰富的分布外行为：原始关节动作探索可直接做任务特定调整但破坏控制器已学到的协调性；隐空间探索保留协调性但难以导向期望的物理效果。两者各有互补优势，需要一种将二者结合的方法。

Method: LJS：针对以期望物理效果指定的任务，先在任务相关的关节动作空间采样扰动，再经策略雅可比的阻尼局部逆(damped local inverse)映射为候选隐指令z；候选指令通过冻结的预训练策略执行，探索方向被引导至新任务所需的物理结果；在仿真中评估候选结果，并将其组合成隐指令序列以完成任务。另做消融分别对比仅动作空间与仅隐空间探索。并扩展到目标条件灵巧手策略（在目标空间直接搜索作对比）。

Result: 消融显示仅动作空间或仅隐空间探索均无法达到二者耦合的能力。在跨栏、操作铰接物体、以非抓握接触开门等多样分布外任务上，方法产生接触丰富的行为；十个随机种子下，LJS 在每项人形任务上的完成度均高于单一空间搜索。在灵巧手策略上，LJS 在四个装配任务中优于直接在目标空间搜索。

Conclusion: 将动作空间采样与冻结策略隐空间语义耦合，可有效引导预训练行为基础模型产生复杂接触丰富的分布外行为，优于任一单一探索空间，并具备跨本体（人形、灵巧手）的通用性。

Abstract: Given a pretrained general latent-conditioned whole-body humanoid controller, say a behavioral foundation model (BFM), how can we elicit complex and contact-rich out-of-distribution behaviors? Raw joint-action exploration enables direct, task-specific adjustments but can disrupt coordination learned by the pretrained controller, whereas latent exploration retains that coordination but is difficult to steer toward desired physical effects. To combine the complementary strengths of these exploration spaces, we propose Latent Jacobian Steer (LJS), which couples sampling in direct joint-action space with the semantics of the learned frozen controller's input latent space. For a task specified through desired physical effects, we sample task-relevant joint-action perturbations and map them through a damped local inverse of the policy Jacobian to obtain candidate latent ($z$) commands. These commands are executed through the frozen pretrained policy, while exploration is directed toward the physical outcomes required by the new task. Candidate outcomes are evaluated in simulation and composed into latent-command sequences that accomplish the task. Ablations show that neither action-space nor latent-space exploration alone provides the same capability as their coupling. Across diverse out-of-distribution tasks, simulation-based searching using this method produces contact-rich behaviors, including hurdling, manipulating articulated objects, and opening doors through non-prehensile contact. Over ten seeds, LJS completes more of each humanoid task than when searching in either space alone. Beyond humanoids, it also steers a goal-conditioned dexterous hand policy through four assembly tasks better than searching in goal space directly.

</details>


### [354] [Learning Interaction Dynamics via Motion-Region Guidance for Robotic Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27344)
*Yongkang He,Xiaofen Xing,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MoReWorld：以共享“运动区域”为统一引导信号的行动条件视频世界模型，聚焦交互相关区域学习与生成，提升物理合理性与交互一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行动条件视频世界模型优化全局重建误差，未强调交互相关区域，导致整体外观得以保留但局部交互细节被忽视，出现虚假接触效应、机器人-物体交互不一致等物理不合理结果。

Method: 统一运动区域引导框架：用同一运动区域（环境中随机器人动作而运动的部分）同时驱动局部运动预测、区域感知监督与运动门控的未来特征条件化；推理时由视觉历史与机器人动作预测运动概率图，无需未来观测或真实运动标签。

Result: 在WorldSimProbe上取得最高总体模拟器保真度得分，并在局部一致性、全局一致性与交互动力学三项上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 运动区域引导能在保持场景级一致性的同时更忠实地预测行动条件交互动力学，说明将学习与监督集中于交互相关区域是提升视频世界模型物理合理性的有效途径。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models imagine future observations under candidate robot actions, providing a promising visual tool for robot policy evaluation and interaction simulation. However, existing video world models often optimize global reconstruction errors instead of emphasizing interaction-relevant regions. As a result, they may preserve overall scene appearance but overlook local interaction details, leading to physically implausible outcomes such as hallucinated contact effects and inconsistent robot-object interactions. We introduce MoReWorld, a unified motion-region-guided framework that uses a shared motion region, the part of the environment that moves in response to the robot's actions, to focus dynamics learning and video generation on interaction-relevant regions. The same motion region guides local motion prediction, region-aware supervision, and motion-gated future-feature conditioning. At inference time, the motion region is predicted as a motion probability map from visual history and robot actions, without requiring future observations or ground-truth motion labels. On WorldSimProbe, MoReWorld achieves the highest overall simulator-faithfulness score, with the strongest performance on local consistency, global consistency, and interaction dynamics. These results indicate that motion-region guidance helps the model preserve scene-level consistency while more faithfully predicting action-conditioned interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [355] [NormFlowSDE: Normalizing Flows for Amortized Bayesian State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27362)
*Yi Zhang,Lars Mikelsons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NormFlowSDE，一种摊销式贝叶斯状态估计器，将神经随机微分方程（SDE）与条件归一化流相结合，直接从观测与控制序列学习状态轨迹分布，无需显式物理模型或逐序列优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对包含摩擦、碰撞、接触形变等难以精确建模效应的系统（如富接触操作），获取准确动力学模型困难；参数、输入、扰动的不确定性及简化噪声模型会引入仿真-现实差距，降低估计精度。因此希望不显式指定物理模型而学习条件于观测与控制的状态轨迹分布。

Method: 1) 以神经 SDE 建模状态的连续时间演化；2) 以条件归一化流刻画变量的随机行为与轨迹分布；3) 训练后对新观测序列直接采样状态路径，无需逐序列优化；4) 理论方面，证明均值中心化、漂移校正的推断方案在给定假设下弱收敛于 Itô 扩散。

Result: 在部分可观测动力系统（仿真系统与真实机器人数据集）上与直接估计器及基于归一化流的学习型粒子滤波对比：NormFlowSDE 取得最低估计误差，并提供校准良好的不确定性区间；在每个数据集上推理速度比最快的粒子滤波快 4 倍。

Conclusion: 将归一化流的分布表达能力与 SDE 的连续时间动力学结合，可构建高效、无需模型且摊销化的贝叶斯状态估计器；其在精度、不确定性校准与计算效率上均优于学习型粒子滤波基线。

Abstract: Bayesian state estimators, such as Kalman filters, estimate states from noisy observations based on dynamical models. However, obtaining accurate models can be challenging when the system involves effects that are difficult to model faithfully, such as friction, impacts, and deformation in contact-rich manipulation. Uncertainty in system parameters, inputs, and disturbances, together with simplified noise models, can further introduce simulation-to-reality mismatch and degrade estimation accuracy. These limitations motivate learning a distribution over state trajectories conditioned on observations and controls without explicitly specifying the underlying physical models. We introduce NormFlowSDE, an amortized Bayesian state estimator that learns a distribution over state trajectories by combining neural stochastic differential equations with conditional normalizing flows. Its key design choice is to utilize the capability of normalizing flows to learn stochastic behavior of variables, while leveraging the continuous-time dynamics of SDEs to model the evolution of states over time. Once trained, the model draws state paths for new measurement sequences without per-sequence optimization. In addition, we connect NormFlowSDE to stochastic continuous-time dynamics by proving weak convergence of a mean-centered, drift-corrected inference scheme to an Itô diffusion under stated assumptions. Finally, we benchmark NormFlowSDE against direct estimators and learned particle filters based on normalizing flows on partially observed dynamical systems, including simulated systems and real-world robot datasets. The results show that NormFlowSDE attains the lowest estimation error with calibrated uncertainty intervals on both robot datasets and filters four times faster than the fastest particle filter on every dataset.

</details>


### [356] [GeoSDF-Diff: Geometry-Aware Diffusion Planning for Collision-Free Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27373)
*Yueming Li,Zhanjiang Yang,Yang Shen,Meng Li,Lijun Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GeoSDF-Diff——一种几何感知扩散规划器，将多尺度SDF条件注入轨迹去噪过程，在未见过的复杂障碍布局中实现可靠几何可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的运动规划器虽能建模高维多模态轨迹分布，但在未见过的复杂杂乱环境中难以可靠满足几何可行性（无碰撞、安全间隙）。

Method: 核心是让去噪器同时获得路径级几何上下文与细粒度局部间隙证据：用几何编码器提取多尺度SDF特征，并通过交叉注意力注入DiT-X去噪器；同时引入轨迹级训练目标，联合SDF间隙、违规强调、平滑性与路径长度正则，使局部不可行性在训练时即被惩罚，而非仅在推理时修正。

Result: 在留出的杂乱环境中，无需事后轨迹修复或采样时引导，GeoSDF-Diff在多种测试场景下平均成功率达65.2%，相较强扩散基线高出25.4%–28.5%。

Conclusion: 将多尺度SDF几何条件与训练期轨迹级可行性约束结合，是提升扩散规划器对未见复杂障碍布局泛化与几何可行性的有效途径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently attracted increasing attention in motion planning due to their ability to model high-dimensional and multi-modal trajectory distributions. However, existing diffusion-based planners still struggle to reliably satisfy geometric feasibility in unseen cluttered environments with complex obstacle layouts. To address this challenge, we propose GeoSDF-Diff, a geometry-aware diffusion planner that incorporates multi-scale SDF conditioning into trajectory denoising. The key idea is to provide the denoiser with both route-level geometric context and fine local clearance evidence, enabling generated trajectories to better adapt to unseen complex obstacle layouts. Specifically, a Geometric Encoder extracts multi-scale SDF features and injects them into a DiT-X denoiser through cross-attention. We further introduce a trajectory-level training objective that combines SDF clearance, violation emphasis, smoothness, and path-length regularization, so that local infeasibilities are penalized during training rather than corrected only at inference time. Experiments on held-out cluttered environments show that, without post-hoc trajectory repair or sampling-time guidance, GeoSDF-Diff achieves a 65.2% average success rate across diverse test scenarios, outperforming strong diffusion baselines by 25.4% to 28.5%.

</details>


### [357] [A Lightweight Method for Validating Simulated LiDAR Configurations Based on Point Density over Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27379)
*Benedikt Degenstein,Michael Spengler,Julian Stähler,Carsten Markgraf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、平台无关的方法，仅用3D包围框与点云评估LiDAR仿真在标注物体上的距离相关点密度保真度，并用密度相似度分数(DSS)量化真实与仿真分布的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真LiDAR对自动驾驶系统验证至关重要，但常见仿真环境的传感器模型简化、可配置性有限，难以达到信号级真实感。

Method: 无需语义标签、详细地图、仿真器内部信息或感知模型；仅基于3D包围框与点云统计标注物体上随距离变化的点密度，将距离分箱后用MAPE导出有界相似度DSS；在真实LiDAR与CARLA三种复杂度配置上对比评估。

Result: 依据数据手册的朴素参数匹配会产生系统性点密度偏差，近场尤为明显；采用传感器非均匀垂直光束角的自定义配置在评估场景中相似度最高（DSS 0.559–0.709）。

Conclusion: 该方法定位为早期信号级一致性检查，用于识别仿真LiDAR配置的显著失配，是补充性验证步骤，而非仿真质量或下游感知性能的完整度量。

Abstract: Simulated LiDAR sensors play a crucial role in validating autonomous systems, yet achieving signal-level realism remains challenging due to simplified sensor models and limited configurability in common simulation environments. This paper presents a lightweight, platform-agnostic method to assess one observable aspect of LiDAR simulation fidelity: the range-dependent point density on annotated objects. The method uses only 3D bounding boxes and point clouds and does not require semantic labels, detailed maps, simulator internals, or perception models. To quantify agreement between real and simulated range-dependent point distributions, we formulate the Density Similarity Score (DSS), a bounded similarity representation derived from the Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) over discretized distance bins. The method is evaluated against a real-world LiDAR using three CARLA configurations of varying complexity. Results show that naive parameter matching based on sensor datasheets leads to systematic point-density deviations, particularly in the near field, while a custom configuration using the sensor's non-uniform vertical beam angles achieves the highest similarity in the evaluated scenario (DSS: 0.559--0.709). The proposed approach is intended as an early-stage, signal-level consistency check for identifying gross mismatches in simulated LiDAR configurations. It should be interpreted as a complementary validation step rather than a complete measure of simulation quality or downstream perception performance.

</details>


### [358] [AnyBodyCodec: A Shared Native Interface for Body-Model Conversion, Extension, and Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27397)
*Qianxing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyBodyCodec 通过"原生转换—复用"学习跨参数化人体模型的共享表示：将 MHR、SMPL-X、SOMA 编码到对齐槽位，再用原生解码器还原目标控制参数，并在冻结核心的前提下扩展到新模型与下游策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化人体模型（MHR、SMPL-X、SOMA 等）的姿态与形状控制互不兼容，导致人形机器人策略被绑定在特定输入格式上，难以跨模型复用。

Method: 提出 AnyBodyCodec：先通过原生转换（native conversion）学习共享表示，再将编码器/解码器核心冻结复用于新体型模型与控制。编码器将各源模型映射到对齐槽位，原生解码器恢复目标控制。评估两类转换器：1.090M 参数、覆盖九条路线的转换器，以及 2.887M 参数的 Transformer bank（含参数匹配版本）；并对 ANNY 进行扩展验证。下游在匹配 PPO 预算下以对齐槽位训练策略，并部署至实体 G1 机器人。

Result: 转换器在六条跨模型路线上达到 16.801 mm 的输入-参考 MPJPE；2.887M Transformer bank 达 16.390 mm，参数匹配版为 18.866 mm。三条实测路线在 RTX 4090 上耗时 7.43–7.67 ms。加入 ANNY 显示：在冻结控制器下，相近转换精度可伴随不同的动作一致性。匹配 PPO 预算下，对齐槽位在三种格式上实现 60.78% 全动作成功率；单独 160 小时训练达到 66.10% 源加权验证成功率；控制器经三个视觉前端在实体 G1 上完成定性部署。

Conclusion: 结果表明该共享表示同时具备原生几何保真度与下游策略兼容性，可实现跨参数化人体模型的有效复用；同时说明转换精度相近并不保证动作一致性，需一并评估。

Abstract: Parametric body models expose incompatible pose and shape controls, tying humanoid policies to particular input formats. AnyBodyCodec learns a shared representation through native conversion, then reuses it for new body models and control with its core frozen. Encoders map MHR, SMPL-X, and SOMA to aligned slots; native decoders recover target controls. The 1.090M-parameter converter covers nine routes and reaches 16.801 mm input-reference MPJPE across six cross routes: the 2.887M Transformer bank reaches 16.390 mm and its parameter-matched version 18.866 mm. Three measured routes take 7.43-7.67 ms on an RTX 4090. Adding ANNY shows that comparable conversion accuracy can accompany different action consistency under one frozen controller. Under a matched PPO budget, aligned slots support 60.78% full-action success across three formats. Separate 160-hour training reaches 66.10% source-weighted validation success, and the controller is deployed qualitatively on a physical G1 through three visual front ends. Together, these results establish reuse through both native geometric fidelity and downstream policy compatibility.

</details>


### [359] [Beyond Future Views: Exploring Future-State World Models for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27418)
*Kangyi Wu,Pengna Li,Yucheng Wang,Yance Fang,Xihan Ma,Zhizhong Su,Yang Wu,Ye Deng,Jinjun Wang,Jianyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FS-Nav 提出以“目标导向空间状态+路线结构”为核心的世界模型，预测近程 waypoint 与中程 corridor，并通过自上而下与第一人称接口送入 VLM 导航器，在标准 VLN 基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN 世界模型以像素或潜在表示预测未来视图，本质上只描述“智能体能观察到什么”，而目标空间常在当前视野之外；受海马体前瞻性表征未来目标与路径中间位置的认知研究启发，作者认为应围绕与导航相关的未来空间状态与路线结构来组织未来表征。

Method: 将未来分解为两种互补抽象：短时域 waypoint（下一个待到达的局部状态）与中时域 corridor（即将经过路线的空间支撑）。FS-Nav 从空间记忆与语言指令中预测 waypoint–corridor 状态，并以自上而下（top-down）与第一人称（first-person）接口将该状态传递给 VLM 导航器，形成未来状态世界建模框架。

Result: 在标准 VLN 基准上实验表明 FS-Nav 达到 state-of-the-art 性能，验证了导航相关未来状态建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 相较于“预测未来视图”，面向导航的未来状态建模是 VLN 中表征未来的一种更有前景的方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation(VLN) requires an embodied agent to follow language instructions through unseen 3D environments, where the target spaces often lie beyond the current observation. Navigation world models address this challenge by predicting future views either as pixels or latent representations, providing valuable look-ahead information for action decision. Both paradigms define the future primarily through what the agent may observe, raising a fundamental question: is predicting future views the ultimate form to represent the future for VLN? Cognitive studies show that the human hippocampus prospectively represents both future goals and intervening locations along planned routes. Inspired by this predictive-map view, we organize the navigation-relevant future around goal-directed spatial states and route structure. We instantiate this perspective with two complementary abstractions: a short-horizon waypoint that specifies the next local state to reach, and a mid-horizon corridor that captures the spatial support of the upcoming route. Based on this design, we propose FS-Nav, a future-state world modeling framework that predicts waypoint-corridor states from spatial memory and language instructions, and communicates them to a VLM navigator through top-down and first-person interfaces. Experiments on standard VLN benchmarks demonstrate that FS-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance. This highlights navigation-relevant future-state modeling as a promising direction for representing the future in VLN.

</details>


### [360] [ADEPT: Adaptive Monocular Depth Estimation via Model-In-The-Loop Photometric Tuning with Capture Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27423)
*GiHyun Kim,JinMo Yang,Hyung-Sin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADEPT是一个利用冻结深度模型的学习反馈来自适应调节曝光与增益的框架，在九种场景-光照条件下将单目深度估计的尺度平移对齐Abs_rel平均降低14.6%（相对内置自动曝光）和10.0%（相对最优先前控制器）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曝光与增益决定了单目深度估计可用的视觉证据，而机器人运动使"多采光"与"少运动模糊"之间形成权衡；现有固定或内置自动曝光策略难以在该权衡下为深度估计提供最优输入。

Method: 训练阶段：以真值深度在一组经物理评估的相机设置中确定最低误差参考；用紧凑预测器结合回归与排序损失学习"参考相对不一致度"（RRD），衡量各候选深度预测与参考预测的差异。部署阶段：无需真值与参考图像，仅用冻结模型特征及相机、运动、光照上下文对实拍图像打分；再由上下文高斯过程控制器依据近期分数，在运动与光度约束下选择后续曝光与增益。

Result: 离线用相对与度量两种 Depth Anything v2 评估相机选择，并在移动机器人上用仿射不变变体实现在线控制。在九种等权场景-光照条件下，ADEPT相较内置自动曝光将平均尺度平移对齐Abs_rel降低14.6%，相较最优已评估先前控制器降低10.0%。

Conclusion: 借助冻结深度模型的自监督式反馈信号即可有效在线调节曝光与增益，显著提升机器人平台单目深度估计精度，且无需真值或参考图像，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Exposure and gain determine the visual evidence available to monocular depth estimation (MDE), particularly when robot motion creates a tradeoff between light collection and motion blur. We present ADEPT, a framework that adapts these settings using learned feedback from a frozen depth model. During training, ground-truth depth identifies the lowest-error reference within a group of physically evaluated camera settings. A compact predictor learns reference-relative disagreement (RRD) between each candidate's depth prediction and the reference prediction using regression and ranking losses. At deployment, it scores observed captures using frozen-model features and camera, motion, and illumination context, without ground truth or reference images. A contextual Gaussian-process controller uses recent scores to select subsequent exposure and gain settings under motion and photometric constraints. We evaluate offline camera selection with relative and metric Depth Anything v2 and demonstrate online control with the affine-invariant variant on a mobile robot. Across nine equally weighted scene-illumination conditions, ADEPT reduces mean scale-and-shift-aligned $\mathrm{Abs}_{\mathrm{rel}}$ by 14.6% relative to built-in auto-exposure and by 10.0% relative to the best-performing evaluated prior controller.

</details>


### [361] [GenPA: Image Generators as Efficient Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27446)
*Zian Deng,Tianyi Zhang,Zilong Zheng,Ziyu Liu,Tao Zhang,Yao Lyu,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GenPA：将图像生成主干直接改造为动作学习器，用19M参数的轻量动作回归适配器微调，并复用原生生成目标做感知协同训练，推理时仅需单次主干前向+动作回归，无需生成未来视觉或迭代去噪；在LIBERO达99.10%、LIBERO-Plus达83.79%，真机任务表现稳健，H100上每动作块20.9ms，较强VLA/WAM基线加速1.7-3.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代表性工作把"未来视觉预测"当作视觉生成式预训练与动作学习之间的核心桥梁，即便推理阶段不再生成未来场景，仍将其保留为训练目标。作者认为图像生成预训练所获得的指令跟随、空间理解与视觉想象能力本身即可支撑控制，无需未来视觉预测作为中介，从而避免多余的计算与架构负担。

Method: 在图像生成主干的原生图像编辑接口上，接入仅19M参数的动作回归适配器进行微调；复用原生的生成目标作为感知协同训练信号；借助统一策略架构在相同框架内公平比较"当前场景感知""未来预测"及二者组合三种视觉目标，无需任务专属感知头；部署时只做一次主干前向与动作回归，不含视觉生成与迭代式动作去噪。

Result: 在无额外大规模机器人数据预训练的前提下，仿真基准LIBERO成功率99.10%、LIBERO-Plus 83.79%，真实世界操作任务亦表现良好且对扰动鲁棒；编译推理下单个H100上每动作块耗时20.9ms，相对强VLA与WAM基线获得1.7–3.4倍加速；延迟分析揭示视觉生成主干在高效机器人控制上的架构优势；跨视觉任务及其组合的消融表明"当前深度监督"是简单有效的训练配方。

Conclusion: 未来视觉预测并非视觉生成式预训练迁移到控制所必需；图像生成模型可直接适配为高性能动作学习器，兼顾控制性能、鲁棒性与推理效率。当前场景（深度）监督是推荐的高性价比训练配方，生成主干的架构特性对实时机器人控制具有实际优势。

Abstract: Pretrained visual generation models have shown great promise in robotic manipulation. However, a representative line of work still treats future visual prediction as the central link between visual generative pretraining and action learning, retaining it as a training objective even when future scenes are no longer generated at inference time. We argue that the instruction-following, spatial understanding, and visual imagination capabilities acquired through image generation pretraining can support control without future visual prediction: image generation models themselves can be directly adapted into powerful action learners. Building on this perspective, we propose GenPA, which fine-tunes an image generation backbone with a lightweight 19M-parameter action regression adapter and reuses its native generative objective for perception co-training. The native image-editing interface accommodates different visual targets without task-specific perception heads, enabling comparisons of current-scene perception, future prediction, and their combinations within a common policy architecture. At deployment, GenPA requires only a single backbone forward pass and action regression, without visual generation or iterative action denoising. Without additional large-scale robot-data pretraining, GenPA demonstrates strong control performance and robustness to perturbations across simulation benchmarks (99.10% success on LIBERO and 83.79% on LIBERO-Plus) and real-world manipulation tasks. Under compiled inference, GenPA takes 20.9 ms per action chunk on a single H100 GPU, achieving 1.7-3.4x speedups over strong VLA and WAM baselines. Our latency analysis further highlights architectural advantages of visual generation backbones for efficient robot control. Ablations across visual tasks and their combinations identify current-depth supervision as a simple, effective training recipe.

</details>


### [362] [Mechanism Parity for Simulation-Based Validation of Autonomous-Driving Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27447)
*Danial Safaei,Siddartha Khastgir,Matthew D. Higgins,Jeroen Ploeg,Mohsen Alirezaei,Son Tong,Paul A. Jennings,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"决定性特征保真度"与"概念-决定性特征保真度"两类诊断指标，用于检验成对真实-合成自动驾驶感知中，输出一致的仿真是否也保持了感知模型作出判断的机制（机理一致性），并在KITTI-Virtual KITTI 2精确几何子集上以10个重训扩散ControlNet生成器和3个冻结SUT进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能车验证高度依赖仿真，但视觉真实感或真伪场景间输出一致性并不能证明感知系统在两域中使用了相同证据，存在"机制一致性鸿沟"（mechanism-parity gap）；现有评估缺乏对决策依据的机理级诊断。

Method: 统一Decisive-Feature Fidelity（在冻结SUT中比较决定性证据图）与Concept-Decisive Feature Fidelity（将决定性证据投影到语义场景概念以作诊断）；在确定性精确几何KITTI-Virtual KITTI 2子集上评估10个重训扩散ControlNet生成器，生成图采用1248×376填充推理画布并裁回原KITTI尺寸；最终基准从单一审计输出集重算目标图像、输出一致性与机理指标，并补充指标分歧案例、bootstrap不确定性、阈值敏感性与概念/缓存完整性审计。

Result: 对全部三个冻结SUT，输出与外观保真度和决定性机制距离仅为至多弱且不一致的秩相关；构造的正对照实验表明该估计器能检出输出一致性所忽略的已知植入机制。

Conclusion: 该框架作为验证诊断工具（而非独立安全证书），支持以决定性证据诊断开展智能车仿真验证；数值结论仅限声明的精确几何基准、10个生成器与3个冻结SUT。

Abstract: Simulation is central to intelligent-vehicle validation, but visual realism or output agreement between a real scene and its synthetic counterpart does not prove that a perception system used the same evidence in both domains. This paper addresses that mechanism-parity gap for paired real-synthetic autonomous-driving perception. It unifies Decisive-Feature Fidelity, which compares decisive evidence maps in a frozen system under test (SUT), with Concept-Decisive Feature Fidelity, which projects decisive evidence into semantic scene concepts for diagnosis. The framework is a validation diagnostic rather than a standalone safety certificate: it asks whether output-consistent simulation also preserves the mechanism by which the perception model reaches that output. The empirical evidence comprises ten retrained diffusion ControlNet generators evaluated on a deterministic exact-geometry KITTI-Virtual KITTI 2 subset. Generated counterparts use a 1248-by-376-pixel padded inference canvas and are cropped back to the exact KITTI dimensions before evaluation. The final benchmark reports target-image, output-consistency, and mechanism metrics recomputed from a single audited output set, then adds metric-disagreement cases, bootstrap uncertainty, threshold sensitivity, and concept/cache integrity audits. Across the benchmark, output and appearance fidelity are at most weakly and inconsistently rank-correlated with decisive-mechanism distance for all three frozen SUTs, and a constructed positive control shows the estimator detects a known planted mechanism that output agreement misses, supporting decisive-evidence diagnostics for intelligent-vehicle simulation validation. All numerical claims are restricted to the declared exact-geometry benchmark, ten generators, and three frozen SUTs.

</details>


### [363] [ROSWORLD: When Digital Task Competence Meets Physical Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27467)
*Caoyuan Ma,Wendi Liu,Zhimin Zhang,Muyao Niu,Yujie Lu,Zengxi Zhang,Ji Zhao,Shuai Dong,Wenpu Liu,Ziyue Wang,Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Weichu Xie,Yuqi Xu,Haiyuan Wan,Xuehui Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Wenqi Shao,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ROSWorld 基准，将物理仿真的机器人工作站与实时 OSWorld 桌面耦合，在同一评测器下衡量机器人通过物理接触操作电脑的能力，揭示了"知道怎么做"与"物理上做到"之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体已能通过直接软件输入完成多数桌面任务，但机器人必须把 GUI 知识转化为对键盘鼠标的物理接触操作。现有基准无法在同一目标和同一官方评测器下比较"软件层操作"与"物理层操作"的能力落差，缺乏可量化该落差的测试环境。

Method: 构建 ROSWorld 基准：一端为物理仿真的机器人工作站，另一端为实时运行的 OSWorld 桌面，两者共享同一任务目标与官方评测器。被测模型仅从工作站相机视角获取观测，并通过单臂刚性探针或双臂灵巧手操作外设。实验覆盖五个前沿模型、十个应用中的 40 个任务，并进行配对轨迹分析。

Result: 桌面端能完成 31–39 个任务的模型，使用探针时最多完成 23 个，使用双臂灵巧手时最多完成 28 个。配对轨迹分析显示，模型会调整交互策略、更多转向键盘输入，但物理执行层面常因按键未命中、目标定位错误和意外接触而失败。

Conclusion: ROSWorld 使"知道在电脑上做什么"与"物理上真正做到"之间的差距变得可测量，为具身计算机使用智能体的研究提供了统一的评测平台与明确的改进方向。

Abstract: Computer-use agents now solve most desktop tasks through direct software input, but a robot using the same computer must turn GUI knowledge into physical contact with a keyboard and mouse. We introduce ROSWorld, a benchmark that couples a physically simulated robot workstation to a live OSWorld desktop, so that a robot and a desktop agent pursue the same goals under the same official evaluators. The evaluated model receives observations only from the workstation camera views and operates the peripherals through either a single-arm rigid probe or dual-arm dexterous hands. Across five frontier models and 40 tasks in ten applications, models that complete 31-39 tasks on the desktop complete at most 23 with the probe and 28 with the hands. Paired trajectory analysis shows that models adapt their interaction strategies toward keyboard input, yet physical execution frequently fails through missed presses, incorrect targets, and unintended contacts. ROSWorld makes the gap between knowing what to do on a computer and physically doing it measurable.

</details>


### [364] [NavRSI: Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27499)
*Guangzhao Dai,Guoxin Lian,Shuo Wang,Shengbin Yue,Qianru Sun,Xiangbo Shu,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavRSI 是一个以"执行框架（harness）"为中心的递归自我改进框架，通过闭环经验迭代优化冻结大模型的导航行为，在 R2R-CE val-unseen 上以 GPT-6-Astra 达到 85.64% 成功率，超过此前最佳 4.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 LLM 已能通过闭环感知—动作实现视觉语言导航（VLN），但性能高度依赖支配 LLM 与环境交互的"harness"（工具、记忆、智能体角色）；现有 harness 多为人工设计，成本高且难以随模型与环境自适应，因此需要一个能自动优化 harness 的机制。

Method: 提出 NavRSI：每轮迭代采集导航轨迹与失败诊断，由 designer agent 重写 harness，仅保留被验证有效的修订，并使 designer 从过往修订中积累经验，实现 harness 与设计经验的协同演化；仿真器的特权诊断信息仅提供给 designer，对导航 LLM 不可见，从而部署时仅需单目 RGB 相机与原子动作，无需任务特定训练或额外导航工具。

Result: 在来自四家厂商的多种 LLM 上均取得稳定提升；使用 GPT-6-Astra 在完整 R2R-CE val-unseen 划分上达到 85.64% 成功率，较此前报告最佳结果提升 4.13%；在 RxR-CE、NavRAG-CE 及真实人形机器人上的评测进一步验证了泛化性。

Conclusion: 以 harness 为中心、基于闭环失败诊断的递归自我改进，可在不更新模型参数、不依赖专用工具的前提下显著提升冻结 LLM 的 VLN 能力，并具备跨数据集与真实平台的可迁移性。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have enabled vision-and-language navigation (VLN) through direct closed-loop perception and action. However, navigation performance depends strongly on the harness governing LLM-environment interactions, while existing harnesses largely rely on manually designed navigation tools, memory, and agent roles. We introduce NavRSI, a harness-centric recursive self-improvement framework that optimizes the navigation behavior of frozen LLMs through closed-loop experience. In each round, NavRSI iteratively collects navigation trajectories and failure diagnostics, employs a designer agent to rewrite the harness, and retains the revisions that prove effective, while the designer accumulates experience from previous revisions so that the harness and this experience co-evolve. Privileged simulator diagnostics guide only the designer and remain inaccessible to the navigating LLM, enabling deployment with a single RGB camera and primitive actions, without task-specific model training or additional navigation tools. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements across LLMs from four vendors, with 85.64% success rate on the full R2R-CE val-unseen split with GPT-6-Astra, surpassing the previously reported best by 4.13%. Evaluations on RxR-CE, NavRAG-CE, and a real humanoid robot further demonstrate its generalizability.

</details>


### [365] [Ego4Gen: Generating Geometry-Grounded Motion Tokens as a World-Action Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27513)
*Yang Liu,Chao Ye,Jiahao Zhang,Zeyuan Hou,Ruijie Fang,Xiyin Ni,Xinyang Ren,Weiyang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Ego4Gen，一个面向机器人操作的第一视角 4D 未来生成世界模型，联合生成时间对齐的 RGB 帧、稠密点图与场景流，并将几何-运动潜变量作为世界-动作接口，配合 FlowInverse 逆动力学网络映射为机器人动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要同时理解外观演化、空间结构与运动，现有视频潜变量或潜动作方法缺乏显式几何约束，在分布外场景下泛化差，且高度依赖动作标注数据。作者希望建立以几何为锚的 4D 表示为世界-动作接口。

Method: 基于 Cosmos3-Nano 预训练生成骨干，通过模态路由 LoRA 与非对称注意力扩展多模态输出；以 MotionCrafter 生成的几何伪标签监督学习未来几何与运动潜变量；潜变量既可解码为显式 3D 结构与运动，也可经两阶段训练的轻量逆动力学网络 FlowInverse 映射为机器人动作。

Result: 在分布外设置下，所提几何与运动潜变量优于对比的潜动作与视频潜变量基线；闭环策略评估进一步验证了将预测 4D 未来转化为机器人控制的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果支持将"几何锚定的运动 token"作为世界-动作接口，将 4D 未来生成与机器人操作相连接，并指向减少对动作标注数据依赖的路径。

Abstract: We present Ego4Gen (Egocentric 4D Future Generation), a world model for 4D future generation in robotic manipu lation. Given a single egocentric image and a text prompt, Ego4Gen jointly generates temporally aligned RGB frames, dense point maps, and dense scene flow, explicitly pre dicting the evolution of scene appearance, spatial struc ture, and motion. Built on Cosmos3-Nano, Ego4Gen ex tends the pretrained generative backbone through modality routed LoRA and asymmetric attention, using geometric pseudo-labels from MotionCrafter to learn to predict future geometry and motion latents. These latents can either be decoded into explicit 3D structure and motion or mapped to robot actions. To this end, we also introduce FlowIn verse, a lightweight inverse dynamics network trained in two stages to translate predicted 4D latents into robot ac tions. Experiments show that these geometry and motion latents outperform the evaluated latent-action and video latent baselines in out-of-distribution settings. Closed loop policy evaluations further demonstrate their utility for translating predicted 4D futures into robot control. To gether, these results support geometry-grounded motion to kens as a world-action interface, connecting 4D future gen eration with robotic manipulation and suggesting a path to ward reduced reliance on action-labeled data.

</details>


### [366] [Joint Optimization of Fleet Size, Localization Infrastructure, and Trajectories for Multi-AUV Persistent Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27539)
*Jan Mikołajczyk,Jakub Bałenkowski,Maciej Borowiecki,Jan Ciecierski,Marcel Kolecki,Julian Maleta,Maksymilian Bernacki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文通过仿真比较车道式覆盖、有偏随机游走与Voronoi分区三类轨迹策略，揭示定位精度、编队规模与轨迹设计对多AUV持续监测"时间覆盖"的交互影响，并提出融合成本感知评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模海底基础设施（海上风电基础、输出电缆、巡检走廊）的持续监测难点在于时间覆盖而非空间覆盖：需在有限感知、导航不确定性与受限载体资源下，保证所有感兴趣区域在限定时间间隔内被重访。现有集成式导航-规划方法常掩盖定位精度提升与轨迹协同设计之间的相对贡献。

Method: 构建基于仿真、定量化的多AUV持续监测研究，解耦三个交互设计维度：(i) 轨迹生成策略——确定性车道式（割草机）、带边界约束的有偏随机游走、Voronoi镶嵌协同分区；(ii) 编队规模；(iii) 由固定声学信标基础设施提供的定位精度。并在两类作业模式下评估（全工作域持续监测 vs 关键设施定向巡检），引入将编队规模、信标数量与任务能耗统一为单一成本函数的成本感知评估框架。

Result: 结果表明：定位精度超过某一阈值后收益递减，其对时间覆盖的边际贡献不及协同轨迹设计，该效应在集成导航-规划方法中常被遮蔽；协同分区轨迹可用更小编队实现更优时间覆盖，全生命周期成本较未针对定位基础设施优化的刚性确定性路径低39–46%。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优策略，策略收益取决于定位精度水平与作业模式；应优先投资协同轨迹设计而非单纯提高定位精度，采用成本感知指标可指导编队规模、信标部署与轨迹策略的联合权衡。

Abstract: Persistent monitoring of large-scale subsea infrastructure, such as offshore wind farm foundations, export cables, and inspection corridors, remains a fundamental challenge for autonomous underwater systems. The problem is not solely spatial coverage, but rather temporal coverage: ensuring that all relevant regions of interest are revisited within bounded time intervals under limited sensing, navigation uncertainty, and constrained vehicle resources. We compare three classes of trajectory strategies: (1) deterministic lane-based coverage (lawnmower patterns), (2) stochastic exploration (biased random walk with boundary constraints), and (3) coordinated partition-based strategies (Voronoi tessellation). Rather than seeking a universally superior method, we evaluate how varying levels of localization accuracy impact temporal coverage across two distinct operational profiles: persistent monitoring of the entire operational workspace versus targeted inspection of critical infrastructure. We present a simulation-based, quantitatively grounded study of temporal coverage in multi-AUV persistent monitoring, disentangling three interacting design dimensions: (i) trajectory generation strategy, (ii) fleet size, and (iii) localization accuracy provided by fixed acoustic beacon infrastructure. We introduce a cost-aware evaluation framework combining fleet size, number of beacons, and mission energy expenditure into a unified cost function. Results demonstrate that beyond a certain accuracy threshold, improvements in localization yield diminishing returns compared to coordinated trajectory design, an effect often obscured in integrated navigation-planning approaches. Coordinated-partition trajectories enable a reduced fleet size to achieve superior temporal coverage at a 39-46% lower overall life-cycle cost than rigid deterministic paths unoptimized for the localization infrastructure.

</details>


### [367] [Where Delay Compensation Belongs in a Decentralized Mean-Field-Game Swarm with a Control-Barrier Safety Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27540)
*Jan Mikołajczyk,Igor Rostkowski,Bartosz Ogórkiewicz,Szymon Skierski,Sylwia Opat,Wojciech Rybak,Mikołaj Sczogiel,Radosław Pałys,Jan Gogulski,Bartosz Kościołek,Andrzej Pijanowski,Szymon Kowalski,Marcin Misiewicz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究TDMA受限通信下无人机集群的MFG路由场+逐机CBF安全滤波的分层架构，发现在仿真中时延只破坏安全层，并用轻量在线预测器补偿时延、恢复安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人团队常采用"先规划后执行"架构，去中心化无人机集群由平均场博弈(MFG)路由场与逐智能体控制障碍函数(CBF)安全滤波组成。但共享TDMA信道下邻居只能通过轮询时隙中的含噪距离/方位测量获知，帧长及所有估计的时延随集群规模增长，需弄清这种时延对两层的影响并加以补偿。

Method: 在仿真中做"层分裂"实验：分别考察时延对MFG路由层与CBF安全层的影响（对比噪声每一步重抽与整帧保持两种情形），并引入轻量在线预测器——从匀速(constant-velocity)拟合到小型学习网络——依据邻居最近的广播估计其位置；同时对比简单的"常值加大安全裕度"方案。
